### [1] [Structured Extraction of Diffraction Data from Covalent Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23680)
*Shenghan Gao,Zhiling Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出视觉大语言模型（vision LLM）与经典计算机视觉（classical CV）结合的流水线，将文献中的粉末X射线衍射（PXRD）图像自动还原为校准曲线，并对3,954篇COF论文提取出11,445条曲线，构建开放数据库COF-PEAK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PXRD是共价有机框架（COFs）等材料的主要结构证据，但数十年的实测图谱仅以已发表图片形式存在，格式差异大（单曲线、密集堆叠、含标签与插图），传统计算机视觉难以自动恢复，导致数据无法复用。

Method: 由视觉大语言模型解析整幅图的版面布局，定位衍射图及其曲线并给出粗略轨迹；再由经典计算机视觉（CV）将每条轨迹精修至像素级精度，从而获得校准后的衍射曲线。

Result: 应用于3,954篇COF文献，产出11,445条校准衍射曲线并发布于开放数据库COF-PEAK；与60个图面板的人工数字化结果相比，提取峰位中位偏差仅0.07度。

Conclusion: 数字化曲线突破了原文仅报告少数峰角的局限，可用于追踪峰显著度（peak prominence，可能作为结晶度（crystallinity）标志）随合成条件的变化，表明视觉LLM与经典CV可将此类图件转化为可复用的实验数据。

Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) is the primary structural evidence for covalent organic frameworks (COFs) and many other materials. Yet decades of measured patterns exist only as images in published figures. Automatic recovery is difficult, because PXRD figures vary widely in format, from a single curve to crowded stacks with labels, insets, and neighboring plots that challenge traditional computer vision. This work presents a pipeline in which a vision large language model (LLM) interprets the layout of each figure, locating the diffraction plot and its curves and sketching a coarse trace of each, while classical computer vision (CV) refines every trace to pixel-level accuracy. Applied to 3,954 COF articles, the pipeline produces 11,445 calibrated diffraction curves, released in the open database COF-PEAK. Compared with a hand digitization of 60 panels, extracted peak positions differ by a median of only 0.07 degrees. Whereas papers usually report only a few peak angles, which limits further analysis, the digitized curves enable tracking how peak prominence, a possible marker of crystallinity, changes with synthesis conditions. Vision LLMs and classical CV can thus turn such figures into reusable experimental data.

</details>

### [2] [Nonlinear optical response with relaxation in the velocity gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23400)
*Carlos M. Pedrosa,João P. C. Ferreira,João Pinho,Yu. V. Bludov,B. Amorim,João M. Viana Parente Lopes*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于速度规范（velocity gauge）与弛豫时间近似（relaxation time approximation, RTA）的框架，用于计算均匀光场驱动下晶体中电子的非线性光电导（nonlinear optical conductivity），并证明其与绝热开关（adiabatic switching）处理耗散的方式存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有基于长度规范（length gauge）的非线性光电导公式需计算布洛赫态（Bloch states）对动量的导数，计算繁琐；同时常用的“频率中加入虚阻尼项”这一绝热开关做法能否等价于弛豫时间近似尚不明确。

Method: 采用最小光-物质耦合（minimal light-matter coupling）的速度规范哈密顿量，对单粒子约化密度矩阵（single-particle reduced density matrix）的运动方程做微扰求解并引入RTA耗散；并提出一种微扰方法评估系统弛豫所趋向的参考平衡密度矩阵（reference equilibrium density matrix），该矩阵在速度规范下绝热跟随矢势并因耗散而展宽。

Result: 方法经与长度规范结果对照验证；在具有交错子晶格势（staggered sublattice potential）与周期调制的类石墨烯紧束缚模型（graphene-like tight-binding model）中计算了二阶与三阶响应。发现RTA结果在定量与定性上均偏离绝热开关：在相同弛豫参数下，带隙以上的主导注入（injection）贡献相差约两倍；带隙以下，数值结果显示在RTA小弛豫极限下存在有限的虚部直流响应。

Conclusion: 绝热开关一般不能复现通过运动方程描述的弛豫过程，即使在小弛豫极限下亦如此；速度规范形式可避免布洛赫态动量导数的繁琐计算，为非线性光电导评估提供了可行替代方案。

Abstract: We develop a framework for the evaluation of the nonlinear optical conductivity of electrons in crystals, driven by homogeneous optical fields, taking into account dissipation effects within the relaxation time approximation (RTA). The present formalism is based on Hamiltonians with minimal light-matter coupling within the velocity gauge, where the electric field is expressed in terms of a vector potential. The nonlinear conductivities are obtained by perturbatively solving the equation of motion for the single-particle reduced density matrix, which includes dissipative effects via the relaxation time approximation. We present a perturbative method to evaluate the reference equilibrium density matrix, towards which dissipation relaxes the system, which in the velocity gauge adiabatically follows the applied vector potential, and is also broadened by dissipative effects. Comparing with previous formulations based on the length gauge, the present approach has the benefit of avoiding the cumbersome evaluation of derivatives of Bloch states with respect to momentum. We test the presented method against length-gauge results, and calculate second- and third-order responses in a graphene-like tight-binding model with a staggered sublattice potential and a periodic modulation. We find that the relaxation time approximation gives results that deviate both quantitatively and qualitatively from the simple inclusion of an imaginary damping term in the frequencies, referred to as adiabatic switching. At equal relaxation parameter, the leading above-gap injection contribution differs by a factor of two; below the gap, the numerical results indicate a finite imaginary dc response in the small-relaxation limit of the relaxation-time calculation. These results show that adiabatic switching does not generally reproduce relaxation through the equation of motion, even in the small-relaxation limit.

</details>


### [3] [Interface Weyl electrons in the kagome-bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23787)
*Keiki Ishida,Kohei Fujiwara,Shun Sasano,Kazuki Nakazawa,Takeshi Seki,Seigo Souma,Daisuke Shiga,Hiroshi Kumigashira,Takafumi Sato,Ryo Ishikawa,Naoya Shibata,Yukitoshi Motome,Atsushi Tsukazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作提出“界面外尔电子（interface Weyl electrons）”新概念，通过在笼目双层（kagome-bilayer）界面处调控费米能（Fermi energy）来激活外尔型拓扑输运；溅射制备的D0₁₉/L1₂双层具有原子级突变界面，并表现出显著增强的电导率与本征机制下约2000 Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹的巨大反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑特征是否显著取决于其特征电子态是否靠近费米能；笼目金属（kagome metals）虽因平带（flat band）与狄拉克带（Dirac band）具备独特拓扑性质，但在该类材料中通过费米能调控来凸显这些性质的研究仍很少。

Method: 采用溅射技术（sputtering）制备保持六方D0₁₉与立方L1₂结构晶体对称性的笼目双层，抑制互扩散以获得原子级突变界面；并结合理论能带计算（band calculations）识别界面处的外尔节点（Weyl nodes）。

Result: 双层样品的电导率远高于单层薄膜，表明界面处涌现出高导电电子态；本征机制下反常霍尔电导约为2000 Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹，异常之大，指向由非平庸电荷转移（charge transfer）触发的D0₁₉结构中的外尔节点。

Conclusion: 结果表明拓扑输运可在金属间化合物（intermetallic compounds）界面处被工程化调控，为器件功能开发提供新的设计原则。

Abstract: What makes a topological character prominent is whether its characteristic electronic states are located near the Fermi energy. While the kagome-metals possess unique topological characteristics owing to their distinctive electronic band structures, i.e. flat and Dirac bands, the control of Fermi energy to accentuate these characteristics has scarcely been studied in these materials. Here, we propose a new concept for activating the topological characteristics of Weyl electrons specifically near the interface of kagome-bilayers, namely interface Weyl electrons. The kagome-bilayers prepared by a sputtering technique surprisingly exhibit atomically abrupt interfaces thanks to the suppression of interdiffusion for maintaining the crystal symmetry of the hexagonal-type D0$_{19}$ and cubic-type L1$_{2}$ structures. Much higher electrical conductivity of the kagome-bilayers compared to single-layer films indicates the emergent contribution of a highly conductive electronic state at the interfaces. The enhanced electrical conductivity and the exceptionally large anomalous Hall conductivity about 2000 $Ω$$^{-1}$cm$^{-1}$ in the intrinsic mechanism indicates that the emergent interface electronic states comprise Weyl nodes in the D0$_{19}$ structure identified in our theoretical band calculations, triggered by nontrivial charge transfer. These results demonstrate that topological transport can be engineered at the interface of intermetallic compounds, leading further design principles towards development of device functionality.

</details>


### [4] [Electrostatic and Quantum Transport Engineering of Multilayer Ferroelectric Tunnel Junctions for Improved TER and RA Trade off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24103)
*Balram Khattar,Manish Anand,Abhishek Sharma*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作采用Miller铁电极化模型与非平衡格林函数（NEGF）方法，系统研究TiN/HZO基铁电隧道结（FTJ）中量子阱（QW）材料（TiO₂与IGZO）与厚度对极化及输运的影响，优选出非均匀TiO₂双量子阱结构，在0.1 V读偏压下实现2.95×10¹¹%的TER与1.25×10³ Ω·cm²的RA乘积，显著改善TER–RA权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电隧道结（ferroelectric tunnel junction, FTJ）在非易失存储与神经形态计算（neuromorphic computing）中具有前景，但难以同时实现高隧穿电阻比（tunnel electroresistance ratio, TER）与低电阻面积（resistance-area, RA）乘积，需要探索量子阱（quantum well, QW）工程对TER–RA权衡的调控潜力。

Method: 构建含0至3层TiO₂或IGZO基量子阱的TiN/Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂（HZO）基FTJ模型，将Miller铁电（FE）极化模型与非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green's function, NEGF）形式结合，计算不同QW材料与厚度下的极化与输运特性，并考察弹性散射（elastic scattering）的影响。

Result: TiO₂基结构极化与TER均高于IGZO基结构：0.1 V读偏压下，均匀TiO₂双量子阱（QW与绝缘层IL均为1 nm）给出TER=2.71×10¹⁰%、RA=5×10⁴ Ω·cm²，而IGZO对应为5.95×10⁷%与2×10⁵ Ω·cm²。均匀与非均匀TiO₂双量子阱均呈现与QW限域态相关的共振隧穿（resonant tunneling, RT）与负微分电阻（negative differential resistance, NDR）；将第一阱厚增至1.38 nm可移动限域态能级，所选非均匀结构在0.1 V下达到TER=2.95×10¹¹%、RA=1.25×10³ Ω·cm²。弹性散射改变共振透射峰幅度与宽度，对低阻态（LRS）影响强于高阻态（HRS）。

Conclusion: 量子阱材料与厚度的优化可改善TER–RA权衡，且电极屏蔽（electrode screening）与弹性散射进一步影响多层FTJ中依赖极化的输运行为，为非易失存储与神经形态器件设计提供依据。

Abstract: Ferroelectric tunnel junctions (FTJs) are promising for non-volatile memory and neuromorphic computing, but achieving a high tunnel electroresistance ratio (TER) with a low resistance-area (RA) product remains challenging. In this work, TiN/Hf$_{0.5}$Zr$_{0.5}$O$_2$ (HZO)-based FTJs containing zero to three TiO$_2$ and IGZO-based quantum wells (QWs) are analyzed using the Miller ferroelectric (FE) polarization model integrated with the non-equilibrium Green's function (NEGF) formalism to examine the effects of QW material and thickness on polarization and transport. TiO$_2$-based structures exhibit higher polarization and TER than IGZO-based structures. At a read bias of 0.1 V, the uniform TiO$_2$ double-QW structure, with 1-nm-thick QW and insulating layer (IL) layers, gives a TER of $2.71\times10^{10}\%$ and an RA product of $5\times10^4~Ω\cdot\text{cm}^2$, compared with $5.95\times10^7\%$ and $2\times10^5~Ω\cdot\text{cm}^2$, respectively, for IGZO. Both uniform and non-uniform TiO$_2$ double-QW structures exhibit resonant tunneling (RT) and negative differential resistance (NDR) associated with QW-confined states. Increasing the first QW thickness to 1.38 nm, while keeping the second QW and both IL layers at 1 nm, shifts the confined-state energies and modifies resonant transport. The selected non-uniform structure achieves a TER of $2.95\times10^{11}\%$ and an RA product of $1.25\times10^3~Ω\cdot\text{cm}^2$ at a read bias of 0.1 V. Elastic scattering changes the amplitude and width of QW resonant-transmission peaks, with a stronger effect on the low resistance state (LRS) than on the high resistance state (HRS). These results show that QW material and thickness optimization can improve the TER--RA trade-off, while electrode screening and elastic scattering further influence polarization-dependent transport in multilayer FTJs.

</details>


### [5] [Conformal defects in a quasi-one-dimensional Kitaev chain: an exactly parametrized Ising defect line and a relevant vacancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24312)
*R. L. Silva. R. C. Silva,A. R. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该论文将 Majumder 提出的六角 plaquette 耦合准一维 Kitaev 链（Kitaev chain）约化为均匀 Majorana 链（Majorana chain），从而精确求得其拓扑临界点、缺陷传输系数与纠缠熵系数，并给出缺陷与 vacancy 的完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 plaquette 模型虽可由自由 Majorana 费米子（free Majorana fermions）精确求解并在 J_x=√2 J 处发生拓扑转变（topological transition），但其缺陷物理（缺陷键、vacancy 等）缺乏统一、可解析的描述，需要一个可归结为均匀链的简化框架。

Method: 通过消除每个 plaquette 上的二配位格点（two-coordinated sites），把零能问题（zero-energy problem）降维为均匀 Majorana 链；所得单 Majorana 锥（Majorana cone）位于 k=0，对应中心荷 c=1/2 的 Ising 临界点。缺陷以弱化 plaquette 间键 λJ_x 表示，并映射到 Ising 共形场论（conformal field theory）的铁磁缺陷线（ferromagnetic defect line）上，用参数 tanφ=λ 刻画传输系数。

Result: plaquette 间缺陷键为严格边缘（exactly marginal），低能传输率 T=4λ²/(1+λ²)²=sin²2φ；plaquette 内部 y、z 键对应 tanφ=(1+λ)/2，仅覆盖 T∈[0.64,1]（因 plaquette 提供并行通道），x 键对应 tanφ=(1+λ)/2λ，覆盖强键半区 φ∈(π/4,π/2)。跨缺陷纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）中 ln L 系数在整条缺陷线上与 Brehm–Brunner 的 CFT 函数 σ(√T)/2 吻合至 0.4% 以内，且无可调参数。vacancy 不束缚 plaquette 磁通（因 plaquettes 不共享格点），但使其单键邻居成为守恒经典自旋，其悬挂 Majorana 为零能共振（zero-energy resonance）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 plaquette Kitaev 链的缺陷物理可完全由 Ising CFT 缺陷线描述，并以传输率与纠缠熵系数两个独立可观测量的定量一致性作为验证；vacancy 则表现出与弱键缺陷相反的、无磁通束缚但带零能共振的行为。

Abstract: The quasi-one-dimensional Kitaev chain of coupled hexagonal plaquettes recently introduced by Majumder is exactly solvable in terms of free Majorana fermions and undergoes a topological transition at the exchange anisotropy $J_x=\sqrt{2}\,J$. By eliminating the two-coordinated sites of each plaquette we reduce the zero-energy problem to a uniform Majorana chain, which yields the critical point, a single Majorana cone at $k=0$ (hence an Ising critical point with central charge $c=\tfrac12$), and closed forms for every defect quantity that follows. A weakened inter-plaquette bond $λJ_x$ is an exactly marginal defect with transmission $T=4λ^2/(1+λ^2)^2$ at low energy, that is, $T=\sin^2 2φ$ with $\tanφ=λ$: the bond traverses the ferromagnetic defect line of the Ising conformal field theory from the transparent point to the cut chain, and we give its exact energy dependence on the lattice. The intra-plaquette bonds land on the same line with different parametrizations: a $y$ or $z$ bond has $\tanφ=(1+λ)/2$ and covers only $T\in[0.64,1]$, because the plaquette provides a parallel path, whereas an $x$ bond has $\tanφ=(1+λ)/2λ$ and covers the strong-link half of the line, $φ\in(π/4,π/2)$. The identification is closed by an independent observable: the coefficient of $\ln L$ in the entanglement entropy across the defect follows the conformal-field-theory function $σ(\sqrt T)/2$ of Brehm and Brunner to within $0.4\%$ along the whole line, with no adjustable parameter. A vacancy behaves in the opposite way. It never binds a plaquette flux, because plaquettes share no sites, but it leaves its singly bonded neighbor as a conserved classical spin whose dangling Majorana is a zero-energy resonance.

</details>

### [6] [Cross-probing van der Waals multiferroics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23337)
*Bo Peng,Zeya Li,Han Wang,Shengdong You,Yangliu Wu,Wei Liu,Jianliang Xie,Haipeng Lu,Peiheng Zhou,Kian Ping Loh,Je-Geun Park,Hongtao Yuan,Longjiang Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦二维多铁材料（2D multiferroics），以实验方法学为主线，评估磁性与铁电性的各类探测手段及其局限，主张采用磁-光-电交叉关联测量（magneto-opto-electric cross-correlative measurements）来确证本征多铁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多铁材料可通过电场替代电流实现磁翻转，有望用于低功耗存储与逻辑器件；将维度从三维降至二维可增强磁电耦合（magnetoelectric coupling）。但本征多铁性的实验判定存在困难，需要系统审视现有探测方法的可靠性。

Method: 采用批判性综述（Review）方式，以实验方法学而非材料分类为核心组织内容；评述磁性（magnetism）与铁电性（ferroelectricity）的传统探测手段，指出单一技术路径的关键局限，并以少层NiI2为例展开讨论。

Result: 分析表明单一技术手段难以无歧义地确认本征多铁性；磁-光-电交叉关联测量对确立本征多铁行为具有关键作用。文中还总结了少层NiI2中手性自旋结构（chiral spin textures）与（反）铁电性共存所衍生的新奇物性。

Conclusion: 确立本征二维多铁性需依赖交叉关联实验证据；文末展望了基础研究与器件应用两方面的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Multiferroic materials, with coupled electric and magnetic orders, hold the potential to enable low-power memory and logic devices by replacing electrical currents with electric fields for magnetic switching. Reducing the dimensionality of multiferroics, from 3D to 2D, has opened new frontiers in this already burgeoning field by enabling unprecedented control over magnetoelectric coupling that is beyond the reach of bulk counterparts, owing to reduced electrostatic screening and enhanced quantum fluctuations at the atomically thin limit. A notable example is a few-layer NiI2 multiferroic, which exhibits coexisting chiral spin textures and (anti)ferroelectricity, accompanied by strong magnetoelectric coupling; by simultaneously breaking time-reversal and spatial-inversion symmetry, NiI2 shows new physical properties such as unique mutual control of chiral magnetism and polarization with giant magnetoelectric coupling, ultrafast electromagnon excitations and odd-parity p-wave magnetism. However, there are experimental challenges in unambiguously identifying intrinsic multiferroicity. This Review critically examines the current landscape of 2D multiferroic materials, with an emphasis on experimental methodologies rather than material taxonomy. We assess different conventional probes for magnetism and ferroelectricity and identify key limitations in single-technique approaches. We emphasize the importance of magneto-opto-electric cross-correlative measurements in establishing intrinsic multiferroic behaviour and outline future research directions for both fundamental studies and device applications.

</details>

</details>


### [8] [Predicting solid-state reaction onset temperatures from high-throughput pairwise synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23527)
*Lauren N. Walters,Matthew J. McDermott,Bernardus Rendy,Yuxing Fei,Gerbrand Ceder*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过自动化实验室对1,035组前驱体组合的Tammann温度实验与文献数据挖掘，构建对数正态加速失效时间模型，将反应起始温度预测的平均绝对误差从131°C降至88°C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固相合成（solid-state synthesis）的反应温度选择长期依赖经验规则，最常用的Tammann规则（Tammann's rule）以最低前驱体熔点的三分之二作为反应起始温度，但从未被系统检验，缺乏数据驱动的可靠依据。

Method: 利用自动化实验室（automated laboratory）在Tammann温度下加热46种常见前驱体的1,035组两两组合，并用X射线衍射（x-ray diffraction）鉴定产物；结合文本挖掘的文献合成数据，训练对数正态加速失效时间模型（log-normal accelerated failure time models），将每个固定温度实验视为对未知起始温度的边界约束。

Result: 仅25%的前驱体对生成同时含两种前驱体元素（both precursors）的产物，说明Tammann规则预测能力有限；在26个由原位X射线衍射（in situ x-ray diffraction）测得起始温度的反应上，三描述符（three-descriptor）模型将平均绝对误差（mean absolute error）由131°C降至88°C，而额外加入29个物理化学描述符（physicochemical descriptors）并未带来改进。

Conclusion: 反应起始温度可由常规的通过/失败（pass/fail）合成结果预测，且随着此类数据积累，预测精度有望进一步提升，为固相合成温度选择提供数据驱动替代方案。

Abstract: Solid-state synthesis still relies on heuristics to choose reaction temperatures. The most widely used, Tammann's rule, places reaction onset at two-thirds of the lowest precursor melting point, but has never been systematically tested. Using an automated laboratory, we heated 1,035 pairwise combinations of 46 common precursors at their Tammann temperatures and identified the products by x-ray diffraction. Only 25% of pairs formed products containing both precursors. Combining these outcomes with text-mined literature syntheses, we trained log-normal accelerated failure time models that treat each fixed-temperature experiment as a bound on the unknown onset temperature. On 26 reactions with onset temperatures measured by in situ x-ray diffraction, a three-descriptor model reduced the mean absolute error from 131°C (Tammann's rule) to 88°C; adding 29 further physicochemical descriptors did not improve the model. Reaction onset can therefore be predicted from routine pass/fail synthesis outcomes, and predictions will improve as such data accumulates.

</details>


### [9] [Application of Pretrained Universal Machine Learning Force-Fields for Phase Transitions Prediction in ZrO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23535)
*Rajan Khadka,Axel van de Walle,Patrick Tepesch*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以氧化锆（ZrO₂）为对象，用声子色散与变胞等温等压分子动力学基准测试十种开源通用机器学习力场，检验其能否复现单斜-四方-立方-液体的相变序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多只考察静态性质，未评估预训练通用机器学习力场（universal machine-learning force fields）能否再现正确的有限温度相变序列，因而对相变预测能力的筛选缺乏依据。

Method: 采用声子色散计算（phonon dispersion）与变胞等温等压分子动力学（variable-cell isothermal-isobaric molecular dynamics），对十种开源通用力场在ZrO₂上进行基准测试，并与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）参考对照；利用熔点前单斜-四方-立方三个热力学稳定相作为检验序列。

Result: 仅7net-l3i5、MatterSim-v1.0.0-1M、MACE-mpa和MACE-D3四种模型复现了单斜-四方-立方序列；正交-四方能量误差超过约10 meV/atom的7net-0、CHGNet和MACE则给出正交中间相；ORB类模型声子频率较DFT参考压缩30-60%，无法维持稳定的变胞动力学。

Conclusion: 两项基态诊断可预测有限温度行为：多形相对能量（relative polymorph energetics）应与匹配的DFT参考在约6 meV/atom内一致，且声子谱不应出现系统性软化（systematic softening），可作为预训练力场相变预测的实用筛选指标。

Abstract: Pretrained universal machine-learning force fields can reduce the cost of finite-temperature molecular dynamics relative to density functional theory. However, most benchmarks test only static properties and do not assess whether these models reproduce the correct phase-transition sequence. Here, we benchmark ten open-source universal machine-learning force fields for zirconia (ZrO$_2$) using phonon dispersion calculations and variable-cell isothermal-isobaric molecular dynamics. Zirconia is a useful test because it has three thermodynamically stable crystalline phases prior to melting, following the sequence monoclinic-tetragonal-cubic. Four models reproduce this sequence: 7net-l3i5, MatterSim-v1.0.0-1M, MACE-mpa, and MACE-D3. Models with orthorhombic-tetragonal energy errors above about 10 meV/atom (7net-0, CHGNet, and MACE) instead produce an orthorhombic intermediate and predict monoclinic-orthorhombic-tetragonal-cubic-liquid upon heating. ORB-based models fail to sustain stable variable-cell dynamics because their phonon frequencies are compressed by 30-60% relative to the density functional theory reference. Across all ten models, two ground-state diagnostics predict finite-temperature behavior. Relative polymorph energetics should agree with the matched density functional theory reference within about 6 meV/atom, and the phonon spectrum should not show systematic softening. These diagnostics are practical screening metrics for assessing pretrained force fields for phase-transition predictions.

</details>


### [10] [Fast closed-loop STEM navigation using vision-language models with typed decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23698)
*Shuichiro Ozawa,Izumi Takahara,Fumihiko Uesugi,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究在模拟原子分辨率STEM图像上比较四类视觉语言模型（vision-language models, VLMs）与三种决策方法，发现Typed Decision在闭环STEM导航中兼具最快延迟（0.13-0.21 s）与最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环扫描透射电子显微镜（closed-loop STEM）依赖决策步骤将每幅图像转化为下一步操作，但哪种决策方法能同时满足快速与准确尚不明确；本文旨在单独隔离并评估该决策步骤。

Method: 使用单层MoS$_2$中替代式W掺杂剂与晶界的模拟原子分辨率STEM图像，在真值位置已知且所有方法初始条件一致的设定下，比较四种VLM与三种决策方法。

Result: Typed Decision（直接从预训练VLM获取候选标签概率）在所有模型上决策延迟最短，且总体导航成功率最高：W掺杂剂导航中所有模型均最优（66-91%），晶界导航中最佳模型Cosmos-Reason2 32B达43%。

Conclusion: Typed Decision是实现快速、准确且灵活闭环STEM的有效决策方法；且无需任务特定训练，仅凭自然语言指定即可切换不同导航目标。

Abstract: Closed-loop scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM), in which each acquired image determines the next field of view and magnification, offers a route to autonomous atomic-scale materials analysis. Such a loop would automate navigation to defects and grain boundaries, a task that has long relied on operator judgment, and would enable systematic analysis of diverse local structures at scale. Realizing it hinges on the decision step, which must translate each acquired image into the next operation both quickly and correctly; yet which decision method can deliver both remains unclear. Here, we isolate this decision step. Using simulated atomic-resolution STEM images of a substitutional W dopant and a grain boundary in monolayer MoS$_2$, where the true target position is known and every method starts from identical conditions, we compared four vision-language models (VLMs) and three decision methods. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of Typed Decision, which directly obtains the probabilities of candidate labels from a pretrained VLM, for closed-loop STEM navigation. Typed Decision showed the shortest decision latency for all models (0.13-0.21 s) and generally achieved the highest navigation success rate among the three methods: it was the best for all models in W dopant navigation (66-91%) and for the best-performing model, Cosmos-Reason2 32B, in grain boundary navigation (43%). Furthermore, navigation to the different targets, the W dopant and the grain boundary, was possible by natural-language specification alone, without task-specific training of the VLM. These results indicate that Typed Decision is an effective decision method for realizing fast, accurate, and flexible closed-loop STEM.

</details>


### [11] [Physics-Guided Crystal Structure Elucidation from Powder X-Ray Diffraction with Universal Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23760)
*Yunsung Lim,Seungwoo Hwang,Seungwu Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: XCAPE 是一个将实验 PXRD 数据与对称性感知进化搜索结合的晶体结构预测框架，可从粉末衍射数据中反演原子坐标并在基准与实验数据上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从粉末 X 射线衍射（PXRD）确定唯一原子坐标是欠定逆问题，受峰重叠、轻元素弱散射、相近原子序数元素散射对比度低及实验噪声制约。

Method: XCAPE（XRD-Constrained Accelerated Prediction Engine）将实验得到的成分、晶胞参数、空间群与 PXRD 数据整合进对称性感知（symmetry-aware）进化搜索，通过定制变异操作探索 Wyckoff 位点构型，以能量稳定性与衍射吻合度双重评价候选，并用通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）加速几何优化。

Result: 在模拟 PXRD 上恢复 77 个数据库基准结构中的 71 个（92.2%），并全部恢复 12 个实验 PXRD 参考结构；另识别出 12 个与实验图谱一致、ICSD 中无匹配条目且在 MLIP 层面热力学具竞争力的候选结构，其中 DFT 计算显示 10 个位于凸包（convex hull）10 meV/atom 以内。

Conclusion: XCAPE 通过桥接实验衍射数据与物理合理的原子尺度模型，可为此前未解析材料提供结构信息，并支持自动化研究工作流中的加速材料发现。

Abstract: Determining unique atomic coordinates from powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) remains challenging because peak overlap, weak scattering from light elements, limited scattering contrast between elements with similar atomic numbers, and experimental noise render the inverse problem underdetermined. Here, we present XCAPE (XRD-Constrained Accelerated Prediction Engine), a crystal structure prediction (CSP) framework that integrates experimentally derived composition, unit cell parameters, space group, and PXRD data into a symmetry-aware evolutionary search. XCAPE explores Wyckoff-site configurations using tailored mutation operations, evaluates candidates by both energetic stability and diffraction agreement, and accelerates geometry optimization using a universal machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP). The framework recovered 71 of 77 database-derived benchmark structures (92.2%) from simulated PXRD patterns and all 12 reference structures from experimental PXRD patterns. It further identified 12 candidate structure assignments that are consistent with experimental PXRD patterns, lack matching entries in the Inorganic Crystal Structure Database (ICSD), and are thermodynamically competitive at the MLIP level. Density functional theory (DFT) calculations showed that 10 of these 12 candidates lie within 10 meV/atom of the convex hull. By bridging experimental diffraction data and physically plausible atomistic models, XCAPE can provide structural insight into previously unresolved materials and support accelerated materials discovery in automated research workflows.

</details>


### [12] [Materials representations organize transferable supervision for chemical extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23837)
*Enda Xiao,Terumasa Tadano*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 预训练晶体图编码器（crystal-graph encoder）的材料表征可在无目标性质标签条件下，按化合物所提供的可迁移监督（transferable supervision）强弱对元素排序；剔除其排名前六元素对应的化合物会显著增大形成能（formation energy）与带隙（bandgap）误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现常需在标注数据之外进行化学外推（chemical extrapolation）：当目标化合物含有训练集中未出现的元素时，哪些其他元素化合物的数据能提供可迁移监督尚不明确。

Method: 利用材料表征（materials representations）在无目标性质标签情况下对元素进行监督价值排序；对比人工设计描述符（engineered descriptors）与预训练晶体图编码器（pretrained crystal-graph encoder）两类排序依据，并在固定训练集规模下通过剔除不同排名元素的化合物考察模型误差变化，跨性质、回归器（regressor）与数据集验证排序稳定性。

Result: 人工描述符的排序主要依据元素性质相似性（elemental-property similarity），而编码器依据其学习表征的几何结构排序；在固定训练集规模下，剔除编码器排名前六元素对应的化合物使形成能误差增加 100–110 meV/atom、带隙误差增加 42–85 meV，而剔除六个排名靠后元素对应的化合物则变化很小；该排序在多种性质、回归器和数据集上均成立，且编码器排序优于元素性质相似性与周期族（periodic groups）依据。

Conclusion: 材料表征能够组织可迁移监督，且该监督价值可在模型训练前被识别，从而为数据集评估（dataset assessment）与标签获取（label acquisition）提供依据；编码器的排序表现提示其刻画了元素在化合物中的行为方式。

Abstract: Materials discovery often requires chemical extrapolation beyond labeled data. When target compounds contain elements absent from training, which compounds of other elements provide transferable supervision remains unclear. Here we show that, without target-property labels, materials representations rank other elements by the supervision their compounds provide. Engineered descriptors rank them largely by elemental-property similarity, whereas a pretrained crystal-graph encoder ranks them by the geometry of its learned representation. At fixed training-set size, excluding the compounds of the encoder's six top-ranked elements raises formation-energy and bandgap errors by 100-110 meV per atom and 42-85 meV, versus little change for six lower-ranked elements. The rankings hold across properties, regressors and datasets. The encoder's ranking outperforms elemental-property similarity and periodic groups, suggesting that it captures how elements behave within compounds. Materials representations thus organize transferable supervision, which can be identified before model training, informing dataset assessment and label acquisition.

</details>


### [13] [Tuning spreading transport in GaN HEMTs with an AlN space layer under non-equilibrium heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23866)
*Daosheng Tang,Rui Hu,Chen Ning,Bingyang Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过在主热源上方插入薄层高导热 AlN 间隔层（AlN space layer）直接调控横向扩展热阻（spreading resistance），表明热输运优化受界面热导而非厚度主导，且在非平衡声子激发下优化次序发生反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaN 高电子迁移率晶体管（high-electron-mobility transistor, HEMT）近结散热以横向扩展输运为主，但既有热管理既未触及核心 AlGaN/GaN 区域，也未考虑热源的光谱非平衡（spectral non-equilibrium）特性。

Method: 在热源上方插入薄的高导热 AlN 间隔层以拦截热量并横向分流，针对 GaN、AlGaN/GaN 与 AlGaN/AlN/GaN 三种结构，在平衡与纵光学（longitudinal-optical, LO）泵浦非平衡加热两种条件下，结合非平衡分子动力学（non-equilibrium molecular dynamics, NEMD）与全带声子蒙特卡罗（full-band phonon Monte Carlo, MC）模拟。

Result: 平衡态下 AlN 层使扩展热阻较 AlGaN 覆盖结构降低 38%、较裸 GaN 降低 17%，而一维热阻基本不变；热阻对 AlN 厚度呈弱且反转的趋势，界面结合影响显著。LO 泵浦下扩展热阻在 NEMD 中上升 58–83%、在 MC 中上升 9–20%，平衡态的空间最优解减弱甚至反转。

Conclusion: 光学—声学转换、界面准入与横向分流构成串联的单一非平衡热阻网络，可预测全带 MC 结果，并将 AlN 间隔层定位为非平衡热源状态下的设计变量。

Abstract: Lateral spreading transport dominates near-junction heat dissipation in GaN high-electron-mobility transistors, yet thermal control has largely overlooked this geometry. It neither reaches the core AlGaN/GaN region nor, beyond the spatial spreading itself, accounts for the spectral non-equilibrium of the heat source. Here we tune the spreading resistance directly by inserting a thin, highly conducting AlN space layer above the source to intercept heat and divert it laterally. We test this approach across GaN, AlGaN/GaN, and AlGaN/AlN/GaN structures under both equilibrium and longitudinal-optical (LO)-pumped non-equilibrium heating, pairing non-equilibrium molecular dynamics (NEMD) with full-band phonon Monte Carlo (MC). In equilibrium, the AlN layer lowers the spreading resistance by 38% relative to the AlGaN-capped structure and by 17% relative to bare GaN, with little change in the one-dimensional resistance. The equilibrium color map over AlN thickness and interfacial bonding shows a strong interface effect but a weak thickness trend that reverses, so the gain is set by interface admission rather than solely by thickness or bulk conductivity. This ordering is independently confirmed by gray MC simulations. Under LO pumping, the spreading resistance rises by 58-83% in NEMD and by 9-20% in MC, and the equilibrium spatial optimum weakens or even reverses. This is because an optical-to-acoustic conversion gate now precedes every spatial gate and locks energy in slow optical modes. The three gates in series (optical-to-acoustic conversion, interface admission, and lateral diversion) close into a single non-equilibrium resistance network that predicts the full-band MC cases and frames the AlN space layer as a design variable within the non-equilibrium source state.

</details>


### [14] [Technical Note on Matlantis PFP v9: Extending the Element and Domain Coverage of the r2SCAN Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23873)
*Daiki Shintani,Shigeru Iwase,Jun Oshiki,Kohei Shinohara,Nontawat Charoenphakdee,Makoto Hiramatsu,Yuta Tsuboi,Yusuke Asano,Hirotaka Akita,Yong-Bin Zhuang,So Takamoto,Chikashi Shinagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Matlantis PFP v9 将基于 r2SCAN 泛函的训练数据扩展至 96 种元素（含 Pr–Lu 镧系与 Po–Cm），并新增团簇与吸附结构等体系；其形成能与吸附能精度分别对标实验生成焓与 DFT-PBE，且在 A100 上推理速度与可处理体系规模均优于 SevenNet-Omni。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（universal machine learning interatomic potentials, uMLIPs）可将原子模拟扩展至密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）难以企及的尺度。前代 PFP v8 虽采用与实验吻合较好的 r2SCAN 泛函数据，但覆盖的元素与体系域有限，限制了其在稀土、锕系及表面吸附等场景的应用。

Method: 以单一模型统一处理晶体、分子与表面等体系；将 r2SCAN 泛函计算的训练数据扩展至 Pr 至 Lu 的镧系元素及 Po 至 Cm 的元素，共 96 种元素，并补充团簇（clusters）与吸附结构（adsorption structures）等体系域，从而训练出 Matlantis PFP v9。

Result: 含新支持元素的晶体形成能（formation energies）对实验标准生成焓（standard enthalpies of formation）的再现精度与仅含原有元素的晶体相当；分子在过渡金属表面的吸附能（adsorption energies）相对实验的整体误差与 DFT-PBE 相当，但化学吸附物种存在过结合（overbound）倾向。在同类 GPU（A100）上，其推理速度与最大体系规模均超越公开的 r2SCAN uMLIP SevenNet-Omni：力评估快 3–4 倍，可处理原子数为 5–10 倍。

Conclusion: PFP-R2SCAN 现已可应用于含稀土与锕系元素的体系以及表面吸附问题，在保持精度的同时具备更高的计算效率与更大的体系规模承载能力。

Abstract: Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) make atomistic simulations possible at scales beyond the reach of density functional theory (DFT). Matlantis PFP is a uMLIP that treats a variety of domains, including crystals, molecules, and surfaces, with a single model. PFP v8 incorporated a training dataset computed with the r2SCAN functional, chosen for its good agreement with experiment, but the elements and domains covered by that dataset were limited. Here we present Matlantis PFP v9, in which the training data computed with the r2SCAN functional are extended to the lanthanides from Pr to Lu and to the elements from Po to Cm, for a total of 96 elements, and to additional domains such as clusters and adsorption structures. The formation energies of crystals containing the newly supported elements reproduce experimental standard enthalpies of formation with the same level of accuracy as crystals composed only of previously supported elements, and the adsorption energies of molecules on transition-metal surfaces have an overall error against experiment comparable to that of DFT-PBE, although chemisorbed species are overbound. On the same type of GPU (A100), the inference speed and the maximum system size exceed those of SevenNet-Omni, a public uMLIP trained on r2SCAN data: forces are evaluated three to four times faster, and five to ten times as many atoms can be handled. As a result, PFP-R2SCAN is now applicable to systems containing rare-earth and actinide elements and to adsorption on surfaces.

</details>


### [15] [Light-Induced Odd-Parity h-Wave Magnetism in Penrose Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23992)
*Yiming Li,Huaqing Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在无需自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的共线反铁磁Penrose准晶（Penrose quasicrystal）中，圆偏振光（circularly polarized light）诱导出奇宇称（odd-parity）的h波磁性（h-wave magnetism），实现超越晶格对称性约束的非常规自旋劈裂（spin splitting）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规自旋劈裂受周期晶体对称性与自旋轨道耦合限制，非晶体学旋转对称（noncrystallographic rotational symmetry）的准晶结构为突破上述约束提供了新途径。

Method: 虚拟两步过程产生的光致手性跃迁（chiral hopping）与交错交换场（staggered exchange）结合，解除未驱动体系的自旋简并；结合准晶自旋群对称性（spin-group symmetry）分析与解析有效准带理论（effective quasi-band theory），并进行动量分辨谱计算。

Result: 准晶自旋群对称性强制奇宇称自旋劈裂并以h波为最低角谐波；解析理论将劈裂与局域跃迁路径几何相联系，预测切换光螺旋度可使其反转；谱计算显示特征性的交替自旋衬度，并提出自旋分辨时间分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（spin-resolved tr-ARPES）作为探测手段。

Conclusion: 研究建立了光学调控高次谐波奇宇称磁性的机制，并表明准晶序可将Floquet工程（Floquet engineering）拓展至晶体学对称性约束之外。

Abstract: Noncrystallographic rotational symmetry offers a route to unconventional spin splitting beyond the constraints of periodic crystals. Light-induced chiral hopping, generated by virtual two-step processes, combines with staggered exchange to lift the spin degeneracy of the undriven system. Quasicrystalline spin-group symmetry enforces odd-parity spin splitting and selects $h$-wave as its leading angular harmonic. An analytical effective quasi-band theory relates this splitting to the geometry of local hopping paths and predicts its reversal upon switching the light helicity. Momentum-resolved spectral calculations reveal the characteristic alternating spin contrast, for which we propose spin-resolved time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy as an experimental probe. Our results establish a mechanism for optically controlling higher-harmonic odd-parity magnetism and demonstrate how quasicrystalline order extends Floquet engineering beyond crystallographic symmetry constraints.

</details>


### [16] [Ultrafast Successive Control and Electrical Detection of Long-Lived Valley Polarization in Room-Temperature Germanium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23998)
*Ami M. Shirai,Yuta Murotani,Shunta Aoki,Shunsuke A. Sato,Tomohiro Fujimoto,Kota Aikyo,Changsu Kim,Hidefumi Akiyama,Jun Yoshinobu,Toshiya Ideue,Ryusuke Matsunaga*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用时间与偏振分辨太赫兹光谱结合形变势理论，在室温Ge(001)近带边测得谷弛豫时间达10 ps，并实现亚皮秒、超低能耗的谷偏振完全反转，为基于体相间接带隙半导体的室温谷电子学（valleytronics）提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锗（Ge）导带底具有四个简并谷（valleys），其高度各向异性的有效质量（effective mass）可借助各向异性电导提供输运中的谷自由度，但谷偏振能否在室温电输运中被检测与操控尚待澄清。

Method: 采用时间分辨与偏振分辨的太赫兹光谱（terahertz spectroscopy），并结合形变势理论（deformation potential theory）分析Ge(001)近带边的谷动力学；同时使用非共振（off-resonant）光脉冲进行谷偏振操控。

Result: 室温下Ge(001)近带边的谷弛豫时间（valley relaxation time）达到10 ps，足以在电输运测量中探测谷偏振；非共振光脉冲可在亚皮秒（sub-picosecond）时间尺度实现谷偏振的完全反转，且能耗极低。

Conclusion: 结果表明体相间接带隙半导体（indirect-gap semiconductors）可在室温实现谷偏振的产生、保持与反转，为与现有电子器件兼容的室温谷电子学奠定基础。

Abstract: Germanium (Ge) hosts four degenerate valleys in the conduction band minima, and their highly anisotropic effective masses offer a valley degree of freedom in transport phenomena via anisotropic conductivity. Using time- and polarization-resolved terahertz spectroscopy combined with deformation potential theory, we reveal a valley relaxation time reaching 10 ps near the band edge of Ge(001) at room temperature, long enough to detect the valley polarization in electrical transport measurements. We also demonstrate sub-picosecond complete reversal of valley polarization by off-resonant light pulses at extremely low energy consumption. These results pave the way for room-temperature valleytronics based on bulk indirect-gap semiconductors compatible with existing electronics.

</details>


### [17] [Two-Color Coherent Control of the Spin Photovoltaic Effect in Centrosymmetric Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24045)
*Songmin Liu,Pan Zhou,Lizhong Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在中心对称晶体中，双色相干光场（ω 与 2ω）可通过宇称偶（inversion-even）的三阶直流响应产生对称性选择的自旋光电流，并实现纯自旋通道的选择与相位调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间反演（spatial inversion）禁止中心对称晶体中的常规电偶极二阶体电荷与自旋光伏响应，因此需要寻找在中心对称体系中产生并调控自旋输运的新机制与主动控制参数。

Method: 采用频率为 ω 和 2ω 的相位相干双色场驱动，利用宇称偶三阶直流响应；并对中心对称磁性点群（magnetic point groups）与共线反铁磁自旋点群（collinear antiferromagnetic spin point groups）进行系统对称性分析，辅以反转堆叠双层 2H-MoS₂ 与中心对称共线交错磁体（altermagnet）的计算。

Result: 双色场的偏振方向与相对相位可直接控制电流方向，并能隔离出电荷电流消失而自旋电流有限的输运通道；对称性分析表明纯自旋通道在一大类中心对称体系中在合适双色驱动下是允许的，相对光学相位还决定响应的大小与符号。计算显示两类互补体系（强与弱自旋轨道耦合，spin-orbit coupling）中均存在共振增强、相位可调的自旋光电流。

Conclusion: 相干双色光的偏振方向与相位可作为中心对称材料中自旋输运的主动控制参数，为纯自旋光电流的产生与调控提供通用方案。

Abstract: Spatial inversion forbids the conventional electric-dipole second-order bulk charge and spin photovoltaic responses in centrosymmetric crystals. Here we show that a phase-coherent two-color field at frequencies $ω$ and $2ω$ can generate symmetry-selected charge and spin photocurrents through an inversion-even third-order dc response. More importantly, the polarization direction and relative phase of the two-color field provide direct control over the current direction and can isolate transport channels in which the charge current vanishes while a finite spin current remains. A systematic symmetry analysis of centrosymmetric magnetic point groups and collinear antiferromagnetic spin point groups shows that pure-spin channels are allowed in a broad class of such systems under appropriate two-color driving. Furthermore, the relative optical phase governs both the magnitude and sign of the response. Our calculations on inversion-stacked bilayer 2H-MoS$_2$ and a centrosymmetric collinear altermagnet demonstrate resonantly enhanced, phase-tunable spin photocurrents in these complementary systems with strong and weak spin-orbit coupling, respectively. Our results establish the polarization direction and phase of coherent two-color light as active control parameters for spin transport in centrosymmetric materials.

</details>


### [18] [A GPU-Native Nested Sampling Framework for Finite-Temperature Materials Modelling with Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24088)
*Nico Unglert,Alessandro Serafini,Jesús Carrete,Georg K. H. Madsen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: JAXRENS 是一个基于 JAX 的 GPU 原生、大规模并行嵌套采样（nested sampling）框架，用于将现代等变机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）与有限温度相稳定性预测相结合，并在 ZnS 相图上完成了此前计算上不可行的演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变 MLIPs 提升了原子尺度模拟的精度与可达尺度，但将其转化为有限温度相稳定性预测所需的玻尔兹曼采样（thermodynamic sampling）方法在计算上未能同步跟进，导致二者结合存在实际瓶颈。

Method: 提出 GPU 原生、大规模并行的嵌套采样框架 JAXRENS，构建于 JAX 之上，提供分布式多 GPU 执行、向量化 walker 集合（vectorised ensembles of walkers）以及与现代表势的直接互操作能力。

Result: 在硬件加速器上展示了良好扩展性；基于 MACE 基础模型（foundation model）完成 ZnS 相图模拟，尽管需要约 10^10 次模型评估，收敛模拟在四块 GPU 上六天内完成。

Conclusion: 该工作使原子尺度嵌套采样与先进 MLIPs 的结合成为可行，是迈向利用机器学习原子间势实现常规化、第一性原理质量（first-principles-quality）有限温度相稳定性预测的重要一步。

Abstract: Modern equivariant machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have dramatically improved the accuracy of and the scales accessible to atomistic simulation, yet the thermodynamic sampling methods needed to turn those potentials into finite-temperature phase stability predictions have not kept pace computationally. We present the GPU-native, massively parallel nested sampling framework JAXRENS, implemented on top of JAX, which removes the practical bottlenecks preventing atomistic nested sampling from being combined with state-of-the-art MLIPs. The framework provides distributed multi-GPU execution, vectorised ensembles of walkers, and direct interoperability with modern force fields. We demonstrate the highly beneficial scaling of JAXRENS on hardware accelerators, and apply it to the simulation of the ZnS phase diagram based on a MACE foundation model, a procedure previously computationally inaccessible. Despite requiring in the order of 10^10 model evaluations, the final converged simulation completed within six days on four GPUs, marking an important step towards routine, first-principles-quality predictions of finite-temperature phase stability using machine-learned interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [19] [Observation of room-temperature intrinsic antiferroelectricity in van der Waals NbOCl2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24092)
*JinKai Huo,Xingyu Chen,Gaolei Zhao,Xuan Chen,Yuchen Meng,Xu Han,Juhe Liu,Egor Kolesov,Konstantin Kozadaev,Alexei Tolstik,Andrei Zheltkovich,Andrey Novitsky,Shenghuang Lin,Changsen Sun,Dawei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在层状过渡金属氧卤化物 NbOCl2 中观察到室温稳定的反铁电性（antiferroelectricity），并实现可逆、外场驱动的反铁电—铁电（AFE-to-FE）相变。该材料被证明是电场可编程非线性光子学的候选平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维铁电性（2D ferroelectricity）已被广泛研究，但本征二维反铁电性（intrinsic 2D antiferroelectricity）极为罕见，限制了其在下一代光子与电子器件中的应用。研究动机在于拓展室温二维反铁电材料体系并实现对其相态的调控。

Method: 采用二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation, SHG）成像、入射角依赖 SHG 测量与拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）联合表征 NbOCl2 薄片；并通过与一维银纳米线（silver nanowires）集成，施加应变与电场（约 20 kV/cm）实现相变调控。

Result: SHG 成像显示从少层到块体状 NbOCl2 薄片存在明确的条纹状 SHG 强度空间分布，源于反铁电相存在及反铁电与铁电相在同一晶体内的共存；应变介导实现空间选择性 AFE-to-FE 相变，超低电场（~20 kV/cm）可诱导可逆 SHG 响应，对应从中心对称反铁电态（SHG 静默）到非中心对称铁电态（SHG 活性）的切换。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了室温二维反铁电材料家族，并建立了动态操控非线性光的通用平台，为电场可编程非线性光子学（electric-field-programmable nonlinear photonics）与光信息处理提供途径。

Abstract: In recent years, two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectricity has been extensively investigated across a wide range of 2D materials, while intrinsic 2D antiferroelectricity remains exceptionally rare, limiting its implementation in next-generation photonic and electronic devices. Here, we report the observation of room-temperature-stable antiferroelectricity in the layered transition metal oxyhalide NbOCl2, and demonstrate a reversible, stimuli-driven antiferroelectric-to-ferroelectric (AFE-to-FE) phase transition in this material. Second-harmonic generation (SHG) mapping reveals a well-defined stripe-like spatial distribution of SHG intensity across as-grown NbOCl2 flakes spanning thickness from few-layer to bulk-like configurations. This distinctive SHG signature originates from the existence of antiferroelectric phase and the coexistence of antiferroelectric and ferroelectric phases within individual crystals, as evidenced by a combined incident-angle-dependent SHG measurement and Raman spectroscopy. By integrating NbOCl2 with one-dimensional silver nanowires, we achieve spatially selective, strain-mediated AFE-to-FE phase transition. Moreover, an ultralow electric field (~20 kV/cm) induces a reversible SHG response, consistent with switching from the centrosymmetric antiferroelectric state (SHG-silent) to the non-centrosymmetric ferroelectric state (SHG-active). This study not only expands the family of room-temperature 2D antiferroelectrics but also establishes a versatile platform for dynamic manipulation of nonlinear light, paving the way toward electric-field-programmable nonlinear photonics and optical information processing.

</details>


### [20] [Uncovering the Structural, Electronic, and Electrostatic Landscapes of SrTiO$_3$ Grain Boundaries via Grand Canonical Monte Carlo with a Machine-Learning Interatomic Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24097)
*Xing He,Kairi Masuda,Aaron M. Schankler,Songsong Zhou,Andrew M. Rappe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合巨正则蒙特卡洛（Grand Canonical Monte Carlo, GCMC）与通用机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potential），系统探索了SrTiO₃钙钛矿晶界（grain boundary）的成分–结构相空间，并揭示了化学计量与原子重构如何耦合电子结构、静电与局域极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿晶界具有丰富的电子、极性与磁性性质，但其原子结构与成分的确定仍具挑战性，缺乏高效探索成分与结构耦合相空间的方法。

Method: 将GCMC方法与通用机器学习原子间势结合，用于Σ5(310)[001] SrTiO₃晶界的成分与结构相空间搜索，并推广至Σ5(210)[001]与Σ17(410)[001]晶界；随后用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算所预测晶界的电子结构，并进行静电学分析。

Result: 化学势相图识别出属于四类基元家族（大Sr kite、小Sr kite、不完整Sr大kite、富Ti结构）的十种稳定结构；不同晶界面与倾角改变小/大kite基元间的竞争；DFT显示出现局域电子掺杂、离域电子掺杂、绝缘与空穴吸引四种电子态，并发现重构晶界核心的短程电场与核心外由空间电荷引起的长程电场共存，且可与局域极性畸变耦合形成成分依赖的极化图样。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了探索氧化物钙钛矿晶界结构的高效框架，阐明了化学计量与原子重构同电子结构、静电及局域极化的耦合关系，为功能钙钛矿的预测性晶界工程（grain boundary engineering）提供了途径。

Abstract: Grain boundaries in perovskites exhibit a rich variety of electronic, polar and magnetic properties, yet identifying their atomic structures and compositions remains challenging. Here, we combine the Grand Canonical Monte Carlo (GCMC) method with a universal machine-learning interatomic potential to explore the coupled compositional and structural phase space of $Σ$5(310)[001] SrTiO$_3$ grain boundaries. The resulting chemical potential phase diagram identifies ten stable structures belonging to four motif families: large Sr kite, small Sr kite, incomplete Sr large kite, and Ti-rich structures. Extending the GCMC method to the Sigma5(210)[001] and Sigma17(410)[001] grain boundaries reveals how grain boundary plane and inclination angle influence the competition between small- and large-kite motifs. Density functional theory calculations of the predicted Sigma5(310)[001] grain boundaries show that the composition and atomic structure strongly govern the spatial distribution of conduction and valence band edge states, interfacial band alignment, and carrier localization. Four distinct electronic regimes emerge: localized electron-doped, delocalized electron-doped, insulating, and hole-attracting states. Our electrostatics analysis reveals the coexistence of the short-range electric fields at the reconstructed GB core associated with local atomic density and bonding, and the longer-range fields outside the core arising from the space charge. These fields can also couple to local polar distortions and generate composition-dependent polarization patterns. These results establish an efficient framework for exploring oxide perovskite grain boundary structures and reveal how stoichiometry and atomic reconstruction couple to electronic structure, electrostatics, and local polarization, providing a route toward predictive grain boundary engineering in functional perovskites.

</details>


### [21] [Properties of [111]-oriented ferroelectric superlattices based on oxides with a perovskite structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24129)
*A. I. Lebedev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理计算了15种[111]取向钙钛矿氧化物铁电超晶格的基态结构，其中11种基态为铁电态；铁电畸变与八面体旋转畸变均可由布里渊区Γ点声子描述，且八面体旋转对铁电性质影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统考察单原子替换构成的[111]取向铁电超晶格（superlattice, SL）的基态结构，并阐明八面体旋转（octahedral rotations）等结构畸变对超晶格铁电性质的影响，同时比较不同生长取向的热力学稳定性。

Method: 基于第一性原理（first principles）计算15种自由-standing、[111]取向的钙钛矿氧化物（perovskite oxides）超晶格基态结构，并结合力常数（force constants）分析及与[100]取向超晶格的热力学稳定性对比。

Result: 在所研究的15种超晶格中，11种基态为铁电态（ferroelectric）；其铁电畸变与八面体旋转畸变均由布里渊区（Brillouin zone）Γ点声子描述；计入八面体旋转畸变在某些情形下会显著改变超晶格铁电性质；力常数分析可解释超晶格及相应固溶体的铁电特性。

Conclusion: [111]取向铁电超晶格的基态结构可由Γ点声子统一描述，八面体旋转畸变不可忽略；力常数与取向稳定性比较为理解超晶格及固溶体铁电性提供了依据。

Abstract: The ground-state structures of fifteen free-standing [111]-oriented ferroelectric superlattices (SLs) based on perovskite oxides and involving a single-atom substitution---BaTiO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$, PbTiO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$, PbTiO$_3$/PbZrO$_3$, SrZrO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$, PbZrO$_3$/BaZrO$_3$, BaTiO$_3$/BaZrO$_3$, PbTiO$_3$/BaTiO$_3$, BaTiO$_3$/CaTiO$_3$, KNbO$_3$/KTaO$_3$, KNbO$_3$/NaNbO$_3$, SrTiO$_3$/SrHfO$_3$, BaTiO$_3$/BaSnO$_3$, PbTiO$_3$/CaTiO$_3$, SrTiO$_3$/CaTiO$_3$, and PbZrO$_3$/SrZrO$_3$---are calculated from first principles. It is shown that in eleven considered SLs the ground state is ferroelectric. A distinctive feature of the structure of the superlattices is that both the ferroelectric distortions and the distortions associated with octahedral rotations are described by phonons at the $Γ$~point of the Brillouin zone. The importance of taking into account structural distortions associated with octahedral rotations, which in some cases lead to significant changes in the ferroelectric properties of SLs, is demonstrated. The experimental data are described by a phenomenological multichannel model comprising a temperature-independent plateau term and two or three effective Arrhenius-type contributions characterized by distinct temperature scales ranging from a few to several hundred kelvin. These results establish a correlation between structural organization and the redistribution of effective thermal-transport contributions in epoxy-GO composites, providing an experimental basis for further microscopic interpretation within the framework of unified vibrational thermal-transport theory.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ni-Fe-Ga磁性形状记忆合金（magnetic shape-memory alloy）已有大量研究，但其马氏体转变温度与磁性转变温度随成分的变化规律仍不清晰，阻碍了同时满足室温以上M_s与高T_c的成分设计。

Abstract: Despite extensive research on Ni-Fe-Ga magnetic shape-memory alloys, the compositional dependence of their martensitic and magnetic transition temperatures remains unclear. At fixed Ga, this creates a trade-off: raising M_s generally lowers T_c and vice versa, making it difficult to keep both above room temperature. Around 25 at.% Ga, bulk data exhibit considerable scatter, partly attributable to gamma-phase formation. Glass-coated microwires produced by the Taylor-Ulitovsky method, in which the gamma phase has generally not been reported, extend the accessible composition range toward lower Ga contents. The Ni(73-x)Fe(x)Ga27 (x = 18-22) series and the Ga-rich Ni50Fe21Ga29 and Ni50Fe23Ga27 microwires broadly follow the corresponding bulk trends. Remarkably, Ni50Fe25Ga25 and Ni50Fe27Ga23 microwires depart sharply from these trends, combining M_s at or above room temperature with T_c > 395 K. Their functional potential is demonstrated by approximately 10% reversible strain in Ni50Fe27Ga23 microwire during thermal cycling under constant tensile stresses up to 200 MPa.

### [28] [Machine-learned potentials for iron oxides at extreme conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24525)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作为五种铁氧化物构建原子团簇展开（atomic-cluster-expansion）势函数，揭示力主导训练会遗留结构族间系统性能量偏移（达41 meV/atom），需通过能量加权与k点收敛加以控制，并强调对每个竞争相进行显式自由能验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的相稳定性依赖准确的相对自由能（relative free energies），而仅靠小的力误差（force errors）并不能保证这一点，因此需要针对铁氧化物势函数的能量偏移问题展开研究。

Result: 力主导训练导致结构族间系统性能量偏移最高达41 meV/atom且难以通过增加数据消除；最终势函数在留出集上能量误差为2.0–5.5 meV/atom，5000–9000 K液态一阶自由能误差在3.4 meV/atom以内；但未训练的NiAs型FeO相即使外推等级（extrapolation grade）较低，自由能误差仍达57 meV/atom。

Conclusion: 可靠的相图（phase-diagram）计算需要控制相对能量偏移，并对每一个竞争相进行显式自由能验证；低外推等级不足以保障自由能精度。

Abstract: Reliable phase stability requires accurate relative free energies, which small force errors alone do not guarantee. We develop atomic-cluster-expansion potentials for five iron oxides from Fe$_2$O to Fe$_2$O$_3$, covering crystalline and liquid states at 140--260\,GPa and 1000--11000\,K, with electronic and ionic temperatures matched. Force-dominated training leaves systematic energy offsets between structure families of up to 41\,meV/atom that persist despite additional training data. Stronger energy weighting and weaker regularization largely remove these offsets, while converged k-point sampling prevents further composition-dependent biases. The final potentials achieve held-out energy errors of 2.0--5.5\,meV/atom and first-order free-energy errors within 3.4\,meV/atom for liquids at 5000--9000\,K. However, an untrained NiAs-type FeO phase exhibits a free-energy error of 57\,meV/atom despite a low extrapolation grade. These results show that reliable phase-diagram calculations require control of relative energy offsets and explicit free-energy validation of every competing phase.

</details>


### [29] [Orientation-Tunable Interfacial Thermal Conductance in Lateral MoSe2/MoS2 Heterostructures: A Deep Potential Molecular Dynamics Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24547)
*Shirin Sabokdast,Pedram Mirchi,Ali Rajabpour*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合深度学习原子间势（deep learning interatomic potential, DLP）与非平衡分子动力学（non-equilibrium molecular dynamics, NEMD），研究横向 MoSe₂/MoS₂ 异质结的界面热导（interfacial thermal conductance, ITC），发现扶手椅（armchair）界面热导高于锯齿（zigzag）界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向二维过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenide, TMD）异质结的界面热流调控是下一代纳电子与光电子器件的关键挑战，需要可靠手段量化并理解其界面热输运。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）数据，使用 DeePMD-kit 训练深度学习势，并与 NEMD 模拟结合，考察扶手椅与锯齿两种界面取向的 MoSe₂/MoS₂ 异质结；模拟胞长 10–40 nm 并外推至无限长，同时分析温度依赖与振动态密度（vibrational density of states）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个用于研究横向 TMD 异质结热输运的机器学习框架，并表明界面取向是调控此类结区散热（heat dissipation）的有效手段。

Abstract: Managing heat flow across interfaces in lateral 2D transition-metal dichalcogenide (TMD) heterostructures is a key challenge for next-generation nanoelectronic and optoelectronic devices. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 研究将极值投影算子的既有结论推广至包含约化有限维李代数与 Virasoro 代数的李超代数类，并提供可操作的递归计算框架；关于 Virasoro 情形分母的猜想仍待完整证明，仅弱化版本已证。

Abstract: Extremal projectors are universal operators projecting onto the highest weight space in a representation. Their existence and uniqueness (as formal series) are known for a broad range of Lie superalgebras and quantum groups. We consider a class of Lie superalgebras that contains reductive finite-dimensional Lie algebras as well as the Virasoro algebra. We prove the existence and uniqueness of an extremal projector for this class. The existence proof is given in terms of a recursive algorithm which can be used to compute the coefficients. To illustrate the method, we compute the terms for the Virasoro extremal projector up to order 6. Furthermore, we formulate a conjecture for the denominators of the truncated extremal projector of the Virasoro algebra. We provide symbolic computational evidence up to order 6 and prove a weaker version of the conjecture.

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于Tevron与LHC的Drell-Yan角不对称对有效轻子弱混合角（effective leptonic weak mixing angle）的精确测定，指出PDF不确定度已成为主导误差，并探讨其来源与缓解途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随统计不确定度大幅下降，部分子分布函数（parton distribution function, PDF）不确定度已成为强子对撞机最精确结果的主要限制，且现代PDF集（CT18、MSHT20、NNPDF）间提取值存在差异，需要厘清其起源。

Method: 综述角观测量与电弱计算方案，介绍replica reweighting与Hessian profiling等从角分布直接约束PDF的方法；采用简化的领头阶Z极点（leading-order Z-pole）计算，将夸克味产生权重与夸克方向稀释相分离；并引入W/Z截面比与W玻色子衰变不对称的互补约束。

Abstract: We review precision determinations of the effective leptonic weak mixing angle, $\sin^2θ^\ell_{\mathrm{eff}}$, from Drell-Yan angular asymmetries at the Tevatron and the LHC. As statistical uncertainties have dramatically decreased, parton distribution function (PDF) uncertainty now dominates the most precise hadron-collider results. We describe the angular observables, electroweak calculation schemes, and advanced methods such as replica reweighting and Hessian profiling, used to constrain PDFs directly from measured angular distributions. To identify the origin of differences between modern PDF sets (e.g., CT18, MSHT20, NNPDF) in the extracted mixing angle, we employ a simplified leading-order Z-pole calculation that separates quark-flavor production weights from quark-direction dilution. We demonstrate how these PDF-induced differences can be mitigated by incorporating complementary constraints from W/Z cross-section ratios and W-boson decay asymmetries. Furthermore, we discuss the requirements for a common reinterpretation and future combination of LHC measurements, highlighting how correlated PDF uncertainties limit the ultimate combined precision. Finally, we outline future prospects, including complementary measurements in $Z+b$ production and high-mass tests of the running $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ weak mixing angle.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学界对复制危机（replication crisis）的担忧在于已发表结果是否如其声明不确定度所示般可靠；大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）上未发现信号的新物理搜寻同时报告排除极限（exclusion limits）与无信号假设下的预期极限及其σ带，为直接检验这一解读提供了场景，且此前已有2019年的初步研究。

Method: 针对ATLAS与CMS于2020–2025年发表的55篇文献（含近400个排除图）开展检验，并重新分析2019年初步研究所用文献；排除极限直接从图形的矢量图（vector graphics）中读取，统计不确定度通过对文献做自助法（bootstrap）估计，另以伪实验（pseudo-experiments）探讨偏离的可能来源。

Result: 对图形等权平均后，CMS有66.1 ± 4.4%与95.3 ± 1.9%的观测点落在1σ与2σ带内，与通常解读一致；ATLAS为73.3 ± 7.6%落在1σ带内（与该解读相容），但99.2 ± 0.4%落在2σ带内，明显高于其应有比例。伪实验表明该现象可能来自保守的不确定度、部分搜寻事件数很少，或预期极限在数据拟合之后计算（常见统计工具的默认做法，而图形本身无法区分）。

Conclusion: ATLAS与CMS在σ带覆盖率上表现不同，ATLAS的2σ带覆盖率偏高且存在多种可能解释，图形信息不足以判别具体成因；因此建议今后发表文献明确说明其预期极限（expected limits）的计算方式。

Abstract: The replication crisis has raised concern across many fields of science about whether published results are as reliable as their stated uncertainties imply. Searches for new physics at the Large Hadron Collider offer a direct test of this for high-energy physics: those that find no signal report exclusion limits together with the limits expected in the absence of a signal and their $1σ$ and $2σ$ bands, which readers take to contain the observed limits with frequencies of 68.3% and 95.4%. Following a preliminary study in 2019, we test this reading for the ATLAS and CMS searches published between 2020 and 2025, 55 publications with nearly 400 exclusion plots, and reanalyze the publications of the earlier study. The limits are read directly from the vector graphics of the figures, and the statistical uncertainties are estimated with a bootstrap over publications. Averaging over the plots with equal weights, the CMS results agree with that reading, with $66.1 \pm 4.4\%$ and $95.3 \pm 1.9\%$ of the observed points within the $1σ$ and $2σ$ bands. For ATLAS, $73.3 \pm 7.6\%$ of the points lie within the $1σ$ band, compatible with it, while $99.2 \pm 0.4\%$ lie within the $2σ$ band, more often than it implies. Pseudo-experiments show that this can arise from conservative uncertainties, from the few events of many searches, or from expected limits computed after a fit to the data, the default of common statistical tools, which the plots do not distinguish. We recommend that publications state how their expected limits are computed.

</details>


### [38] [A fully differential charged-particle phase-space measurement of $Z$+jets production in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24270)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用 OmniFold 方法对 ATLAS 探测器收集的 140 fb⁻¹、√s = 13 TeV 质子-质子碰撞数据，首次给出非分箱（unbinned）、高度微分的 Z+jets 截面测量，并以粒子级蒙特卡罗事件加展开权重的形式公开结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Z+jets 测量以预先设定的有限观测量分箱微分截面（binned differential cross section）形式报告，信息保留受限且不利于再解释（reinterpretation）；该过程本身具有干净实验特征与高选择效率与纯度，是检验标准模型（Standard Model）动力学的基准过程。

Method: 采用 OmniFold 展开（unfolding）方法，基于 ATLAS 探测器 140 fb⁻¹、质心能量 √s = 13 TeV 的质子-质子碰撞数据，在定义明确的相空间区域内对单个带电粒子运动学进行非分箱、全微分测量。

Result: 获得首个已发表的对撞机截面非分箱全微分测量；以该非分箱事件表示构建了四个示例应用，表明单一测量可约束广泛观测量，其中若干观测量用传统展开方法难以或无法获得。

Conclusion: 测量结果以粒子级蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）事件及展开权重形式发布，使发表后可构造任意观测量，支持广泛的后续应用与再解释。

Abstract: The production of a $Z$~boson decaying to muons in association with hadronic jets is a benchmark process in proton-proton collisions at the Large Hadron Collider. It provides a clean experimental signature with high selection efficiency and purity while probing a wide range of Standard Model dynamics. Measurements of this process are typically reported as binned differential cross sections in a limited set of predefined observables, which reduces the information retained and hinders reinterpretation. In this paper, the OmniFold method is used to produce an unbinned and highly differential measurement of the $Z$+jets cross section using 140 $\text{fb}^{-1}$ of $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV proton-proton collision data collected by the ATLAS detector. This constitutes the first published unbinned, fully differential measurement of a collider cross section, describing the kinematics of individual charged particles within a well-defined region of phase space. This unbinned event representation is used to construct measurements in four example applications that illustrate the broad range of observables constrained with a single measurement, including several that would be difficult or impossible to access using traditional unfolding methods. The measurement is published as particle-level Monte Carlo events together with unfolded event weights, allowing arbitrary observables to be constructed after publication and enabling a broad range of future applications and reinterpretations.

</details>


### [39] [Deployment of a Graph-Based Track Seeder for the ATLAS Phase-II Tracking Upgrade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24307)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS 实验为高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）开展软件现代化，采用实验无关的 ACTS（A Common Tracking Software）工具包，并开发了基于图的径迹种子构建器（Graph-Based Track Seeder, GBTS），本文介绍其方法、开发部署进展及预期径迹性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC 阶段的探测器、计算与软件升级要求软件具备可维护性、线程安全与高性能，并需在满足物理径迹性能要求的同时降低事件重建（event reconstruction）的计算开销，因此需要采用统一的径迹重建工具与更高效的种子构建方法。

Method: ATLAS 协作组在重建软件中引入实验无关的 ACTS 工具包用于带电粒子径迹重建（charged-particle track reconstruction），并基于该方法学开发了基于图的径迹种子构建器（GBTS）。

Result: 摘要仅给出定性表述：采用 ACTS 有利于新算法的开发与集成，并促进线程安全（thread safety）、高性能与代码可维护性；GBTS 的具体性能结果未在摘要中量化，仅提及将给出其在 HL-LHC 下的预期径迹性能。

Conclusion: 软件现代化与 ACTS 的引入是 ATLAS 面向 HL-LHC 满足径迹性能需求并控制计算需求的关键途径，GBTS 是其中近期部署的组成部分，其方法学与预期性能在文中详述。

Abstract: The ATLAS experiment is undergoing significant detector, computing and software upgrades for the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC). The ongoing software modernisation is essential to leverage future processing technologies and ensure maintainability throughout the operation of the experiment, while meeting the tracking performance requirements for physics and reducing the compute needs for event reconstruction. To meet these goals, the ATLAS Collaboration is adopting the ACTS (A Common Tracking Software) toolkit, an experiment-independent software for charged-particle track reconstruction. Using ACTS in the ATLAS reconstruction software at the HL-LHC facilitates the development and integration of new algorithms while promoting thread safety, high performance, and code maintainability. This document describes the development and recent deployment of a Graph-Based Track Seeder (GBTS), its underlying methodology, and its expected tracking performance at the HL-LHC.

</details>

Abstract: The first observation of the Cabibbo-suppressed decay $B^+_c \to J/ψD^{\ast+}$ using proton-proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment between 2011 and 2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $9\,\rm{fb^{-1}}$, is presented. The observed signal has a significance of 5.4 standard deviations and the branching fraction relative to the normalisation mode $B^+_c \to J/ψD^+_s$ is measured to be $$\frac{\mathcal{B}(B^+_c \to J/ψD^{\ast+})}{\mathcal{B}(B^+_c \to J/ψD^+_s)} = 0.067 \pm 0.016 \pm 0.003,$$ where the first uncertainty is statistical and the second is systematic. The result is used to test theoretical models of two-body hadronic decays of $B^+_c$ mesons and agrees with several predictions in the literature.

Method: 基于 ORGAN-Q 实验的高分辨率数据，扫描九种线宽（0.466 Hz 至 119.209 Hz，等效品质因数 1.3×10^10 至 5.2×10^7），并在分析中计入本征频率bin相关性（native-bin correlations）、数据质量与干扰否决（interference vetoes）、频率依赖的系统噪声、腔体响应（cavity response）及滤波衰减（filter attenuation）。

Abstract: We search for narrow axion dark-matter flows using the high-resolution data acquired during the ORGAN-Q experiment. The analysis covers nine potential axion linewidths from 0.466$~$Hz to 119.209$~$Hz, equivalent to axion effective quality factors $1.3\times 10^{10}$ to $5.2\times 10^7$, over the range 6.1550$~$GHz to 6.3496$~$GHz, corresponding to axion masses of approximately 25.455$~μ\mathrm{eV}$ to 26.260$~μ\mathrm{eV}$. Once contributions from native-bin correlations, data-quality and interference vetoes, frequency-dependent system noise, the cavity response, and filter attenuation are accounted for, no candidate satisfies the required persistence ratio in any search bandwidth. Across the nine assumed linewidths, the strongest $95\%$ KSVZ axion-flow density limits range from 22.8$~\mathrm{GeVcm^{-3}}$ for a $0.466~\mathrm{Hz}$ signal to $153.7~\mathrm{GeVcm}^{-3}$ for a $119.209~\mathrm{Hz}$ signal, with both attained near $m_a=25.857~μ\mathrm{eV}$.

</details>

### [42] [Commuting integrals of motion for free bosonic CFTs revisited: certified densities, spectral rigidity, and an exact identification at the c=1 branch point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23237)
*Evgueniy Vitchev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用精确算术算符乘积引擎重新研究了自由玻色子共形场论（free bosonic CFT）中交换的局部运动积分（local integrals of motion），并将过定的双线性对易系统化归为一个特征奇宇称荷 D 加线性核问题。作者给出 paperclip 族自旋-7 密度并证明相关对易子恒等成立，为 A_2^(2) 族建立精确库仑气体字典，并厘清 c=1 时层级与量子 KdV 的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hep-th/0404195 中构造的自由玻色子 CFT 交换局部运动积分是可积边界态（integrable boundary states）的输入数据，但原结果缺乏系统核验与推广，本文旨在以精确计算重新审视并严格化这些构造。

Method: 采用精确算术算符乘积引擎（exact-arithmetic operator-product engines），借助自举引理（bootstrap lemma）将过定的双线性对易系统约化为特征奇荷 D 加线性核问题；并结合库仑气体字典（Coulomb-gas dictionary）、仿射 Toda 定理（affine-Toda theorem）与符号化 Q(t) 计算。

Result: 对 Z_2×Z_2 “paperclip”族给出自旋-7 密度 P_8，证明 [I_3,I_5]、[I_3,I_7]、[I_5,I_7] 对 n 恒等为零，并在取样 n 上验证 LVZ 四屏表征；对 A_2^(2) 族给出到对偶 Phi_{1,2}/Phi_{2,1} 扰动的精确字典；在 c=1 时识别定义荷为 c_{1,4}=-25/2 的量子 KdV，并核验至自旋 15；三个柱状族的一般中心化子在奇自旋为一维、偶自旋为零（至自旋 11）；同时确认 2004 年低自旋公式并记录五处修正。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于经认证的字典与有限程核验，而非新的全自旋构造；所有全自旋论断仅在已证明处给出，其余为有限程结果，并以连接双线性系统秩亏与屏核特征标的 corank 特征猜想收尾。

Abstract: We revisit the commuting local integrals of motion for free bosonic CFTs constructed in hep-th/0404195 as input data for integrable boundary states, using exact-arithmetic operator-product engines. A bootstrap lemma reduces its overdetermined bilinear commutativity systems to a distinguished odd charge D plus linear kernel problems -- conditionally on even-spin vacancy, a machine-verified but finite-range input. For the Z_2 x Z_2 "paperclip" family we publish the spin-7 density P_8, prove [I_3,I_5]=[I_3,I_7]=[I_5,I_7]=0 identically in n, and verify the Lukyanov-Vitchev-Zamolodchikov four-screening characterization at spins 7 and 9 at sampled n. For the A_2^(2) family we give an exact Coulomb-gas dictionary to the dual Phi_{1,2}/Phi_{2,1} perturbations (that identification itself being classical, Zamolodchikov; Smirnov), whence the infinite hierarchy at all spins 6n+-1 follows by the Feigin-Frenkel affine-Toda theorem; our contribution is the certified dictionary, not a new all-spin construction. At c=1 the tower's defining charges are exactly quantum KdV at c_{1,4}=-25/2 -- the all-odd sector of the Freeman-Hornfeck-West family: the hierarchy embeds at every odd spin, equality verified through spin 15, all-spin multiplicity one open. We exclude or disfavor the named non-KdV candidates at the tested loci and identify the self-dual degeneracy as L(1,0). Through spin 11, the generic centralizer of I_3 is, for all three Z_2 x O(N) "cylindrical" families, one-dimensional at odd and zero at even spins, with Q(t)-symbolic charges at spins 7, 9, 11 and an explained fiber jump at N=-2. We certify the published 2004 low-spin formulas and record five corrections (one presumed). All-spin statements are made only where proved, finite-range otherwise. We close with a corank-character conjecture linking the rank deficiencies of the bilinear systems to characters of screening kernels.

</details>


### [43] [Exact Solutions to Matrix Models and String Theories: The Global Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23366)
*Jasper Kager,João Rodrigues,Ricardo Schiappa,Maximilian Schwick,Noam Tamarin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过离散傅里叶/Zak变换（discrete-Fourier/Zak-transform）表示配分函数的复原超瞬级数（resurgent transseries），给出矩阵模型与弦理论的精确非微扰（nonperturbative）闭式解，并获得完整的非线性斯托克斯数据（Stokes data）与连接公式，从而实现跨相图的全局检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作仅在被称作"局部"层面验证了这些精确解，要研究其在完整相图上的全局行为，仍需完整的非线性斯托克斯数据及相应的连接公式（connection formulae），这一缺失构成本文动机。

Method: 研究（反）本征值（anti-eigenvalues）在其谱曲线（spectral curves）全部多叶结构上的隧穿，以及结果如何从多割光滑曲线（multi-cut smooth-curves）退化到单割奇异曲线（single-cut singular-curves）；并利用所得精确斯托克斯自同构（Stokes automorphisms）在所有斯托克斯线交叉处正确实施斯托克斯跃迁。

Result: 由大阶分析（large-order analysis）与矩阵积分计算对上述结构进行了广泛校验；在从矩阵模型到非临界弦（non-critical strings）的多个例子中，解析与数值检验给出非常精确的吻合，且跨弱到强 't Hooft 耦合、大 N 到小 N 及任意复参数值均成立。

Conclusion: 所得精确解析表达式得到数值验证；反本征值或负张力 D-膜（negative-tension D-branes）是获得此类高精度吻合的必要组成部分。

Abstract: Exact nonperturbative solutions to matrix models and string theories are obtained in closed-form via a discrete-Fourier/Zak-transform representation of their partition-function resurgent-transseries. Examples include all hermitian one-matrix models (paired with their topological string duals) alongside all their multicritical and minimal-string double-scaling limits. The resulting structure is resonant, necessarily including both eigenvalues (D-branes) and anti-eigenvalues (negative-tension-branes). In a complementary preceding paper these solutions were extensively verified at the local level. In order to study them globally across their phase diagrams, complete non-linear Stokes data (and corresponding connection formulae) are still needed -- all of which are obtained herein. This involves a careful study of (anti-) eigenvalue tunneling across the complete multi-sheeted structure of their associated spectral-curves, and how the result descends from multi-cut smooth-curves to single-cut singular-curves -- all of which are extensively checked via large-order analysis and matrix-integral computations. Making use of the resulting exact Stokes automorphisms, nonperturbative solutions can be fully tested, from weak-to-strong 't Hooft coupling, as well as from large-to-small N, and for any complex-values of all parameters, by correctly implementing Stokes-transitions at the crossing of all possible Stokes-lines. Several analytical and numerical tests are carried through in diverse examples covering from matrix models through non-critical strings, which produce very sharp numerical matches fully validating our exact analytical expressions. Anti-eigenvalues or negative-tension D-branes are an absolutely essential part of the story in order to find such precise agreements.

</details>


### [44] [Localizing the M-Theory Path Integral: Membrane Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23369)
*Pietro Benetti Genolini,Jerome P. Gauntlett,Jaeha Park,James Sparks*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出 M2-膜瞬子（M2-instanton）对 M-理论路径积分贡献的新公式，通过等变局域化（equivariant localization）在普朗克长度全阶量子修正下计算单瞬子贡献，并在 ABJM 及 V^{5,2} 理论中获得与场论结果一致及新的解析预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 M-理论瞬子计算缺乏包含普朗克长度（Planck length）全阶量子修正的系统表述，且需与超共形场论（如 ABJM）的已知瞬子系数对照验证。

Method: 以相对等变局域化（relative equivariant localization）确定 M2-膜作用量，用等变 Dolbeault 特征（equivariant Dolbeault character）计算单圈行列式（one-loop determinant）；对连续族 M2-膜在其模空间（moduli space）上作等变局域化。

Result: 对偶于 ABJM 理论的背景中，由线性全纯填充（linear holomorphic fillings）计算球面配分函数（sphere partition function）与超对称 AdS_4 黑洞相关的拓扑扭曲指标（topologically twisted index）的单瞬子贡献，复现已知场论瞬子系数，并在任意 Chern-Simons 能级下给出质量与压扁形变的新解析预言；对 V^{5,2} 理论计算新瞬子贡献并建立其特殊压扁配分函数与圆球面上 ABJM 配分函数的精确关系。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了 M2-膜瞬子贡献的全阶量子修正表述，兼具与已知场论结果的一致性及新的解析预言，并揭示 V^{5,2} 与 ABJM 理论配分函数间的精确对应。

Abstract: We propose a formula for M2-instanton contributions to the M-theory path integral, incorporating quantum corrections to all orders in the Planck length. The M2-brane action is determined by relative equivariant localization, while an equivariant Dolbeault character computes the one-loop determinant. Continuous families of M2-branes are treated by equivariant localization on their moduli spaces. For M-theory backgrounds dual to ABJM theory, we compute single instanton contributions from linear holomorphic fillings to the sphere partition function and the topologically twisted index associated with supersymmetric $AdS_4$ black holes. Our results reproduce known field theory instanton coefficients and give new analytic predictions for mass and squashing deformations at all Chern-Simons levels. For the $V^{5,2}$ theory, we compute new instanton contributions and establish an exact relation between its partition function at special squashing and that of ABJM theory on the round three-sphere.

</details>


### [45] [A Positive Metric on Four-Dimensional QFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23370)
*Thomas Hartman,Grégoire Mathys,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出 Jack-Osborn 度规的非微扰推广，并证明其非负性由平均零能量条件（ANEC）保证，从而由 ANEC 推出强 a-定理（strong a-theorem）及沿重整化群流的单调函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jack 与 Osborn 在局域重整化群（local renormalization group）经典工作中引入了四维量子场论空间上的自然度规，微扰论下该度规决定 a-反常（a-anomaly）的流动，其正性蕴含 a-定理。本文旨在将该结构推广至非微扰层面。

Method: 对三点函数 ⟨O_I ℰ O_J⟩ 进行谱分析，其中 O_I 为相关算符（relevant operators），ℰ 为平均零能量（averaged null energy）。该三点函数在固定光线（light ray）的零旋转（null rotations）谱下分解，从而在每个碰撞参数（impact parameter）处给出一个正性条件。

Result: 所导出的 Jack-Osborn 度规非微扰推广在 ANEC 下被证明为非负；由此 ANEC 蕴含强 a-定理，并提供沿流（flow）的单调函数。

Conclusion: 平均零能量条件（ANEC）可作为非微扰层面 a-定理正性与单调性的基础，其与 Jack-Osborn 度规的非微扰推广相结合，给出强 a-定理的推导及流动单调函数的存在性。

Abstract: A natural metric on the space of four-dimensional quantum field theories was introduced by Jack and Osborn in classic work on the local renormalization group. In perturbation theory, the Jack-Osborn metric determines the flow of the $a$-anomaly, and positivity implies the $a$-theorem. We derive a non-perturbative generalization of the Jack-Osborn metric and show that it is non-negative by the averaged null energy condition (ANEC). Consequently, the ANEC implies the strong $a$-theorem, and provides the monotonic function along the flow. The method is based on spectral analysis of the three-point function $\langle \mathcal{O}_I \mathcal{E} \mathcal{O}_J\rangle$, where the $\mathcal{O}_I$ are relevant operators and $\mathcal{E}$ is the averaged null energy. This three-point function is decomposed under the spectrum of null rotations that fix the light ray, leading to a positivity condition at each impact parameter.

</details>

Our computer census finds new fortuitous classes and one-loop lifts in $\mathcal N=4$ Yang--Mills theory with several gauge groups. We have found no two-loop lift in the sectors tested. </details>


### [63] [Holography at finite resolution: zero-point length in AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24779)
*Ankit Anand,Kimet Jusufi,Amir A. Khodahami,Adrinil Paul,Ahmad Sheykhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息（holography）框架下考察 T-对偶（T-duality）启发有效场论中的零点长度（zero-point length）$l_0$ 的两种解读：作为边界分辨尺度可给出紫外有限、可精确求解的边界响应与有限纠缠熵；作为体固有长度可使单圈蝌蚪图（tadpole）有限，但其传播子（propagator）的 Källén-Lehmann 分解含负连续权重，阻碍幺正解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T-对偶启发有效场论中的零点长度 $l_0$ 在全息语境下含义并不唯一，需澄清其物理地位：它究竟是边界上的分辨尺度，还是体内在 AdS 不变意义下的固有长度。本文旨在区分这两种解读并检验各自的可解性、有限性与幺正性后果。

Method: 将 $l_0$ 作为边界分辨尺度时，用其对 AdS 体中标量场源做滤波，并证明该滤波器等价于 $ν=1$ 标量在径向深度 $z=l_0$ 处经源归一化的体到边界传递函数（bulk-to-boundary transfer function）；把 Ryu-Takayanagi 面锚定于同一径向面以计算纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）与互信息（mutual information），并在 AdS$_3$ 双区间情形下求闭式解。作为体固有长度时，则计算单圈蝌蚪图与边界两点函数，并进行 Källén-Lehmann 分解。

Result: 作为滤波尺度，边界响应无需求硬截断（hard cutoff）即紫外有限，并继承零点长度核的幂律尾部（power-law tails）；纠缠熵保持周长律（perimeter law）与普适球面自由能（sphere free energy）。AdS$_3$ 双区间给出任意区间长度下互信息连通性转变（connectivity transition）的闭式结果：固定总长时等长区间使连通窗口最大，次分辨区（subresolution regime）中临界间距呈普适标度律，重标度互信息塌缩为分段线性剖面。作为体固有长度，单圈蝌蚪图有限且边界两点函数保持共形形式，但 Källén-Lehmann 分解自阈值起出现负连续权重。

Conclusion: 零点长度的两种解读在数学上均可操作，但物理地位不同：滤波解读给出紫外有限且保留全息纠缠结构的边界理论；体固有长度解读虽改善紫外行为，却因负连续权重而无法支持该传播子的幺正解释。比值 $L/l_0$ 标识的是形状因子（form factor）的交叠尺度（crossover scale），而非某个幺正完备理论所导出的谱。

Abstract: We explore the zero-point length $l_0$ of T-duality-inspired effective field theory in holography, distinguishing two readings. As a boundary resolution scale, $l_0$ filters the source of a bulk scalar in anti-de Sitter space and yields an exactly solvable boundary response that is ultraviolet finite without a hard cutoff and inherits the power-law tails of the zero-point-length kernel. We show that this filter is the source-normalized bulk-to-boundary transfer function of a $ν=1$ scalar evaluated at radial depth $z=l_0$. Anchoring Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces on the same radial surface gives finite entanglement entropies that retain the perimeter law and the universal sphere free energy. For two intervals in AdS$_3$, the prescription yields the mutual-information connectivity transition in closed form for arbitrary interval lengths; equal intervals maximize the connectivity window at fixed total length, and in the subresolution regime the critical separation exhibits a universal scaling law with a rescaled mutual information collapsing onto a piecewise-linear profile. As an AdS-invariant bulk proper length, $l_0$ renders the one-loop tadpole finite while preserving the conformal form of boundary two-point functions. Its exact Källén-Lehmann decomposition, however, has negative continuum weight beginning at threshold, obstructing a unitary interpretation of this propagator. The ratio $L/l_0$ identifies a crossover scale of the form factor rather than a derived spectrum of a unitary completion.

</details>
