  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶曲率修正（higher-curvature corrections）对黑洞热力学的影响，并将既有基于静态球对称黑洞的作用量展开推广到旋转情形，以避免慢转近似（slow-rotation expansion）的限制。

Method: 采用微扰欧几里得作用量（perturbative Euclidean-action）方法：以静态球对称黑洞作为作用量展开的基准，主体计算针对五维、渐近平直、等角动量的旋转黑洞，在爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）上加入高斯-博内（Gauss–Bonnet）与三次准拓扑相互作用，将吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）算至次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-leading order）。

Result: 吉布斯自由能结果保留对背景自旋参数的完整依赖，且适用于二次与三次曲率不变量的任意耦合；由此导出修正后的质量、熵与角动量，并显示旋转如何影响修正量的大小与符号。

Conclusion: 准拓扑引力的高阶曲率修正可系统纳入旋转黑洞热力学，旋转效应对修正项的量级与符号具有实质影响，且该框架无需慢转展开即可给出一般耦合下的结果。

Abstract: We investigate higher-curvature corrections to black-hole thermodynamics in quasitopological gravity using perturbative Euclidean-action methods. Static, spherically symmetric black holes provide a benchmark for the action expansion, while our main application concerns five-dimensional, asymptotically flat, rotating black holes with equal angular momenta. For Einstein gravity supplemented by Gauss--Bonnet and cubic quasitopological interactions, we compute the Gibbs free energy through next-to-next-to-leading order, retaining both quadratic and cubic corrections. The result retains the full dependence on the background spin parameter, without a slow-rotation expansion, and extends to arbitrary couplings of quadratic and cubic curvature invariants. We derive the corrected mass, entropy and angular momenta, and show how rotation affects the size and sign of the corrections.

</details>

*Laurentiu Rodina*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将 Tr(φ³) 理论中的“隐藏零点（hidden zeros）”结构推广至任意 Tr(φᵐ) 理论，提出块零点（block zeros）与杆零点（rod zeros）两族新零点，并证明其可唯一重构树振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏零点此前已被证明是重构 Tr(φ³) 彩色标量理论振幅的有力工具，但尚不清楚该结构是否可推广到更高次相互作用的 Tr(φᵐ) 理论。

Method: 对奇数 m，用连续动量的正交和构造块零点（block zeros），推广原立方隐藏零点；对 m≥4（含偶 m），通过“杆堆积问题（rod packing problem）”描述第二族非线性杆零点（rod zeros），并在 Tr(φ⁴) 中化为动量收缩矩阵的秩一条件。

Result: 杆零点与 Voloshin 的经典阈值湮灭零点（threshold nullification zeros，即大质量 φ⁴ 理论中所有产生粒子静止时振幅消失）相联系；在假定定域性（locality）下证明杆零点可将 m≥4 的所有 Tr(φᵐ) 树振幅在归一化意义下唯一确定，并推测块零点对奇数 m 理论同时强制定域性与幺正性（unitarity）。

Conclusion: 隐藏零点结构可系统推广至一般 Tr(φᵐ) 理论，构成一组具备强大重构能力的振幅约束；块零点对奇数 m 是否同时蕴含定域性与幺正性仍为待验证猜想。

Abstract: Hidden zeros have proven powerful tools for reconstructing amplitudes in Tr$(φ^3)$, a theory of colored scalars with cubic interactions. We show that these structures extend to any Tr$(φ^m)$ theory. For odd $m$, we construct \emph{block zeros} from orthogonal sums of consecutive momenta, generalizing the original cubic hidden zeros. For every $m\geq4$, including even $m$, we describe a second family of nonlinear \emph{rod zeros} through a rod packing problem. In Tr$(φ^4)$ this construction reduces to a simple rank-one condition on a matrix of momentum contractions. We connect this new zero to the classic threshold nullification zeros of Voloshin, where certain amplitudes in massive $φ^4$ theory vanish when all produced particles are at rest. Finally, we demonstrate the reconstruction power of these zeros. Assuming locality, we prove that the rod zeros uniquely fix all Tr$(φ^m)$ tree amplitudes for $m\geq4$ up to normalization. We also conjecture that the block zeros enforce both locality and unitarity for any Tr$(φ^m)$ theory with odd $m$.

</details>

### [66] [Producing $μ$Bq/m3-level low-226Ra ultrapure water for the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23711)

TL;DR: 江门地下中微子实验（JUNO）为满足液闪（LS）与超纯水（UPW）直接接触时镭（Ra）浓度<50 μBq/m3 的要求，研制了低至 3.9 μBq/m3 的高灵敏 Ra 测量系统，并优化 100 t/h 超纯水制备工艺，最终稳定产出 Ra<4 μBq/m3 的超纯水。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JUNO 作为 20 千吨低本底液闪探测器，主要目标是测定中微子质量顺序（neutrino mass ordering）；由于探测器灌装过程中液闪与超纯水直接液-液接触，因此对超纯水的镭（Ra）浓度提出 <50 μBq/m3 的严格要求。

Method: 研制可在水中检测低至 3.9 μBq/m3 的高灵敏 Ra 测量系统，并优化 100 t/h 的超纯水制备流程；通过将选择性离子交换树脂（selective ion-exchange resin）与膜分离技术（membrane-based separation technologies）集成，构建净化系统。

Result: 系统持续产出 Ra 浓度 <4 μBq/m3 的超纯水，优于 JUNO 指标要求，且据作者所知为同类工程规模下报道的最低水平之一；论文同时给出净化流程各环节 Ra 去除效率的系统评估与最终水质的验证结果。

Conclusion: 所设计的超纯水系统与高灵敏 Ra 测量平台满足并超越 JUNO 的严格要求，验证了该方案在工程规模上去除 Ra 的有效性。

Abstract: The Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO), a 20 kton low-background liquid-scintillator (LS) detector, was designed primarily to determine the neutrino mass ordering. JUNO requires ultrapure water (UPW) with a Ra concentration < 50~$μ$Bq/m3 because of the direct liquid-liquid contact between LS and UPW during detector filling. To meet this stringent requirement, a high-sensitivity measurement system capable of detecting Ra in water down to 3.9 $μ$Bq/m3 was developed, and the 100 t/h UPW production process was optimized. By integrating selective ion-exchange resin with membrane-based separation technologies, the system consistently produced UPW with a Ra concentration of <4$μ$Bq/m3, exceeding the JUNO specification and, to our knowledge, representing one of the lowest reported levels achieved at this engineering scale. This paper describes the design and implementation of JUNO's UPW system and the high-sensitivity Ra measurement platform, together with a systematic evaluation of Ra removal efficiencies across the purification train and validation of the final water quality.

</details>


### [67] [The ALICE asynchronous software trigger processing for LHC Run 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24061)
*Sara Pucillo for the ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: ALICE 实验在 LHC Run 3 中面临数据量激增问题，通过实现物理驱动的异步软件触发（asynchronous software trigger）并在压缩时间帧（Compressed Time Frames, CTFs）上执行略读（skimming），将保留数据量降至原体积约 4.5%，同时保留未来以更优校准与软件重新重建（reconstruction）的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Run 3 的探测器与计算升级使 ALICE 能以最高 1.5 MHz 的质子-质子（pp）和 50 kHz 的铅-铅（Pb--Pb）碰撞率连续采集数据，pp 数据集比 Run 1 与 Run 2 之和还大三个数量级以上，受存储容量限制，完整长期保存不可行。

Method: 采用物理驱动的异步软件触发：在完整的异步重建之后，由中央事件过滤处理器（Central Event Filter Processor）应用覆盖 ALICE pp 物理项目的 100 多条基于分析的筛选通道（selection channels），再据此对原始 CTFs 进行略读，仅在探测器特定的时间窗内保留相关探测器信息。

Result: 该流程将保留的 CTF 数据量压缩至原始大小的约 4.5%，同时保留未来利用改进的校准（calibrations）与软件进行重建的可能性。

Conclusion: 该策略使 ALICE 能够获取其物理项目中的稀有信号与过程，报告介绍了从碰撞筛选到 CTF 略读的异步软件触发的实现与运行情况。

Abstract: The detector and computing upgrades for LHC Run 3 enable ALICE to continuously acquire proton--proton (pp) and lead--lead (Pb--Pb) collisions at interaction rates of up to 1.5 MHz and 50 kHz, respectively. The resulting pp dataset is more than three orders of magnitude larger than the combined Run 1 and Run 2 samples, and its long-term retention in full would be impractical given the available storage capacity.
  To address this challenge, ALICE has implemented a physics-driven asynchronous software trigger designed to retain only collisions containing signals of physics interest. Following full asynchronous reconstruction, the Central Event Filter Processor applies more than 100 analysis-based selection channels spanning the ALICE pp physics programme. The selected collisions are then used to skim the original Compressed Time Frames (CTFs), preserving the relevant detector information within detector-specific time windows. This procedure reduces the retained CTF volume to approximately 4.5% of its original size, while preserving the possibility of future reconstruction with improved calibrations and software.
  This contribution describes the implementation and operation of the asynchronous software trigger, from collision selection to CTF skimming, and highlights how this strategy provides access to rare signals and processes across the ALICE physics programme.

</details>

The results are validated using simulation and discussed in the context of future Research and Development (R&D) goals.

### [69] [4D-TEMPO: Differentiable 4D-STEM ptychography via fast Physics Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24776)
*Ramani Duraiswami,Yichao Zhang*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 提出可微分物理渲染器 4D-TEMPO，在泊松似然下直接将原子、显微镜与探测器拟合到4D-STEM电子计数，输出带Fisher信息误差棒的原子列表，实现高精度、快速、可定标的3D原子成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子叠层成像（electron ptychography）方法或先重建图像再读取原子，或在L1损失下拟合原子，均无法给出逐原子不确定性；缺乏误差棒限制了定量化应用。

Method: 构建可微分物理渲染器（differentiable physical renderer），在泊松似然（Poisson likelihood）下将原子、显微镜与探测器直接拟合到记录的电子计数；原子表示为带3D位置与热振动宽度的高斯基元（Gaussian primitive），误差棒由Fisher信息（Fisher information）导出。

Result: 模拟WSe2双层上，面内误差降至3.2 pm（对比4.8 pm，减少约三分之一），深度误差降低40–61%；模型设定正确时误差棒校准良好（69%位置坐标落在1σ内）。实验数据上对留出衍射图的预测优于三种已发表代码，且速度显著更快（5分钟对比最长20.5小时；在PrScO3上比torchslice快180倍）。

Conclusion: 逐原子误差棒、可规划剂量与快速重建共同指向低剂量、实时、面向束敏感与动态材料的3D成像。

Abstract: Electron ptychography recovers atomic structure from 4D-STEM data: diffraction patterns at multiple positions of a scanned probe. Current codes reconstruct an image and read atoms off it, or fit atoms under an $L_1$ loss; neither gives per-atom uncertainty. We propose 4D-TEMPO, a differentiable physical renderer that fits the atoms, the microscope, and the detector directly to the recorded electron counts under a Poisson likelihood. The output is a list of atoms, each a Gaussian primitive with a 3D position, a thermal width, and a model-conditional error bar from the Fisher information. The Fisher information also conditions the solver, gives the dose (electrons per Å$^2$) each parameter needs, and separates calibration from imaging. On a simulated WSe$_2$ bilayer, starting from the published reconstruction, fitted atoms cut in-plane error by a third (3.2 vs. 4.8 pm) and depth error by 40-61% against its 3D peaks. Depth comes from multiple scattering into the dark field, the faint counts outside the direct beam. The two layers, $10\times$ closer than the depth of field, separate; per-atom depth then follows the layer geometry. When the model is well specified, the bars are calibrated (69% of position coordinates within $1σ$). On experimental WSe$_2$, fitted atoms predict held-out patterns better than the three published codes we test. With free probe modes where atoms are incomplete, the same likelihood predicts best on all three held-out benchmarks, matches every WSe$_2$ baseline in 5 min against up to 20.5 h, and passes torchslice at its released setting 180$\times$ sooner on PrScO$_3$. Per-atom error bars, planned doses, and fast reconstruction point to low-dose, real-time 3D imaging of beam-sensitive and dynamic materials.

</details>

### [70] [Quantum Monge-Kantorovich optimal transport in spacetime through spatiotemporal Kirkwood-Dirac distributions and spatiotemporal states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23462)
*Zhian Jia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将经典 Monge-Kantorovich 最优传输（optimal transport）推广至时空（spacetime）情境，通过时空相空间 Kirkwood-Dirac 准概率分布（quasiprobability distribution）与时空态（spatiotemporal state）两种等价形式构建量子最优传输问题，并给出对偶性（duality）与时空量子 Wasserstein 度量的严格定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的量子推广以密度算子（density operator）替代概率分布、以半正定代价算子（cost operator）替代代价函数，属于空间型量子最优传输；本文旨在将其拓展到时空（spatiotemporal）层面，以刻画同时涉及空间与时间维度的传输问题。

Result: 两种形式化被证明等价并通过时空算子框架相联系；Kirkwood-Dirac 框架下时空分布与代价函数为复值，耦合给出正确的局域边缘分布；Margenau-Hill 分布对应实值最优传输，在时空态形式中对应厄米（Hermitian）时空耦合态；论文给出严格定义及对偶性的细致讨论，并提出基于时空相空间度量的时空量子 Wasserstein 度量，验证其满足度量公理（metric axioms）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了时空量子最优传输的统一框架，涵盖复值准概率与厄米/非厄米算子两种表述及其等价关系，并给出满足公理的时空量子 Wasserstein 度量，为量子最优传输向时空维度的扩展提供了严格基础。

Abstract: Monge-Kantorovich optimal transport is a mathematical framework that determines the most efficient way to transform one probability distribution into another while minimizing a given cost function. Its central constructions include the Wasserstein distances, which quantify the space of probability distributions, and dual formulations that link to convex analysis. Its standard quantum generalization replaces probability distributions with density operators and cost function with positive semidefinite cost operator, which should be regarded as spatial quantum optimal transport. In this work, we propose the quantum optimal transport problem in spacetime through the spatiotemporal phase-space Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distribution and spatiotemporal states and through spatiotemporal state. We show that the two formalisms are equivalent and related via spatiotemporal operator frames. In the Kirkwood-Dirac phase-space formalism, the spatiotemporal distribution and the cost function are complex-valued, and the spatiotemporal couplings are Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distributions that yield the correct marginals for the local states. In the spatiotemporal state formalism, the cost operators are generally non-Hermitian, and the spatiotemporal couplings are generally non-Hermitian and yield the correct reduced local states. The Margenau-Hill phase-space distribution gives rise to a real-valued optimal transport in spatiotemporal phase space; in the spatiotemporal state formalism, this corresponds to a Hermitian spatiotemporal coupling state. We give a rigorous definitions and a careful discussion of its duality. We also introduce a spatiotemporal quantum Wasserstein metric based on a metric in spatiotemporal phase spaces and show that it satisfies the metric axioms.

</details>

Conclusion: 三角Rosen-Morse势在孤立与周期两种框架下均可精确求解，其强吸引区呈现端点重整化控制的两参数谱与Efimov型序列，周期情形下能带宽度随Coulomb耦合增强而指数收缩。

Abstract: We study the trigonometric Rosen-Morse potential as an isolated singular well and as an exactly solvable periodic lattice. The potential combines inverse-square singularities with Coulomb terms of opposite signs. In the strongly attractive regime, independent renormalization at the two endpoints gives a two-parameter family of spectra. Deep negative-energy levels form two asymptotic sequences with the same Efimov-like scaling and opposite first-order Coulomb corrections. In the weak-medium regime, self-adjoint matching conditions allow transmission between neighboring cells. We obtain the exact Bloch dispersion relation and determine the band widths, gaps, and electron and hole effective masses. At fixed band index, the bandwidth becomes exponentially small in the limit of large Coulomb coupling.

</details>

TL;DR: 在一致子域乘积展开（uniform subfield product expansion）假设下，本文构造了距离为 Ω_ε(N/log²N)、且支持至少 N^{1−ε} 个可独立寻址逻辑 CCZ 门的量子低密度奇偶校验码（qLDPC），并据此给出近最优的魔术态（magic state）协议。

Abstract: Assuming uniform subfield product expansion, we construct quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes with distances $Ω_ε(N/\log^2N)$ and at least $N^{1-ε}$ individually addressable logical CCZ gates on pairwise disjoint triples of logical qubits, for every fixed $0<ε<1$. With all logical qubits outside these triples initialized to $|0\rangle$, any subset of the corresponding logical CCZ gates can be implemented with a transversal physical CCZ circuit. As applications, we obtain two nearly optimal magic state protocols: CCZ distillation with arbitrarily small overhead exponent $γ\rightarrow 0$ and error suppression of nearly linear order, and a magic state fountain that directly prepares $N^{1-ε}$ encoded CCZ magic states with nearly linear distance $Ω_ε(N/\log^2N)$. The construction uses balanced products of expanding graph covers. Without the product expansion assumption, the graph covers also yield classical LDPC codes over a fixed field with almost linear dimension and linear distance that satisfy a multiplication property: the coordinatewise product of any three codewords lies in a second LDPC code of linear distance.

Abstract: We revisit the time-dependent harmonic oscillator (TDHO) in quantum mechanics based on the notion of equivalence transformations and Lie point symmetries. Within this framework, we give the most general point transformations depending on seven parameters which map from the TDHO to the usual harmonic oscillator (HO) and the other way around. As a byproduct we reconstruct the Schrödinger propagator (Mehler's kernel) for the time-independent harmonic oscillator. We demonstrate, without recourse to the Lewis--Riesenwert (LR) invariant, how Lie algebraic methods enable alternative derivation of the normalized wave functions of the TDHO by constructing solutions invariant under some specific subgroup of $\SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ combined with the gauge group. We further show that, on the relevant $\SL(2,\mathbb{R})$-invariant solution family, the Lewis--Riesenfeld operator defines a compatible second-order differential constraint, thereby clarifying its relation to the Lie symmetry structure of the TDHO.

</details>


### [74] [Instanton tunnelling as a verifiable benchmark for digital quantum simulation: circuit structure, classical simulability and hardware reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24624)
*Gerhard Hellstern,Marc Maussner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作以四分之一四次双势阱（quartic double well）中的瞬子（instanton）介导隧穿作为可在每一步与精确解对照的数字量子模拟基准，提出幺正单激发编码与逐键XX/YY Trotter 方案，证明任意切分处 Schmidt 秩有界，并在超导处理器上以约百分之几精度复现晶格能级劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个在每一步都能与精确答案核对的数字量子模拟（digital quantum simulation）基准，用以区分算法误差与硬件误差；瞬子介导隧穿提供了经典分析上可定义、且可由晶格模型动力学检验的参照量（劈裂，splitting）。

Method: 采用一元/单激发编码（unary, single-excitation encoding）将 N 个晶格站点映射到 N 个量子比特；对每键角度相等的相邻 XX/YY 对构成的二阶 Trotter 电路，证明任意切分处 Schmidt 秩上界为 4、每对之后为 2，且与 N 无关；用矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state）模拟并与精确解对比，并在两块超导处理器上于 N=8 实测全转移（full transfer）。

Result: MPS 模拟将精确劈裂复现至 N=48；逐键公式下允许的 Trotter 步长约按 N^-2 收缩，单次隧穿周期约需 N^3.3 个两比特门，而动能/势能分裂（kinetic/potential splitting）在 N=128 内步长保持约 0.065。实测在 N=8 上以几个百分点精度复现晶格劈裂及其对势垒高度的依赖，该分辨率下势垒由晶格而非瞬子设定。9 次重复中，相隔数分钟的运行符合散粒噪声（shot noise），相隔数小时至数周者差异可达 6.1%（同一固定比特集亦如此）；以会话为抽样单位时，处理器间在阻尼上存在差异，而劈裂差异未被确立。以实测单激发布居归一化使可用深度扩展约 4 倍但不改善劈裂；无需对角化的高斯初态（Gaussian start state）与基于本征向量的初态在劈裂上相差不超过 1.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 单激发编码下的逐键 XX/YY Trotter 电路具有与 N 无关的纠缠（entanglement）上界，构成可逐步验证的量子模拟基准；当前硬件分辨率下劈裂主要由晶格决定，且硬件漂移而非统计误差是重复性的主要限制，高斯初态可作为免对角化的实用替代。

Abstract: We use instanton-mediated tunnelling in the quartic double well as a benchmark for digital quantum simulation that is checkable against an exact answer at every step. The instanton enters through the classical analysis; the hardware tests the dynamics of the lattice model. A unary (single-excitation) encoding maps N lattice sites to N qubits, with the single-excitation dynamics as a reference that remains classically cheap at any N. For a second-order Trotter circuit with adjacent, equal-angle XX/YY pairs on each bond, we prove that the Schmidt rank across any cut is at most four at every gate boundary and at most two after each pair, independently of N. Matrix-product-state simulations of this gate list reproduce the exact splitting up to N = 48. For this bond-wise formula the admissible Trotter step shrinks roughly as N^-2, so one tunnelling period costs about N^3.3 two-qubit gates; a kinetic/potential splitting keeps the step near 0.065 up to N=128, so this scaling belongs to splitting the kinetic term bond by bond. At a coarser, separately bounded step we measure the full transfer at N=8 on two superconducting processors, recovering the lattice splitting to a few percent and its dependence on the barrier height, which at this resolution is set by the lattice rather than by the instanton. Over 9 repetitions, runs taken minutes apart agree to shot noise, while runs hours to weeks apart differ by up to 6.1%, also on a fixed set of qubits; with sessions as the sampling unit, the processors differ in the damping, while a difference in the splitting is not established. Normalising by the measured single-excitation population extends the usable depth about 4-fold but does not improve the splitting. A Gaussian start state, which needs no diagonalisation, reproduces the splitting of the eigenvector-based start to within 1.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [75] [A provably efficient classical algorithm for simulating OTOC$^{(1)}$ in 1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24640)
*Laura Lewis,Antonio A. Mele,Ronald de Wolf,Jarrod R. McClean,Amira Abbas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出"窗口传播（window propagation）"，首个可证明的多项式时间经典算法，用于近似一维 Haar 随机砖墙电路中任意深度的第一阶非时序关联子（OTOC），均方误差为逆多项式量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTOC 与信息扰乱（information scrambling）和量子混沌相关，且高阶 OTOC 估计被视为量子优势（quantum advantage）候选任务，但其经典难度尚不清楚；即使第一阶 OTOC 已有可经典处理的证据，此前仍无可证明的严格高效经典算法。

Result: 给出首个可证明的多项式时间经典算法，可在任意深度以逆多项式均方误差近似一维 Haar 随机砖墙电路的第一阶 OTOC；数值实验在最多 512 量子比特（Clifford + 768 T 门）和最多 22 量子比特（Haar 随机）规模上验证了模拟与精确计算的一致性。

Conclusion: 结果不直接适用于二维第二阶 OTOC（近期量子优势提案的目标对象）；但为理解 OTOC 估计的经典复杂度提供了严格进展，并确定了一类存在可证明多项式时间算法的非平凡量子电路，为理解这一广泛使用的量子混沌诊断工具奠定基础。

Abstract: Out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) are quantities of broad interest in many-body physics due to their connection with information scrambling and quantum chaos. Recently, estimating higher-order OTOCs in random quantum circuits was proposed as a promising avenue for quantum advantage, yet the classical hardness of this task remains poorly understood. Even for first-order OTOCs, where growing evidence suggests classical tractability, no efficient classical algorithm with rigorous guarantees has been established to date. In this work, we introduce "window propagation," the first provably polynomial-time classical algorithm for approximating first-order OTOCs in 1D Haar-random brickwork circuits, at arbitrary depths and with inverse-polynomial mean-squared error. We also conduct numerical experiments comparing our simulation results to exact computation on up to $512$ qubits for Clifford circuits with $768$ $T$-gates and up to $22$ qubits for Haar-random circuits. We emphasize that our results do not directly apply to second-order OTOCs in 2D, which is the subject of recent quantum advantage proposals. Nevertheless, our work provides a rigorous step towards understanding the classical complexity of OTOC estimation and represents a family of non-trivial quantum circuits for which a provable, polynomial time algorithm is now known, establishing foundations for understanding these widely used diagnostics of quantum chaos.

</details>


### [76] [Symmetry classification of hyperfine rovibrational molecular phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23406)
*Elin Ranjan Das,Jing-Chen Zhang,Victor V. Albert,Yuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构建分子相空间理论，将转动、振动与核自旋自由度统一视为取向与简正坐标对分子点群的商，并揭示分子物种与绝对最大纠缠（AME）载体态之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性结构虽已分别推动分子光谱学与量子信息科学的发展，但两个领域之间的联系在很大程度上仍未被探索。本文旨在建立这一联系。

Method: 将转动、振动和核自旋自由度联合处理为取向与简正坐标（normal coordinates）对分子点群（point group）的商空间，构建分子相空间理论；依据三种自由度在对称变换下的组合方式对分子态分类为分子物种（molecular species），并分析其载体态结构及商空间上的傅里叶变换。

Result: 每个分子物种由一个特定的绝对最大纠缠（absolutely maximally entangled, AME）载体态定义，其中45个物种对应二维载体态空间；在局域酉变换（local unitaries）与扇区次序意义下，75个点群中的分子物种共实现21种AME态，包括1个直积态、4个最大纠缠对与16个真正三体纠缠态。

Conclusion: 商空间上的傅里叶变换联系取向描述与角动量描述，并保持这些载体态不变；文中以CaOCH₃为例提出制备与驱动对称适配弯曲态的示例实验方案。

Abstract: Symmetry-based structures have advanced molecular spectroscopy and quantum information science, yet connections between these fields remain largely unexplored. We construct a phase space theory of a molecule by jointly treating rotational, vibrational, and nuclear-spin degrees of freedom as the quotient of orientations and normal coordinates by the molecule's point group. The various combinations of how these three degrees of freedom transform under symmetries organize a molecule's states into molecular species, and we show that each species is defined by a particular absolutely maximally entangled (AME) carrier state, or, for 45 species, by a two-dimensional space of them. Up to local unitaries and the order of the sectors, molecular species realize 21 AME states across 75 point groups: a product state, four maximally entangled pairs, and 16 genuinely tripartite states. A Fourier transform on the quotient relates orientation and angular-momentum descriptions while preserving these carrier states. We propose an example experimental protocol for preparing and driving symmetry-adapted bend states of $\mathrm{CaOCH_3}$.

</details>


### [77] [Quantum resources and the onset of the quantum Monte Carlo sign problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23514)
*Naga Dileep Varikuti,Leela Ganesh Chandra Lakkaraju,Philipp Hauke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过调控随机 k-local 哈密顿量的非斯托克斯性（nonstoquasticity），揭示蒙特卡罗符号问题（sign problem）与量子资源（纠缠 entanglement、非稳定子性 nonstabilizerness）之间的微妙关联，发现基态量子蒙特卡罗（QMC）的失效更取决于态的量子资源含量而非哈密顿量的符号结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡罗符号问题（Monte Carlo sign problem）是量子多体系统经典模拟的根本障碍，但其与量子资源之间的联系尚不清楚，本文旨在系统探究二者的关系。

Method: 采用数值张量网络（tensor-network）研究，系统调节随机 k-local 哈密顿量的非斯托克斯性程度，考察基态的符号复杂度及纠缠、非稳定子性等量子资源；并结合数值量子蒙特卡罗（QMC）模拟进行对比，辅以在斯托克斯极限下构造近似基态的解析论证。

Result: 在直至显著非斯托克斯性的广泛区间内，符号复杂度与量子资源被强烈抑制；随非斯托克斯性进一步增强，量子资源出现急剧转变（crossover）并显著增强，而 QMC 模拟在该区域急剧恶化，尽管哈密顿量此前已相当非斯托克斯。此外，图态（graph states）的非稳定子性始终为零，但二部图（bipartite graphs）上的符号可被轻易消除，多部图（multipartite）则不能。

Conclusion: 基态 QMC 的失效与态的量子资源含量相关性更强，而非哈密顿量的符号结构；结果揭示了量子资源与 QMC 计算困难之间高度微妙的相互作用。

Abstract: The Monte Carlo sign problem poses a fundamental obstacle to classical simulation of quantum many-body systems, yet its connection to quantum resources remains poorly understood. We probe this connection by systematically tuning the degree of nonstoquasticity in random k-local Hamiltonians. In numerical tensor-network studies, we find the sign complexity and the quantum resources of entanglement and nonstabilizerness of the ground state to be strongly suppressed in a broad regime up to substantial nonstoquasticity. As nonstoquasticity is further increased, the considered quantum resources experience sharp crossovers into a regime where they are strongly enhanced. Surprisingly, numerical quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations drastically deteriorate in the same region, even though the Hamiltonian is already considerably nonstoquastic well before it, indicating that the failure of ground-state QMC is correlated more with the resource content of the state than the sign structure of the Hamiltonian. We corroborate these findings analytically by constructing an approximate ground state of k-local Hamiltonians in the stoquastic limit. Moreover, we identify a distinct behaviour in graph states. Their nonstabilizerness always vanishes, but, as we demonstrate analytically, on bipartite graphs the sign can be easily cured while in multipartite graph states it cannot. Our results thus shed light on the highly subtle interplay between quantum resources and the computational difficulty encountered by QMC methods.

</details>


### [78] [Fe$^{2+}$:forsterite Qubit System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23550)
*Dishaanth S. Pillai,G. M. Wysin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究分析了一种Fe²⁺:镁橄榄石（forsterite）量子比特系统，通过求解哈密顿量的幺正时间演化，表明其在10 K以下可高保真地实现态间布居转移且抗热噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算机受量子退相干（quantum decoherence）制约，热涨落与振动等环境扰动会破坏脆弱的量子态，通常需依赖稀释制冷机（dilution fridge）维持低温，因此需要探索对热噪声不敏感的量子比特体系。

Method: 针对所提出的Fe²⁺:forsterite量子比特系统，利用已知的轴向与横向各向异性项（axial and traverse anisotropy terms），求解该体系哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）下量子态矢量的幺正时间演化（unitary time-evolution），温度条件设定至10 K。

Result: 系统可在最低两个能级间以拉比半周期（Rabi half-period）所预测的速率有效转移布居（population），保真度达99.9%；并能使布居保持在目标态而不发生退相干，且在最高10 K范围内结果保持一致。

Conclusion: 与许多常见量子比特系统不同，该Fe²⁺:forsterite量子比特系统无需稀释制冷机即可工作，且对热噪声不敏感，具备在较高温度下运行的潜力。

Abstract: Quantum Computing uses quantum mechanical phenomena, such as superposition and entanglement of quantum bits (qubits), to represent and manipulate information in multidimensional state spaces. This approach allows complex mathematical functions to be executed simultaneously rather than sequentially, making them much more efficient than classical computers. However, current quantum computers suffer from quantum decoherence, where small changes to their environment, such as thermal fluctuations and vibrations, affect the systems' fragile states. The goal of this study is to discuss the functionality of a proposed Fe$^{2+}$:forsterite qubit system by solving the unitary time-evolution of a quantum state vector for the system's Hamiltonian, using known axial and traverse anisotropy terms at temperatures up to 10 K. The system can effectively transfer population between its two lowest states at rates predicted by the Rabi half-period, with 99.9% fidelity. Additionally, it can hold the population at a desired state without docohering. The results remained consistent for temperatures up to 10 K, meaning, unlike many common qubit systems, the proposed qubit system could function without a dilution fridge and is not fragile to thermal noise.

</details>


### [79] [Parisi Energy before Parisi Geometry in the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23645)
*Maxime Dupont,Bhuvanesh Sundar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作把量子近似优化算法（QAOA）的输出分布当作统计物理对象，在热力学极限下直接刻画其几何结构；发现典型样本宏观不相关，而低能尾部呈现与热自旋玻璃转变临界行为一致的 Parisi 相，且随电路深度加深，该组织逐渐向典型输出移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃（spin glass）能量景观的碎裂结构使优化极为困难；深度较大的计算表明 QAOA 能逼近 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 模型（SK 模型）的 Parisi 基态能量，但其实现机制并不清楚，因此需要从分布几何层面理解 QAOA 的行为。

Method: 将 QAOA 分布视为统计物理对象，在同一问题实例上抽取两条比特串（bitstring），通过其宏观相似性（macroscopic similarity）进行耦合，在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下考察分布几何随电路深度 p 的变化。

Result: 典型 QAOA 样本即使在耦合下仍保持宏观不相关，但对耦合的敏感性随深度 p 增强；低能尾部则表现出更强的重叠倾向，并呈现与热自旋玻璃转变相同的临界行为的类 Parisi 相（Parisi-like phase）；随 p 增大，这种组织出现的起点向典型输出移动，与二者差异仅在 p→∞ 时消失相一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明 QAOA 走的是一条区别于利用结构的经典算法与热采样的路径：它在 Parisi 几何成为典型之前就已接近 Parisi 能量。

Abstract: The fractured low-energy landscape of spin glasses presents a notorious barrier to optimization. While high-depth calculations indicate that the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) can approach the Parisi ground-state energy of the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick spin glass, how it does so remains elusive. Here, we treat the QAOA distribution as a statistical-physics object and probe its geometry directly in the thermodynamic limit. We draw two independent bitstrings from the same problem instance and couple them through their macroscopic similarity. We find that typical QAOA samples remain macroscopically uncorrelated, even as their sensitivity to this coupling grows with circuit depth $p$. By contrast, the low-energy tail shows a much stronger tendency to overlap and reveals a Parisi-like phase with the same critical behavior as the thermal spin-glass transition. The onset of this organization moves toward the typical output as the circuit deepens, consistent with the separation vanishing only as $p\to\infty$. Together, these findings suggest a route distinct from structure-exploiting classical algorithms and thermal sampling, one in which the QAOA approaches the Parisi energy before Parisi geometry becomes typical.

</details>


### [80] [Hierarchical coupling in tripartite van der Waals cavity magnonics for a low-loss and tunable coupled-mode Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23756)
*Taehee Kim,Sunghun Park,Byoung-moo Ann,Sung Jin An,Seung-Bo Shim,Hakseong Kim,Haydar Sahin,Zhuo Bin Siu,Mansoor B. A. Jalil,Youngwook Kim,Thomas K. M. Graham,Brian B. Zhou,Myung-Joong Hwang,Yaroslav M. Blanter,Jungpil Seo,Hee Chul Park,Minkyung Jung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在基于 CrCl3 的三体范德华腔磁子学（cavity magnonics, CM）体系中实现层级反交叉（hierarchical anti-crossing）结构，并构造等效耦合双模哈密顿量，在保持强耦合（strong coupling）条件的同时将损耗（loss）降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔磁子学（cavity magnonics, CM）可借助磁振子共振与磁振子—光子耦合（magnon–photon coupling）的大范围原位磁通可调性（in situ flux tunability）研究丰富的耦合模式物理，而范德华（van der Waals, vdW）磁体提供了无需复杂制备工艺的超紧凑集成实现；但显著的磁振子损耗（magnon loss）阻碍了这些优势的结合。

Method: 在含 CrCl3 的三体 vdW 腔磁子学体系中构建层级反交叉结构，并建立等效耦合双模哈密顿量（coupled-dual-mode Hamiltonian）；同时通过理论分析考察改变磁场方向与强度对损耗与耦合的影响。

Result: 在维持强耦合条件下实现损耗约一个数量级的降低；理论证实通过调节磁场方向与强度可同时且原位地调控损耗与耦合；器件厚度为微米量级、宽度仅数毫米，显著小于基于块体晶体（bulk crystals）或晶体阵列（crystal arrays）的器件。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了范德华腔磁子学作为研究耦合模式系统（coupled-mode systems）所预言的多种有趣现象的通用平台（versatile platform）。

Abstract: One of the greatest merits of cavity magnonics (CM) is the ability to explore rich coupled-mode physics, driven by the large \textit{in situ} flux tunability of magnonic resonances and magnon--photon coupling. Meanwhile, van der Waals (vdW) magnets offer an ultra-compact and highly integrated implementation of CM without complex fabrication processes. Nonetheless, the substantial magnon losses significantly hinder the combination of all these advantages. Here, we realize a hierarchical anti-crossing structure in a tripartite vdW CM with CrCl3 and realize an effective coupled-dual-mode Hamiltonian. We achieve an order-of-magnitude reduction in loss while maintaining a strong coupling condition. Additionally, we theoretically confirm the feasibility of simultaneous and \textit{in situ} tuning of both loss and coupling by varying the magnetic field direction and strength. Featuring a micrometer-scale thickness and a width of a few millimeters, our device is significantly smaller than those based on bulk crystals or crystal arrays. Our results establish vdW cavity magnonics as versatile platform for investigating intriguing phenomena predicted in coupled-mode systems.

</details>


### [81] [Topological Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24013)
*Zhi-Lei Zhang,Lianxiang Cui,C. P. Sun,Kohei Nakajima,Kazuyuki Aihara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出拓扑量子储备池计算（topological quantum reservoir computing），利用受保护自由度存储信息、宿主系统内禀动力学处理信息，在一维Kitaev链上验证了Majorana边缘模式与体态快速响应的协同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展通用量子计算（universal quantum computation）与实现量子机器学习（quantum machine learning）仍具挑战，需要利用现有容错量子平台的算力潜力，因而探索以受保护逻辑量子比特（logical qubits）支撑计算的新范式。

Method: 采用一维Kitaev链模型，将长寿命的Majorana边缘模式（Majorana edge modes）用于记忆存储、体态（bulk）快速响应用于信息处理，并分析受保护存储访问权的权衡关系，设计匹配接口实现可编程写入，借助相互作用实现信息从体态到边缘的自主整合。

Result: 匹配接口支持可编程写入（programmable writing），相互作用促成体态到边缘的自主信息整合（information consolidation）；这些机制提升了需要长期保留时序信息（temporal information）任务的性能。

Conclusion: 研究将拓扑（topology）确立为量子储备池计算的新资源，为近期量子计算与量子机器学习提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling universal quantum computation and implementing quantum machine learning with protected logical qubits remain challenging. To harness the computational potential of existing fault-tolerant quantum platforms, we introduce topological quantum reservoir computing, a novel approach using protected degrees of freedom for storage and the host system's intrinsic dynamics for information processing. Using a one-dimensional Kitaev chain, we show how long-lived Majorana edge modes and fast bulk responses support these complementary functions within the same many-body system. We identify the tradeoffs governing access to protected memory and show how matched interfaces enable programmable writing and interactions enable autonomous information consolidation from the bulk to the edge. These mechanisms improve performance on tasks requiring long-term retention of temporal information. Our results establish topology as a new resource for quantum reservoir computing and open a route toward near-term quantum computing and quantum machine learning.

</details>


### [82] [Quantum Annealing with a Quantum Neural Network: Width of Annealing Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24089)
*Mojtaba Asadollahi,Danial Khajehzadeh,Abolfazl Ramezanpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子退火（quantum annealing）扩展为量子神经网络（quantum neural network），并研究其在典型优化问题上的表现。结果显示宽度 W=2 的随机量子退火网络即可在量子搜索问题中指数级降低演化层数，且经参数调节后 W=2、深度 L=1 的量子感知机（quantum perceptron）能以指数级小误差完成搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子退火硬件虽可处理一大类哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）问题，但在不连续量子相变（discontinuous quantum phase transition）处性能受限，此时能隙（energy gap）随系统规模指数收缩，导致算法效率显著下降。

Method: 提出将量子退火扩展至量子神经网络的形式，并在若干原型优化问题上评估其性能；通过调节网络参数，考察随机量子退火网络的宽度与深度对量子搜索问题求解的影响。

Result: 宽度 W=2 的随机量子退火网络即可使量子搜索问题的演化层数（深度）指数级减少；经参数调节后，深度 L=1、宽度 W=2 的量子感知机能够以指数级小误差求解搜索问题。

Conclusion: 量子退火网络的宽度对提升算法成功概率具有关键作用，其机制在于为退火过程提供了更大且可学习的演化空间（learnable evolution space）。

Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising quantum algorithm for dealing with computationally hard optimization problems. Currently available hardware can implement the algorithm for a significant class of Hamiltonian problems. However, its performance is especially limited in discontinuous quantum phase transitions, where the energy gap shrinks exponentially with system size. In this paper, we introduce an extension of quantum annealing to a quantum neural network and study its performance in a number of prototypical optimization problems. In particular, we observe that already a random quantum annealing network of width W = 2 can exponentially reduce the number of evolution layers or depth of the annealing process in the quantum search problem. By tuning the network parameters the search problem can be solved with exponentially small errors by a quantum perceptron of depth L = 1 and width W = 2. The findings show that width of a quantum annealing network is critical to enhance the success probability of the algorithm by providing a larger and learnable evolution space for the annealing process.

</details>


### [83] [Virtual Autopurification in Translationally Invariant Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24448)
*Atsushi Iwaki,Hideaki Hakoshima,Nobuyuki Yoshioka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出虚拟自纯化（VAP）协议，在单一平移不变系统内利用空间分离子区域构造“虚拟副本”，避免标准虚拟纯化所需的多副本开销与全局纠缠测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准虚拟纯化（virtual purification）虽能抑制热涨落与退相干噪声，但需多个状态副本和全局纠缠测量，资源代价高，难以在近期设备上扩展。

Method: VAP 从空间分离的子区域（subregions）构造虚拟副本，仅在这些子区域之间执行纠缠测量；在关联聚类（clustering of correlations）假设下分析其与标准协议的偏差。

Result: 理论上 VAP 可复现标准虚拟纯化协议的期望值，偏差随缓冲长度（buffer length）与子区域间距呈指数衰减；数值模拟支持该预测，并展示以更低资源需求准确实现虚拟冷却（virtual cooling）。

Conclusion: VAP 是一种资源高效且可扩展的量子态纯化方案，适用于近期量子设备。

Abstract: We introduce virtual autopurification (VAP), a protocol for virtual purification that operates entirely within a single translationally invariant system. Virtual purification suppresses thermal fluctuations and decoherence noise by amplifying the dominant pure component of a mixed state, but its standard implementation requires multiple copies of the state and global entangled measurements. VAP avoids the multiple-copy overhead by constructing virtual copies from spatially separated subregions and performing entangled measurements only between them. Assuming clustering of correlations, we show that VAP reproduces the expectation values of the standard virtual purification protocol, with discrepancies that decay exponentially in both the buffer length and the inter-subregion distance. Numerical simulations support the theoretical predictions and demonstrate accurate implementation of virtual cooling with reduced resource requirements. Our results establish VAP as a resource-efficient and scalable approach to quantum-state purification in near-term devices.

</details>


### [84] [Gutzwiller Quantum-Classical Embedding for Dynamics Simulations of Correlated Electron Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24592)
*Joshua Aftergood,Daniele Guerci,Jacob Brunton,Feng Zhang,Thomas Iadecola,Peter P. Orth,Cai-Zhuang Wang,Nicola Lanatà,Yong-Xin Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出时间依赖的Gutzwiller量子-经典嵌入框架（t-GQCE），将晶格动力学映射为非相互作用准粒子晶格并由经典方法演化，同时把有限尺寸相互作用嵌入模型交由量子处理器演化，二者仅交换嵌入哈密顿量参数与单粒子密度矩阵。在Bethe晶格Hubbard模型的相互作用淬火中，该方法抑制了传统Gutzwiller近似的虚假无阻尼振荡，并趋近非平衡动力学平均场理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联电子系统（correlated electron systems）的实时动力学（real-time dynamics）需要长时间、非微扰（nonperturbative）地处理局域关联，这对经典方法构成挑战，因而需要引入量子计算资源。

Method: 基于时间依赖的ghost Gutzwiller近似（time-dependent ghost Gutzwiller approximation），将晶格动力学映射为非相互作用准粒子晶格并经典演化，与在量子处理器上演化的有限相互作用嵌入模型耦合；两部分仅交换嵌入哈密顿量参数和单粒子密度矩阵（single-particle density matrix），框架对量子动力学求解器保持无关。采用自适应变分量子动力学模拟（adaptive variational quantum dynamics simulations）进行验证，并结合电路压缩、旋转轨道基测量与后选择、噪声缓解（noise mitigation）等手段。

Result: 无ghost轨道时嵌入问题退化为单量子比特，在IBM硬件上以含辅助比特（ancilla）的两量子比特实现；增加ghost轨道后t-GQCE抑制传统Gutzwiller近似的虚假无阻尼振荡，并在弱、强淬火下趋近非平衡动力学平均场理论（nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory）结果。每自旋2、4、6个ghost轨道（即8、12、16量子比特）的状态矢量模拟（statevector simulation）中，自适应电路保真度误差低于5×10⁻⁴。在囚禁离子噪声模型下，测量误差降低数倍；在硬件标定噪声模型三分之一的错误率下，每时间步10⁵次采样可达单粒子密度矩阵与双占据（double occupancy）0.01的精度，Quantinuum H2-Emulator给出类似精度。

Conclusion: t-GQCE提供了一种适用于含时哈密顿量的通用多轨道晶格模型的量子-经典混合方案，能够以有限量子比特资源获得接近非平衡动力学平均场理论的动力学精度，并具备在当前含噪量子硬件上运行的可行性。

Abstract: Simulating the real-time dynamics of correlated electron systems requires a nonperturbative treatment of local correlations over long times, a challenge for classical methods. We present the time-dependent Gutzwiller quantum-classical embedding framework, t-GQCE, for generic multi-orbital lattice models with time-dependent Hamiltonians. Within the time-dependent ghost Gutzwiller approximation, the lattice dynamics is mapped onto a noninteracting quasiparticle lattice, propagated classically, coupled to finite interacting embedding models propagated on quantum processors. The two parts exchange only embedding Hamiltonian parameters and single-particle density matrices, and the framework is agnostic to the quantum dynamics solver. We demonstrate it with adaptive variational quantum dynamics simulations of interaction quenches in the Hubbard model on the Bethe lattice. Without ghost orbitals, the embedding problem reduces to a single qubit, simulated on IBM quantum hardware with two qubits, including an ancilla. With more ghost orbitals, t-GQCE suppresses the artificial undamped oscillations of the conventional Gutzwiller approximation and approaches nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory results for weak and strong quenches. Statevector simulations with 2, 4 and 6 ghost orbitals per spin, or 8, 12 and 16 qubits, give adaptive circuits that follow the embedding states with infidelities below $5\times10^{-4}$. Under trapped-ion noise models, circuit compression, measurements in rotated orbital bases with post-selection, and noise mitigation reduce the measurement errors severalfold. At one third of the error rates of a hardware-benchmarked noise model, they reach an accuracy of $0.01$ for the single-particle density matrix and the double occupancy with $10^5$ shots per time step, and the Quantinuum H2-Emulator gives similar accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [The magical memory cost of entanglement spectrum compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24710)
*Matias Karjula,Vilma Nieminen,Teemu Ojanen,Tapio Ala-Nissila,Moein N. Ivaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出以“量子记忆（quantum memory）”成本为操作量度，刻画局域酉操作（local unitaries）提取贝尔对（Bell pairs）后忠实保留纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）所需的代价，并给出贝尔对产量、残余记忆与重构保真度之间的精确权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是在通过局域酉操作分离贝尔对后，忠实保留二分纯态纠缠谱究竟需要多少量子记忆；现有纠缠度量未直接刻画这一操作层面的谱复杂度。

Method: 定义纠缠谱复杂度的操作量度；用平滑纠缠展宽（smoothed entanglement spread）给出记忆成本下界，并分析非局域魔性（nonlocal magic）发散导致的记忆无界性；进一步考察混沌线路动力学、Haar 随机态与稳态费米子高斯态（fermionic Gaussian states）等典型情形。

Result: 推导出贝尔对产量、残余记忆与重构保真度间的精确权衡；平滑纠缠展宽构成记忆成本下界，发散的非局域魔性使任意固定正保真度下记忆无界；混沌线路出现随系统尺寸增长的瞬态记忆峰值，随后成本下降而熵仍增长；典型 Haar 随机态仅需依赖精度的记忆，稳态费米子高斯态成本随尺寸增长。

Conclusion: 纠缠催化（entanglement catalysis）相关的多尺度谱结构对矩阵积态（matrix product state）压缩具有实际影响，产生传统基于熵的键维估计无法捕捉的乘性开销。

Abstract: How much quantum memory is needed to faithfully retain the entanglement spectrum of a bipartite pure state after separating out Bell pairs through local unitaries? We derive the exact tradeoff between Bell-pair yield, residual memory, and reconstruction fidelity under local unitaries, defining an operational measure of entanglement-spectrum complexity. Smoothed entanglement spread bounds this memory cost from below, while diverging nonlocal magic forces unbounded memory at any fixed positive fidelity. Chaotic circuit dynamics exhibit a transient memory peak whose height increases with system size, followed by declining cost despite continued entropy growth. A large typical Haar-random state requires only an accuracy-dependent memory, whereas stationary fermionic Gaussian states retain a growing cost with size. In the circuits studied, the multiscale spectral structure associated with entanglement catalysis also has a practical consequence for matrix product state compression, yielding a multiplicative overhead not captured by the conventional entropy-based bond dimension estimate.

</details>


### [86] [A Nonsecular Quantum Master Equation with Detailed Balance and Second-order Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24911)
*Ji-Hui Pei,Jin-Fu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个满足时间反演Kubo-Martin-Schwinger（TR-KMS）细致平衡的非久期（nonsecular）量子主方程，并证明其对约化密度矩阵的误差在时间上一致地满足O(λ²)界，从而在久期与非久期区域均保持热力学一致性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统的有效理论需同时继承微观可逆性带来的细致平衡结构与在弛豫全过程中保持准确；但量子多体系统中稠密谱使久期近似（secular approximation）即使在弱耦合下也失效，二者兼顾尤为困难。

Method: 通过对Redfield生成元施加细致平衡约束，并在共振处匹配其耗散与相干系数，构造满足TR-KMS细致平衡的非久期量子主方程；随后证明预测与精确约化密度矩阵之差在时间上一致地为O(λ²)（λ为系统-浴耦合强度）。

Result: 理论上证明了时间一致的O(λ²)误差界，并指出TR-KMS细致平衡与共振匹配相容，而不含时间反演的对应版本则不相容；在两个耦合振子（coupled oscillators）与精确微观动力学的基准测试中，该方程在久期与非久期区域均保持O(λ²)精度，优于所测试的现有细致平衡方程。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从微观模型出发、超越久期近似获得热力学一致的开放量子动力学描述的系统性途径。

Abstract: An effective theory of open quantum systems should preserve the detailed balance structure inherited from microscopic reversibility and remain accurate throughout relaxation. Meeting both requirements is particularly challenging in quantum many-body systems, where dense spectra can invalidate the secular approximation even at weak coupling. Here we construct a nonsecular quantum master equation that satisfies time-reversal Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (TR-KMS) detailed balance. Under suitable conditions, we prove a time-uniform $O(λ^2)$ bound on the difference between the predicted and exact reduced density matrices, where $λ$ is the system-bath coupling strength. Our construction completes the Redfield generator by imposing detailed balance while matching its dissipative and coherent coefficients at resonance. In this approach, TR-KMS detailed balance is compatible with resonance matching, whereas its counterpart without time reversal is not. In a benchmark against the exact microscopic dynamics of two coupled oscillators, our equation maintains $O(λ^2)$ accuracy across secular and nonsecular regimes, outperforming the existing detailed-balance equations tested. These results provide a systematic route from microscopic models to thermodynamically consistent descriptions of open quantum dynamics beyond the secular approximation.

</details>

### [87] [A characterization of uniformly discrete crystalline measures with unit weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24286)
*Lior Alon,Mario Kummer*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文证明：满足傅里叶求和公式（Fourier summation formula）的均匀离散集（uniformly discrete set）必由基于 Lee–Yang 簇（Lee–Yang variety）的 Alon–Kummer–Kurasov–Vinzant 构造产生，从而将一维结果推广到高维并刻画了单位权水晶测度（crystalline measure）的傅里叶准晶（Fourier quasicrystal）结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果（如 Olevskii–Ulanovskii 的一维定理）表明此类集合可表示为三角多项式（trigonometric polynomials）的公共零点集，但高维情形缺乏统一的结构性刻画；本文旨在给出该现象在高维中的来源与几何解释。

Method: 核心手段是证明任一带有离散频率集（discrete set of frequencies）的傅里叶求和公式的均匀离散集都来自使用 Lee–Yang 簇的 Alon–Kummer–Kurasov–Vinzant 构造；作为证明的一部分，将 Meyer 的灯塔测度（lighthouse measures）推广到高余维（higher codimension），引入灯塔流形（lighthouse manifolds），并在单项式坐标变换（monomial change of coordinates）意义下将其刻画为 Lee–Yang 簇的环面部分（torus parts）。

Result: 得到三项结论：其一，上述构造覆盖所有此类集合；其二，具有均匀离散支撑与单位权的水晶测度均为来自该构造的傅里叶准晶；其三，此类集合 Λ⊂ℝ^d⊂ℂ^d 均为有限多个三角多项式在 ℂ^d 中的公共零点集，从而推广 Olevskii–Ulanovskii 的一维定理。

Conclusion: 傅里叶求和公式所刻画的均匀离散集在高维中具有统一的代数–几何来源，即 Lee–Yang 簇构造；灯塔流形（lighthouse manifolds）的引入为该结构提供了可刻画的高维推广框架。

Abstract: We prove that every uniformly discrete set $\emptyset\neΛ\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ admitting a Fourier summation formula with a discrete set of frequencies $S(Λ)\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ arises from the Alon--Kummer--Kurasov--Vinzant construction using a Lee--Yang variety. In particular, every crystalline measure with uniformly discrete support and unit weights is a Fourier quasicrystal coming from the above construction. It also implies that every set $Λ\subset\mathbb{R}^d\subset\mathbb{C}^d$ as above is the common zero set in $\mathbb{C}^d$ of finitely many trigonometric polynomials, extending the one-dimensional theorem of Olevskii and Ulanovskii. As part of the proof, we generalize Meyer's notion of lighthouse measures to higher codimension and introduce lighthouse manifolds, which we characterize, up to a monomial change of coordinates, as the torus parts of Lee--Yang varieties.

</details>

### [88] [Reliable arithmetic circuits using less reliable (sub-digital) logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24591)
*Trevor McCourt,Ila R. Fiete,Neil Gershenfeld,Isaac L. Chuang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该文提出一条统计电路定律（statistical circuit law），证明超低电压下CMOS逻辑门的静态失效（static failure）并非极端电压缩放的严格障碍：门与电路的静态失效概率遵循普适幂律（universal power law），电路失效需整个门簇（gate cluster）同时崩溃，从而赋予亚数字逻辑（sub-digital logic）固有的失效韧性（resilience）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，超低电压下CMOS逻辑门频繁的静态失效是极端电压缩放（extreme voltage scaling）的严格壁垒。作者旨在纠正这一直觉，并寻求降低AI能耗的可行路径。

Method: 提出统计电路定律，发现连接门级与电路级静态失效概率的普适幂律；并开发新的理论工具以量化静态失效，其能力超越常规电压裕度分析（voltage-margin analysis）。

Result: 电路静态失效要求整个门簇同时击穿，而非单一器件失效；随着电压下降所需门簇尺寸迅速增大，故低电压亚数字逻辑对静态失效展现出出人意料的固有韧性。

Conclusion: 亚数字处理器（sub-digital processors）有望成为近期可行方案，大幅降低AI的能耗需求，为极端电压缩放提供了理论与应用依据。

Abstract: The frequent static failure of CMOS logic gates at ultra-low voltages has historically been perceived as a strict barrier to extreme voltage scaling: this paper introduces a statistical circuit law proving this intuition wrong. We discovered a universal power law connecting gate and circuit static failure probabilities, revealing that circuit failure requires the simultaneous breakdown of an entire gate cluster rather than a single component. Because the required cluster size increases rapidly as voltage drops, low-voltage \textit{sub-digital} logic exhibits surprising inherent resilience to static failure. This insight stems from new theoretical tools we developed to quantify static failure, extending well beyond conventional voltage-margin analysis. Consequently, sub-digital processors could potentially offer a viable, near-term solution to drastically reduce the energy demands of AI.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Extreme value statistics for majorisation partial orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23612)
*Elia Bisi,Fabio Deelan Cunden,Giovanni Gramegna,Marilena Ligabò*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究偏序集（partially ordered sets）中随机样本的极值现象，以交（meet）与并（join）作为最小值与最大值的序论类比，在配备 UT-优序（UT-majorisation）或优序（majorisation）的概率单纯形（probability simplex）上分析均匀样本的极值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极值理论（extreme-value theory）主要针对全序结构，而偏序集缺乏自然的最小/最大运算，需借助格论中的交与并加以推广；概率单纯形上的优序结构为这一问题提供了具体且有应用背景的载体。

Method: 在配备 UT-优序或优序的概率单纯形上，从相应单纯形中均匀抽样，通过样本的交与并构造极值统计量，并分别在固定维数与维度增长的两种设定下进行分析。

Result: 识别出样本极值几乎必然（almost surely）坍缩至底元素或顶元素的现象，并在临界尺度（critical scaling）下得到非退化极限分布（nondegenerate limiting laws）。

Conclusion: 偏序集上的交与并为随机极值现象提供了有效框架；在优序型单纯形中，极值行为随维数呈现坍缩与临界标度两类机制，并存在非退化极限刻画。

Abstract: We study extreme-value phenomena for random samples in partially ordered sets through their meets and joins, the order-theoretic analogues of minima and maxima. In this paper we consider the probability simplex equipped with either UT-majorisation or majorisation, with samples drawn uniformly from the corresponding simplex. We analyse these extrema both at fixed dimension and as the dimension grows, identifying almost-sure collapse to the bottom or top elements and critical scalings with nondegenerate limiting laws.

</details>


### [90] [Asymptotic analysis of a $3\times3$-doubly periodic random lozenge tiling model of a hexagon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24278)
*Max J. van Horssen,Arno B. J. Kuijlaars*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 研究周期为 3 的双周期加权正六边形随机菱形镶嵌（random lozenge tiling）模型，给出光滑相极限形状（limit shape）与关联核（correlation kernel）局部标度极限的显式描述，二者均为 Harnack 曲线及其双覆盖上的围道积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型具有含六个尖点（cusp points）的光滑相，需刻画其宏观极限形状与微观局部涨落，并将结果统一表述于底层 Harnack 曲线几何之上。

Method: 基于 Duits–Kuijlaars 的关联核双围道积分表示（double contour integral representation），其中被积因子由矩阵值正交多项式（matrix-valued orthogonal polynomials）的 Riemann-Hilbert 问题解给出；结合 Deift-Zhou 最速下降法（steepest descent method）的渐近结果，将围道移至 Harnack 曲线的双覆盖并对前两次变换的效应与鞍点（saddle points）形变进行分析，借助 Harnack 曲线的 amoeba 进行可视化。

Result: 获得极限形状与关联核局部标度极限的显式刻画，均表示为 Harnack 曲线上的围道积分；双覆盖由沿外场平衡测度（equilibrium measure in an external field）支撑集粘合两份拷贝得到。

Conclusion: 通过在双覆盖上对双围道积分作渐近分析，补充并完善了既有 Riemann-Hilbert 渐近研究，为双周期菱形镶嵌模型提供了统一的几何化极限描述。

Abstract: We study a random lozenge tiling model of a regular hexagon with weights that are doubly periodic with period $3$. This model features a smooth phase with six cusp points. We obtain explicit descriptions of the limit shape and the local scaling limits of the correlation kernel. Both limits are expressed as contour integrals on the Harnack curve that underlies the model, or on its double cover that is obtained by gluing together two copies along the support of an equilibrium measure in an external field.
  Our asymptotic analysis is based on the double contour integral representation of the correlation kernel due to Duits and Kuijlaars. This double contour integral contains a factor that is given in terms of the solution of a Riemann-Hilbert problem for matrix-valued orthogonal polynomials. The asymptotic behavior of this Riemann-Hilbert problem was recently studied by one of the authors using the Deift-Zhou method of steepest descent. In this work, we complement this approach with the asymptotic analysis of the double contour integral. The integrals are moved to the double cover of the Harnack curve. We analyze the effect of the first two transformations of the Deift-Zhou method, and deform the contours to the relevant saddle points. We visualize our asymptotic analysis on the amoeba of the Harnack curve, in the spirit of the work of Berggren and Borodin on the doubly periodic Aztec diamond.

</details>


### [91] [Recent developments in stochastic systems with singular dynamics -- a brief survey --](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24610)
*Sergio Andraus,Nicole Hufnagel,Jacek Małecki,Vlad Margarint*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文为综述性（expository）工作，梳理含奇异漂移（singular drifts）的扩散过程相关模型与方法，并探讨其与多重Loewner链、随机矩阵及机器学习交叉的新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含奇异漂移的扩散过程自然出现在若干兴趣粒子系统（particle systems）的研究中；由于其方程含奇异性，超出文献中对向量场（vector fields）的常规正则性假设，故需引入不同方法加以研究，其中对称多项式（symmetric polynomials）被视为特别有前景的途径。

Method: 采用综述与阐释的方式，概述相关模型与方法，并进一步探讨其与多重Loewner链（Multiple Loewner chains，特别包括多重Schramm-Loewner演化）、随机矩阵（Random Matrices）新联系以及“奇异动力学的机器学习”（Machine Learning for Singular Dynamics）这一新领域的交互。

Result: 摘要未给出具体技术结果或定量结论，仅说明综述涵盖若干模型与方法，并指出对称多项式方法的前景及其与上述若干领域的交叉联系。

Conclusion: 含奇异漂移的扩散过程构成一个兼具趣味性与挑战性的研究方向，借助对称多项式等不同方法可望推进对其动力学的研究；其与多重SLE、随机矩阵及机器学习的交叉为值得关注的探索方向。

Abstract: Diffusions with singular drifts appear naturally in the study of certain classes of interesting particle systems. Due to the singularities involved in the equations describing them, studying their dynamics is both interesting and challenging as they fall outside of the usual regularity assumptions on the vector fields in the literature. One needs to introduce and study them with different methods, with the use of symmetric polynomials being a particularly promising approach. In this expository work, we give an overview of some models and methods, and we further explore their interactions with the areas of Multiple Loewner chains (in particular, multiple Schramm-Loewner Evolutions), the new links with Random Matrices, and the novel area of Machine Learning for Singular Dynamics.

</details>


### [92] [Cumulant Generating Function of the Hill-Lane-Sudderth urn model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23778)
*Simone Franchini*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文求解 Hill–Lane–Sudderth 瓮模型在瓮函数（urn function）于不动点外侧分支可逆且逆映射满足 Lipschitz 条件时，极限归一化累积量生成函数（limiting normalized cumulant generating function）的方程，并给出由三次求积与根式复合极限构成的参数化表示及不累积的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Hill–Lane–Sudderth 瓮模型中黑球比例依当前比例概率演化的随机过程，需刻画其极限归一化累积量生成函数，但直接求解该方程通常依赖瓮函数的导数或逆，限制了解析处理与误差控制。

Method: 在瓮函数于不动点之外的外侧分支（outer branches）上可逆、且相应逆映射为 Lipschitz 的假设下求解该方程；解通过瓮函数的三个求积（quadratures）与根式（radicals）复合的极限给出参数化表示，最终公式不含导数或逆。

Result: 得到不含瓮函数导数与逆的参数化解；证明了一个沿分割（partition）不累积、且从初始奇点（initial singularity）出发仍成立的误差界；以四个具有精确解的算例说明构造的两种取向、非仿射情形（non-affine case）及零点（zeros）的存在。

Conclusion: 所提参数化表示与误差界在较弱的可逆性与 Lipschitz 假设下即可有效计算极限归一化累积量生成函数，避免了对瓮函数求导或求逆，并经四例精确解验证其适用性。

Abstract: We consider the Hill, Lane and Sudderth urn, in which a black or white ball is added at each step with probabilities depending on the current proportion of black balls. We solve the equation for the limiting normalized cumulant generating function when the urn function is invertible on the outer branches beyond the fixed points and the corresponding inverses are Lipschitz. The solution has a parametric representation through three quadratures of the urn function and a limit of compositions of radicals, with no derivatives or inverses of the urn function in the final formula. We prove an error bound that does not accumulate along the partition and remains valid when starting from the initial singularity. Four examples with exact solutions illustrate the two orientations of the construction, the non-affine case and the presence of zeros.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Real-space electrostatic sums through truncation error analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23510)
*Chuin Wei Tan,William C. Witt,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作指出Wolf与Pickard实空间静电方法的修正项可视为对实空间静电求和截断误差的近似，据此提出简化变体并给出非经验参数选择关系及恢复倒空间贡献的系统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模原子模拟倾向于避免显式倒空间（reciprocal-space）求值的实空间静电方法，但此类局域电荷中和（local charge-neutralization）方案与完整Ewald求和误差贡献的关系尚未厘清，且缺乏有原理依据的参数选择准则。

Method: 将Wolf与Pickard实空间方法的修正项解释为实空间静电求和截断误差（truncation error）的近似；在此基础上构造简化的固定半径（fixed-radii）与力平移（force-shifted）Pickard变体，保证截断处能量与力的连续性；并推导截断半径与屏蔽距离参数空间中最优路径的非经验标度关系。

Result: 所提出的变体在保持截断误差补偿行为的同时满足截断处能量与力的连续性；所得标度关系推广了Pickard的经验启发式（empirical heuristic），并解释了其失效的条件与原因；同时给出可加速收敛至完整Ewald能量的倒空间贡献恢复方案。

Conclusion: 以截断误差补偿为统一视角，可将实空间静电方法纳入与Ewald求和相联系的误差框架，从而为参数选择提供非经验依据，并提供向完整Ewald结果收敛的系统途径。

Abstract: Methods that treat electrostatics in real space, without explicit reciprocal-space evaluation, are appealing for large-scale atomistic simulations. They typically employ local charge-neutralization schemes, but their relationship to the underlying error contributions of the full Ewald sum has not been fully characterized. A principled basis for parameter selection is also lacking. In this work, we show that the correction terms of Wolf's and Pickard's real-space methods approximate the truncation error of real-space electrostatic sums. This perspective motivates simplified fixed-radii and force-shifted Pickard variants that preserve the truncation-error compensation behavior while enforcing continuity of pair energy and force at the cutoff. We derive a nonempirical scaling relation for the optimal path in the parameter space of the cutoff radius and screening distance, generalizing Pickard's empirical heuristic and explaining when and why it can fail. A systematic scheme for restoring omitted reciprocal contributions is introduced, which accelerates convergence to the full Ewald energy.

</details>

### [94] [A structure-based model of the bacterial chemosensory array reveals how the scaffolding protein CheW tunes sensory responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23921)
*David Hathcock,Yingxin Zheng,Yuhai Tu*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究构建基于结构的伊辛模型（structure-based Ising model），揭示支架蛋白 CheW 通过增强核心信号单元间的有效耦合提高细菌趋化感受阵列（bacterial chemosensory array）的响应协同性，并预测 CheW 丰度可驱动从弛豫到振荡的动力学转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验显示 CheW 在六聚环（hexameric rings）中的参与度提高会增强非适应细胞的响应协同性与适应细胞的敏感性，但其机制尚不明确，需要理论模型阐释支架蛋白如何调控信号处理。

Method: 构建与冷冻电镜（cryoEM）结构一致的格点伊辛模型，将核心信号单元与六聚环内 CheW 亚基的活性状态相耦合，并结合平均场理论（mean-field theory）分析有效耦合，比较完整六聚环与随机分布两种 CheW 排布统计。

Result: 模型定量重现了两类细胞的实验效应；平均场理论表明 CheW 提高核心单元间有效耦合从而锐化响应；CheW 排布统计显著改变响应随丰度的变化；不同耦合强度可产生相同静态响应但活动涨落与动力学差异明显，且 CheW 丰度控制弛豫型向振荡型涨落的转变，其位置依赖 CheW 空间分布。

Conclusion: 模型预测的近天然 CheW 水平下的振荡等动力学特征可通过单细胞动力学测量检验，结合 CheW 滴定实验可探测阵列组装动力学与变构耦合；该方法可推广至其他大型蛋白复合物，为研究支架蛋白与变构界面的功能角色提供框架。

Abstract: We introduce a structure-based Ising model of the bacterial chemosensory array to investigate how the scaffolding protein CheW tunes signaling responses. The model couples the activity states of core signaling units and of CheW subunits within hexameric rings on a lattice with the structure observed in cryoEM experiments. Recent experiments show that increasing CheW participation in hexameric rings raises response cooperativity in non-adapting cells and sensitivity in adaptation-proficient cells. Our lattice model quantitatively reproduces both effects, while mean-field theory shows that CheW increases the effective coupling between core units, sharpening the response in both cell types. The statistics of CheW placement (complete hexameric rings versus random distribution) substantially alter how responses vary with CheW abundance. Moreover, different coupling strengths can yield the same static response yet markedly different activity fluctuations and dynamics. CheW abundance further controls a crossover from relaxational to oscillatory fluctuations, whose location depends on the spatial distribution of CheW. These dynamical signatures, including oscillations predicted near native CheW levels, are testable by single-cell dynamics measurements. Combined with CheW titration and our modeling results, these measurements can probe the array's assembly dynamics and allosteric couplings. Beyond the chemosensory array, our approach can generalize to other large protein complexes, providing a framework to study the functional role of scaffolding proteins and allosteric interfaces in signal processing.

</details>

### [95] [Instability of layered two-phase flow in a vertical plane channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24749)
*Antonio Barletta,Michele Celli,Vittorio Di Federico,Sandro Longo*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文采用类Boussinesq近似模型研究垂直平面通道内的平衡两相流，考察中心核心层密度低于两侧层的三层结构，指出阿基米德数与界面无量纲位置是驱动线性失稳的关键参数，并发现完全反对称流是最不稳定的构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画由相间密度差驱动的纯浮力型平衡两相流，并识别决定其向线性不稳定性（linear instability）转变的控制参数，从而理解垂直通道内多层分层流动的稳定性特征。

Method: 采用近似类Boussinesq（Boussinesq-like）模型，针对具有三层构型的定常平行流（stationary parallel flow）建立简化表述；分析涵盖对称与非对称流动，并将两相分别贴近左右壁面的两层构型作为特例纳入。

Result: 失稳转变由阿基米德数（Archimedes number）与相间界面的无量纲位置共同控制；在所考察的各类构型中，平衡速度廓线为横向坐标奇函数的完全反对称流（completely antisymmetric flow）表现出最低的稳定性。

Conclusion: 简化类Boussinesq框架足以刻画此类纯浮力驱动的三层平衡两相流的线性稳定性，且界面位置与浮力强度共同决定稳定边界；完全反对称构型构成该研究范围内的最不利情形。

Abstract: The equilibrium two-phase flows in a vertical plane channel are explored by adopting an approximate Boussinesq-like model. The class of stationary parallel flows examined in this paper features three-layer configurations in which the central core is less dense than the peripheral layers. Such flows are considered purely buoyant, induced by the density difference between the phases. The simplified scheme leads to a formulation where the parameters driving the transition to linear instability of the equilibrium flow are the Archimedes number and the dimensionless positions of the interfaces between the phases. The analysis takes into consideration symmetric and asymmetric flows, including, as special cases, also two-layer configurations with one phase close to the left-hand boundary wall and the other close to the right-hand wall. In particular, the completely antisymmetric flow, where the equilibrium velocity profile is an odd function of the transverse coordinate, shows up as the least stable configuration among those examined in this study.

</details>


### [96] [Construction of a Deployable Hard-Constrained Turbulence Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23539)
*Tamerlan Hajiyev,Emre Sefer,Ivan Otić,Ismail Ari,Özgür Ertunç*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出模块化框架，用于构建可部署、严格满足约束的雷诺应力与湍流耗散率输运方程未封闭项湍流封闭模型，并在衰减均匀各向同性湍流上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流输运方程中未封闭项（unclosed terms）的封闭（closure）建模需求迫切，且现有数据驱动封闭难以直接部署、缺乏参考封闭值作为训练标签，约束难以严格满足。

Method: 框架由各司其职的模块构成：封闭模块用小型网络表示封闭项，状态模块表示训练中的流场状态，二者通过控制方程残差（governing equation residuals）耦合，无需参考封闭值；坐标模块学习单调输入坐标（monotone input coordinates）以分辨剧烈变化；边界条件（BC）模块基于函数连接理论（Theory of Functional Connections）精确施加边界值，可实现性（realizability）模块保证封闭可实现。应用于含实验与直接数值模拟（DNS）轨迹的衰减均匀各向同性湍流（HIT），状态模块为 DeepONet。

Result: 在10个随机种子下，相较无约束模型，训练与留出区间内种子间封闭均方根（RMS）变异降低约95%；部署的18参数封闭网络仅需泰勒尺度雷诺数（Taylor scale Reynolds number）即可作为独立C函数导出。

Conclusion: 该模块化框架可生成稳定、严格约束且可独立部署的低参数湍流封闭模型，为湍流封闭建模提供可推广路径。

Abstract: We present a framework for constructing deployable hard-constrained turbulence closures of unclosed terms in the transport equations of Reynolds stresses and turbulent dissipation rate. The framework is built from modules, each performing a single task. The closure module represents the closure with a small network, while the state module represents the flow state during training. The closure network is coupled to the state network through the governing equation residuals, allowing the closure to be learned without reference closure values being supplied as training data. The coordinate module learns monotone input coordinates that help both networks resolve sharp changes. Within the network architecture, the boundary condition (BC) module imposes boundary values exactly using the Theory of Functional Connections, and the realizability module keeps the closure realizable. We apply the framework to decaying homogeneous isotropic turbulence (HIT) with experimental and direct numerical simulation trajectories. Here, the state module is a Deep Operator Network (DeepONet), the BC module imposes the initial state and the closure's low and high Reynolds number limits, and the realizability module ensures growth of the Taylor microscale. Across ten random seeds, the framework reduces root-mean-square seed-to-seed closure variation by about 95\% relative to the unconstrained model over the training and held-out range. The deployed 18-parameter closure network requires only the Taylor scale Reynolds number and can be exported as a standalone C function.

</details>

### [97] [Amplifying or Redistributing Coupling: Synchronization in Sparse Adaptive Oscillator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24268)
*Yousef Jamali*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 该研究以稀疏有向连接的振子网络（oscillator network）为模型，比较固定连接与两种偏好相位对齐邻居的自适应规则，发现自适应对集体同步（synchronization）的影响取决于强化是“增加影响”还是“重新分配影响”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中协调时间性的连接本身会变化，而连接强化既可能提高单元收到的总耦合，也可能在固定总量中占据更大份额，这两种机制对集体同步的后果尚不明确。

Method: 构建由稀疏有向连边连接的振子数学模型，对比恒定连接与两种自适应规则：无约束的 Hebbian 耦合（Hebbian coupling）与稳态归一化（homeostatic normalization，保持每个振子的总有效入耦合不变），并进行匹配的数值实验。

Result: 精确关系式确立了归一化下的耦合守恒，并表明平均 Hebbian 权重（mean Hebbian weight）沿网络连边弛豫到由对齐程度决定的值；数值实验中，无约束自适应在较弱外加耦合下即可达到给定同步水平，而归一化网络在相同观测窗口内需要更强耦合；更长观测显示出上升、维持与下降等不同的个体相干性轨迹（coherence trajectories）。

Conclusion: 自适应的集体后果不仅取决于哪些连边被强化，还取决于强化是增加影响还是重新分配影响；该研究为理解可塑连接网络中的同步提供了机制性区分。

Abstract: Synchronization -- different units falling into a common rhythm -- matters in systems ranging from electrical generators to populations of neurons. But what happens when the connections that coordinate their timing can also change? If two units become more closely aligned and their connection strengthens, that extra influence can either increase the total coupling received by a unit or take a larger share of a fixed total. We examine these alternatives in a mathematical network of oscillators, each representing a repeating cycle, connected by sparse directed links. We compare constant connections with two adaptive rules that favor phase-aligned neighbors: unconstrained Hebbian coupling and homeostatic normalization, which keeps the total incoming effective coupling of each oscillator constant. Exact relationships establish this conservation and show that the mean Hebbian weight relaxes toward a value set by alignment along the network links. In matched numerical experiments, unconstrained adaptation reaches a given level of collective synchrony at weaker imposed coupling than fixed connections, whereas the normalized network requires stronger coupling within the same observation window. Longer observations reveal distinct individual trajectories, including increasing, sustained, and declining coherence. The central lesson is that the collective consequences of adaptation depend not only on which links are strengthened, but also on whether their strengthening adds influence or redistributes it.

</details>

### [98] [Mid-Infrared Integrated Acousto-Optic Phase Modulation on GaAs/AlGaAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24530)
*Thomas Gérodou,Mathilde Urbain,Luca Lucia,Etienne Herth,Jean-René Coudevylle,Martina Morassi,Aristide Lemaître,Damien Teyssieux,Raffaele Colombelli,Adel Bousseksou,Mathieu Jeannin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作演示了基于GaAs/AlGaAs异质结构、工作于460 MHz的7.5–10 μm中红外集成声光相位调制器，并通过仿真与实验的高度一致验证了其设计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中红外波段缺乏可单片集成全部器件功能的材料平台，且中红外光波长与声波波长的量级相近，给声波与导波光的同时传输与限制带来挑战。

Method: 利用GaAs基结构的宽中红外透明性与压电特性实现电声换能与声光互作用的单片集成；采用深蚀刻亚波长光栅（sub-wavelength grating）同时限制表面声波与导波光；建立定量仿真框架进行设计，用外差式振动计（heterodyne vibrometer）表征声学特性，并将器件嵌入集成马赫-曾德尔干涉仪（Mach-Zehnder interferometer）进行定量性能分析。

Result: 仿真与实验测量结果高度一致（remarkable agreement），验证了所提出的设计流程。

Conclusion: 该材料平台与设计方法为在中红外长波端实现性能更优的集成声光器件开辟了路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate a mid-infrared (7.5-10 um) integrated acousto-optic phase modulator operating at 460MHz based on a GaAs/AlGaAs heterostructure. The combination of broad mid-infrared transparency and piezoelectric properties of GaAs based structures allows monolithic integration of all the device functions (electro-acoustic transduction and acousto-optic interaction) on a single material platform. Addressing the challenge presented by comparable mid-infrared and acoustic wavelengths, simultaneous propagation and confinement of surface acoustic waves and guided light is achieved using a deep-etched sub-wavelength grating. We develop a comprehensive and quantitative simulation framework to design the modulator devices, characterize their acoustic properties in a heterodyne vibrometer and perform a full, quantitative analysis of the device performance by inserting them in an integrated Mach-Zehnder interferometer. Remarkable agreement with simulations validates the proposed design flow, and opens the way to improved integrated acousto-optic devices operating in the longer end of the mid-infrared.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>

# physics.geo-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Machine-learned acceleration of earthquake sequence simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23561)
*Reo Yanagi,Taeho Kim,Kaushik Bhattacharya,Nadia Lapusta*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种混合建模框架（hybrid modeling framework），用傅里叶神经算子（Fourier neural operator, FNO）学习地震破裂前后的状态跳跃映射，以替代昂贵的地震期间全动力学（fully dynamic）求解，从而在降低计算成本的同时更好地逼近全动力学模拟结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震序列模拟（earthquake sequence simulations）同时包含缓慢的震间过程（interseismic processes）与快速的动态破裂（dynamic ruptures）。震间期可采用大时间步高效计算，但地震期必须大幅缩小时间步，且弹性动力学波介导的记忆效应（wave-mediated memory effects）显著，导致长期模拟计算代价高昂。

Method: 基于高保真全动力学模拟数据训练傅里叶神经算子（FNO），学习从地震破裂前到破裂后的状态跳跃映射（jump map）；并构建混合框架，由传统准动力学求解器（quasi-dynamic solver）模拟缓慢震间演化，在地震期以训练好的 FNO 替代求解器、计入波介导的惯性效应（wave-mediated inertial effects）。

Result: 混合模型再现了全动力学模拟的关键长期统计特征，包括事件震级分布（distributions of event magnitudes）与复发时间分布（recurrence times）；且在更低计算开销下，其对全动力学模拟的近似显著优于常用的准动力学近似（quasi-dynamic approximation）。

Conclusion: 神经算子可有效加速地震序列模拟：以 FNO 处理计算密集的地震期、保留准动力学方法处理震间期，是一种兼顾效率与精度的可行策略。

Abstract: Earthquake sequence simulations involve slow interseismic processes and rapid dynamic ruptures. During interseismic periods, a numerical solver can take large time steps efficiently. During seismic periods, in contrast, the solver must drastically reduce the time step, and elastodynamic wave-mediated memory effects become significant; long-term simulations are therefore computationally expensive. We investigate the use of neural operators to accelerate earthquake sequence simulations. We train a Fourier neural operator (FNO) on data from high-fidelity, fully dynamic simulations to learn a jump map that advances the system state from before a seismic rupture to after it. We propose a hybrid modeling framework in which a conventional quasi-dynamic solver simulates the slow interseismic evolution, and the trained FNO replaces the solver during the computationally intensive seismic periods with wave-mediated inertial effects. The hybrid model reproduces key long-term statistics of fully dynamic simulations, including distributions of event magnitudes and recurrence times. Furthermore, it provides a much better approximation to the fully dynamic simulations than the commonly used quasi-dynamic approximation at a reduced computational expense.

</details>


<div id='math.CV'></div>

# math.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Behind the equivalent descriptions of the Loewner energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24774)
*Yilin Wang*

Main category: math.CV

TL;DR: 本文为解释性综述（expository paper），回顾论文《Equivalent descriptions of the Loewner energy》背后的研究历程，该工作通过 Loewner 能量（Loewner energy）揭示了随机分形 SLE 曲线（Schramm–Loewner evolution curves）与万有 Teichmüller 空间（universal Teichmüller space）的 Kähler 几何（Kähler geometry）之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撰写目的是作为 2026 年 Frontiers of Science 奖项的总结论文，发表于 ICBS 会议论文集（Proceedings of ICBS），因而以叙述研究背景与思想脉络的方式呈现该成果，而非提出新结果。

Method: 采用解释性综述（expository）写法，重述原论文的发现过程；核心工具为 Loewner 能量，用以建立随机分形曲线与 Kähler 几何之间的等价描述。

Result: 摘要指出原论文揭示了随机分形 SLE 曲线与万有 Teichmüller 空间的 Kähler 几何经由 Loewner 能量相互关联，该关联由标题中的“等价描述（equivalent descriptions）”体现。

Conclusion: 该工作将概率论中的随机分形对象与复几何／Teichmüller 理论统一于 Loewner 能量框架之下；本文本身作为获奖总结性综述，旨在向更广泛读者传达这一联系及其由来。

Abstract: This expository paper retells the story behind the paper "Equivalent descriptions of the Loewner energy," which uncovered the link between random fractal SLE curves and the Kähler geometry of universal Teichmüller space via the Loewner energy. It is written for the Proceedings of ICBS as the summary paper for the 2026 Frontiers of Science award.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [A multipurpose system of ordinary differential equations with exponential nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23515)
*Piotr P. Goldstein*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究一类系数矩阵非奇异对称的常微分方程组（ODE）$\ddot{y}_k=\sum_l M_{kl}\exp y_l$，给出其双参数精确解族并证明其一般不稳定，同时表明完全可积情形等价于含非近邻相互作用的 Toda 晶格（Toda lattice）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对形如 $\ddot{y}_k=\sum_l M_{kl}\exp y_l$ 的指数型非线性 ODE 系统，需要厘清其精确解结构、稳定性与可积性条件，以判定其与经典可积模型的联系。

Method: 在 $M$ 为 $n\times n$ 非奇异对称矩阵（nonsingular symmetric matrix）的前提下，构造并证明一个双参数精确解族（two-parameter family of exact solutions），并借助 Adler 与 Moerbeke 的已有结果分析系统的完全可积性（complete integrability）。

Result: 所构造的双参数精确解族对一般 $n$ 被证明是不稳定的（unstable）；依据 Adler–Moerbeke 的结果，该系统唯一的完全可积情形均等价于带有非近邻相互作用（non-nearest neighbour interactions）的 Toda 晶格。

Conclusion: 该类指数型 ODE 系统的可积性受到严格限制，可积情形归约为推广的 Toda 晶格；文中还针对若干特殊 $n$ 值给出了若干有意义的应用。

Abstract: A system of ordinary differential equations (ODE) $\ddot{y}_k=\sum_{l=1}^n M_{kl}\exp y_l,~k=1,...,n$, where $M$ is an $n\times n$ nonsingular symmetric matrix, is discussed in detail. A two-parameter family of exact solutions is found and proved to be unstable for general $n$. A result of Adler and Moerbeke is shown to apply to our systems; it states that the only completely integrable cases of this system are equivalent to the Toda lattice with included non-nearest neighbour interactions. A few interesting applications are shown for some special $n$'s.

</details>

### [102] [An Atiyah--Hirzebruch to Adams map and the hastened Adams spectral sequence for supercohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24725)
*Arun Debray,Weicheng Ye,Matthew Yu*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文构造一个过滤谱（filtered spectra）之间的映射，将 Atiyah–Hirzebruch 谱序列（AHSS）映到 $\mathit{ko}$-模的 Baker–Lazarev Adams 谱序列；由此结合 Bruner–Rognes 定理，为 $\mathit{ko}$ 及连通 Adams 直和项的 Postnikov 截断建立加速 Adams 谱序列（HASS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来自量子场论、物质拓扑相与张量范畴论（tensor category theory）中的问题，需要一种在相关例子中比 AHSS 或通常 Adams 谱序列更易计算的工具。

Method: 构造诱导 AHSS 到 $\mathit{ko}$-模 $M$ 的 Baker–Lazarev Adams 谱序列之映射的过滤谱映射；据此并借助 Bruner–Rognes 定理，对 $\mathit{ko}$ 与连通 Adams 直和项的 Postnikov 截断（Postnikov truncations）构造 HASS，并应用于超上同调（supercohomology）与上同调理论 $I_{\mathbb{C}^\times}(\tau_{\le 2}\mathit{ko})$。

Result: 在所关注的例子中，HASS 的计算比 AHSS 或通常 Adams 谱序列更为容易；文中给出 HASS 使用指南、若干计算实例，以及常见 $\mathcal{A}(1)$-模的加速 Adams 图表（hastened Adams charts）。

Conclusion: HASS 为特定 $\mathit{ko}$ 相关截断与上同调理论提供了一条高效的谱序列计算途径，并配有可直接使用的图表与操作指南。

Abstract: We construct a map of filtered spectra inducing a map from the Atiyah--Hirzebruch spectral sequence (AHSS) to the Baker--Lazarev Adams spectral sequence of a $\mathit{ko}$-module $M$. Using this and a theorem of Bruner--Rognes, we construct a hastened Adams spectral sequence (HASS) for the Postnikov truncations of $\mathit{ko}$ and the connective Adams summand. Then, motivated by questions in quantum field theory, topological phases of matter, and tensor category theory, we apply the HASS to supercohomology, the cohomology theory $I_{\mathbb{C}^\times}(τ_{\le 2}\mathit{ko})$, and observe that in examples of interest it is easier to calculate with than either the AHSS or the usual Adams spectral sequence. We include a User's Guide to the HASS and several worked examples, as well as hastened Adams charts for many commonly encountered $\mathcal{A}(1)$-modules.

</details>
