### [103] [Comparing Data-Driven and Numerical Weather Prediction Forecasts of Extreme Weather: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23863)
*Gabriele Messori,Hiu-Wang Lau,Chenyu Dong,Gianmarco Mengaldo,Leonardo Olivetti*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 该系统综述比较了数据驱动（data-driven）天气预报模型与物理数值天气预报（NWP）在极端天气上的表现：温度极端、热带气旋路径与天气系统位置方面数据驱动模型多占优；极端降水与事件识别证据混杂；事件强度与破纪录事件（record-breaking events）上物理模型仍占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动模型在多数主要评分指标（headline scores）上已可比或超越物理NWP，但其在极端天气上的优势存在争议，需要系统梳理同行评审证据加以厘清。

Method: 综述直接比较数据驱动预报与NWP在极端事件上表现的同行评审研究，时间尺度从临近预报（nowcasting）延伸至次季节（subseasonal）预报；灰色文献（grey literature）仅在前景讨论中提及，不纳入正式综述。

Result: 温度极端、热带气旋路径及天气尺度系统位置方面共识较好，数据驱动模型多优于NWP；极端降水及基于特征的极端事件识别（如大气河，atmospheric rivers）证据混杂；事件强度估计偏向NWP更优；超出训练分布的破纪录事件证据较少，但系统性全球评估与个案研究均显示物理模型保持优势，数据驱动模型低估破纪录事件的频率与幅度。

Conclusion: 以机器学习（ML）后处理NWP或将数据驱动预报与物理模型耦合的混合系统（hybrid systems）带来最大且最一致的增益。作者认为主要评分指标、检验方法选择与案例选择可解释文献中的分歧，并提出面向更可信、可解释评估的建议。

Abstract: Data-driven weather forecasting models, chiefly trained on reanalysis data, now match or beat physics-based numerical weather prediction (NWP) models on most headline scores, but whether this advantage extends to extreme weather is contested. We review peer-reviewed studies that directly compare data-driven forecasts with NWP for extremes, from nowcasting to subseasonal lead times. We discuss grey literature in the outlook but do not include it in the formal review. There is good agreement on the fact that data-driven models mostly outperform NWP on temperature extremes, tropical cyclone tracks and the location of synoptic features across time scales. The evidence is mixed for extreme precipitation and feature-based extreme event detection such as extremes related to atmospheric rivers, while it leans toward better NWP performance for estimating event intensity. For record-breaking events that lie outside the training distribution, the evidence is narrower. Nonetheless, both a systematic global evaluation and several case studies find that physics-based models retain the advantage, with data-driven models underestimating both the frequency and the magnitude of records. Hybrid systems that post-process NWP with machine-learning (ML), or couple data-driven forecasts with physics-based models, deliver some of the largest and most consistent gains. We argue that headline scores, verification choices and case selection explain much of the apparent disagreement in the literature, and provide recommendations for a more trustworthy and interpretable evaluation of data-driven models for extreme and record-breaking weather.

</details>

### [104] [Probing horizonless compact objects through observable signatures and images of accretion disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23452)
*Gargi Sen,Rajibul Shaikh,Sayan Chakrabarti,Santabrata Das*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文在Zipoy-Voorhees（γ-metric）时空中求解相对论流体动力学方程，研究稳态、无粘、轴对称的吸积流，并计算其能谱、光度和合成图像，指出γ对O型与I型临界点吸积流的光度影响相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope）对M87*与Sgr A*的观测促使研究者超越Kerr/Schwarzschild情形，检验替代时空几何及其吸积流动力学。

Method: 采用相对论流体动力学方程求解γ-metric时空中的吸积流，分析形变参数γ对有效势、临界点位置与性质及吸积流拓扑的影响；继而用相对论热轫致辐射（relativistic thermal bremsstrahlung）发射率计算能谱分布与总光度，并结合广义相对论光线追踪（general relativistic ray tracing）生成合成图像。

Result: γ对外临界点（O-type）和内临界点（I-type）吸积流的光度产生相反方向的影响；合成图像的观测通量分布独立印证了光度和能谱分析所得趋势；γ-metric虽与M87*、Sgr A*观测阴影半径相符，但其吸积流拓扑、辐射性质与图像均不同于Schwarzschild情形。

Conclusion: 吸积流拓扑、辐射性质与合成图像可作为区分γ-metric与Schwarzschild黑洞的互补探针，为检验对Schwarzschild范式的偏离提供新途径。

Abstract: The observed images of M87$^*$ and Sgr A$^*$ by Event Horizon Telescope motivate us to study the spacetime geometry and accretion flow dynamics around compact objects beyond the Kerr/Schwarzschild scenario. In this work, we investigate the properties of a steady, inviscid, axisymmetric, advective accretion flow around the Zipoy-Voorhees ($γ$-metric) spacetime by solving the relativistic hydrodynamic equations. The $γ$-metric is a static, axisymmetric generalization of the Schwarzschild geometry and is characterized by a mass ($M_\bullet$) and a deformation parameter ($γ$). We examine how the deformation parameter $γ$ influences the effective potential, the location and nature of the critical points, and the resulting accretion flow topologies. Then, using relativistic thermal bremsstrahlung emissivity, we compute the spectral energy distribution and bolometric luminosity of the flow. We find that $γ$ affects the luminosity in opposite ways for the flow having outer critical point (O-type) and that having inner critical point (I-type). Finally, combining the flow properties with general relativistic ray tracing, we construct synthetic images of the accretion disk for different values of $γ$ and observer inclination angles. The resulting observed flux distribution independently confirms the trends obtained from the luminosity and spectral analyses. Although the shadow of the $γ$-metric has been shown to be consistent with the observed shadow radii of M87$^*$ and Sgr A$^*$, its accretion flow topology, the resulting radiative properties and image differ from the Schwarzschild case, thereby giving a complementary way to probe the deviations from the Schwarzschild paradigm.

</details>


### [105] [The Geometric Imprint of a Tilted Accretion Disk in Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23914)
*Jing-Ze Xia,Chen Yuan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 倾斜吸积盘（tilted accretion disk）产生的引力波（gravitational waves）携带盘取向的几何印记：倾斜时高|m|模式相对贡献增大，随盘与黑洞自旋对齐而下降，且与倾角强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学为强场引力提供了新窗口，吸积流（accretion flows）则是探测强场物理的互补天然实验室；本文旨在检验黑洞周边物质的几何取向能否通过引力波辐射被探测。

Method: 开展三维广义相对论磁流体动力学（general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic, GRMHD）模拟，并通过 Teukolsky 方程计算引力波，比较对齐盘与初始倾斜盘的方位角量子数（azimuthal quantum number）m 模式分布。

Result: 对齐盘的辐射集中于低|m|模式；初始倾斜盘中高|m|模式相对贡献更大。随倾斜流与黑洞自旋对齐，|m|≥2 模式的贡献下降，并与倾角呈强相关。

Conclusion: 黑洞环境产生的引力波携带周围物质的几何信息，为利用多模（multimode）引力辐射探测强场区吸积流的几何与动力学演化提供了途径。

Abstract: Gravitational wave astronomy has opened a new window onto black holes and the dynamics of strong-field gravity, while accretion flows offer a complementary and natural laboratory for probing strong-field physics. In this work, we show that the gravitational wave emission from an initially tilted accretion disk carries a distinct imprint of its orientation. We perform three dimensional general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations and evaluate the gravitational waves through Teukolsky equation. For an aligned disk, the emission is concentrated in low-$|m|$ modes, where $m$ is the azimuthal quantum number. For the initially tilted disk, higher-$|m|$ modes make a larger relative contribution. As the tilted flow subsequently aligns with the black hole spin, the contribution of $|m|\ge2$ modes decreases, showing strong correlation with the tilt angle. This indicates that the gravitational waves generated by the black hole environment carries geometric information about the surrounding matter. Our results establish a route towards using multimode gravitational radiation to probe the geometry and dynamical evolution of accretion flows in the strong-field region.

</details>


### [106] [Rapid Equation of State Inference from Multiprobe Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24664)
*Tamal RoyChowdhury,Anarya Ray,Jolien Creighton,Shaon Ghosh*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过联合引力波、NICER X射线脉冲星与射电脉冲星计时观测，对中子星物态方程进行多信使贝叶斯推断与模型选择，得到1.4太阳质量中子星半径约11.93 km、最大非自转质量约2.31太阳质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整约束中子星物态方程需综合探测不同密度区间的多信使观测，单一观测通道难以覆盖全部密度范围。

Method: 联合双中子星并合引力波观测、NICER脉冲星质量—半径测量与大质量脉冲星射电计时数据进行多信使贝叶斯推断，约束参数化物态方程，并对候选物态方程集合开展贝叶斯模型选择。

Result: 得到规范1.4太阳质量中子星半径R_{1.4}=11.93^{+1.02}_{-0.83} km，最大非自转中子星质量M_max=2.31^{+0.23}_{-0.23}太阳质量（90%可信区间）；引力波与NICER约束中低密度区，大质量脉冲星观测通过要求物态方程能支撑相应质量而约束高密度区。

Conclusion: 未发现物态方程刚度与其贝叶斯证据之间存在简单相关性，因各观测探测的是不同密度区间的压强；该分析将宏观中子星测量与候选核物质模型的微观性质相联系。

Abstract: Fully constraining the neutron star equation of state (EoS) requires combining observations from various channels that probe different density regimes. We perform a joint multimessenger inference of the equation of state using gravitational-wave observations from binary neutron star mergers, NICER mass-radius measurements of X-ray pulsars, and radio timing measurements of massive pulsars. We constrain the parametrized equation of state and perform Bayesian model selection among a set of candidate equations of state. The joint analysis produces a radius of $R_{1.4} = 11.93^{+1.02}_{-0.83} \, \rm km$ for a canonical $1.4 \, M_\odot$ neutron star, and a maximum non-rotating neutron star mass of $M_{\rm max} = 2.31^{+0.23}_{-0.23} \, M_\odot$ with $90\%$ credible intervals. Gravitational-wave and NICER observations constrain the equation of state at low to intermediate densities, while massive pulsar observations constrain it at high densities by requiring the equation of state to support the neutron star masses. We find no simple correlation between the stiffness of an EoS and Bayesian evidence because the observations probe the pressure at different density regimes. By combining constraints on the equation of state with Bayesian model selection using multimessenger data, our analysis connects macroscopic neutron star measurements to microscopic properties of candidate nuclear matter models.

</details>

### [107] [Vague rotational symmetry at BCC [100] twist grain boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23892)
*Kai Jiang,Suining Xiong*

Main category: math.MG

TL;DR: 该文针对体心立方（body-centred cubic, bcc）[100]扭转晶界的平面模型，证明并量化了"模糊旋转对称（vague rotational symmetry）"的分类：在0–45°范围内最大旋转阶数分别为30°处十二重、45°处八重、其余取向四重，并给出各有限阶旋转所需最小均匀位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作（arXiv:2603.21158）提出用"模糊旋转对称"刻画晶界附近近重合点集的旋转可实现性，但尚缺乏对该分类的具体证明与位移代价的定量评估。本文旨在对bcc [100]扭转晶界平面模型严格检验该框架，并计算实现各旋转所需的最小位移。

Method: 以每对容差ε为尺度，要求将每个被容许原子对的中点移动不超过ε/2；利用模糊n重对称迫使傅里叶模（Fourier module）M满足ξ_n M = M、从而在晶体坐标下ℤ[ξ_n] ⊆ M的代数条件。对保持M的每个有限阶旋转，在ε充分小、允许独立点移动与自由旋转中心的条件下，确定其最小均匀位移。

Result: 分类结果为：30°处最大旋转阶数为十二，45°处为八，其余失配角均为四。位移代价方面，30°处十二重对称代价为ε/(2√3)，45°处八重对称代价为零；而在约25.53°扭转角处傅里叶模虽具八重对称，但在平面中实现需√2·ε。

Conclusion: 结果表明模糊旋转对称的分类可由傅里叶模的代数结构（ℤ[ξ_n] ⊆ M）精确刻画，且不同失配角下同一对称阶数的实现代价差异显著（如45°处为零而25.53°处为√2·ε），提示"模具有对称性"与"该对称性可在平面中低成本实现"之间存在本质区别。

Abstract: In earlier work (arXiv:2603.21158) we introduced vague rotational symmetry for proximal coincidence point sets at grain boundaries. A prescribed rotation must be realizable at every pair tolerance $\varepsilon$ by moving each midpoint of an admitted atomic pair by at most $\varepsilon/2$, half the maximum pair separation. We prove the proposed classification for the planar model of a body-centred cubic $[100]$ twist boundary. On $0\leqθ\leq45^\circ$, the maximal rotational order is twelve at $30^\circ$, eight at $45^\circ$, and four at every other misorientation. Vague $n$-fold symmetry forces $ξ_n M=M$ for the Fourier module $M$, hence $\mathbb{Z}[ξ_n]\subseteq M$ in crystal coordinates. For every rotation of finite order preserving $M$, we determine the least uniform displacement for all sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, allowing independent point motions and a free rotation centre. In this regime twelvefold symmetry at $30^\circ$ costs $\varepsilon/(2\sqrt3)$, and eightfold symmetry at $45^\circ$ costs zero. At a twist of approximately $25.53^\circ$, the Fourier module also has eightfold symmetry, but realizing it in the plane costs $\sqrt2\,\varepsilon$.

</details>

### [108] [Light fermions can trigger a Brout-Englert-Higgs effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24658)
*Axel Maas,Sofie Martins,Georg Wieland*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该文利用格点模拟（lattice simulations）证据表明，Brout-Englert-Higgs 效应可能由费米子的轻质量触发，从而暗示存在完全在标准模型（Standard Model）框架内解释该效应的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brout-Englert-Higgs 效应产生了标准模型粒子质量，但其成因未知；超出标准模型（beyond-the-Standard-Model）的理论（如复合 Higgs、动力学产生真空期望值）试图寻找该效应来源，本文则探索标准模型内部解释的可能性。

Method: 采用矢量耦合费米子（vectorially coupled fermions）且不含 Yukawa 耦合的格点模拟（lattice simulations），考察费米子质量与 Higgs 势及真空期望值的关系。

Result: 模拟显示费米子轻质量可触发 Brout-Englert-Higgs 效应；结果与弱耦合（weak couplings）一致，费米子仅在 NNLO 影响 Higgs 势；当费米子质量低于 W/Z 质量的两倍时，系统从类 QCD（QCD-like）行为转变为类 Higgs（Higgs-like）行为，该阈值为整数值但与其他临界物理行为无关。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一个尚未识别的机制，在 SU(2) 标量-费米子-规范系统中产生非零真空期望值，并提示标准模型内部自洽解释该效应的可行性。

Abstract: The Brout-Englert-Higgs effect produces the masses of the Standard Model particles. However, the cause of this effect is unknown. Beyond-the-Standard-Model theories aim to find the source of this effect, for example using a composite Higgs and dynamically generating its vacuum expectation value. In this paper, we show evidence from lattice simulations with vectorially coupled fermions and without Yukawa couplings that the Brout-Englert-Higgs effect could be triggered by the light masses of fermions. This suggests that an explanation entirely within the Standard Model could be possible. Moreover, our results are consistent with weak couplings and fermions can only impact the Higgs potential at NNLO. This implies a so far unidentified mechanism in an SU(2) scalar-fermion-gauge system that produces a non-zero vacuum expectation value. In addition, we find that the transition from a QCD-like behavior to a Higgs-like behavior happens when the fermion mass falls below twice the W/Z mass: a threshold that is integer valued but not connected to other critical physical behavior.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Topological aspects of generalized Sklyanin algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24349)
*Giorgi Khimshiashvili*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文完整刻画了原始Sklyanin代数Casimir叶的拓扑类型与分支，并将方法推广至Jacobian Poisson结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始问题在于Sklyanin代数Casimir叶层（Casimir foliations）的拓扑结构缺乏完整描述，且既有结果来自E. Sklyanin的原始论文，有待改进与扩展。

Method: 采用基于实代数簇Euler示性数（Euler characteristic）的"signature formula"方法，对Casimir叶的拓扑类型与分支（bifurcation）进行分析。

Result: 给出了原始Sklyanin代数Casimir叶拓扑类型与分支的完整描述，改进并扩展了E. Sklyanin原有的相关结果；同一方法对Jacobian Poisson结构也给出类似结果。

Conclusion: 基于Euler示性数signature公式的方法可统一处理Sklyanin代数及其Jacobian Poisson结构推广的Casimir叶层拓扑问题。

Abstract: We are concerned with the topology of Casimir foliations of Sklyanin algebras and their certain generalizations. In particular, we give a complete description of the topological types and bifurcations of Casimir leaves of the original Sklyanin algebras, which improves and extends analogous results from the original papers of E. Sklyanin. Our approach is based on the so-called signature formula for the Euler characteristic of real algebraic varieties. Moreover, we show that the same approach yields similar results for certain generalizations of the original Sklyanin algebras known as Jacobian Poisson structures.
  Keywords: Poisson structure, Sklyanin algebra, Casimir function, Casimir foliation, Nambu bracket, Jacobian Poisson structure, Euler characteristic, algebraic formula for the Euler characteristic, bifurcation diagram, isolated singularity of complete intersection, Milnor number

</details>


### [110] [Gopakumar-Vafa invariants and Mordell-Weil lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23735)
*Lutian Zhao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究从光滑 Calabi–Yau 三维体（Calabi–Yau threefold）到 del Pezzo 曲面的整纤维 Weierstrass 纤维化（Weierstrass fibration）X→B，沿光滑曲线 D⊂B 分解欧拉示性数（Euler characteristic）为 1、投影轮（projected cycle）为 D 的稳定层（stable sheaves）模空间纤维，并按纤维次数（fiber degree）对其分支进行分类与 GW/GV 计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解 Calabi–Yau 三维体上稳定层模空间的纤维结构，并借助 Mordell–Weil 高度配对（Mordell–Weil height pairing）将模空间分支与纤维化截面的几何联系起来，进而将 GV 不变量（Gopakumar–Vafa invariants）与 $E_8$ 相关结构对应起来。

Method: 设 X→B 为整纤维 Weierstrass 纤维化，D⊂B 为光滑曲线且逆像 Y 光滑。对纤维次数 k，按满足 ½⟨C,C⟩≤k 的 Y→D 的所有截面（section）C 来标号模空间纤维的分支，其中 ⟨,⟩ 为 Mordell–Weil 高度配对；并对无穷小刚性（infinitesimally rigid）截面计算 GV 贡献。

Result: 模空间纤维在给定纤维次数 k 下的分支由满足 ½⟨C,C⟩≤k 的截面 C 全体标号；对无穷小刚性截面算得 GV 贡献，并在 $(-1)$-曲线（$(-1)$-curve）上复原出 $E_8$ theta 乘积（$E_8$ theta product）。

Conclusion: 作者据此为截面族（families of sections）提出相应猜想，将单截面情形下的分支分解与 GV 计算结果推广至族的情形。

Abstract: Let $X\to B$ be a Weierstrass fibration with integral fibers from a smooth Calabi--Yau threefold to a del Pezzo surface. For a smooth curve $D\subset B$ with smooth inverse image $Y$, we decompose the moduli fiber of stable sheaves with Euler characteristic one and projected cycle $D$. In fiber degree $k$, its components are indexed by all sections $C$ of $Y\to D$ with $\tfrac12\langle C,C\rangle\leq k$, where $\langle\, ,\,\rangle$ is the Mordell--Weil height pairing. For infinitesimally rigid sections we compute the GV contributions, recovering the $E_8$ theta product over a $(-1)$-curve. We formulate the corresponding conjecture for families of sections.

</details>

### [111] [Confidence-Based Materials Data Quality Assessment via Domain Knowledge and Graph-Structured Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23342)
*Sihan Liaing,Ji Zhang,Kehan Wang,Zihao Gao,Wei Ma,Yangang Wang,Zongguo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于置信度的材料数据质量评估框架，将数据质量建模为连续置信估计问题，结合领域知识、图结构学习与历史参考评分，在23个ODDS基准上平均AUC-ROC达0.866，并通过规则校准与图精炼显著提升下游回归性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料机器学习（materials machine learning）依赖大规模异构数据集，但来自实验、模拟与数据库整合的数据常含测量误差、失败计算、缺失或重复记录及隐含的物理化学约束违背；此类损坏样本在统计上仍可能显得合理，难以作为显式离群点被识别，从而损害模型可靠性。

Method: 将数据质量构建为连续的置信度估计（confidence estimation）问题而非二分类异常识别；设计九维特征质量先验（feature-quality prior）与重复感知样本初始化，并依据材料领域规则与统计证据进行校准；通过样本-特征二部图传播（bipartite graph propagation）与弱监督图注意力（weakly supervised graph attention）精设置信度，并引入Memory-GAT模块进行历史参考评分。

Result: 在23个ODDS基准数据集上通用模块平均AUC-ROC为0.866；在mp_20上五次重复混合污染实验中，规则校准使HistGradientBoostingRegressor测试R²由0.538±0.008提升至0.616±0.014；图精炼使平均精度（average precision）由0.943±0.004提升至0.953±0.004，规则校准配置在评估的筛选配置中平均测试R²最高；五次重复MACE子集实验中，基于规则的筛选使R²由0.590±0.053提升至0.880±0.027。

Conclusion: 融合领域规则校准、图结构传播与历史参考评分的置信度框架可有效评估材料数据质量，并在下游回归任务中带来一致的性能改善。

Abstract: The rapid development of materials machine learning relies on large-scale, heterogeneous datasets, yet model reliability is often compromised by imperfect data from diverse experiments, simulations, and database integration. Real-world materials databases may contain measurement errors, failed calculations, missing or duplicate records, and hidden violations of physicochemical constraints. Such problems are difficult to identify because corrupted samples may remain statistically plausible rather than appearing as explicit outliers. In this paper, we propose a confidence-based materials data quality assessment framework that integrates domain knowledge with graph-structured learning. Data quality is formulated as a continuous confidence estimation problem rather than a binary anomaly identification task, enabling sample-level assessment and historical-reference-based batch comparison. A nine-dimensional feature-quality prior and duplicate-aware sample initialization are calibrated using materials domain rules and statistical evidence. Confidence is further refined through sample-feature bipartite graph propagation and weakly supervised graph attention, while a Memory-GAT module provides historical reference scoring. Experiments on 23 ODDS benchmark datasets show that the generic module achieves an average AUC-ROC of 0.866. In five repeated mixed-contamination experiments on mp\_20, rule calibration improves HistGradientBoostingRegressor test $R^2$ from $0.538\pm0.008$ to $0.616\pm0.014$. Graph refinement increases average precision from $0.943\pm0.004$ to 0.953\pm0.004$, while the rule-calibrated configuration has the highest mean test $R^2$ among the evaluated screening configurations. Five repeated MACE subset experiments further show an increase in $R^2$ from $0.590\pm0.053$ to $0.880\pm0.027$ after rule-based screening.

</details>


<div id='math.GT'></div>

# math.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Protection Group of Colored Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23519)
*Radin Zahedigolpayegani,Toni Annala*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 该文提出彩色链环（colored links）的普适不变量——保护群（protection group），用以统一刻画三维拓扑涡旋（topological vortex）构型的拓扑稳定性与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前针对物理上感兴趣情形的分类依赖各系统特定的不变量（system-specific invariants），缺乏统一框架；本文旨在建立可通用分类的普适不变量。

Method: 以彩色链环的数学框架为基础，引入普适不变量"保护群"，并将已有分类结果重新解释为该群的具体计算，同时在新示例中完成计算。

Result: 证明先前基于系统特定不变量的分类结果均可表述为保护群的计算，并在新示例中给出该群的计算结果。

Conclusion: 保护群为三维拓扑涡旋构型的拓扑稳定性与分类提供了统一的不变量框架，把以往零散的分类结果纳入同一体系并支持新情形下的计算。

Abstract: Colored links provide a mathematical framework for studying the topological stability and classification of three-dimensional topological vortex configurations. Earlier classifications in settings of physical interest relied on system-specific invariants. Here, we introduce a universal invariant of colored links, the \textit{protection group}, interpret earlier classification results as computations of this group, and compute it in new examples.

</details>


### [113] [Whitehead doubles and the failure of the AJ conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24826)
*Jinzheng Li,Yuqi Li,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 该文证明：对互素参数 \(2\le r<s\) 的环面纽结的带标记 Whitehead 双结（marked Whitehead doubles of torus knots），在两种夹扣符号（clasp signs）、两种伴随手性（companion chiralities）及任意整数扭转下，AJ 猜想（AJ conjecture）均不成立，其根源是一个在有理意义下饱和的非零零化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AJ 猜想（AJ conjecture）将彩色琼斯多项式（colored Jones polynomial）的递推结构与经典 \(A\)-多项式（\(A\)-polynomial）相联系；已有反例存在，故需明确哪些纽结族构成反例及其机制。本文针对环面纽结的带标记 Whitehead 双结，检验该猜想在全部符号、手性与扭转参数下是否成立。

Method: 利用零框带符号未约化彩色琼斯序列（zero-framed signed unreduced colored Jones sequence）构造有理饱和零化子（rationally saturated annihilator），并考察其在 \(t=-1\)、经线变量（meridian variable）\(M\) 独立时的特化。借助真实琼斯序列选取卫星转移算子（satellite transfer operator）的右因子，并用有理因子检验（rational factor tests）判断该二次式是否保留于该因子中。

Result: 特化中含不可约二次式：负夹扣为 \(E=L^2-(M^4-4M^2+1)L+M^4\)，正夹扣为 \(M^4E(M^{-1},L)\)（\(L\) 为经线变量 longitude）。该二次式与完整经典 \(A\)-多项式（含 \(L-1\)）在 \(\mathbb{Q}(M)[L^{\pm1}]\) 上互素，故即使每个不可约因子只计一次，偏差仍存在；且 \(\pi m(E)\) 等于该二尖点外部（two-cusped exterior）的无符号体积。

Conclusion: \(E=0\) 曲线来自轴向尖点完备化的带标记 Whitehead 链环外部（marked Whitehead link exterior）；该二次因子在卫星转移算子的右因子中存活，并被两种带标记伴随填充（marked companion fillings）排除为卫星边界曲线的分支，从而给出 AJ 猜想的一族系统性反例。

Abstract: For marked Whitehead doubles of torus knots with coprime parameters $2\le r<s$, the AJ conjecture fails for both clasp signs, both companion chiralities, and every integer twist. The zero-framed signed unreduced colored Jones sequence has a nonzero rationally saturated annihilator. Every nonzero primitive representative specializes nontrivially at $t=-1$ with the meridian variable $M$ independent. With $L$ the longitude variable, the specialization contains the irreducible quadratic $E=L^2-(M^4-4M^2+1)L+M^4$ for the negative clasp and $M^4E(M^{-1},L)$ for the positive clasp. The corresponding quadratic is coprime to the full classical $A$-polynomial, including $L-1$, over $\mathbb{Q}(M)[L^{\pm1}]$, so the discrepancy persists when each irreducible factor is counted once. The curve $E=0$ comes from the marked Whitehead link exterior with its axis cusp complete. For the displayed monic polynomial, $πm(E)$ is the unsigned volume of that two-cusped exterior. We use the actual Jones sequence to select a right divisor of the satellite transfer operator. Rational factor tests show that the quadratic survives in this divisor, and the two marked companion fillings exclude it as a component of the satellite boundary curve.

</details>

### [114] [Rabi-Engineered spin-selective transport of bright soliton in a binary spin-1/2 condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24112)
*Swarup K. Sarkar,Sh. Mardonov,E. Ya. Sherman,Pankaj K. Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文将自旋电子学（spintronics）概念拓展到非线性物质波，研究不同自旋对称性的亮孤子（bright solitons）在空间依赖拉比场（coordinate-dependent Rabi field）中的动力学与散射行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋电子学局限于固体中电子或空穴等单粒子的自旋相关动力学；将自旋自由度概念推广到集体、类粒子的非线性激发，可能为控制其输运提供新途径，而玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensates）平台使研究外场下亮孤子运动成为可能。

Method: 在类空间变化磁场的坐标依赖拉比场中，考察不同自旋对称性的亮孤子运动，分析受速度扰动的孤子动力学，对比完美与提升的Manakov对称性（perfect and lifted Manakov symmetry）两种情形，并将速度控制的散射问题与已知的拉比淬火协议（Rabi quenching protocol）进行比较。

Result: 受速度扰动的孤子运动在完美与提升的Manakov对称性下均表现出非常有趣的散射动力学；速度控制的散射问题与拉比淬火协议之间的比较为控制孤子动力学提供了有力方法。

Conclusion: 该结果为控制孤子动力学提供途径，有望以孤子作为自旋载流子（spin carriers）实现非线性自旋电子学（nonlinear spintronics）的控制。

Abstract: Spintronics is usually concentrated on spin-dependent dynamics of single particles such as electrons or holes in solids. Extending this concept to nonlinear matter waves offers an interesting possibility of controlling the transport of collective, particle-like excitations through their spin degrees of freedom. Recent advances in Bose-Einstein condensates have provided a versatile platform for realizing nonlinear spin-1/2 systems that support particle-like objects such as bright solitons, making studies of the motion of these solitons under external fields an interesting and important problem. Here, we study the dynamics of bright solitons with different spin symmetries moving in a coordinate-dependent Rabi field, which acts analogously to a spatially varying magnetic field. In that setup, the velocity perturbed soliton motion possesses very interesting scattering dynamics for both perfect and lifted Manakov symmetry. Lastly, we compare both the velocity-controlled scattering problem with the well-known Rabi quenching protocol. This comparison provides a powerful approach to control the soliton dynamics that may open an avenue to control nonlinear spintronics with solitons as spin carriers.

</details>


### [115] [Curvature Gradients Turn Shear into Superflow in Supersolids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24354)
*Ygor de Oliveira Souza,Piotr Surówka*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文构建了曲面超固体（supersolids）的伽利略协变有效场论（Galilean-covariant effective field theory），揭示高斯曲率梯度（Gaussian curvature gradient）将晶格剪切与压缩耦合，从而产生剪切驱动超流等几何效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲面上的超固体已逐步具备实验可及性，但缺乏描述曲率与超固体内部自由度（晶格与超流）耦合的系统理论框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 建立曲面超固体的伽利略协变有效场论，推导高斯曲率梯度以剪切模量（shear modulus）和曲率梯度确定的强度耦合晶格剪切与压缩，并分析其在椭球面（spheroid）上的后果。

Result: 曲率梯度导致剪切驱动超流（shear drives superflow）；在椭球面上产生几何选择定则（geometric selection rules）与多重态劈裂（multiplet splittings）；在模式交叉（mode crossings）附近，弱形变产生超流与晶格动能相当的杂化态，剪切以密度响应形式显现。

Conclusion: 曲面几何通过曲率梯度与剪切模量的耦合深刻影响超固体的动力学，为在曲面体系中探测剪切与超流耦合提供了可观测的密度响应信号。

Abstract: Supersolids on curved surfaces are becoming experimentally accessible. We develop a Galilean-covariant effective field theory of supersolids on curved surfaces and show that gradients of Gaussian curvature couple lattice shear to compression with a strength fixed by the shear modulus and the curvature gradient. As a result, shear drives superflow. On spheroids this yields geometric selection rules and multiplet splittings; near mode crossings, weak deformations produce hybrids in which superfluid and lattice carry comparable kinetic energy, and shear becomes visible as a density response.

</details>


### [116] [A Bose-Hubbard quantum thermal machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24483)
*Felipe Isaule,Juan E. Bernate,Francisco J. Peña,Patricio Vargas,Thomás Fogarty*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作提出由一维光晶格中相互作用的超冷玻色子构成的量子热机（quantum thermal machine），采用含相互作用强度变化的类奥托循环（Otto-inspired cycle），证明相互作用调控可作为提升热机性能与实现制冷的热力学资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超冷原子体系中相互作用强度这一可控自由度能否作为热力学资源（thermodynamic resource），以超越无相互作用循环的性能。

Method: 以玻色-哈伯德哈密顿量（Bose-Hubbard Hamiltonian）描述一维光晶格中相互作用的超冷玻色子，构建在标准热化与压缩/膨胀冲程之外还改变玻色子间相互作用强度的类奥托循环（Otto-inspired cycle）。

Result: 相互作用变化可被调控以增强热机性能并优于无相互作用循环；作为热机时，在强排斥下将介质压缩至莫特绝缘体（Mott insulator）态可获得最大功输出与接近卡诺效率（near-Carnot efficiency）；压缩冲程后减弱相互作用有利于并增强热机运行，而增强相互作用则有利于制冷循环（refrigeration cycles）。

Conclusion: 跨越物质量子相（quantum phases of matter）调控相互作用可作为一种有用的热力学资源加以利用。

Abstract: This work proposes a quantum thermal machine comprised of interacting ultracold bosons confined in a one-dimensional optical lattice and modeled by the Bose-Hubbard Hamiltonian. We consider an Otto-inspired cycle with strokes that change the interaction strength between bosons, in addition to standard thermalization and compression/expansion strokes. We find that these interaction changes can be manipulated to enhance the performance of the thermal machine, outperforming non-interacting cycles. In particular, when operating as a heat engine, the cycle can achieve its largest work output and near-Carnot efficiency by compressing the medium at strong repulsion into a Mott insulator state. Additionally, we find that decreasing interactions after the compression stroke favors and enhances the engine operation, whereas increasing interactions favors refrigeration cycles. The findings of this work further show that controlling the interactions across quantum phases of matter can be harnessed as a useful thermodynamic resource.

</details>


### [117] [Finite-temperature effects on the single-particle Green's function of a mobile impurity in a Luttinger liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24730)
*Lorenzo Gotta,Thierry Giamarchi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用基于链接团簇展开（linked-cluster expansion）的场论方法，研究具有二次色散（quadratically-dispersing）的量子杂质与一维超流（Luttinger 液体）通过接触密度-密度相互作用耦合的模型，定量给出有限温度对动量空间单粒子格林函数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注零温下杂质—一维量子浴的相互作用，而一维浴的有限温度如何导致单粒子信号的热致指数衰减、其时间尺度如何与浴的微观参数关联，尚缺乏定量的场论刻画。

Method: 采用基于链接团簇展开（linked-cluster expansion）的场论方法，仔细识别共振声子-杂质散射（resonant phonon-impurity scattering）过程，并分析 Luttinger 液体描述下 Lieb-Liniger 模型的微观参数。

Result: 获得热致指数衰减在大时间极限下的渐近估计，分别覆盖以宿主杂质有限寿命为主的准粒子（quasiparticle）区域，以及杂质引发 Anderson 正交灾变（Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe）的红外主导（infrared-dominated）区域；两种情形下均识别出温度诱导的相干时间（coherence time）。

Conclusion: 温度诱导的相干时间设定了零温单粒子格林函数振幅的指数衰减修正的特征时间尺度，并可将其倒数与 Lieb-Liniger 模型的微观参数（浴内相互作用）联系起来，从而揭示杂质不同动力学区域下逆相干时间对浴内相互作用的依赖。

Abstract: We consider the problem of a quadratically-dispersing quantum impurity interacting through a contact density-density term with a one-dimensional (1D) superfluid described by Luttinger liquid theory. By means of a field-theoretical approach based on the linked-cluster expansion, we quantitatively address the effects of a finite temperature of the 1D bath on the momentum-space single-particle Green's function. Through a careful identification of the resonant phonon-impurity scattering processes, we obtain asymptotic large-time estimates for the thermally-induced exponential damping of the zero-temperature signal both in the $\textit{quasiparticle}$ regime, dominated by the presence of a finite lifetime for the host impurity, and in the $\textit{infrared-dominated}$ regime, where the impurity causes a Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe of the 1D bath. In both cases, we identify a temperature-induced coherence time that sets the characteristic timescale of the exponentially-decaying finite-temperature correction to the amplitude of the zero-temperature single-particle Green's function. Moreover, we draw connections between the aforesaid thermal timescale and the microscopic parameters of the Lieb-Liniger model, which admits an effective Luttinger-liquid description, highlighting the dependence of the inverse coherence time on the intra-bath interactions across the different dynamical regimes of the impurity.

</details>

### [118] [Localized active phase in the coevolving voter model with fixed links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23870)
*Hoseung Jang,Maxi San Miguel,Byungjoon Min*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在共演化投票者模型（coevolving voter model, CVM）中引入不可重连的固定连边（fixed links），揭示出由固定连边巨型连通分量（giant component）维持的局域活跃相（localized active phase），该相可由超越标准对近似（pair approximation）的矩闭合近似（moment-closure approximation）预测，并在有限系统中表现为非对称碎片化相（asymmetric fragmented phase）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CVM假设所有连边均可重连（rewiring），忽略了家庭、亲密友谊等不可断裂的现实关系。为刻画此类结构约束对集体动力学的影响，论文引入固定连边并研究其如何改变CVM的相行为。

Method: 在CVM的复制与重连动力学基础上，加入不可重连的固定连边；采用超越标准对近似的矩闭合近似进行理论预测，并结合有限系统分析讨论该相的有限尺寸表现。

Result: 除CVM原有的活跃相（active phase）与冻结相（frozen phase）外，发现新的局域活跃相：在逾渗转变（percolation transition）之后，活动由固定连边的巨型连通分量维持。该相的有限尺寸表现为非对称碎片化相；同时固定连边使活跃—冻结转变（表现为有限系统的碎片化转变）推迟至更大的重连率（rewiring rate）。

Conclusion: 不可重连的结构约束会显著改变CVM的集体动力学：产生新的局域活跃相并移动相变阈值，表明现实社会关系中的不可断裂连边对网络共演化（coevolution）结果具有重要影响。

Abstract: The coevolving voter model (CVM) describes the evolution of node states and network topology through copying and rewiring dynamics. It traditionally assumes that all ties can be rewired, overlooking unbreakable real-world relationships like family or close friendships. To address this, we introduce fixed links that cannot be rewired and study how these structural constraints modify the collective dynamics of the CVM. In addition to the active and frozen phases of the CVM, we find a new localized active phase in which activity is sustained, beyond a percolation transition, by a giant component of fixed links. The existence of this new phase is predicted by a moment-closure approximation that goes beyond the standard pair approximation. The finite-size manifestation of this phase is an asymmetric fragmented phase. In addition, the active-frozen transition of the CVM, manifested as a fragmentation transition for finite systems, is delayed by the existence of fixed links to larger values of the rewiring rate.

</details>

### [119] [Formalizing local gauge freedom via bi-equivariant groupoid algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23475)
*Emil Prodan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要阐述了由具有内部对称性的自耦合块构成的人工超材料（architected metamaterials）中规范自由度（gauge degrees of freedom）的涌现，并用群胚C*-代数（groupoid C*-algebra）及其2-作用（2-action）刻画可实验实现的动力学矩阵集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范自由度出现在许多不同语境中，本文意在说明其如何在人工超材料（architected metamaterials）这一具体平台上产生：描述此类体系动力学需要选择局部参考系（local frames），而这些可选参考系即构成问题的局部规范自由度。摘要未给出更具体的应用背景或动机细节。

Method: 方法基于与架构（architecture）自然关联的群胚C*-代数（groupoid C*-algebra）：无内部对称性时，动力学矩阵由该代数的左正则表示（left regular representation）构造；当谐振器（resonators）具有内部对称性时，该群胚C*-代数承载内部对称群的自然2-作用（2-action），规范自由度被编码为由该2-作用生成的双等变子群胚代数（bi-equivariant sub-groupoid algebra）。

Result: 摘要给出两项主要结果：一是在无内部对称性的情形下证明动力学矩阵全部来自相应群胚C*-代数的左正则表示；二是证明在给定架构与离散内部对称群下，双等变子群胚代数是包含所有可在实验室中构造的动力学矩阵的最小C*-代数。

Conclusion: 以群胚C*-代数及其2-作用为核心的代数框架，可统一刻画人工超材料中规范自由度的涌现，并精确界定实验可实现的动力学矩阵所构成的代数类。摘要未涉及实验验证或具体物理预言。

Abstract: Gauge degrees of freedom appear in many different contexts. Here, we describe their emergence in the context of architected metamaterials built from self-coupling blocks with internal symmetries. Quantifying the dynamics requires choices of local frames and the many available options constitute the local gauge degrees of freedom of the problem. In the absence of internal symmetries, the dynamical matrices generating the dynamics are all built from the left regular representation of a groupoid C*-algebra canonically associated to the architecture. If the resonators display internal symmetries, we show that the mentioned groupoid C*-algebra accepts a natural 2-action by the group of internal symmetries and that the local gauge freedom can be encoded in a bi-equivariant sub-groupoid algebra generated from the mentioned 2-action. The latter is shown to be the smallest C*-algebra containing all dynamical matrices that can be engineered in a laboratory, for a given architecture and discrete group of internal symmetries.

</details>


### [120] [Norm Approximation with Linear Growth of Error for Bosonic Dynamics in the Mean-Field Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23493)
*Niels Benedikter,Chiara Boccato,Umberto Morellini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维均匀囚禁玻色气体（Bose gas）在平均场标度（mean-field scaling）下的量子多体动力学，证明有限激发的多体演化可由准自由演化（quasifree evolution）在 Fock 空间范数下逼近，误差随时间线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Bogolyubov 理论结果通常依赖有效方程的色散性（dispersive）来抑制误差增长，本文旨在处理有效方程非色散情形下仍能获得缓慢（线性）时间误差增长的问题。

Method: 针对描述玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensate）上有限多个激发（excitations）的一类初值，研究其 Cauchy 问题，并采用严格化版本的 Bogolyubov 理论（rigorous Bogolyubov theory）进行逼近分析。

Result: 证明了多体演化在 Fock 空间范数（Fock space norm）下可由激发的准自由演化逼近，误差随时间呈线性增长；且该缓慢增长在有效方程不具色散性时依然成立。

Conclusion: 结果表明，非色散有效方程情形下亦可获得随时间线性增长的误差界，从而推广了以往依赖色散性假设的结果。

Abstract: We consider the quantum many-body dynamics of a homogeneous confined Bose gas in three dimensions in the mean-field scaling regime. We study the Cauchy problem for a class of initial data describing finitely many excitations over a Bose-Einstein condensate. Using a rigorous version of Bogolyubov's theory, we prove that the many-body evolution can be approximated in Fock space norm by a quasifree evolution of the excitations with an error which grows linearly in time. In distinction from earlier results, the slow growth in time holds even though the effective equation is not dispersive.

</details>


### [121] [Charged Schrödinger blocks in $1+1$ dimensions from the quartic Casimir and endpoint equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23758)
*Hirohiko Shimada*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在无动力学实现的前提下，构造了一维空间中的荷二（charge-two）薛定谔共形块（Schrödinger conformal blocks），并借助Kummer提升（Kummer lift）由两个时间交比函数重建完整空间依赖，进而给出OPE唯一性、Calogero模型与扩散限制不动点上的具体应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是独立于具体动力学模型地刻画薛定谔共形块，并探索超越共线数据（collinear data）的非负性约束，为薛定谔自举（Schrödinger bootstrap）提供具体限制。

Method: 考虑维数为1/2的基本场有序真空四点函数，在首末插入点施加齐次薛定谔方程、中间双粒子区间允许相互作用；对在带号空间坐标（signed spatial coordinates）解析且空间反演下为偶的约化块，用共线值（collinear value）与重标度的混合空间导数两个时间交比函数表征空间依赖，并由合流超几何函数（confluent hypergeometric functions）构成的精确Kummer提升实现重建；再利用算符恒等式将四次Casimir方程化为该函数对的耦合系统，并用OPE条件筛选块。

Result: 在一般交换维数下，OPE条件对每种初级宇称（primary parity）给出归一化意义下唯一的块；得到共线${}_4F_3$函数与Kummer乘积之和（含影子分支，shadow branches），且块依赖带号坐标的相对符号，区分带拐点的分段线性世界线与单调段速度情形。在Calogero模型中恢复了已有的玻色型四点函数并得到其费米型对应，并有独立积分检验；在$A+B\to\emptyset$的扩散限制不动点处，两个相反初级宇称的块给出相同的共线异种响应。

Conclusion: 对所有位置与有序时间要求非负性可选出偶宇称族，从而确立按种类求和后的响应包含维数$Δ=1,2$的偶影子对（even shadow pair）；该非负性论证展示了薛定谔自举中一个具体的、超越共线数据的约束。

Abstract: We construct charge-two Schrödinger conformal blocks in one spatial dimension, independently of any dynamical realization. We consider ordered vacuum four-point functions of fundamental fields of dimension $1/2$, with homogeneous Schrödinger equations at the earliest and latest insertions and interactions allowed in the intermediate two-particle interval. For reduced blocks analytic in signed spatial coordinates and even under simultaneous spatial inversion, two functions of the time cross-ratio determine the full spatial dependence: the collinear value and a rescaled mixed spatial derivative. An exact Kummer lift, built from confluent hypergeometric functions, gives this reconstruction. An operator identity reduces the quartic Casimir equation, modulo the endpoint equations, to an equivalent coupled system for this pair. At generic exchanged dimension, OPE conditions select a unique block up to normalization for each primary parity. We obtain collinear ${}_4F_3$ functions and sums of Kummer products, including the shadow branches. The blocks depend on the relative sign of the signed coordinates, distinguishing piecewise linear worldlines with an inflection from those with monotone segment velocities. In the Calogero model, we recover the previously derived bosonic four-point function and obtain its fermionic counterpart, with an independent integral check. At the diffusion-limited fixed point of $A+B\to\emptyset$, two blocks of opposite primary parity give the same collinear unlike-species response. Nonnegativity for all positions and ordered times selects the even family, thereby establishing that the response summed over species contains the even shadow pair with dimensions $Δ=1,2$. The nonnegativity argument demonstrates a concrete constraint for a Schrödinger bootstrap beyond collinear data.

</details>


### [122] [Spectrum of the parity operator for parafermions and parabosons from character formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23957)
*N. I. Stoilova,J. Van der Jeugt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用特征标公式（character formulas）这一新方法，重新计算扩充型 Green 三重关系中新引入的宇称算符（parity operator）P 在 Fock 空间表示中的谱，并据此导出 osp(2|2n) 特定表示的新特征标公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作将 parafermion 与 paraboson 的 Green 三重关系（Green's triple relations）通过引入宇称算符 P 加以扩展，并借助产生湮灭算符的作用计算了 P 的谱；本文旨在用不同方法独立复现该谱，并揭示其与李超代数表示特征标之间的联系。

Method: 以特征标公式（character formulas）为工具：对 n 个 parafermion，从 so(2n+2) 的特征标公式推导 P 的作用；对 n 个 paraboson，则反向利用已知的 P 谱来获得 osp(2|2n) 特定表示的新特征标公式。

Result: 对 parafermion 情形，由 so(2n+2) 的特征标公式成功推出 P 的作用；对 paraboson 情形，已知的 P 谱给出了 osp(2|2n) 特定表示的新特征标公式。

Conclusion: 特征标公式方法可作为计算宇称算符谱的替代途径，且 P 谱与正交（超）代数表示的特征标公式之间存在双向联系：既可由特征标得到谱，也可由谱反推新的特征标公式。

Abstract: In a recent paper [J. Phys. A: Math. Theor. 59 175201 (2026)] Green's triple relations for parafermions and parabosons were extended by including a new operator $P$, referred to as the parity operator. The spectrum of $P$ in Fock space representations was computed, making use of the action of parafermion (resp.\ paraboson) creation and annihilation operators. In the current paper, we adopt a different method to compute the spectrum of $P$, namely using character formulas. For a set of $n$ parafermions, the action of $P$ is deduced from character formulas of $so(2n+2)$. For a set of $n$ parabosons, the known spectrum of $P$ yields new character formulas of particular $osp(2|2n)$ representations.

</details>


### [123] [Free energy distribution for two non-crossing directed polymers on the cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23981)
*Andrea De Luca,Pierre Le Doussal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究柱面几何（cylinder）上两条不可交（non-crossing）连续定向聚合物（continuum directed polymers）配分和的高阶累积量，借助一维吸引型 Lieb-Liniger 模型（attractive Lieb-Liniger model）的嵌套 Bethe 拟设（nested Bethe ansatz）与复本解析延拓（replica analytic continuation），计算各阶累积量振幅并导出自由能的大偏差（large-deviation）普适标度函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画共享随机介质中两条不可交定向聚合物的自由能涨落统计，特别是其长时间极限下各阶累积量的增长振幅，并揭示不可交约束在涨落中的作用。

Method: 先计算整数矩（integer moments）⟨Z_L(t)^n⟩，其长时间行为由对应于 Young 图 (n,n) 对称扇区的一维吸引型 Lieb-Liniger 模型基态能量决定；该能量通过含 2n 个物理快度（rapidities）与 n 个辅助快度的嵌套 Bethe 拟设求得。随后将 Brunet–Derrida 方法推广至两条聚合物情形，对复本数 n 进行解析延拓（analytic continuation）以获得累积量 c_k(L)。

Result: 得到均值 c_1(L) 与方差 c_2(L) 的完全显式公式，并给出计算所有高阶累积量 c_k(L) 的系统方案；在 L→+∞、n→0 且 nL^{1/2} 固定的极限下，导出描述两聚合物自由能大偏差统计的普适标度函数（universal scaling function），并解析获得该大偏差函数两侧尾部，显示不可交约束在有利与不利涨落中的不同作用。

Conclusion: 结果预期在一维双层周期 SHE/KPZ 类（two-layer periodic SHE/KPZ class）内具有普适性，表明嵌套 Bethe 拟设与复本延拓方法可系统处理多聚合物不可交约束下的自由能涨落统计。

Abstract: We consider the partition sum $Z_L(t)$ of two non-crossing continuum directed polymers of length $t$, in a common random medium with the geometry of a cylinder of circumference length $L$. In the limit $t \to +\infty$, the $k$-th cumulant of (minus) the free energy $\log Z_L(t)$, grows as $c_k(L) t$, and we compute the amplitudes $c_k(L)$. To this aim we first study the integer moments $\langle Z_L(t)^n \rangle$, whose long-time behavior is determined by the ground-state energy of the one-dimensional attractive Lieb--Liniger model in the symmetry sector associated with the Young diagram $(n,n)$ (a two-component bosonic nested Bethe ansatz). This ground state energy is obtained using the nested Bethe-Ansatz involving $2n$ physical rapidities and $n$ auxiliary rapidities. The analytic continuation in the replica number $n$, which yields the cumulants $c_k(L)$, is performed through a non-trivial generalization of the method developed by Brunet and Derrida for a single polymer on the cylinder. We obtain fully explicit formulas for the mean $c_1(L)$ and the variance $c_2(L)$, and construct a systematic scheme for computing all higher cumulants $c_k(L)$. Finally, taking $L\to+\infty$ and $n\to0$ with $nL^{1/2}$ fixed, we derive a universal scaling function which characterizes the large-deviation statistics of the two-polymer free energy and is expected to be universal within the one-dimensional two-layer periodic SHE/KPZ class. The two tails of the large-deviation function are obtained analytically, revealing different roles of the non-crossing constraint in favorable and unfavorable fluctuations of the common disorder.

</details>


### [124] [Multiplicity criteria for the absolutely continuous spectrum of finite hopping Hermitian operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24407)
*Boris Bermúdez-Cárdenas,Christian Sadel,Amal Taarabt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 Sadel 的径向型转移矩阵谱平均公式从标量谱测度推广到根矩阵（root matrix）对应的矩阵值谱测度，并据此给出绝对连续谱（absolutely continuous spectrum）重数下界的判据，证明梯形图上的随机衰减壳层矩阵势与 Bethe 格上的算子均几乎必然具有无穷重绝对连续谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始谱平均公式仅适用于标量谱测度，难以处理矩阵值测度情形，从而限制了对绝对连续谱重数（multiplicity）的定量刻画。

Method: 针对满足 Sadel 意义下径向型转移矩阵集（radial-type transfer matrix sets）的局部有限跳跃 Hermitian 算子，把谱平均公式推广至根矩阵关联的矩阵值谱测度，由此导出绝对连续谱重数下界的判据。

Result: 作为应用，对宽度递增导线上的梯形图（stair-like graphs）随机衰减壳层矩阵势，以及 Bethe 格（Bethe lattice）上的算子，均证明其绝对连续谱几乎必然具有无穷重数。

Conclusion: 矩阵值形式的谱平均公式为绝对连续谱重数提供了有效下界工具，并在两类典型图模型上给出了无穷重绝对连续谱的结论。

Abstract: We consider locally finite-hopping Hermitian operators that admit radial-type transfer matrix sets in the sense of Sadel. We extend his spectral averaging formula from scalar spectral measures to matrix-valued spectral measures associated to a root matrix. This extension yields criteria for lower bounds on the multiplicity of absolutely continuous spectrum. As applications, we consider random decaying shell-matrix potentials on stair-like graphs with increasing-width wires and operators on the Bethe lattice. In both cases, we establish that absolutely continuous spectrum has almost surely infinite multiplicity.

</details>


### [125] [On the thermal behavior of composites embedding extended general interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24414)
*Soheil Firooz,Kashish Veda Eluri,Ali Javili,Paul Steinmann*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文放宽传统界面模型中“界面温度为两侧基体温度平均值”的假设，允许两侧基体对界面温度有非等权重贡献，建立包含扩展广义界面的有限元计算框架与均匀化框架，并推导纤维/颗粒增强复合材料有效热导率的闭式解析式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质材料中表面粗糙度、化学不相容、力学失配与制造缺陷导致组分间粘结不完美，形成具有一定厚度、呈梯度或非均匀微结构的界面相（interphase）；该界面相几乎无法直接解析，通常以具有自身能量结构的零厚度界面（interface）替代。但传统界面模型将界面温度取为两侧基体温度的平均，仅适用于均匀同质界面相，无法准确刻画梯度或非均匀界面相。

Method: 允许两侧基体相对界面温度具有不相等的贡献，系统重新推导相应的平衡方程（balance equations），并给出该模型的有限元（finite element）实现计算框架；进一步将模型纳入均匀化（homogenization）框架，推导考虑扩展广义界面（extended general interface）影响的纤维与颗粒增强复合材料有效热导率（effective thermal conductivity）闭式解析表达式；最后通过系列数值算例验证模型能力。

Result: 建立了考虑两侧基体非等权重贡献的界面热力学模型及其有限元实现与均匀化解析框架，得到纤维增强与颗粒增强复合材料有效热导率的闭式表达式；数值算例展示了模型的能力，并揭示模型参数对复合材料尺寸相关（size-dependent）热行为的影响。

Conclusion: 通过放宽界面温度的等权平均假设，所提扩展广义界面模型可更准确地描述梯度/非均匀界面相的热行为，并借助有限元与均匀化框架实现对复合材料有效热导率的预测及尺寸相关效应的参数化分析。

Abstract: In heterogeneous materials, surface roughness, chemical incompatibility, mechanical mismatch or manufacturing defects result in imperfect bonding between the constituents. Such imperfections introduce a finite-thickness interphase zone between the constituents that possess a graded or inhomogeneous micro-structure. It is virtually impossible to resolve the behavior of such interphases. Instead, a well-established approach is to replace the interphase by a zero-thickness interface endowed with its own energetic structure. Conventional interface models define the interface temperature as an average of the temperatures of the surrounding bulk phases. However, this assumption is only adequate to represent uniform homogeneous interphases and it fails to accurately capture the behavior of graded or inhomogeneous interphases. In this work, we address this limitation by allowing for unequal contributions of the surrounding bulk phases to the interface temperature. We systematically revisit the resulting balance equations and present the computational framework for the finite element implementation of the model. We further incorporate the proposed model within the homogenization framework and derive closed-form analytical expressions for the effective thermal conductivity of fiber- and particle-reinforced composites, accouting for the influence of the extended general interface. Finally, through a series of numerical examples, we demonstrate the capabilities of the proposed model and investigate the influence of its parameters on the size-dependent thermal behavior of composites.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Wei-Norman Factorization of Frenet-Serret equations and the Cardan Lock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23665)
*Julio Guerrero,Rafael Florencio*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在旋转群李代数框架下用魏-诺曼分解（Wei-Norman factorization）处理非恒定曲率与挠率（curvature and torsion）的Frenet-Serret方程，指出采用Cartan基可解耦为Riccati方程，且奇点处传播子（propagator）大多仍良定义，从而在多数情形下避免万向节锁（gimbal lock）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对沿可微曲线运动粒子的Frenet-Serret方程，在曲率与挠率非恒定的情况下，需寻找便于求解的传播子表示形式；同时欧拉角（Euler angles）或卡尔丹角（Cardan angles）会导致强耦合方程，故需要更优的基选择。

Method: 在旋转群（rotation group）李代数框架下对传播子作魏-诺曼因子分解（Wei-Norman factorization），并比较不同基下的方程结构，采用Cartan基进行解耦。

Result: 使用欧拉角或卡尔丹角得到高度耦合的线性方程组；改用李代数中的Cartan基可实现解耦，本质上化为一个Riccati方程。

Conclusion: 由于旋转代数非可解代数（solvable algebra），全局魏-诺曼定理不适用，方程解在特定点出现与万向节锁/卡尔丹锁（gimbal or Cardan lock）相关的奇点；但对这些点的极限分析表明，多数情况下传播子仍良定义，万向节锁被避免。

Abstract: Frenet-Serret equations for particles moving along differentiable curves, in the case of non-constant curvature and torsion, are considered in the framework of the Wei-Norman factorization of the propagator using the Lie algebra of the rotation group. It leads to a system of coupled linear equations that are highly coupled if Euler or Cardan angles are used, but that can be uncoupled if the Cartan basis in the Lie algebra is used, resulting essentially in a Ricatti equation. However, since the rotations algebra is not a solvable algebra, the global Wei-Norman Theorem does not apply and singularities in the solutions of the system of equations appear at certain points, related to the \text{gimbal o Cardan lock}. Notwidthstanding, a detailed study of the limit at these points shows that in most of the cases the propagator is well defined and gimbal lock is avoided.

</details>


### [127] [A Trajectory-Based Mathematical Framework for Wildfire Front Propagation in Heterogeneous Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24551)
*Hengameh R. Dehkordi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于轨迹（trajectory）的野火锋面传播数学建模框架，结合方向依赖传播律与Finsler正交性，并在巴西潘塔纳尔（Pantanal）地区以VIIRS活跃火点数据进行空间对照实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对异质景观（heterogeneous landscapes）中野火锋面传播缺乏严格数学表述的问题，构建不预设固定全局锋面几何的传播模型，并检验其与实际观测的空间一致性。

Method: 采用方向依赖传播律（direction-dependent propagation law）与沿移动边界的Finsler正交性（Finsler orthogonality），导出外向火锋轨迹的非线性局部系统；以严格凸的平移椭圆表示保证外向速度的存在唯一性。传播系数随空间与逐时风强迫变化，环境强迫由ERA5风场与ERA5-Land植被数据描述，结果与VIIRS活跃火点观测进行地理配准与各向同性空间配准。

Result: 2020年9月3—5日探索性实验的全时段Dice系数（Dice coefficient）分别为0.578、0.571和0.467；对空间传播系数、风速大小与速率律指数（rate-law exponents）的逐一敏感性扰动（one-at-a-time sensitivity）量化了环境强迫与参数变化对火势空间范围的影响。

Conclusion: 所得为事件内空间一致性（within-event spatial agreement）而非独立预测验证，因缺少卫星过境精确时间戳。该框架为后续不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）、多事件独立验证与分部门火势评估提供数学基础。

Abstract: We present a mathematical formulation based on trajectories for modeling the propagation of wildfire fronts in heterogeneous landscapes and apply it to the Brazilian Pantanal. The formulation is made up of a direction-dependent propagation law and Finsler orthogonality along a moving boundary. This results in a nonlinear local system for outward fire front trajectories. We provide a strictly convex translated ellipse representation at each point of the evolving boundary which guaranties uniqueness and existence of the chosen outward velocity. The effective propagation coefficients that are dependent on space and the hourly wind forcing permit the perimeter to evolve irregularly without imposing a fixed global front geometry. Computed trajectories were georeferenced and isotropically spatially registered with VIIRS active-fire observations. ERA5 wind and ERA5-Land vegetation data were used to describe the environmental forcing and landscape heterogeneity. The full-run Dice coefficients for the observation-guided spatial comparisons for the exploratory experiments for September 3-5, 2020 are 0.578, 0.571, and 0.467 respectively. One-at-a-time sensitivity perturbations of the spatial propagation coefficient, wind speed magnitude and rate-law exponents quantify the governing influence of the environmental forcing and parameter variations on the spatial extent of the calculated fire advance. These observed patterns also guided calibration and, in the absence of exact timestamps of satellite acquisition, the results show within-event spatial agreement rather than independent predictive validation. The framework provides a mathematically rigorous basis for subsequent uncertainty quantification, independent validation over multiple events and prospective sector-wise assessments of simulated fire spread.

</details>

### [128] [Analytical Interacting Tight-binding Model for Fractional Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23409)
*Lexu Zhao,Jayden S. Chang,Awwab A. Azam,Jiabin Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个二维两带紧束缚（tight-binding）模型，跳变与相互作用均以高斯形式指数局域，在无任何能带投影的情况下，1/3填充的基态对任意有限晶格与任意边界条件均精确为分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）态，并解析证明单准空穴（quasihole）态为零能基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 FCI 研究多依赖能带投影（band projection）或连续统中的 onsite spinor 结构，缺乏一个无需投影、相互作用天然局域且可精确求解的晶格模型，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造二维 2-band 紧束缚模型，跳变与相互作用在实空间均呈高斯衰减的指数局域；相互作用由成对湮灭算符构成，其系数为跳变振幅除以相对距离的正幂次；不做能带投影，直接在周期性/扭曲边界条件下求解 1/3 填充的多体哈密顿量。

Result: 对任意有限晶格尺寸与任意边界条件，1/3 填充基态精确为 FCI 态且具有非零能隙；解析证明在保持电子数不变、增加一个元胞后，任意位置的单准空穴态为全哈密顿量的零能基态，类比 Haldane V1 赝势下的 Laughlin 准空穴；数值结果支持在相互作用强度远小于单粒子能隙时，热力学极限下该 FCI 态可能保持非零能隙。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个无需能带投影、无需连续统 spinor 结构的精确可解 FCI 平台，其准空穴任意子（anyonic quasihole）性质可严格解析验证，为晶格分数量子霍尔物理提供了新的可控范例。

Abstract: We construct a two-dimensional 2-band tight-binding model with two-body interactions and without magnetic lattice translation, in which both the hopping and interaction are exponentially local (with Gaussian decay) in real space. The interaction is built from pair annihilation operators whose coefficients are hopping amplitudes divided by positive powers of relative distance. Without any band projection, the ground states at $1/3$ filling are exactly fractional Chern insulator (FCI) states (and thus have nonzero gap) for any finite lattice size with any periodic/twist boundary conditions. The FCI ground states are analogous to the discretized version of the Laughlin-like states in the continuum Chern-ideal band, though our FCI states do not require the onsite spinor structure that is necessary in the continuum. We further show analytically that, after adding one unit cell while keeping electron number fixed, the anyonic one-quasihole states are zero-energy ground states of the full Hamiltonian for any finite system size and any quasihole position, analogous to Laughlin quasiholes under the Haldane $V_1$ pseudopotential. Numerical evidence is provided for the possible nonzero gap of the FCI states at $1/3$ filling in the thermodynamic limit, when interaction strength is much smaller than the single-particle band gap.

</details>


### [129] [Synthesis-route-dependent state selection in the frustrated spinel ZnV$_2$O$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23500)
*Jem Pitcairn,Jennifer N. Graham,Jacob L. Brownlee,Shruti Chakravarty,Joya A. Cooley,Giorgia Confalonieri,A. Dominic Fortes,David W. Tam,J. Ross Stewart,Lucy Clark*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究表明，固相合成路径（synthesis pathway）可作为强关联材料（strongly correlated materials）中的实验变量，在尖晶石ZnV₂O₄中可重复地选取不同的结构与磁基态（ground state）。微波辅助固相合成（microwave-assisted solid-state synthesis, MASS）将立方尖晶石结构稳定至低温，并保留高度阻挫的S=1烧绿石（pyrochlore）晶格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联材料的新奇性质对其制备方式高度敏感，但合成路径很少被视为决定基态的基本变量。ZnV₂O₄中反铁磁耦合的V³⁺ S=1矩构成烧绿石晶格，存在竞争性的自旋与轨道简并（spin and orbital degeneracy），不同合成路线会带来显著不同的低温行为，从而构成检验该理念的模型体系。

Method: 通过改变固相合成路径进行对比，采用微波辅助固相合成（MASS）并与常规合成对照；结合高分辨衍射（high-resolution diffraction）、磁测量（magnetometry）与非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）表征结构与磁性质。

Result: MASS可重复稳定立方尖晶石结构至低温，抑制常规的立方—四方相变（cubic-to-tetragonal transition），保留高度阻挫的S=1烧绿石晶格；其磁基态表现出持续的类自旋液体动力学（spin-liquid-like dynamics）以及部分自旋冻结（partial spin freezing）。

Conclusion: 研究确立了合成路径作为在强关联材料中选取竞争性涌现态（emergent states）的实验变量，并揭示了一条获得常规合成无法实现的基态的途径。

Abstract: The emergent properties of strongly correlated materials can be highly sensitive to how they are made, yet synthesis pathway is rarely treated as a fundamental variable governing their ground states. The spinel ZnV$_2$O$_4$ provides a model system in which to test this idea: its pyrochlore lattice of antiferromagnetically coupled V$^{3+}$ $S=1$ moments hosts competing spin and orbital degeneracies, while different synthesis routes yield markedly different low-temperature behaviour. Here, we show that changing the solid-state synthesis pathway reproducibly selects a distinct structural and magnetic ground state. Microwave-assisted solid-state synthesis (MASS) stabilises the cubic spinel structure to low temperature, suppressing the conventional cubic-to-tetragonal transition and preserving the highly frustrated $S=1$ pyrochlore lattice. Combining high-resolution diffraction, magnetometry and inelastic neutron scattering, we find a magnetic ground state exhibiting persistent spin-liquid-like dynamics together with partial spin freezing. Our results establish synthesis pathway as an experimental variable for selecting between competing emergent states in strongly correlated materials, revealing a route to accessing ground states not realised by conventional synthesis.

</details>


### [130] [Implementation of LDA+Gutzwiller with Newton's method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23842)
*Jian Zhang,Ming-Feng Tian,Guang-Xi Jin,Yuan-Feng Xu,Xi Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出在LDA+Gutzwiller方法中引入牛顿法（Newton's method）并结合黄金分割搜索（golden section search）等改进手段，显著提升计算的自洽过程、效率与收敛性，并在多种强关联材料上验证了结果可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为计算关联材料（correlated materials）的电子结构，需改进既有LDA+Gutzwiller实现中的自洽求解过程，提高效率与收敛性。

Method: 采用牛顿法（Newton's method），辅以黄金分割搜索（golden section search）及其他改进方案，对LDA+Gutzwiller方法进行实现，并与先前使用的线性混合（linear mixing）方法进行对比。

Result: 牛顿法使自洽过程、计算效率与收敛性得到显著改善；将其代码应用于SrVO$_3$、LaCoO$_3$与La$_2$O$_3$Fe$_2$Se$_2$等典型强关联材料，计算结果与既有研究吻合良好。

Conclusion: 基于牛顿法的LDA+Gutzwiller实现可有效提升计算性能，并在典型强关联体系上给出与文献一致的结果，具备实用价值。

Abstract: In order to calculate the electronic structure of correlated materials, we propose implementation of the LDA+Gutzwiller method with Newton's method. The self-consistence process, efficiency and convergence of calculation are improved dramatically by using Newton's method with golden section search and other improvement approaches. We compare the calculated results by applying the previous linear mix method and Newton's method. We have applied our code to study the electronic structure of several typical strong correlated materials, including SrVO$_3$, LaCoO$_3$, and La$_2$O$_3$Fe$_2$Se$_2$. Our results fit quite well with the previous studies.

</details>


### [131] [Local spin constraints and symmetry in single-$Q$ spiral magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23882)
*Lasin Thaivalappil,Shin-Ming Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文针对经典自旋模型的 Luttinger–Tisza（LT）下界忽略了局域自旋长度约束的问题，发展了一类带约束的精确表述，用于单/双磁性亚晶格的一般双线性哈密顿量，并揭示对称性与各向异性对螺旋序的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LT 方法虽给出经典自旋模型能量的下界，却放松了局域自旋长度约束；对一般非公度单-Q 螺旋，固定自旋长度要求每个磁性亚晶格上呈圆偏振，而最低 LT 本征模中亚晶格权重相等并不能保证此条件，故需建立带约束的严格表述。

Method: 对单亚晶格情形，极小化归结为关于螺旋面法向（spiral-plane normal）的久期方程（secular equation）；对双亚晶格情形，精确消去相对相位，在两个单位法向构成的空间上得到与参考标架无关的能量。同时区分保持/反转傅里叶臂（Fourier arm）以及保持/交换亚晶格的对称操作以导出对称性约束。

Result: 对具对称各向异性的模型，在两个螺旋面法向上做数值优化所得能量低于任一受约束的纯交换扇区（pure exchange-sector）态，尽管线性哈密顿量可块对角化。另一各向异性双亚晶格基准表明，即使最低 LT 本征模的亚晶格权重相等，施加局域圆偏振约束仍可能改变优选的序波矢（ordering wave vector）。

Conclusion: 局域自旋长度约束并非平凡修正，可实质影响经典螺旋态的基态能量与优选波矢，因此基于无约束 LT 本征模的推断需谨慎；文中所建立的约束表述与对称性分类为分析一般双线性经典自旋哈密顿量提供了系统框架。

Abstract: The Luttinger--Tisza (LT) method provides a lower bound on the energy of classical spin models but relaxes the local spin-length constraints. For a generic incommensurate single-$Q$ spiral, fixed spin length requires circular polarization on every magnetic sublattice, a condition not guaranteed by equal sublattice weights in the lowest LT eigenmode. We develop a constrained formulation for general bilinear classical spin Hamiltonians with one or two magnetic sublattices. For one sublattice, minimization reduces to a secular equation for the spiral-plane normal. For two sublattices, exact elimination of the relative phase gives a frame-independent energy on the space of two unit normals. We derive symmetry constraints by distinguishing operations that preserve or reverse the Fourier arm and those that preserve or exchange sublattices. For a model with symmetric anisotropy, numerical optimization over both spiral-plane normals yields a lower energy than either constrained pure exchange-sector state, despite the block diagonalization of the linear Hamiltonian. A separate anisotropic two-sublattice benchmark demonstrates that enforcing local circularity can shift the preferred ordering wave vector even when the lowest LT eigenmode has equal sublattice weights.

</details>


### [132] [Molecular Kondo Ball Based on an Endohedral Fullerene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23918)
*Xiangyang Li,Xiao Zheng,Bing Wang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文理论预测 TiSc$_2$N@C$_{80}$/Al(111) 体系中存在“分子近藤球（molecular Kondo ball）”：富勒烯封闭笼内的 Ti 局域自旋通过笼层调制的巡游型电子库实现近藤屏蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用封闭富勒烯 π 笼同时充当局域自旋宿主与巡游型电子库（itinerant-like electronic reservoir），从而构建可容忍吸附取向的分子近藤体系。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）考察分子—衬底杂化与 Ti 局域矩，并结合层级运动方程（hierarchical equations of motion, HEOM）模拟输运/谱函数行为。

Result: 计算显示中等强度分子—衬底杂化将离散 C$_{80}$ 分子能级展宽为巡游型电子态，同时保留 Ti 局域矩；HEOM 给出温度依赖的 Fano 共振（Fano resonance），近藤温度约 60 K；Cd(0001) 与 In(101) 衬底上电子条件类似。

Conclusion: 近藤态的关键电子特征对吸附取向不敏感，表明封闭笼几何为近藤屏蔽提供了一种取向容忍的分子架构（orientation-tolerant molecular architecture）。

Abstract: We theoretically predict a molecular Kondo ball in TiSc$_2$N@C$_{80}$/Al(111), where a Ti-centered $S = 1/2$ local moment is encapsulated within a closed fullerene $π$ cage that serves as an itinerant-like electronic reservoir for Kondo screening. First-principles calculations show that intermediate molecule--substrate hybridization broadens the discrete C$_{80}$ molecular levels into itinerant-like electronic states while preserving the Ti local moment, with similar electronic conditions found for Cd(0001) and In(101).Hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) simulations reveal a temperature-dependent Fano resonance with a Kondo temperature of about 60 K.The key electronic characteristics underlying the Kondo state remain robust against adsorption orientation, demonstrating that the closed cage geometry provides an orientation-tolerant molecular architecture for Kondo screening.

</details>


### [133] [Stabilizer models of achiral abelian topological orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24105)
*Marvin Qi,Wilbur Shirley*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文构造了实现所有非手性（achiral）二维阿贝尔拓扑序（2D abelian topological order）的连续变量稳定子码（continuous-variable stabilizer code），玻色型与费米型兼有，方法基于任意零签名整数 $K$-矩阵（signature-zero integer $K$-matrix）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是统一实现玻色与费米两类非手性二维阿贝尔拓扑序，并将拓扑序的构造归结为可微观实现的凝聚（condensation）机制。

Method: 从任意零签名整数 $K$-矩阵出发，在 $\mathbb{R}^N$ 规范理论（$\mathbb{R}^N$ gauge theory）中凝聚由两两相互编织平凡的双子（dyon）构成的格；微观上通过规范化 $\mathbb{R}^N$ 环面码（toric code）的非局域 1-形式对称性（non-on-site 1-form symmetry）实现该凝聚。

Result: 证明了任意零签名整数 $K$-矩阵对应的拓扑序均可由上述双子凝聚获得，并给出其连续变量稳定子码的构造。

Conclusion: 非手性二维阿贝尔拓扑序（含玻色与费米）可由连续变量稳定子码统一刻画，其微观实现等价于对 $\mathbb{R}^N$ 环面码非局域 1-形式对称性的规范化。

Abstract: We construct continuous-variable stabilizer codes realizing every achiral 2D abelian topological order, both bosonic and fermionic. Starting from an arbitrary signature-zero integer $K$-matrix, we show that the corresponding topological order can be obtained by condensing a lattice of dyons with trivial pairwise mutual braiding in an $\mathbb{R}^N$ gauge theory. We implement this condensation microscopically by gauging a non-on-site 1-form symmetry of the $\mathbb{R}^N$ toric code.

</details>


### [134] [From geometric curvature to effective backgrounds: a dictionary for Dirac fermions in one, two and three spatial dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24878)
*David Valenzuela,Alfredo Raya*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文从单一Clifford代数恒等式出发，将弯曲背景上的Dirac方程统一改写为平直空间方程，几何效应表现为张量型Fermi速度与一组背景场，并系统追踪一维、二维、三维中自旋联络的两种收缩及其物理含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立弯曲背景Dirac方程与平直空间有效描述之间的系统性对应关系，厘清几何（自旋联络）如何转化为Fermi速度、规范场、质量与轴矢势等背景场，并将其与应变样品中占主导的晶格赝规范场区分开。

Method: 以单一Clifford代数恒等式为推导起点，将自旋联络用Ricci旋转系数（Ricci rotation coefficients）表示，分类出迹收缩与全反对称收缩两种情形，并给出一、二、三维的展开及在局域标架旋转下的变换律。

Result: 迹收缩在各维度均给出矢量势型项，携带虚因子i，正是位置依赖Fermi速度下维持算符厄米性（Hermitian）的项；全反对称收缩依赖Levi-Civita符号可用指标数，分别表现为线上的迹、面上的赝标量质量、体中的轴矢势。二维中迹项的旋度可由标架旋转消去，其散度正比于高斯曲率（Gaussian curvature）。应用给出：高斯凸起中心局域态密度（local density of states）低于平直值且低能有闭式；非均匀lapse可在能隙内束缚态而非均匀速度；二维定常标架的环流拖曳可局域移动Dirac质量且符号可正可负；体中扭曲/旋转标架的轴矢项表现为标架诱导的手征化学势（chiral chemical potential）或使Weyl节点分离。

Conclusion: 几何对Dirac型激发的效应可归约为张量Fermi速度加两类自旋联络收缩产生的背景场，二者均非局域旋转不变；该框架统一解释曲率、lapse、拖曳与旋转引起的谱与输运改变，并将几何贡献与应变样品中主导响应的晶格赝规范场分离开来。

Abstract: Dirac equation on curved backgrounds can be rewritten as a flat-space equation in which geometry appears as a tensorial Fermi velocity together with a set of background fields. We derive this correspondence from a single Clifford-algebra identity and follow it through one, two, and three spatial dimensions. Once the spin connection is written in terms of Ricci rotation coefficients, only two contractions can occur. The trace produces a term with the index structure of a vector potential in every dimensionality; it enters with an imaginary factor i and is precisely the term that keeps the operator Hermitian when the Fermi velocity depends on position. The totally antisymmetric contraction produces an object that depends on the number of indices available to the Levi-Civita symbol: a trace on a line, a pseudoscalar mass on a surface, and an axial vector potential in a volume. Neither contraction is invariant under local rotations of the frame, and we give the transformation laws. On a surface, the curl of the trace term can always be removed by a frame rotation, while its divergence is proportional to the Gaussian curvature. We apply the correspondence to a Gaussian bump, where the local density of states at the center falls below its flat value and obeys a closed form at low energy; to a wire, where a nonuniform lapse binds states inside the gap and a nonuniform velocity cannot; to stationary frames on a surface, where a circulating drag shifts the Dirac mass locally with either sign; and to twisted and rotating frames in a volume, where the axial term takes the form of a chiral chemical potential produced by the frame alone, or separates Weyl nodes in a rotating sample. We separate the geometric contribution from the lattice pseudogauge field that dominates the response of strained samples

</details>


### [135] [Hidden topological signatures in a van der Waals ferromagnet revealed by spin-resolved circular-dichroic photoemission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24468)
*Jay R. Paudel,Federico Mazzola,Lidia A. Lapinski,Barun Ghosh,Valeria Rosa Rocha,Abigail M. Derrico,Nikola Maksimovic,Iulia Cojocariu,Daniel Baranowski,Christoph Klewe,Vitaliy Feyer,Ivana Vobornik,Claus M. Schneider,Giancarlo Panaccione,Arun Bansil,James G. Analytis,Alexander X. Gray*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作结合自旋分辨角分辨光电子能谱、圆二色性测量与密度泛函理论，揭示关联范德华铁磁体 Ni1/4TaSe2 的自旋极化电子结构与拓扑特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状强关联铁磁体（layered ferromagnet）为研究磁性、自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与非平庸能带拓扑（nontrivial band topology）之间的相互作用提供了平台；Ni1/4TaSe2 的转变温度 58 K，且其成分邻近 Ni 缺失后出现的超导相，因而具有研究价值。

Method: 采用自旋分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）、圆二色性测量（circular-dichroism measurements）以及密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算相结合的方式研究其自旋分辨电子结构。

Result: 观察到穿越费米能级（Fermi level）的自旋极化态（spin-polarized states），以及铁磁态中时间反演对称性破缺（broken time-reversal symmetry）的信号；自旋分辨圆二色性测量显示依赖螺旋度（helicity）的自旋响应，与有限的自旋贝里曲率（spin Berry curvature）一致，并暗示局域拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 结果在 Ni1/4TaSe2 中建立了铁磁性、自旋极化电子态与拓扑信号之间的实验关联，为研究这些性质如何与 Ni 缺失后超导性的出现相关联奠定基础。

Abstract: Layered ferromagnets with strongly correlated electrons provide a platform for exploring the interplay among magnetism, spin-orbit coupling, and nontrivial band topology. $\mathrm{Ni}_{1/4}\mathrm{TaSe}_2$ is a correlated van der Waals ferromagnet with a transition temperature of 58 K that lies close in composition to a superconducting phase that emerges upon Ni depletion. Here, we combine spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, circular-dichroism measurements, and density functional theory calculations to investigate its spin-resolved electronic structure. We observe spin-polarized states crossing the Fermi level and signatures of broken time-reversal symmetry in the ferromagnetic state. Spin-resolved circular-dichroism measurements further reveal a helicity-dependent spin response consistent with a finite spin Berry curvature and suggestive of local topological character. Together, these results establish an experimental connection between ferromagnetism, spin-polarized electronic states, and topological signatures in $\mathrm{Ni}_{1/4}\mathrm{TaSe}_2$, providing a foundation for investigating how these properties may relate to the emergence of superconductivity upon Ni depletion.

</details>


### [136] [Exact Parent Hamiltonians for Partially Magnetized Chiral Spin Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24935)
*Xin Shen,Guangyue Ji,Jie Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作构造了实现部分磁化手征自旋液体（chiral spin liquids, CSLs）的精确母体哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonians），覆盖阿贝尔（Abelian）与非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）分数量子霍尔态，并给出可在Hofstadter-Hubbard模型中通过二阶超交换（superexchange）实现的微观方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征自旋液体是典型的分数化相（fractionalized phases），但简单且精确的母体哈密顿量极为稀少，限制了对其严格研究与微观实现。

Method: 基于对玻色晶格分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall, FQH）态在任意排斥相互作用强度下成立的Hubbard型精确母体哈密顿量，取在位排斥趋于无穷并结合局域玻色子-自旋映射（boson-spin mappings），构造两类自旋的CSL母体哈密顿量；再通过广义自旋极化Hofstadter-Hubbard模型中的二阶超交换推导其受控微观实现。

Result: 所得双自旋构造将Kalmeyer-Laughlin自旋哈密顿量推广到更广的玻色FQH模型态族，涵盖阿贝尔态与非阿贝尔部分子态（parton states）；更一般的局域约束与相互作用进一步给出高自旋与多自旋的精确母体哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 该构造为部分磁化手征自旋液体提供了系统化的精确母体哈密顿量框架，并指明其在冷原子/晶格量子模拟体系中的可实验实现路径。

Abstract: Chiral spin liquids (CSLs) are paradigmatic fractionalized phases, yet simple exact parent Hamiltonians for them remain scarce. In this work, we construct exact spin parent Hamiltonians for partially magnetized CSLs realizing various bosonic topological orders. Our construction relies on an exact Hubbard-type parent Hamiltonian for bosonic lattice fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states, valid at arbitrary repulsive interaction strengths. Taking the on-site repulsion to infinity and applying local boson-spin mappings yields exact parent Hamiltonians for the CSLs. Our two-spin construction generalizes the Kalmeyer-Laughlin spin Hamiltonian to a broader class of bosonic FQH model states, including Abelian states and non-Abelian parton states. We derive controlled microscopic realizations of these two-spin Hamiltonians via second-order superexchange in generalized spinful Hofstadter-Hubbard models with tailored hoppings and interactions. With more general local constraints and interactions, boson-spin mappings yield exact higher-spin and multispin parent Hamiltonians for partially magnetized CSLs.

</details>

### [137] [Critical--localized reentry under periodic spacer control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23796)
*Xin Wang,Pei-Jie Chang,Dong Ruan,Gui-Lu Long*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在匹配二聚体链（matched-dimer chain）中仅调节间隔位点能量（spacer on-site energies），并追踪固定的积分态密度（IDS）标签区间，证明了临界-局域交替（critical–localized alternation）可在非零谱权重上重复出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验准周期谱（quasiperiodic spectrum）中重复的临界-局域交替是否延伸至非零的谱权重（spectral weight），而非仅限于单点或零测集。

Method: 利用精确的Bloch行列式恒等式（Bloch-determinant identity）将物理转移判别式与周期参考算子的能带相联系；对黄金格频率（golden cell frequency），在相位满测集上对谱类型进行严格证明，并用已验证的IDS界认证交替现象。

Result: 在参考能带内部，物理谱为纯奇异连续谱（purely singular-continuous spectrum）且Lyapunov指数为零，呈临界性；能带严格外部为纯点谱（pure point），本征函数指数衰减。随间隔控制参数（spacer control）逐次变化，同一固定谱份额出现重复的临界-局局交替。

Conclusion: 间隔平移（spacer shift）以不同速率移动物理能级与参考带边，从而可重塑有限链回返窗口（return windows）并将端点置于指定控制值；仿射重标定（affine recalibration）可精确平移该控制族。

Abstract: Tracking a fixed fraction of a quasiperiodic spectrum tests whether repeated critical--localized alternation extends over a nonzero spectral weight. We find such alternation in a matched-dimer chain by varying only the spacer on-site energies and following a fixed interval of integrated-density-of-states (IDS) labels. An exact Bloch-determinant identity links the physical transfer discriminant to the bands of a periodic reference operator. For the golden cell frequency, we prove that, on a common full-measure set of phases and for every finite value of the spacer control, the physical spectrum in the open reference bands is critical, with purely singular-continuous spectrum and zero Lyapunov exponent, while the spectrum strictly outside the bands is pure point, with exponentially decaying eigenfunctions. Validated IDS bounds certify repeated critical--localized alternation of the same fixed spectral fraction at successive spacer controls. Spacer shifts move the physical levels and reference-band edges at different rates, allowing us to reshape finite-chain return windows and place their endpoints at prescribed controls. An affine recalibration relocates the control family exactly.

</details>


### [138] [Wegner model in high dimension: singular continuous spectrum from U(1) symmetry breaking and AAT self-consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24515)
*Julian Arenz,Martin R. Zirnbauer*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文从场论与统计力学视角分析高维晶格上Wegner单轨道模型的能谱分解，在Anderson转变处识别出奇异连续型局域谱测度，并归因于非紧致整体对称群的新型自发对称破缺机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为与奇异连续谱相关的无序现象（disorder phenomena）提供场论基础，深化对Anderson转变处谱性质的理解。

Method: 采用场论与统计力学思想，分析Wegner单轨道模型（Wegner one-orbital model）在高维晶格上的能谱分解；借助Abou-Chacra–Anderson–Thouless自洽局域化理论（self-consistent theory of localization）近似描述对称破缺的极值态。

Result: 在Anderson转变（Anderson transition）处识别出无序模型的奇异连续型（singular continuous type）局域谱测度；金属相中对称性破缺至紧致子群（compact subgroup），临界点则自发破缺至幂零子群（nilpotent subgroup）。

Conclusion: 奇异连续谱的产生机制源于场论整体对称群非紧致性（non-compactness）所导致的新型自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）情景，该工作为相关无序现象奠定了场论基础。

Abstract: We analyze the energy-spectral decomposition of the Wegner one-orbital model in high lattice dimension using ideas from field theory and statistical mechanics. Most notably, we identify a local spectral measure of singular continuous type for the disordered model at the Anderson transition. The key mechanism that leads to a singular continuous spectrum is a novel scenario of spontaneous symmetry breaking, intimately connected to the non-compactness of the global symmetry group of the field theory. While that symmetry breaks down to a compact subgroup in the metallic phase, it spontaneously breaks down to a nilpotent subgroup at criticality. We describe the symmetry-breaking extremal states approximately, by applying the self-consistent theory of localization due to Abou-Chacra, Anderson, and Thouless. The thrust of our work is to provide a field-theoretical foundation for disorder phenomena associated with singular continuous spectra.

</details>
