### [139] [Fuzzy Dark Matter as a diffractive barrier: Wave-optics Signatures of Ultralight Fields on Lensed Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23580)
*Manish Tamta,Han Gil Choi,Diego Blas,Miguel Zumalacárregui*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 超轻暗物质（ultralight dark matter, ULDM）晕中的密度涨落可作为衍射光栅，在宿主的引力透镜效应之上对引力波（gravitational waves, GWs）叠加频率依赖的随机衍射调制；该信号在菲涅耳尺度（Fresnel scale）与颗粒相干长度相当、或宏观像靠近临界曲线（critical curve）/为Type II像时显著增强，并有望被LISA用于探测或限制ULDM粒子质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移源发出的引力波在传播途中会穿越中间物质分布的引力场，而由超轻玻色子构成的ULDM晕具有强密度涨落，其场分布的干涉图案可能对GW产生额外的、此前较少讨论的印记，因此需要评估这类衍射信号的可观测性。

Method: 在波动光学（wave-optics）区制下，将ULDM涨落视为衍射光栅；由于透镜效应依赖投影面密度（projected surface density），推导其近似概率分布，并考察其偏离高斯分布（Gaussian distribution）的程度与晕轮廓（halo profile）及相干长度（coherence length）的关系；随后量化颗粒产生的GW衍射特征对宏观像放大率（macroimage magnification）与宇称（parity）的依赖。

Result: 当菲涅耳尺度与颗粒相干长度满足 r_F(f) ~ ℓ_c 时，ULDM干涉网络在受到宿主中等程度透镜化的GW上留下 O(1)% 量级的随机衍射涨落；当宿主宏观像形成于临界曲线附近或属于Type II宏观像时，该涨落可增强至 ≳10%。对LISA观测的大质量黑洞双星（massive black hole binaries），该衍射特征在ULDM粒子质量 m_φ ~ 10^{-21} eV 附近可探测。

Conclusion: ULDM干涉网络产生的频率依赖衍射涨落为引力波观测提供了潜在的“确凿证据”（smoking-gun signature），可用于证认超轻暗物质或对粒子质量 m_φ 给出新约束。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) propagate through the universe from sources at high redshift, experiencing the gravitational fields of the intervening matter distribution. Halos composed of ultralight dark matter (ULDM) contain strong density fluctuations, reflecting the interference pattern in the field distribution of the ultralight bosons. In the wave-optics regime, ULDM fluctuations act as a diffractive grating, imprinting frequency-dependent modulations of GWs, in addition to magnification and other gravitational lensing effects. Since lensing depends on the projected surface density, we derive its approximate probability distribution and show how it departs from a Gaussian distribution depending on the halo profile and coherence length. We then quantify how the GW diffraction signatures produced by the granules depend on macroimage magnification and parity. The ULDM interference network imprints stochastic diffraction fluctuations at the $O(1)\%$ level on GWs moderately lensed by the host when the Fresnel scale is comparable to the granule coherence length $r_F(f)\sim\ell_c$. These fluctuations can be enhanced to $\gtrsim10\%$ when the host macroimage forms near the critical curve or for Type II macroimages. For massive black hole binaries observed by LISA, the diffraction signature can be detectable around the ULDM particle mass $m_φ\sim10^{-21}$ eV, providing either a smoking-gun signature of ULDM or new constraints on $m_φ$.

</details>


### [140] [Reconstructing the mass-redshift distribution of supermassive black holes using multiband gravitational-wave observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24281)
*Alorika Kar,Souvik Jana,Enrico Barausse,Parameswaran Ajith*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文探讨多频段引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）观测对超大质量黑洞（supermassive black hole, SMBH）并合质量—红移分布的重构能力，采用非参数推断代码 FIGARO 对 LISA、LGWA 与下一代地面探测器模拟观测进行重建分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMBH 的形成与演化仍是天体物理学重大开放问题，高红移类星体与活动星系核观测虽可约束相关场景，但形成与增长机制仍不确定；未来毫赫兹、分赫兹与音频频段的引力波观测可提供互补且直接的探针。

Method: 模拟不同天体物理模型预言的黑洞并合族群，筛选可被毫赫兹探测器 LISA、分赫兹探测器 LGWA 及音频频段下一代地面探测器观测的事件，并使用非参数推断代码 FIGARO 重构其质量—红移分布。

Result: 对轻种子（light-seed）模型（并合族群跨越较宽质量与红移范围），跨频段联合观测显著改善重构效果；而对重种子（heavy-seed）模型，仅 LISA 即可恢复大部分可及的质量—红移分布，多频段网络的额外改进有限。

Conclusion: 多频段引力波观测对宽分布的轻种子模型重构增益明显，而重种子模型主要依赖 LISA 单探测器即可获得较完整信息，说明多频段联合的收益依赖于种子模型类型。

Abstract: The formation and evolution of supermassive black holes (SMBHs) remain major open questions in astrophysics. Astrophysical models of SMBHs invoke black-hole (BH) seeds formed at high redshift, which subsequently grow through gas accretion and mergers. Although observations of high-redshift quasars and active galactic nuclei constrain these scenarios, the mechanisms governing SMBH formation and growth remain uncertain. Future gravitational-wave (GW) observations across the millihertz, decihertz, and audio-frequency bands will provide a complementary and direct probe of SMBH assembly by tracing their merger history. We investigate the extent to which multiband GW observations can reconstruct the mass-redshift distribution of these mergers. We simulate populations of BH mergers predicted by different astrophysical models and identify those observable by the millihertz detector LISA, the decihertz detector LGWA, and next-generation ground-based detectors operating in the audio-frequency band. We reconstruct the mass-redshift distribution of these mergers using the nonparametric inference code FIGARO. We find that combining observations across frequency bands substantially improves the reconstruction for the light-seed models considered here, whose merger populations span broad ranges of mass and redshift. In contrast, for the heavy-seed models considered here, LISA alone recovers most of the accessible mass-redshift distribution, with little additional improvement from the multiband network.

</details>


### [141] [Cosmological First-Order Phase Transitions with Full Nonlinear Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24581)
*Campbell Benedix,Eve Currens,John T. Giblin,Chloe Richards*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该工作采用 BSSN 形式在完全非线性广义相对论（general relativity）框架下模拟宇宙学一阶相变（first-order phase transition），直接由无迹外曲率（trace-free extrinsic curvature）计算引力波谱，所得今日峰值振幅与线性微扰结果一致，并发现晚期标量场碎裂为振荡子（oscillons）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究长期将一阶相变产生的引力波（gravitational waves）视为 FLRW 背景上的线性微扰，非线性引力效应是否改变谱形尚不明确，故需在完全非线性广义相对论下进行检验。

Method: 使用 BSSN 形式模拟含标量场与相对论辐射流体（radiation fluid）的宇宙，聚焦气泡在视界尺度附近成核的 GUT 尺度相变；提出从类 Coleman–de Luccia 气泡剖面出发、通过调节气泡半径构造满足哈密顿与动量约束（Hamiltonian and momentum constraints）初值的方法。

Result: 由无迹外曲率直接算得今日峰值振幅 Ω_GW,0 h² ~ 5×10⁻¹¹，与未含非线性引力的先前结果相符；峰值频率依赖对称破缺能标（symmetry-breaking scale），可落在 LISA 频段至 GHz 范围。

Conclusion: 非线性引力未显著改变一阶相变引力波谱的峰值振幅；晚期标量场碎裂为长寿命振荡子（oscillons），使有效物态方程（equation of state）趋近物质主导。

Abstract: Cosmological first-order phase transitions can contribute to the stochastic gravitational wave background. For decades, these first-order phase transitions have been studied using a Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker background where the gravitational waves are treated as linear perturbations. Here, we present simulations of a first-order phase transition in the presence of fully nonlinear general relativity, using the BSSN formalism. We simulate a universe that contains a scalar field alongside a relativistic radiation fluid. We focus on GUT-scale transitions in which bubbles nucleate near the horizon size. We describe a method to construct initial data that satisfies the Hamiltonian and momentum constraints, starting from a Coleman--de~Luccia-like bubble profile and adjusting the bubble radius. We compute the gravitational wave spectrum directly from the trace-free extrinsic curvature and find a present-day peak amplitude of $Ω_{\rm GW,0}h^2 \sim 5\times 10^{-11}$, consistent with earlier results that do not include nonlinear gravity. The peak frequency depends on the symmetry-breaking scale and could fall anywhere from the LISA band to GHz frequencies. At late times, the field fragments into long-lived oscillons, which push its effective equation of state toward that of matter.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [From Powers--Stormer to Loewner Inertia: Weighted Trace Functionals and Orthogonal Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23814)
*Trung Hoa Dinh,Cong Trinh Le,Minh Toan Ho*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究加权迹泛函（weighted trace functional）$\TT_{f,h}(A,B)=\Tr h^\circ(A-B)[f(A)-f(B)]$，借助$A-B$的Jordan分解（Jordan decomposition）给出算子单调与算子凸函数的精确公屋顶分解，并刻画Powers–Størmer与Ricard迹比较的等号条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典迹比较（trace comparison）与Löwner矩阵判据隐含正交交叉项信息，但缺乏统一的分解框架与显式等号条件；本文旨在通过加权迹泛函的结构分析补足这一缺口。

Method: 利用$A-B$的Jordan分解导出公屋顶分解（common-roof decomposition），把泛函拆为对角贡献与正交交叉项；对幂函数借助预解式平方（resolvent squares）在Mellin条带（Mellin strip）内分析谱相互作用，并用合流行列式（confluent determinant）计算补充惯性分类所需的符号数据。

Result: 正交交叉项给出算子凸性（operator convexity）的经典条件Löwner矩阵判据的有限秩一实现；幂情形得到依赖耦合的Mellin条带、端点恒等式与离扇区谱相互作用的定量界；线性化产生幂Löwner矩阵的正向与逆向矩子空间，并给出显式等号条件及Powers–Størmer等号情形的有限副本应用。

Conclusion: 该框架将迹比较、Löwner判据与幂Löwner矩阵的惯性分类统一于公屋顶分解之下，并提供可量化的误差界与等号刻画。

Abstract: We study the weighted trace functional $\TT_{f,h}(A,B)=\Tr h^\circ(A-B)[f(A)-f(B)]$, where $h^\circ(H)=h(H_+)-h(H_-)$. The Jordan decomposition of $A-B$ yields exact common-roof decompositions for operator monotone and operator convex functions, including the Powers--Størmer and Ricard trace comparisons. The orthogonal cross term gives a finite rank-one realization of the classical conditional Löwner-matrix criterion for operator convexity. For powers, resolvent squares yield a coupling-dependent Mellin strip, endpoint identities, and quantitative bounds on off-sector spectral interactions. Linearization produces positive and inverse moment subspaces of power Löwner matrices. A confluent determinant calculation provides the remaining sign data in the known inertia classification. We give explicit equality conditions and a finite-copy application of the Powers--Størmer equality case.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Topological signatures of criticality: the Ising case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23586)
*Tak-Shing Chan,Paweł Dłotko,Jacek Gulgowski,Biagio Lucini,Mateusz Masłowski,Davide Vadacchino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过将伊辛模型（Ising model）的正负自旋畴（spin domains）关联到胞腔复形（cell complexes），构造由二者欧拉示性数（Euler characteristic）之差定义的拓扑观测量，并证明其在铁磁相变附近呈序参量式行为，可用来定位相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学观测量之外，微观构型的拓扑性质可能为相变研究提供额外信息；作者希望在二维伊辛模型中检验这一想法，并寻求在无法构造序参量时仍可用的几何化观测量。

Method: 在正方与三角晶格上，把正、负自旋畴分别与胞腔复形对应，取其欧拉示性数之差构成自旋奇（spin-odd）拓扑观测量；推导该量与常规观测量的关系以理解其物理行为，并用蒙特卡罗模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）结合多直方图重加权（multihistogram reweighting）及有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析。

Result: 该拓扑观测量在铁磁转变附近表现类似序参量；其磁化率（susceptibility）的有限尺寸标度给出的赝临界温度（pseudocritical temperature）在两种晶格几何下均收敛到已知临界温度。正方晶格上临界指数（critical exponent）的首阶估计对拟合区间依赖强于磁化估计，与更强的次领头有限尺寸修正一致；三角晶格上估计与理论伊辛值相符。此外证明任意有限正温度下欧拉示性数期望超过与晶格尺寸无关的正常数下界，从而否证此前的猜想。

Conclusion: 拓扑定义的量（如欧拉示性数）可为本文所考察系统提供定位有序—无序相变的替代途径；由于其定义纯属几何，在无法构造序参量的情形下亦可能提供有用洞见。

Abstract: Topological properties of microscopic configurations may provide additional insight into phase transitions beyond conventional thermodynamic observables. We investigate this idea in the two-dimensional Ising model on square and triangular lattices by associating cell complexes with the positive and negative spin domains and constructing an Euler-characteristic observable from their difference. We provide the expression relating this quantity to conventional observables of the Ising model. This derivation allows us to understand its expected behavior physically. As a byproduct, we show that at any finite positive temperature the expectation of the Euler characteristic exceeds a positive, lattice-size-independent bound, hence disproving a previously suggested conjecture. Monte Carlo simulations combined with multihistogram reweighting show that this spin-odd topological observable exhibits order-parameter-like behavior near the ferromagnetic transition. Finite-size scaling of its susceptibility yields pseudocritical temperatures that converge to the known critical temperature for both lattice geometries. On the square lattice, the leading-order estimate of the critical exponent obtained from the topological observable depends more strongly on the fitting range than the magnetization estimate. This behavior is consistent with stronger subleading finite-size corrections, as supported by fits including an additional correction term. On the triangular lattice, the corresponding estimate remains consistent with the theoretical Ising value. Our study suggests that topologically defined quantities such as the Euler characteristic can provide an alternative way to locate order-disorder phase transitions in the systems considered here. Since the definition of these observables is purely geometrical, they can provide useful insights in cases in which order parameters cannot be constructed.

</details>


### [144] [Persistent Hydration and Ionic Mobility: When the Ion Is Not the Only Transported Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23605)
*Biman Bagchi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个分子框架，将离子、其第一水合壳层与外部溶剂视为动态耦合的组分，以解释强水合离子在水中输运的异常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有百余年理论研究，小离子在水中的输运仍未被充分理解；对Li⁺、Mg²⁺、Ca²⁺等强水合离子尤为困难，因其第一水合壳层的寿命可与平动运动时间尺度相当甚至更长。

Method: 构建将离子、第一水合壳层与外部溶剂作为动态耦合组分的分子框架：用平均力势（potential of mean force）与配位动力学描述局部水合结构与交换，用分子记忆函数（molecular memory functions）描述溶剂的集体响应。

Result: 该理论阐明了水合效应通常无法用可加性摩擦（additive friction）或单一放大的半径（enlarged radius）来表征。

Conclusion: 该框架为超越传统连续介质（continuum）与分子描述提供了新路径。

Abstract: Small-ion transport in water remains only partially understood despite more than a century of theoretical work. The difficulty is especially acute for strongly hydrated ions such as Li$^{+}$, Mg$^{2+}$, and Ca$^{2+}$, whose first hydration shells can persist on timescales comparable to or longer than translational motion. We develop a molecular framework in which the ion, its first hydration shell, and the outer solvent are treated as dynamically coupled components. Local hydration structure and exchange are represented through a potential of mean force and coordination dynamics, while collective solvent response is described through molecular memory functions. The theory clarifies why hydration cannot generally be represented by an additive friction or a single enlarged radius, and provides a route beyond conventional continuum and molecular descriptions.

</details>


### [145] [Finite-time stochastic resetting in bounded domains: competing targets, energetic cost, and optimal search strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23790)
*Hanshuang Chen,Chongyao Tang,Guofeng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究有界一维区间（含两个吸收边界）中布朗粒子在有限时间随机重置（finite-time stochastic resetting）下的首达动力学与能量代价，通过求解后向方程给出平均全局首达时间与分裂概率的解析式，并推导总功生成函数与平均功的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机重置（stochastic resetting）多假设瞬时返回重置位置，而实际返回过程需要有限时长；本文旨在刻画有限时长返回过程（由线性陷阱势驱动）如何改变首达时间对重置率的依赖，并量化其能量成本与搜索效率之间的权衡。

Method: 在带两个吸收边界的一维有界域中，将粒子通过线性陷阱势（linear trapping potential）在有限时间内拉回指定重置位置；求解相应的后向方程（backward equations）得到平均全局首达时间（mean global first-passage time）与分裂概率（splitting probabilities）的解析表达式，并推导总功生成函数（generating function of the total work）以获得平均功的解析式。

Result: 有限返回时长可显著改变平均全局首达时间对重置率的依赖；存在一个临界陷阱强度（critical trapping strength），超过该强度时重置可加速搜索过程，且有利重置位置的范围随陷阱强度变化。对于线性陷阱势，平均总功与平均累积返回时间之间存在精确关系，据此可建立首达时间与能量消耗之间的定量权衡。

Conclusion: 有限时间重置下的搜索效率受陷阱强度与重置位置范围共同调控，且首达时间与能量代价之间存在可解析刻画的权衡关系；布朗动力学模拟（Brownian dynamics simulations）验证了理论预测。

Abstract: We investigate the first-passage dynamics and energetic cost of a Brownian particle subject to finite-time stochastic resetting in a bounded one-dimensional domain with two absorbing boundaries. Unlike conventional instantaneous resetting, the particle is returned to a prescribed resetting position through a finite-duration process driven by a linear trapping potential. By solving the corresponding backward equations, we derive analytical expressions for the mean global first-passage time and the splitting probabilities. Our results show that the finite duration of the return process can substantially modify the dependence of the mean global first-passage time on the resetting rate. In particular, we identify a critical trapping strength above which resetting can accelerate the search process, and characterize how the range of favorable resetting positions depends on the trapping strength. We further characterize the energetic cost of resetting by deriving the generating function of the total work and obtaining an analytical expression for its mean value. For the linear trapping potential considered here, we establish an exact relation between the mean total work and the mean cumulative return time. This relation allows us to establish a quantitative trade-off between the mean global first-passage time and the energetic expenditure, revealing how the finite duration of the return process affects search efficiency. Brownian dynamics simulations confirm our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [146] [Numerical integration of non-local master equations for renewal shot noise with drift and state-dependent amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24390)
*Marco Bianucci,Riccardo Mannella*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一种高精度数值方法，用于求解由漂移（drift）与状态依赖振幅的更新踢序列（renewal train of kicks）驱动的系统所满足的主方程（master equation）；该方程在空间与时间上均非局部（non-local）。方法被验证至密度约 10⁻³⁷ 的量级，并用于评估局部近似与泊松近似（Poissonian approximation）的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类主方程因踢算符（kick operator）为无穷阶而在空间上非局部，非指数等待时间（waiting times）下又因含漂移传播子（propagator）的记忆核（memory kernel）而在时间上非局部，且解中含准奇异（quasi-singular）的“从未被踢质量”（never-kicked mass），使常规数值方法难以兼顾精度与效率。作者据此需要一个准确且成本可控的数值方案。

Method: 踢算符被视为沿 Marcus 流（Marcus flow）的前推（push-forward），在 Lamperti 变量下化为平移，其格间权重（cell-to-cell weights）可由踢的累积分布（cumulative distribution）精确得到而无需数值积分；漂移用时间精确的保守半拉格朗日步（semi-Lagrangian step）积分；记忆以指数和表示，辅助场随漂移平流（advected），代价随步数线性增长；“从未被踢质量”被精确输运。文中还对每个环节量化了更简单替代方案的误差。

Result: 该方法与精确解及使用精确流映射（flow maps）的事件驱动蒙特卡洛（event-driven Monte Carlo）模拟相一致，在 10⁸ 条轨迹下统计上不可区分；为高斯踢（Gaussian kicks）调好的参数可原样用于其他踢分布；在耦合零点附近，解在低至 10⁻³⁷ 的密度下仍遵循已知渐近律，远超蒙特卡洛可达范围。作为应用，与局部近似及泊松近似的比较显示局部方程偏差可达密度宽度的 8%，即使等待时间均值有限亦然。

Conclusion: 所提数值方法在空间、时间非局部主方程上兼具高精度与线性成本，并可扩展到任意踢分布。局部近似的偏差与误差估计一致，即慢漂移、长时情形下偏差更大；作者提供了 Fortran 实现。

Abstract: The probability density of a system driven by a drift and a renewal train of kicks with state-dependent amplitude obeys a master equation that is non-local in space, because the kick operator $\hat{p}(i\partial_x I(x))$ is of infinite order, and, for non-exponential waiting times, non-local in time, with a memory kernel containing the propagator of the drift. Its solutions contain quasi-singular components, the never-kicked mass, which collapse onto the attractors of the drift. We present an accurate numerical method for such equations. The kick operator is a push-forward along the Marcus flow; in the Lamperti variable it becomes a translation, whose exact cell-to-cell weights follow from the cumulative distribution of the kicks, without quadrature. The drift is integrated by a conservative semi-Lagrangian step exact in time. The memory is represented by a sum of exponentials whose auxiliary fields are advected by the drift, at a cost linear in the number of steps. The never-kicked mass is transported exactly. For each ingredient we quantify the errors of simpler alternatives. The method is verified against exact solutions and event-driven Monte Carlo simulations with exact flow maps, from which it is statistically indistinguishable with $10^8$ trajectories. Parameters tuned for Gaussian kicks carry over unchanged to other kick distributions, and near a zero of the coupling, where the density vanishes faster than any power, the solution follows the known asymptotic law down to densities of order $10^{-37}$, far beyond Monte Carlo reach. As an application we compare the exact equation with its local and Poissonian approximations: in the range explored, the local equation deviates by up to 8% of the density width, also for waiting times with finite mean, consistently with an error estimate predicting larger deviations for slow drifts at long times. A Fortran implementation is available.

</details>


### [147] [Blanket theory for dense networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23960)
*Simone Franchini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出利用 Blanket theory（毯式理论）在稠密自旋网络（dense spin networks）中构造性地计算腔泛函（cavity functionals），并借助 Aizenman-Sims-Starr 随机重叠结构（random overlap structure）与递归分数均值（recursive fractional means）给出显式自由能泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为稠密自旋网络中的腔泛函提供一种构造性计算方法，并解释线性能量下随机能量模型（random energy model, REM）普适性的机制，以及层级化能量与 Gibbs 权重（Gibbs weights）的组织方式。

Method: 从基态附近的低温涨落（low-temperature fluctuations）出发，通过界面与磁化壳层（interfaces and magnetization shells）描述稀薄缺陷（dilute defects）；利用其在层间的共享与更新形成层级结构，将新鲜腔场（fresh cavity fields）的协方差与 Aizenman-Sims-Starr 随机重叠结构对应，并以递归分数均值评估腔贡献。

Result: 得到以层磁化（layer magnetizations）与层级指数（hierarchical exponents）为参数、系数由相互作用方差与网络归一化决定的显式自由能泛函；在给定矩与归一化条件下，通过压强比较（comparison of pressures）把极限平均压强从高斯代表模型迁移到具有相同方差的独立中心化非高斯耦合网络。

Conclusion: 方法以确定性 Curie-Weiss 模型示例说明，重现 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 模型的 Parisi 泛函，并导出受限与深度玻尔兹曼机（restricted and deep Boltzmann machines）的泛函，包括全复本对称破缺（full replica symmetry breaking, fullRSB）极限。

Abstract: We show how Blanket theory provides a constructive method for calculating cavity functionals in dense spin networks. Building on the description through interfaces and magnetization shells, we develop the calculation from the low-temperature fluctuations around the ground state. In this regime, dilute defects reveal the mechanism of random energy model (REM) universality for linear energies, while their sharing and renewal across layers organize the energies and Gibbs weights into a hierarchy. Because the same configurations determine the covariances of the fresh cavity fields, this construction identifies the fields and weights with an Aizenman-Sims-Starr random overlap structure. The cavity contributions can then be evaluated through recursive fractional means, yielding an explicit free-energy functional parametrized by layer magnetizations and hierarchical exponents, with coefficients determined by the interaction variances and the network normalization. Under the stated moment and normalization conditions, comparison of pressures transfers the limiting mean pressure from the Gaussian representative to networks with independent centered non-Gaussian couplings having the same variances. We illustrate the method with the deterministic Curie-Weiss model, recover the Parisi functional for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model, and derive the functionals for restricted and deep Boltzmann machines, including the full replica symmetry breaking (fullRSB) limit.

</details>


### [148] [Geometric Decomposition of Entropy Production Based on General $f$-Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24024)
*Rihito Nagase,Shoki Sugimoto,Takahiro Sagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文针对过阻尼朗之万系统，基于 $f$-散度（$f$-divergence）构造了一类一般的熵产生（entropy production）的过剩-管家（excess–housekeeping）分解，并将 Hatano–Sasa 与 Maes–Netočný 分解作为特例纳入统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Hatano–Sasa 与 Maes–Netočný 等熵产生分解在松弛（relaxational）与最优输运（optimal transport）方面各有侧重，缺乏统一结构；本文旨在建立更一般的分解框架以统一并推广这些结果。

Method: 在过阻尼朗之万（overdamped Langevin）系统下，以 $f$-散度构造过剩熵产生率（excess entropy production rate），使其等于 $f$-散度向松弛目标（relaxation target）的松弛速率，并给出其最优输运表示；同时要求管家速度（housekeeping velocity）保持相同的松弛目标。

Result: 所构造的框架可将 Hatano–Sasa 与 Maes–Netočný 分解作为特例恢复，并在统一结构下整合二者的松弛与最优输运方面；此外，通过选取期望分布作为松弛目标，可得到超越这两种分解的、具有物理意义的分解。

Conclusion: 基于 $f$-散度的过剩-管家分解为熵产生分解提供了统一且可推广的构造方式，其松弛目标的选择决定了分解的物理含义，并允许导出新的有意义的分解形式。

Abstract: We construct a general class of excess--housekeeping decompositions of entropy production based on $f$-divergences for overdamped Langevin systems. The excess entropy production rate equals the relaxation rate of the $f$-divergence toward a relaxation target and also admits an optimal-transport representation, while the housekeeping velocity preserves the same relaxation target. Our framework recovers the Hatano--Sasa and Maes--Neto{č}n{ý} decompositions as special cases, and unifies their relaxational and optimal-transport aspects within a common structure. Moreover, our framework yields physically meaningful decompositions beyond these two by taking a desired distribution as the relaxation target.

</details>


### [149] [Neither graphs nor hypergraphs: structural moments of higher-order interactions determine stochastic social-impact dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24252)
*Emanuele Cozzo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究超图上二元状态随机动力学中，高阶交互何时可被约化为更简单的结构描述。作者证明：对于q次多项式的响应函数（response function），动力学仅由至多涉及q个邻居的“结构矩（structural moments）”决定，且这些矩是充分必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶交互（higher-order interactions）能否被约化为简单表示，这一问题不仅关乎结构本身，更取决于其驱动的动力学：凡动力学"看不到"的结构信息皆可丢弃。作者希望将这一直觉精确化，明确哪些群体交互过程的统计量真正决定了马尔可夫动力学（Markov dynamics）。

Method: 研究对象为二元状态随机动力学：每个个体被更新时与随机选取的群体交互，并依据持不同意见成员的比例改变状态，涵盖超图（hypergraph）上的广义投票者模型（generalized voter model）与社会影响模型（social-impact model）。作者引入一类规模加权的包含矩（size-weighted inclusion moments），称为结构矩，用以刻画决定动力学的群体交互统计量，并论证其充分性与必要性。

Result: 对q次多项式响应函数，决定动力学的是至多同时涉及q个邻居的结构矩层级，与群体规模无关。该矩集既充分又必要：两种交互结构对所有此类规则产生相同动力学，当且仅当其结构矩相等。此外，每类规则都对应各自的最小结构描述，从线性规则下的加权成对连边（weighted pairwise links），到任意规则下按群体规模划分的共同成员分布（co-membership distribution）。对投票者型规则，这些矩还能精确重构该动力学的成对复制表示（pairwise copying representation）。

Conclusion: 高阶交互的可约化性由动力学规则决定：不同规则类别选出各自的最小结构描述，结构矩为此提供了充分必要且与群体规模无关的刻画，并为投票者型动力学给出精确的成对表示。该框架为超图动力学的模型约化与表示选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Whether higher-order interactions can be reduced to simpler representations is a question about the dynamics they drive, not only about their structure: what can be discarded is whatever the dynamics does not see. We make this precise for binary-state stochastic dynamics in which each agent, when updated, interacts with a randomly selected group and changes state according to the fraction of disagreeing members, as in generalized voter and social-impact models on hypergraphs. We ask which statistics of the group-interaction process determine the resulting Markov dynamics. For response functions that are polynomials of degree $q$, the answer is a hierarchy of size-weighted inclusion moments, which we call structural moments, involving at most $q$ neighbors at a time, regardless of how large the groups are. These moments are not only sufficient but also necessary: two interaction structures produce identical dynamics for all such rules if and only if their moments coincide. More generally, each class of rules selects its own minimal structural description, ranging from weighted pairwise links for linear rules to the full distribution of co-membership by group size for arbitrary rules. For voter-type rules, the same moments also reconstruct exact pairwise copying representations of these dynamics.

</details>


### [150] [Phase transitions in work fluctuations in correlated N-particle resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24318)
*Felix Herber,Deepak Gupta,Supriya Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究N个粒子在外部势同时切换（随机或周期）下、从任意关联初态出发时热力学功（thermodynamic work）的分布，并在包含粒子数、初态关联与约化单粒子分布的参数空间中刻画功方差的“相”结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注共同环境随机变化导致的粒子间位置关联（interparticle positional correlations）动态涌现，但其热力学代价（thermodynamic cost）及与初始分布的相互作用尚未被研究。

Method: 考虑N个独立无关联粒子同时经历外部势（external potential）切换（随机或周期性），初态可为任意关联态；在包含N、任意初始粒子间关联与任意初始约化单粒子分布的完整参数空间中，映射热力学功的分布。

Result: 参数空间被非平凡地、且依赖于N地划分为不同“相”（phases）；功的方差（variance of the work）的函数形式在相之间发生不连续变化，且相的数目取决于动力学中随机性的来源。

Conclusion: 共同环境切换下粒子系统热力学功分布呈现依赖于N的相结构，表明关联生成的热力学代价与初始分布之间存在非平凡的耦合，且相数目由随机性来源决定。

Abstract: Systems of $N$ independent uncorrelated particles subjected to the same stochastic change in the environment have recently been studied both theoretically and in experiments, to elucidate the interesting phenomenon of dynamically emergent interparticle positional correlations. However, the thermodynamic cost of creating such correlations, as well as its interplay with the initial distribution of the $N$ particles has not been studied. In this paper, we study systems of $N$ particles that are simultaneously subjected to a switch of an external potential, either stochastically or periodically, beginning from arbitrarily correlated initial states. We map out the distribution of thermodynamic work for such a system in the full parameter space of the problem that includes the number of particles $N$, arbitrary initial correlations between particles and arbitrary initial reduced single particle distributions. We demonstrate that the parameter space is partitioned non-trivially, and in an $N$-dependent way, into distinct "phases", whereby the functional form of the variance of the work changes discontinuously across phases. The number of such phases depends on the source of stochasticity in the dynamics.

</details>


### [151] [A Monte Carlo study of closely packed two-patch particles with frustrated interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24560)
*Rui Ding,Yan-Wei Li,Hao Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作指出，在具有排斥性补丁-补丁（patch-patch）、吸引性补丁-非补丁（patch-nonpatch）及中等强度非补丁-非补丁（nonpatch-nonpatch）相互作用时，密堆二补丁颗粒（two-patch particles）体系存在阻挫（frustration）；结合新设计的单缺陷传播（single-defect propagation）马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗（Markov Chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）算法求得基态为无序态，关联函数随距离指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究发现密堆二补丁颗粒表现出丰富的取向有序行为（如向列序参量的非单调变化与双重 BKT 相变），本工作旨在探究该体系中由相互作用竞争所导致的阻挫及其对基态有序性的影响。

Method: 设定排斥性 patch-patch、吸引性 patch-nonpatch 与中等 nonpatch-nonpatch 相互作用以引入阻挫，并设计带单缺陷传播的马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗（MCMC）算法用于基态搜索。

Result: 模拟结果显示基态为无序态（disordered），且关联函数（correlation functions）随距离呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 结果表明二维密堆（2D close packing）体系可通过设计补丁颗粒（patchy particles）来构建和研究取向无序体系。

Abstract: Recently it was found that closely packed two-patch particles exhibit rich orientational ordering behaviors, e.g., nonmonotonic changes of the nematic order parameter and double BKT phase transitions [arXiv:2509.03981]. In this work, we show that, with repulsive patch-patch, attractive patch-nonpatch and intermediate nonpatch-nonpatch interactions, there exist frustrations in the system of closely packed two-patch particles. To investigate the ground states of the system, we design a Markov Chain Monte Carlo algorithm with single-defect propagation. The simulation results show that the ground states are disordered and the correlation functions decay exponentially with the distance. These results demonstrate that in 2D close packing one could design patchy particles to explore orientationally disordered systems.

</details>


### [152] [When Time-Reversible Sensor Records Require Hidden Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24898)
*Andrey V. Dobrynin,Anastasia Stroujkova*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 二元传感器记录即使时间可逆，也可能在所有兼容的有限状态马尔可夫模型（finite-state Markov model）中都需要耗散。四项态传感器给出精确平衡判据与熵产生（entropy production）正下界，并有显式构造给出最小耗散上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究时间可逆（time reversible）的观测记录是否意味着底层过程可无耗散实现，即观测层面的可逆性（reversibility）与隐变量模型耗散需求之间的张力。

Method: 对具有相关驻留时间（correlated dwell times）的四状态传感器，利用两个可观测的切换统计量（switching statistics）建立精确的平衡判据；在排除平衡后，推导与隐状态数无关的熵产生正下界，并构造显式四状态模型给出最小耗散的上界；对含噪泊松传感器（noisy Poisson sensor）比较上下界。

Result: 得到精确平衡判据及不依赖隐状态数的熵产生正下界；显式四状态构造给出最小耗散上界；对含噪泊松传感器，上下界相差不到两倍，且在某一动力学匹配点（kinetic matching point）处界趋零而传感器仍具耗散性。

Conclusion: 观测可逆性不足以排除耗散，耗散可由可观测切换统计量界定；所得界在含噪泊松情形下较紧，并揭示界趋零与真实耗散并存的现象，提示仅凭此类界不能判定无耗散。

Abstract: A binary sensor record can be time reversible yet require dissipation in every compatible finite-state Markov model. For a four-state sensor with correlated dwell times, two observed switching statistics give an exact equilibrium criterion and, when equilibrium is excluded, a positive lower bound on entropy production independent of hidden-state number. An explicit four-state construction provides an upper bound on the minimum dissipation. For a noisy Poisson sensor, the bounds differ by less than a factor of two and vanish at a kinetic matching point while the sensor remains dissipative.

</details>

### [153] [Kinetic theory of dry granular particles under simple shear: steady flows and relaxation dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23635)
*Shunsuke Iizuka,Satoshi Takada,Hisao Hayakawa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文基于Enskog近似与Grad矩方法，构建了中等密度干颗粒气体在简单剪切流下的动理学理论，保留剪切率参数的各阶项并给出稳态与松弛预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对体积分数φ≤0.5的中等密度干颗粒气体，需要在简单剪切流下更准确地描述动能应力与碰撞应力，并改进各向异性高斯理论在动能温度等方面的预测不足。

Method: 采用Enskog近似（Enskog approximation）结合Grad矩方法（Grad's moment method），将碰撞矩与应力表示为广义超几何函数（generalized hypergeometric functions），在所采用的Grad封闭内保留无量纲剪切率参数的各阶项，推导动能应力与应力张量碰撞贡献的完整方程组。

Result: 稳态解在较宽参数范围内复现剪切黏度（shear viscosity），并在所考察参数值下相对各向异性高斯理论（anisotropic Gaussian theory）改进了动能温度预测；强非弹性条件下温度仍有明显偏差。理论能捕捉法向应力差（normal stress differences）的总体趋势，其定量精度依赖密度与恢复系数（restitution coefficient）。对φ由0.35瞬时降至0.20、恢复系数0.90的松弛过程，理论复现了温度与黏度的松弛行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的Enskog–Grad动理学框架可统一描述中等密度干颗粒气体在简单剪切下的稳态输运与密度跃变后的松弛，但在强非弹性区温度预测仍待改进。

Abstract: We develop a kinetic theory for dry granular particles under simple shear flow using the Enskog approximation combined with Grad's moment method for moderately dense granular gases with the volume fraction $\varphi\le 0.5$. The collisional moments and stresses are expressed in terms of generalized hypergeometric functions, retaining all orders in the dimensionless shear-rate parameter within the adopted Grad closure. Within this framework, we derive a complete set of equations governing the kinetic stress and the collisional contribution to the stress tensor. The steady-state solutions reproduce the shear viscosity over a broad parameter range and improve the predictions of the kinetic temperature relative to the anisotropic Gaussian theory at the parameter values examined. Temperature still shows appreciable deviations under strongly inelastic conditions. Additionally, the theory captures the overall trends of the normal stress differences, while their quantitative accuracy depends on density and restitution coefficient. We further examine relaxation following an instantaneous reduction in $\varphi$ from $0.35$ to $0.20$ at a restitution coefficient of $0.90$. The theory reproduces the temperature and viscosity relaxation for this protocol.

</details>

### [154] [Hydrodynamics of robotic micro-swimmers in confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24631)
*Giovanni Corsi,Giovanni Noselli,Antonio DeSimone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受Purcell经典工作启发，该研究结合数值流体求解、厘米级样机实验与流体力学标度分析，评估受限环境中自主游动机器人的性能与可微型化潜力，认为流体动力学未对人类体内狭窄腔道中的微型机器人构成根本障碍，但为不同尺度下的理性设计提供严格准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对自主机器人（autonomous robots）在受限空间（confinement）中游动时性能如何预测与优化的问题，并检验毫米及亚毫米尺度微型机器人（micro-robots）在狭窄血管/腔道（narrow vessels/lumens）中航行的可行性。

Method: 基于对游动体所诱发流场（fluid flow）的显式数值求解（numerical resolution），并用由载货头部与旋转推进尾部构成的厘米级原型（cm-size prototype）进行实验验证；进一步借助流体力学标度（hydrodynamic scaling）外推至毫米与亚毫米尺度复制体。

Result: 理论、计算与实验分析一致表明：流体动力学似乎并未对人类体内狭窄腔道中自主微型机器人这一设想构成根本性障碍（no fundamental obstruction），但会给出在不同长度尺度（length scales）下进行理性设计（rational design）所需的有用且严格的指导准则。

Conclusion: 受限游动的性能可通过数值流场求解加标度分析进行预测与优化；微型化在流体力学上可行，但设计需遵循随尺度变化的严格约束，标度分析可作为跨尺度设计工具。

Abstract: Inspired by seminal work by E.M. Purcell, we discuss the problem of predicting and optimizing the performance of autonomous robots swimming in confinement. Our approach is based on the explicit numerical resolution of the fluid flow induced by the swimmer, and it is validated experimentally on a cm-size prototype composed of a cargo head and a rotating propulsive tail. Using hydrodynamic scaling, we predict the performance of replicas of our prototype scaled down to mm- and sub-mm-size to test the feasibility of autonomous micro-robots navigating in narrow vessels. The main result of our theoretical, computational, and experimental analysis is that, while hydrodynamics seems to place no fundamental obstruction to the vision of autonomous micro-robots navigating in narrow lumens inside the human body, it does provide useful and strict guidelines for rational design at different length scales.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Layer-dependent $s^{\pm}$ superconducting pairing and influence of incipient bands in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ thin films on real LaSrAlO$_4$ substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23595)
*Yang Zhang,Philip M. Dee,Ling-Fang Lin,Adriana Moreo,Thomas A. Maier,Elbio Dagotto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过显式纳入 LaSrAlO₄ 衬底对常压超导 La₃Ni₂O₇ 薄膜进行理论研究，揭示其费米面拓扑接近高压体相，且对称性允许的电荷歧化使三层双层呈现分层超导配对特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期常压超导 La₃Ni₂O₇（LNO）薄膜实验进展激发了理论探究需求，而以往研究多忽略真实衬底效应，因此需要显式引入 LaSrAlO₄（LSAO）衬底以厘清薄膜的电子结构与配对机制。

Method: 构建包含真实 LSAO 衬底的 LNO 薄膜模型；采用弱耦合随机相位近似（Random Phase Approximation, RPA）分析配对不稳定性，并借助超越弱耦合的动力学团簇近似（Dynamical Cluster Approximation, DCA）纳入初生 γ 带。

Result: 费米面拓扑与高压体相相近（含 α、β 面及源于表面双层 BL3 的小空穴型 γ 口袋）；界面双层 BL1 的成键 d₃z²₋r² 态仅低于费米能约 5 meV，形成初生 γ 带，BL2 中则低至约 95 meV；未发现衬底向薄膜的显著自发电荷转移，取而代之的是 BL 间对称性允许的电荷歧化（charge disproportionation），BL3 空穴掺杂、BL1 与 BL2 电子掺杂。RPA 给出 BL3 中强 s± 波配对不稳定性，BL1 与 BL2 中 d_{x²−y²} 波与 s± 波近乎简并；DCA 纳入初生 γ 带后，三层均出现稳健的 s± 波配对。

Conclusion: 结果表明 LNO 薄膜的超导配对以 s± 波为主导，且初生 γ 带对实现三层普适的 s± 波配对具有关键作用，衬底效应与层间电荷歧化共同塑造了薄膜的电子结构和配对行为。

Abstract: Motivated by recent advances in ambient-pressure superconducting La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ (LNO) thin films, we theoretically investigate these films by explicitly incorporating the realistic LaSrAlO$_4$ (LSAO) substrate. The Fermi-surface topology of the nickelate thin film closely resembles that of the high-pressure bulk phase, with similar $α$ and $β$ sheets and a small hole-like $γ$ pocket originating from the surface bilayer (BL3). The bonding $d_{3z^2-r^2}$ state of the interfacial bilayer (BL1, adjacent to the LSAO substrate) lies only $\sim 5$ meV below the Fermi level, giving rise to an incipient $γ$ band, while for the middle bilayer (BL2) its energy is much lower ($\sim 95$ meV). Remarkably, no significant spontaneous charge transfer from the LSAO substrate to the LNO film is found. Instead, {\it symmetry-allowed charge disproportionation} occurs between the BLs, with hole doping in BL3 and electron doping in BL1 and BL2. Via the weak-coupling Random Phase Approximation, we find a strong $s^{\pm}$-wave superconducting pairing instability in BL3, while $d_{x^2-y^2}$ and $s^{\pm}$ are nearly degenerate in the other bilayers BL1 and BL2. Including the incipient $γ$ band via the Dynamical Cluster Approximation beyond weak coupling, robust $s^{\pm}$-wave pairing is observed in all three layers

</details>


### [156] [Reconciling Models for Infinite-Layer Nickelate Superconductors: Role of Interstitial-$s$ Band or $A$-Pocket](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24288)
*Ao Zhang,Chao Deng,Leonard M. Verhoff,Mi Jiang,Run Yang,Zhixiang Shi,Karsten Held,Liang Si*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文利用第一性原理密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT），系统比较了LaNiO$_2$从单带至6带等多种有效模型的强关联电子结构与光学谱，指出无限层镍酸盐超导性可在单带Hubbard框架下可靠描述，而输运等轨道间性质需至少纳入间隙s带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐（infinite-layer nickelate）超导体的最小有效模型尚不明确，即究竟需包含哪些轨道才能刻画其本质物理，是当前的核心挑战。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT），计算并比较LaNiO$_2$的多种有效模型——从仅含$d_{x^2-y^2}$的1带模型，到包含全部Ni-$3d$及间隙s轨道的完整6带模型——的强关联电子结构与光学谱（optical spectra）。

Result: Ni-$3d$ 5带模型高估了Hundness图像；而显式包含间隙s带并具有A口袋（A-pocket）的6带模型能恢复特征的Mottness物理。间隙s带对$d_{x^2-y^2}$带关联效应的影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 无限层镍酸盐的超导性可在单带Hubbard框架内可靠描述；但轨道间性质（如光学电导率）的准确刻画至少需纳入间隙s带。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal model that captures the essential physics of infinite-layer nickelate superconductors remains a central challenge. Using first-principles density-functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT), we compute and compare the strongly correlated electronic structures and optical spectra of various effective models for the infinite-layer nickelate LaNiO$_2$, ranging from a $d_{x^2-y^2}$ 1-band model to a full 6-band model with all Ni-$3d$ plus an interstitial-$s$ orbital. The results demonstrate that Ni-$3d$ 5-band models overestimate the Hundness picture, whereas 6-band models, which explicitly include the interstitial-$s$ band and have an $A$-pocket, recover the characteristic Mottness physics. Importantly, the influence of the interstitial-$s$ band on the correlations of the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ band is negligible. Thus, superconductivity in infinite-layer nickelates can be reliably described within a single-band Hubbard framework, while the accurate description of inter-orbital properties such as (optical) conductivities requires the inclusion of at least the interstitial-$s$ band.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Where the Tokens Go: Heat, Entropy and Error in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23375)
*Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Varinia Bernales*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“软件热力学（software thermodynamics）”理论，将代码智能体（coding agent）生成的 token 视为成本，并拆分为功（work）与热（heat），通过“编码—解码”循环实验定义软件温度并以类似 Jarzynski 等式的思路估计自由能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体按 token 计费，但软件工程领域缺乏对“一个 token 究竟买到了什么”的理论刻画，因此需要引入热力学类比来量化编码过程的成本结构。

Method: 借鉴经典与随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）并加以扩展：把测试套件视为软件版麦克斯韦妖（Maxwell's daemon）判定每次修改；仿照单分子拉伸—回折实验，让智能体对仓库反复执行修改（coding）与撤销（uncoding），从存储的参考树（reference tree）起始，统计每次模型调用的 token，并以重复循环中成本的离散度定义软件温度。

Result: 成功运行中约 80% 的 token 产生于未改变目标修改位置的模型调用，被定义为热；软件温度随模型采样温度（sampling temperature）升高；用 Jarzynski 等式类比估计的修改自由能约为成功编码平均成本的 14%–22%；编码成本约为解码成本的 1.1–1.5 倍；测试套件判定随环境变化而不完美，接受错误修改的套件会带来额外 token 成本。

Conclusion: 该理论有望提供以热力学视角分析和优化代码智能体的新工具。

Abstract: Coding agents are billed by the token, yet software engineering says nothing about what a token actually buys. Here we introduce a theory of software thermodynamics. The theory draws analogues from classical and stochastic thermodynamics, extends them, and applies them to modern agentic coding. We count the tokens an agent generates as its cost and divide that cost into work and heat. The test suite acts as a software Maxwell's daemon that judges every coding change. We test the theory with an analogue of the experiments in which a single molecule is pulled open and let fold again: an agent makes a change to a repository (coding) and then undoes it (uncoding), many times over, and we count the tokens of every model call. The cycle returns the requested edits to where they began, with each direction started from a stored reference tree, and the spread of its cost over repeated cycles defines a software temperature, which rose with the sampling temperature of the model. About 80 per cent of the tokens of a successful run are generated in model calls that leave the requested change where it was, and we call these tokens heat. The free energy of a change, which we estimate by analogy with the equality of Jarzynski, is 14 to 22 per cent of the mean cost of a successful coding run. Coding costs 1.1 to 1.5 times as much as uncoding. The daemon is imperfect: the verdict of a test suite varies with its environment, and a suite that accepts a broken change has a cost in tokens. The practical implication of the proposed theory could be new tools for analysing and optimising coding agents through the lens of thermodynamics.

</details>
