# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [A Minimal Toy Model of Charged Superradiant Spin-Down in Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23360)
*Bo Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 构建一个刻意简化的玩具模型（toy model），以有效隐藏电荷（effective hidden charge）开启带电超辐射（charged superradiance）通道，描述黑洞向玻色子云（bosonic cloud）转移能量与角动量，并给出质量损失与自旋下降的解析关系及波形快照演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命极端质量比旋进（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, EMRIs）对中心黑洞质量与自旋的缓慢变化高度敏感，因此需要一个可控框架来刻画黑洞-云能量交换对EMRI可观测特征的影响。

Method: 模型并非Kerr-Newman微扰计算，而由一个阈值函数（threshold function）、一个唯象速率系数（phenomenological rate coefficient）以及守恒黑洞-云总能量的平衡方程（balance equations）构成，从而导出质量损失与自旋下降之间的解析关系；并以初始质量2×10^6 M_⊙、自旋0.90、有效荷质比0.30、演化一年为示例，将末态参数作为两个静态Kerr快照输入FastEMRIWaveforms。

Result: 基准算例得到M/M_0=0.8933与a_⋆=0.7704；波形图显示末态相对初态具有时域振荡图样偏移与更低频的谐波结构，但因快照独立生成，二者并非相位连接（phase-connected）的时间依赖波形，故不能据此主张可探测性（detectability）。

Conclusion: 该构造的价值在于诊断性（diagnostic）：隔离一组紧凑假设、揭示参数简并（parameter degeneracies），并确定未来自洽计算所需的最小要素；它在带电超辐射直觉与EMRI波形研究之间提供了可复现的桥梁，但不构成天体物理预言（astrophysical forecast）。

Abstract: Long-lived extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) are sensitive to slow changes in the mass and spin of their central black holes. We construct a deliberately minimal toy model in which an effective hidden charge opens a charged-superradiance channel, a bosonic cloud grows from a seed, and energy and angular momentum are transferred from a rotating black hole to the cloud. The model is not a Kerr-Newman perturbation calculation. Instead, it consists of a threshold function, one phenomenological rate coefficient, and balance equations that conserve the combined black-hole-cloud energy. This reduction yields an analytic relation between mass loss and spin-down and makes the roles of the growth coefficient, seed energy, and superradiant margin transparent. For an illustrative system with initial mass $2\times10^6 M_\odot$, spin 0.90, effective charge-to-mass ratio 0.30, and a one-year evolution time, the chosen benchmark reaches $M/M_0=0.8933$ and $a_\star=0.7704$. These values are then passed to FastEMRIWaveforms as two stationary Kerr snapshots. The resulting figures show that the endpoint state has a shifted time-domain oscillation pattern and a lower-frequency harmonic structure relative to the initial state. Because the snapshots are regenerated independently, they do not constitute a phase-connected, time-dependent waveform and cannot by themselves support a detectability claim. The value of the construction is instead diagnostic: it isolates a compact set of assumptions, exposes parameter degeneracies, and identifies the minimum ingredients required for a future self-consistent calculation. In this sense, the model provides a reproducible bridge between charged-superradiance intuition and EMRI waveform studies without presenting its benchmark as an astrophysical forecast.

</details>


### [159] [Separable equations of motion for spinning test bodies in spacetimes with Killing--Yano symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23401)
*Abhinove N. Seenivasan,Paul Ramond,Soichiro Isoyama,Sam R. Dolan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用Killing–Yano对称性的协变构造，将Kerr时空中世界线平移方法推广到所有容许非退化Killing–Yano张量的四维时空，证明自旋在平移后表现为由轨道角动量方向自旋分量设定的电荷，从而获得可分离的运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前结果仅在Kerr时空（世界线平移映射到测地线）和一般Killing–Yano时空（可积性）两类情形分别成立，作者希望把世界线平移方法从Kerr推广至整个容许Killing–Yano张量的时空类，并给出可积性的构造性实现。

Method: 基于Killing–Yano对称性构建协变的世界线平移（worldline-shift method）：平移后自旋体运动等效为无自旋虚构粒子，其电荷由沿轨道角动量的自旋分量确定，并满足洛伦兹力方程（Lorentz-force equation），有效场强由Ricci张量与Killing–Yano张量的对偶构造。

Result: 有效场强在Ricci平坦时空中消失，退化为Kerr中已知的测地线描述；在一般的离壳（off-shell）容许Killing–Yano张量的度规中，显式导出可分离运动方程，其可约化为求积（quadratures），从而构造性地实现已知的可积性。

Conclusion: 由于未假定任何场方程，结果适用于任意此类时空，包括由物质源支持或在修改引力理论（modified theories of gravity）中出现的时空，并以Kerr–de Sitter时空作为示例说明。

Abstract: At linear order in spin, the motion of a spinning compact body in curved spacetime is known to have two remarkable properties: (i) in Kerr spacetime, a suitable shift of the worldline maps the motion onto fiducial geodesics, yielding separable equations of motion; and (ii) in four-dimensional spacetimes admitting a non-degenerate Killing--Yano tensor, the dynamics are integrable. In this work, we generalize the worldline-shift method of (i) to the whole class of spacetimes in (ii), using a covariant construction built on the Killing--Yano symmetry. We show that spin then plays the role of charge: after the shift, the motion is described by a fictitious spinless particle, with charge set by the spin component along the orbital angular momentum, obeying a Lorentz-force equation. The effective field strength is built from the Ricci tensor and the dual of the Killing--Yano tensor. It vanishes in Ricci-flat spacetimes, recovering the geodesic description known in Kerr. In the general off-shell metric admitting a Killing--Yano tensor, we derive explicitly separated equations of motion, which reduce to quadratures and provide a constructive realization of the known integrability. Since no field equations are assumed, our results apply to any such spacetime, including those sourced by matter or arising in modified theories of gravity, and we illustrate them in Kerr--de Sitter spacetime.

</details>


### [160] [Filling the interior of the light cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23407)
*Töre Boybeyi,Lorenzo Giombi,Niko Jokela,K. Kajantie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将平直FLRW宇宙中引力波推迟格林函数的“尾部（tail）”表述为一个Goursat问题，即由两条相交特征线上边界条件定义的双曲偏微分方程，并在位置空间对任意膨胀历史直接求解，用ΛCDM精确解与收敛双级数及近似解析模型交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波推迟格林函数除光锥项外还包含光锥内部的尾部贡献，而此前缺乏对尾部在整个宇宙膨胀历史中的系统性刻画与方法框架。

Method: 对均匀各向同性背景，把尾部构造为Goursat问题（Goursat problem）的解，从而填充光锥内部；在位置空间（position space）对任意膨胀历史直接求解，并将ΛCDM的精确解与关于重合点（coincidence point）的收敛双级数及近似解析相互作用模型分别比对。

Result: 所给解可追踪尾部从电弱时期（electroweak epoch）至今的全部演化：早期源的尾部记录了宇宙流体沿光锥、按推迟时间（retarded time）排序的痕迹——辐射时期（radiation era）单次散射即可解释其特征，物质时期（matter era）则需多次散射。位置空间数据还给出传播子（propagator）的大波数（large-k）展开，其首个修正项即尾部效应，依赖于沿光锥积分的曲率势（curvature potential）。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了尾部计算的精度，并为后续应用奠定基础；尾部在暴发背景功率谱与单次暴发的记忆波形（memory waveform）等方面的应用，需将格林函数与源卷积，将在后续论文中给出。

Abstract: The retarded Green's function of gravitational waves in a spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker universe contains, besides the light-cone term, a tail supported inside the light cone. We formulate the tail, for homogeneous and isotropic backgrounds, as the solution of a ``\textit{Goursat problem},'' {\emph{i.e.}}, a hyperbolic partial differential equation with boundary conditions given on two intersecting characteristic curves. In this sense, we fill the interior of the light cone. We solve this problem directly in position space for any expansion history, and compare exact solutions in $Λ$CDM independently with a convergent double series about the coincidence point, and with an approximate analytic interaction model. We discuss the interpretation of the tail as the sum of the waves scattered off the background curvature, as suggested by its expansion in powers of the curvature potential. Our novel solutions allow us to follow the tail throughout the entire expansion history, from the electroweak epoch to today. We find that the tail of an early source records the trace of the cosmic fluid along the light cone, ordered in retarded time: single scattering accounts for its features in the radiation era, while multiple scattering is needed in the matter era. This work establishes the accuracy of our calculation of the tail and lays the groundwork for its applications. From the position-space data we obtain the large-$k$ expansion of the propagator. Its first correction, the effect of the tail, depends on the curvature potential integrated along the light cone. The applications of the tail, the power spectrum of a background of bursts, and the memory waveform of a single burst, both of which require convolving the Green's function with its source, are presented in follow-up papers.

</details>


### [161] [Thermodynamic Degeneracy and Distinct Horizon Geometries of Rotating Kaluza-Klein Black Holes in Independent External Electric and Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23425)
*Hai Huang,Juhua Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构造了四维 Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton（爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-伸缩子）理论在 Kaluza-Klein 耦合下的精确有限转动解，置于独立外电场与磁场中，并构造出两个热力学读数完全相同但视界内禀几何不同的正则分支，从而说明热力学量不能唯一确定黑洞视界的内禀几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验黑洞热力学读数（能量、温度、角速度、电磁势等）能否唯一确定视界的内禀几何，即热力学与几何之间是否存在一一对应关系。为此需要一个具备精确可控性质、且存在几何简并的解析解实例。

Method: 方法上，构造四维 Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton 理论在 Kaluza-Klein 耦合下的精确有限转动解，并引入独立的外电场与磁场；借助精确的代数平衡恒等式（algebraic balance identities）识别出两个非极端分支，并在统一的参考约定（轴钟、转动标架与电磁势）下比较二者的热力学量与视界几何（含标量-Laplace 本征值）。同时分析外部的渐近匹配与全局双曲性。

Result: 结果显示，两个非极端正则分支具有相同的视界面积、准局域角动量、电磁通量，以及相同的能量、温度、角速度和电磁势；但二者的内禀视界几何并不等距，且所有正轴对称标量-Laplace 本征值在两个分支间严格有序。此外，外部可一致地渐近匹配到与南、北极区对应的两个不同精确转动柱状背景，各场在角度重叠区相容，并均匀延伸至两条对称轴；所有正则非临界外部（含超临界分支）均为全局双曲。

Conclusion: 结论是：相同的热力学读数并不能唯一确定黑洞视界的内禀几何，热力学与视界几何之间存在实质性的非唯一性。该精确解为在渐近非平凡背景下研究转动黑洞的热力学与几何关系提供了显式例证。

Abstract: We construct an exact finite-rotation solution of four-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory at the Kaluza-Klein coupling, immersed in independent external electric and magnetic fields. Exact algebraic balance identities reveal two distinct regular nonextremal branches with identical horizon area, quasi-local angular momentum, and electromagnetic fluxes. Under a common, explicitly specified reference prescription for the axial clock, rotational frame, and electromagnetic potentials, the two branches also have identical thermodynamic readouts, including energy, temperature, angular velocity, and electric and magnetic potentials. Nevertheless, the two branches possess nonisometric intrinsic horizon geometries, with every positive axisymmetric scalar-Laplacian eigenvalue strictly ordered between them. Thus, identical thermodynamic readouts do not uniquely determine the intrinsic geometry of a black hole horizon. We further establish that the exterior admits consistent asymptotic matching to two distinct exact rotating cylindrical backgrounds associated with its northern and southern polar regions. Their metric, dilaton, and electromagnetic fields become compatible in the angular overlap region, with uniform matching extending to both symmetry axes. Every regular noncritical exterior, including the supercritical branches, is globally hyperbolic.

</details>


### [162] [Dynamics of a spinning body with scalar multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23426)
*Jacopo Lestingi,Thomas P. Sotiriou,Andrew Spiers,Andrea Maselli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文为含无质量、非最小耦合标量场（scalar field）的引力理论中的自旋致密天体构建了有效世界线作用量（worldline action），推导出推广的Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon方程，并针对极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）给出至一阶后绝热（1PA）阶的动力学，发现标量情形与伪标量（pseudoscalar）情形存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论（General Relativity）中骨架化（skeletonized）方法已成熟，但需推广至含非最小耦合标量场的引力理论，以刻画自旋小天体在背景时空中的应力-能量张量（stress-energy tensor）及其多极结构，服务于不对称双星系统演化的精确建模。

Method: 将标准骨架化方法推广，用标量场对多极结构进行"修饰"；在极点-偶极（pole-dipole）近似下导出修正的Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon运动方程及标量场与度规的场方程；同时考虑宇称偶（parity-even）与宇称奇（parity-odd）标量；并按质量比幂次系统展开，得到至1PA阶的动力学及所需的自力（self-force）方程。

Result: 对宇称偶标量，1PA阶动力学仅依赖次天体的质量、自旋、标量荷（scalar charge）以及单个自耦合常数；对伪标量，双星动力学在1PA阶与广义相对论一致。

Conclusion: 所构建的形式体系可系统描述标量-张量（scalar-tensor）类引力中自旋致密天体的世界线动力学，并表明宇称奇标量在1PA阶不产生可观测偏离，而宇称偶标量的效应由少数天体参数控制，为EMRIs等不对称双星的波形建模提供理论基础。

Abstract: We construct an effective worldline action for a spinning compact object in theories of gravity with massless, non-minimally coupled scalar fields. To this end, we generalize the standard skeletonized approach used in General Relativity to model the stress-energy tensor of a small body in a background spacetime, dressing its multipolar structure with the scalar field. Within a pole-dipole approximation, we derive a modified set of Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon equations of motion for the spinning body, together with the field equations for the scalar field and the metric. We consider both parity-even and parity-odd scalars. We tailor this formalism to model the evolution of asymmetric binaries, such as Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs), by systematically expanding the relevant equations in powers of the binary mass ratio and deriving the dynamics up to first post-adiabatic (1PA) order, along with the self-force equations needed to determine the particle's acceleration. For a parity-even scalar, the 1PA dynamics depends only on the mass, spin, and scalar charge of the secondary, plus a single self-coupling constant. For a pseudoscalar, we find that the binary dynamics coincides with General Relativity through 1PA.

</details>


### [163] [Comment on "Exact, nonsingular black holes from a phantom DBI field as primordial dark matter"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23597)
*Mustapha Azreg-Aïnou,Marco A. A. de Paula*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文指出参考文献[1]提出的度规与标量场构型不满足最小耦合Dirac-Born-Infeld（DBI）场论与广义相对论（general relativity）耦合的完整场方程（field equations），该引力设置下的物理DBI分支在事件视界（event horizon）之外即终止，因此该构型并非该理论的黑洞解（black hole solution）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对参考文献[1]所声称的DBI场论与广义相对论最小耦合框架下的黑洞解提出质疑，检验其构型能否满足完整场方程，以判定该解的有效性。

Method: 在包含事件视界（event horizon）的径向区域（radial domain）上，检验参考文献[1]给出的度规（metric）与标量（scalar）构型是否满足DBI场论与广义相对论最小耦合（minimally coupled）的完整场方程组，并分析DBI物理分支（physical branch）的定义域。

Result: 该度规与标量构型不满足完整场方程；对该引力设置而言，物理DBI分支在事件视界之外终止（terminates），无法延伸至视界内部。

Conclusion: 参考文献[1]提出的构型不能描述该理论下的黑洞解（black hole solution），其解的地位被否定。

Abstract: We demonstrate that the metric and scalar configuration proposed in Ref.~[1] do not satisfy the complete set of field equations of the Dirac-Born-Infeld (DBI) field theory minimally coupled to general relativity on a radial domain containing the event horizon. The physical DBI branch for this gravitational setup terminates outside the event horizon. Thus, the proposed configuration does not describe a black hole solution of that theory.

</details>


### [164] [Helicity-squared scaling of post-eikonal phases for electromagnetic and gravitational waves in Ricci-flat spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23706)
*C. A. S. Almeida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量、电磁与线性化引力波在Ricci平坦背景上第一后程函修正（first post-eikonal correction）中的螺旋度偶（helicity-even）部分，发现相对标量波的螺旋度偶相位满足螺旋度平方律 $\Delta_2=4\Delta_1$，并在Schwarzschild黑洞弱偏折散射中得到 $\Delta_s=-3\pi s^2/(8M\omega\rho^3)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有自旋—光学（spin-optical）描述主要刻画对螺旋度（helicity）为奇数的有限频率修正，而螺旋度偶的互补部分尚缺乏系统研究；本文旨在厘清后程函修正中螺旋度偶项的来源与标度规律，特别是引力情形中Weyl耦合所起的作用。

Method: 在平行传播零四元标架（parallel-propagated null tetrad）中，将引力场的直接Weyl耦合投影到圆偏振横向无迹态，得到恒等式的 $2C_{\bar m m\bar m m}$ 倍；借助自旋系数 $\mu$ 的Ricci恒等式把该投影改写为全导数（total derivative）加光学项，从而导出螺旋度偶相位；并以Regge–Wheeler相移结合旋转函数 $d^l_{ss}$ 的渐近行为，及径向方程直接数值积分作独立验证。

Result: 螺旋度偶相位相对标量波满足螺旋度平方律 $\Delta_2=4\Delta_1$（至端点项）；引力情形下正是Weyl耦合补全了 $h^2$ 标度。对Schwarzschild黑洞弱偏折散射得 $\Delta_s=-3\pi s^2/(8M\omega\rho^3)$，其中 $\rho=b/M$；该系数亦由Regge–Wheeler相移与 $d^l_{ss}$ 渐近行为无参考系、无规范依赖地独立得到，并经数值积分确认。在 $M$ 的一阶，螺旋度偶相位差为零，与Born振幅（Born amplitudes）的要求一致。

Conclusion: 螺旋度奇的（helicity-odd）自旋—光学部分与螺旋度偶的后程函部分分别由螺旋度的第一、第二幂次组织，表明两类修正具有由螺旋度幂次区分的结构。

Abstract: Spin-optical descriptions of electromagnetic and gravitational waves isolate finite-frequency corrections that are odd in the helicity. We study the complementary, helicity-even part of the first post-eikonal correction for scalar, electromagnetic, and linearized gravitational waves on Ricci-flat backgrounds. In a parallel-propagated null tetrad, the direct Weyl coupling of the gravitational field projects onto the circular transverse-traceless states as the multiple $2C_{\bar m m\bar m m}$ of the identity. The Ricci identity for the spin coefficient $μ$ then rewrites this projection as a total derivative plus optical terms. The helicity-even phase of a spin-$s$ wave, measured relative to the scalar wave, then obeys a helicity-squared law, $Δ_2=4Δ_1$, up to endpoint terms. The Weyl coupling is what completes the $h^2$ scaling in the gravitational case. For weak-deflection scattering by a Schwarzschild black hole we evaluate the relevant integrals along the ray and obtain $Δ_s=-3πs^2/(8Mωρ^3)$, with $ρ=b/M$. The same coefficient follows independently, and without reference to any frame or gauge, from the Regge--Wheeler phase shifts combined with the asymptotics of the rotation functions $d^l_{ss}$. Direct numerical integration of the radial equations confirms it. At first order in $M$ the helicity-even phase difference vanishes, as required by the Born amplitudes. The helicity-odd spin-optical sector and the helicity-even post-eikonal sector are therefore organized by the first and second powers of the helicity, respectively.

</details>


### [165] [Projected Sensitivity of a Differential Torsion Sensor for Tests of the Weak Equivalence Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23897)
*Jiayi Qin,Sheon S. Y. Chua,Ling Sun,Bram J. J. Slagmolen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文评估差分扭秤传感器（differential torsion sensor）在弱等效原理（WEP）检验与第五种力（fifth force）搜寻中的潜力，投影结果显示其噪声受限灵敏度可优于现有地面扭秤极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱等效原理检验是探测超越广义相对论（general relativity）与标准模型（Standard Model）的新型长程、组分依赖相互作用的最灵敏手段之一，因而需要评估更灵敏的实验平台方案。

Method: 采用结合长机械相干时间（mechanical coherence time）、光学腔读出（optical-cavity readout）与共模抑制（common-mode rejection）的差分扭秤传感器；基于已有原型，以Be-Ti检验质量、180天相干积分进行代表性配置的灵敏度投影。

Result: 对Eötvös参数（Eötvös parameter）的投影噪声受限灵敏度为η≃7.5×10⁻¹⁶（2 mHz调制）与2.4×10⁻¹⁶（20 mHz），低于当前地面扭秤平衡极限；对地球源重子耦合Yukawa相互作用（Yukawa interaction），在λ≳10⁷ m范围内耦合强度投影灵敏度为α≃3.1×10⁻¹³与9.8×10⁻¹⁴。

Conclusion: 调制方案（modulation scheme）、材料对比度（material contrast）与源几何（source geometry）是决定投影探测reach的主要因素，系统耦合（systematic couplings）的控制是达成该灵敏度的主要实验要求；差分扭秤传感器是未来实验室WEP检验的有前景平台。

Abstract: Tests of the weak equivalence principle (WEP) are among the most sensitive probes of new long-range, composition-dependent interactions beyond general relativity and the Standard Model. We evaluate the potential of a differential torsion sensor, which combines long mechanical coherence times with optical-cavity readout and common-mode rejection, for laboratory WEP tests and fifth-force searches. For a representative configuration based on an existing prototype, with Be-Ti test masses and 180 days of coherent integration, the projected noise-limited sensitivity to the Eötvös parameter is $η\simeq7.5\times10^{-16}$ and $2.4\times10^{-16}$ for signal modulation at 2 and 20 mHz, respectively, below current ground-based torsion-balance limits. For a baryon-coupled Yukawa interaction sourced by the Earth, the corresponding projected sensitivity to the coupling strength is $α\simeq3.1\times10^{-13}$ and $9.8\times10^{-14}$ for ranges $λ\gtrsim10^{7}$ m. We identify the modulation scheme, the material contrast, and the source geometry as the main factors that set the projected reach, and the control of systematic couplings as the main experimental requirement for attaining it. These results indicate that differential torsion sensors are a promising platform for future laboratory WEP tests.

</details>


### [166] [Long lived QNM for multiple black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24063)
*Ning Tang,Zhongkai Tao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文为含多个极端黑洞（extremal black holes）的 Majumdar–Papapetrou 时空给出了准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNM）的严格数学定义，并揭示多黑洞情形下零测地线流的陷获集（trapped set）可出现稳定椭圆轨道，从而产生向实轴指数快速收敛的 QNM；这被视为多黑洞 QNM 的首个数学结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 QNM 研究主要集中于单黑洞（single black holes），多黑洞情形缺乏严格的数学刻画；本文旨在填补这一空白，为多极端黑洞时空建立 QNM 的严格定义并分析其谱性质。

Method: 在 Majumdar–Papapetrou 时空框架下给出 QNM 的严格定义；通过分析零测地线流（null geodesic flow）的陷获集（trapped set）结构，并以两个等质量黑洞的构型为具体案例，研究稳定椭圆轨道（stable elliptic orbits）对 QNM 分布的影响。

Result: 对某些构型，陷获集中存在稳定（椭圆）轨道；在两个等质量黑洞的情形下，该现象导致 QNM 以指数速度快速收敛至实轴。

Conclusion: 多黑洞时空的 QNM 可利用陷获集的轨道结构加以刻画，稳定椭圆轨道的存在直接决定了 QNM 向实轴指数收敛的行为；该工作给出了多黑洞情形下 QNM 的首个数学结果。

Abstract: We provide a rigorous definition of quasinormal modes (QNM) for the Majumdar--Papapetrou spacetimes with multiple extremal black holes. For certain configurations, the trapped set of the null geodesic flow contains stable (elliptic) orbits. We analyze this phenomenon in the case of two black holes of equal mass and show that it produces QNM converging exponentially fast to the real axis. QNM for black holes have been extensively studied for $\textit{single}$ black holes and this appears to be the first $\textit{mathematical}$ result for the case of multiple black holes.

</details>


### [167] [Gauge and scale dependence of the quadratic backreaction source during inflation: Uniform-expansion gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24127)
*Inyong Cho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在均匀膨胀（uniform-expansion, UE）切片下计算慢滚暴胀（slow-roll inflation）中标量扰动的二次有效能量-动量张量（quadratic effective energy-momentum tensor, 2EMT），给出红外与紫外极限下的分量表达及对应的态参数（w_raw），并证明该张量的一般标量规范依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学反作用（cosmological backreaction）最直接地体现在时空叶理（foliation）的局域膨胀中，因此需要选取合适的几何切片来考察标量扰动的2EMT；UE切片被提出作为自然的几何设定，但其在严格红外、中间红外与紫外各区间的行为尚需系统刻画。

Method: 施加完整的标量规范条件（gauge conditions）κ=0 与 E=0，UE条件给出关于移位（shift）Q 的微分约束，逐傅里叶模（Fourier mode）解为 Q_UE(k)=-3(Ψ'+ℋΨ)/(k²+3εℋ²)；在此规范下求取2EMT的源项及各渐近展开的首项系数。

Result: 严格红外区UE源在零梯度处消失，首个非零项为 τ̂_00^UE/F_LW≃8σ_1²/ε、τ̂_ii^UE/F_LW≃-8σ_1²/(3ε)（领头慢滚阶），给出 w_raw^UE≃-1/3，且有限梯度系数与共动规范（comoving gauge）及均匀密度规范（uniform-density gauge）不同；在梯度主导的中间红外深处趋于空间平坦结果；紫外区 τ̂_00^UE≃2F_SW、τ̂_ii^UE≃(2/3)F_SW，得 w_raw≃1/3。

Conclusion: UE切片下的2EMT在红外与紫外呈现不同的态参数与系数结构，严格红外与中间红外之间的交叉区域需用完整表达式处理；同时论文论证了2EMT在一般标量规范变换下的规范依赖性，并讨论了UE规范的完全固定方式。

Abstract: Cosmological backreaction is most directly expressed through the local expansion of the spacetime foliation, so uniform-expansion (UE) slicing provides a natural geometrical setting in which to examine the quadratic effective energy-momentum tensor (2EMT) of scalar perturbations during slow-roll inflation. We impose the complete scalar gauge conditions $κ=0$ and $E=0$. The uniform-expansion condition gives a \textit{differential} constraint for the shift $Q$ in configuration space and satisfies $Q_{\mathrm{UE}}(k)=-3(Ψ'+\mathcal HΨ)/(k^2+3ε\mathcal H^2)$ for each Fourier mode. In the strict infrared, the UE source vanishes at zero gradient, while its first nonzero terms are $\widehatτ_{00}^{\mathrm{UE}}/\mathcal F_{\mathrm{LW}} \simeq8σ_1^2/ε$ and $\widehatτ_{ii}^{\mathrm{UE}}/\mathcal F_{\mathrm{LW}} \simeq-8σ_1^2/(3ε)$ at leading slow-roll order. Thus $w_{\rm raw}^{\mathrm{UE}}\simeq-1/3$, but the first finite-gradient coefficients differ from the comoving and uniform-density gauges. Sufficiently deep in the gradient-dominated intermediate infrared, the leading UE behavior approaches the spatially-flat result, whereas the crossover region between the strict and intermediate infrared regimes requires the full expressions for the 2EMT. In the ultraviolet, we have $\widehatτ_{00}^{\mathrm{UE}}\simeq2\mathcal F_{\mathrm{SW}}$ and $\widehatτ_{ii}^{\mathrm{UE}}\simeq(2/3)\mathcal F_{\mathrm{SW}}$, yielding $w_{\mathrm{raw}}\simeq1/3$. Separately, we demonstrate the gauge dependence of the 2EMT under general scalar gauge transformations and discuss the complete fixing of the UE gauge.

</details>


### [168] [Rotational Splitting of Reflectionless Matching and Virtual Absorption in a Semiopen BTZ Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24176)
*Sheng Long,Manman Sun,Qin Tan,Yu Shi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了有限旋转 BTZ 区域中标量场的复频率反射零点（reflection zeros），结合解析径向解与波包演化，揭示旋转对零点分裂、时间增长/衰减以及二阶奇异点（EP2）的影响，并区分散射零点与系统本征共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定反射为零的条件并检验其有限时间行为，厘清旋转、近视界部分反射壁与外部传播通道共同作用下反射零点的结构，并辨析散射零点（scattering zeros）与局域源格林函数极点所代表的本征共振之间的差别。

Method: 采用精确径向解（exact radial solutions）确定零反射条件，并通过波包演化（wave packet evolution）检验有限时间行为；分析近视界极限下壁反射系数的幅度与相位的作用，考察外部匹配（exterior matching）带来的修正，并用近视界展开定位 EP2 及其所需的小反射率。

Result: 旋转使 m=±1 扇区的反射零点分裂；对代表性分支对，被动壁在一定反射系数范围内，共转（co-rotating）入射波须随时间增长而同转反向（counter-rotating）波衰减。近视界极限下幅度主控增长率、相位主控振荡频率。调谐波包将反射能量份额从 (3.5–7.6)×10⁻⁴ 降至 1.8×10⁻⁴，壁吸收与腔内峰值储能变化很小。两个共转分支在 EP2 合并；扰动下反射零点呈平方根分裂，而格林函数极点不合并、响应保持线性。

Conclusion: 旋转 BTZ 区域中的反射零点具有可调谐且受 EP2 支配的结构，其平方根分裂行为与格林函数极点的稳定线性响应形成对照，从而将散射零点与系统的内禀共振区分开来。

Abstract: We study complex frequency reflection zeros of a scalar field in a finite rotating BTZ region. The region is bounded by a partially reflecting near horizon wall and connected to an exterior propagation channel. We use exact radial solutions to determine the conditions for zero reflection and test their finite time behavior through wave packet evolution. Rotation splits the reflection zeros in the $m=\pm1$ sectors. For a representative pair of branches, there is a range of reflection coefficients for a passive wall in which the co-rotating incident wave must grow in time, while the counter-rotating one must decay. In the near horizon limit, the magnitude of the wall reflection coefficient mainly controls the growth or decay rate, while its phase mainly controls the oscillation frequency. Exterior matching introduces corrections to these relations. We examine four truncated wave packets, including one tuned case and three reference cases. Tuning reduces the reflected energy fraction from $(3.5$--$7.6)\times10^{-4}$ to $1.8\times10^{-4}$, with little change in wall absorption or the peak energy stored in the cavity. We also find that two co-rotating branches merge at a second-order exceptional point (EP2). A near horizon expansion determines its location and the small wall reflectivity required for its formation. Under perturbations, the reflection zeros exhibit square-root splitting, while the poles of the local-source Green's function do not merge and its response remains linear. These results distinguish scattering zeros from the intrinsic resonances of the system.

</details>


### [169] [A relativistic gravitisation of quantum theory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24227)
*Samuel Fedida,Adrian Kent*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出“局域半经典引力（local semiclassical gravity）”模型，以由局域量子态（经过去光锥内坍缩更新的约化密度矩阵）为源的分布型半经典爱因斯坦场方程为基础，构建相对论因果且广义协变的量子理论“引力化”方案。其弱场极限显式展现因果动力学结构，非相对论极限退化到薛定谔-牛顿（Schrödinger-Newton, SN）系统，可视为SN方程的因果完备化，且不导致超光速信号传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于将半经典引力与量子力学结合为相对论因果、广义协变的框架，并把非线性的薛定谔-牛顿系统推广为满足因果性的完备理论；同时检验该非线性设定下量子测量是否引发超光速信号传递（superluminal signalling）问题。

Method: 方法上采用分布型半经典爱因斯坦场方程（semiclassical Einstein field equations, EFEs），其源为局域系统的约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrix），且仅在系统过去光锥内的坍缩更新；在1+3维时空中推导弱场极限，并在非相对论极限恢复SN系统，进而分析相对论量子测量、空间叠加态与纠缠子系统的显式测量例子。

Result: 结果显示：若量子态仅在测量区域的未来光锥内更新，则该非线性框架不产生超光速信号；弱场极限下，应力-能量张量（stress-energy tensor）期望值的不连续可与（线性化）半经典EFEs的分布解相容。理论通过非线性允许“读出装置”式非标准测量，可近似确定量子态而不影响之，从而实现近似量子态克隆（quantum state cloning）。但该理论违反量子无信号原理（quantum no-signalling principle），且经典引力场在Bell意义上一般不满足局域因果性（local causality）。

Conclusion: 结论认为，局域半经典引力可作为相对论量子理论的因果引力化基础与SN方程的因果完备化，虽允许近似克隆与非标准测量，但仍保持无超光速信号；文中并提出若干可在桌面实验（tabletop experiments）中与低能量子引力学说相检验的路径。

Abstract: We develop a model, that we call local semiclassical gravity, based on the distributional semiclassical Einstein field equations (EFEs) sourced by the local quantum state, i.e. the reduced density matrix of the local system updated by collapses (only) in its past light cone. We propose this as the basis of a relativistically causal generally covariant gravitisation of relativistic quantum theory in $1+3$ spacetime dimensions. We derive the weak-field limit which exhibits the explicitly causal dynamical structure of the theory, and recover the Schrödinger-Newton (SN) system in the nonrelativistic limit. This proposal can thus be understood as a causal completion of the SN equation, a nonlinear extension to quantum theory. We analyze relativistic quantum measurements in this nonlinear setup, showing that these do not lead to superluminal signalling if quantum states update (only) in the future light-cone of the measurement region. We give explicit examples of quantum measurements on a spatial superposition and on entangled subsystems showing how, in the weak field limit, discontinuities in the expectation value of the stress-energy tensor can be consistent with distributional solutions to the (linearised) semiclassical EFEs. We also highlight that the theory allows non-standard measurements via its nonlinearities. These effective ``readout devices'' can approximately determine a quantum state without affecting it through the state's influence on the classical gravitational field and hence can implement approximate quantum state cloning. Nonetheless, the theory does not allow superluminal signalling, although it does violate the quantum no-signalling principle. We further show that the classical gravitational field is generally not locally causal in the sense of Bell. We present several paths to test the model against low-energy quantum gravity in tabletop experiments.

</details>


### [170] [Lorentz and Poincaré holonomies of an acoustic vortex:ergoregion transition, Sagnac time, and beyond-Aharonov--Bohm phonon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24298)
*Claudio Furtado,A. M. de M. Carvalho,L. C. Garcia de Andrade,Fernando Moraes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了无旋涡旋精确声学几何（acoustic geometry）在任意半径圆环上的洛伦兹与庞加莱完整群（Lorentz and Poincaré holonomies），发现洛伦兹完整群在能层（ergosurface）处改变共轭类，并证明几何的非拓扑部分对声子散射留下长程印记，使横向截面偏离 Aharonov–Bohm 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡旋在远离核心处等价于无质量旋转宇宙弦（massless spinning cosmic string），声子散射化为 Aharonov–Bohm 问题，但该近似之外的精确几何效应尚不明确，本文旨在超越此极限给出完整刻画。

Method: 采用在能层内部正则的 Painlevé–Gullstrand 四元标架（tetrad），以闭式求出无旋涡旋精确声学几何在半径为 r 的圆环上的洛伦兹与庞加莱完整群，并借助 Levi-Civita 联络的嘉当展开（Cartan development）导出平移分量，无需引入挠率（torsion）。

Result: 洛伦兹完整群在 r=r_e=Γ/2πc 处改变共轭类：外部为 2π√(1−r_e²/r²) 的旋转、能层上为零旋转（null rotation）、内部为快度 2π√(r_e²/r²−1) 的推动。平移分量为时间平移 τ(r)=(Γ/c²)(1−v_s²/c²)^(−1/2)，等于静态观察者固有时下的声学 Sagnac 延迟的一半，r→∞ 时退化为 Γ/c²。离心项为 m²+2mα 而非 AB 形式 (m+α)²，s 波无相移；横向截面 qσ_⊥=2πα−π³α³/3+O(α⁵)，其中 α=ωΓ/2πc²。

Conclusion: 近区曲率与任何短程、宇称对称的核心物理仅以 O(α⁵) 进入 σ_⊥，故 α³ 项具有普适性（universal）。文中并给出对超流 ⁴He、原子凝聚体与浅水模拟体系的估计。

Abstract: Far from its core, a vortex in a superfluid is acoustically equivalent to a massless spinning cosmic string, and the scattering of phonons reduces to an Aharonov--Bohm (AB) problem. Beyond this limit, we compute the Lorentz and Poincaré holonomies in closed form, the Lorentz and Poincaré holonomies of the \emph{exact} acoustic geometry of an irrotational vortex for circular loops of arbitrary radius~$r$. Using the Painlevé--Gullstrand tetrad, which is regular inside the ergoregion, we show that the Lorentz holonomy changes conjugacy class at the ergosurface $r=\re=Γ/2πc$: it is a rotation by $2π\sqrt{1-\re^{2}/r^{2}}$ outside, a null rotation on, and a boost of rapidity $2π\sqrt{\re^{2}/r^{2}-1}$ inside. The invariant translational component of the Poincaré holonomy is a time shift $τ(r)=(Γ/c^{2})(1-v_s^{2}/c^{2})^{-1/2}$, which we identify with half the acoustic Sagnac delay measured in the proper time of a static observer; it reduces to the spinning-string value $Γ/c^{2}$ as $r\to\infty$. No torsion is required: the translational holonomy follows from the Cartan development of the Levi-Civita connection. The non-topological part of the geometry leaves a long-range imprint on phonon scattering: the centrifugal term is $m^{2}+2mα$ instead of the AB form $(m+α)^{2}$, with $α=ωΓ/2πc^{2}$, so the $s$ wave is not phase shifted. As a consequence the transverse cross section, which controls the Iordanskii force, is $qσ_{\perp}=2πα-π^{3}α^{3}/3+O(α^{5})$, rather than the AB value $\sin 2πα$. We show that the near-zone curvature and any short-range, parity-symmetric core physics enter $σ_\perp$ only at $O(α^{5})$, so the $α^{3}$ term is universal. We give estimates for superfluid $^4$He, atomic condensates, and shallow-water analogues.

</details>


### [171] [Acoustic scattering and radiation force on a global-monopole defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24339)
*Átila Pereira Ribeiro,Alexandre M. de M. Carvalho,Claudio Furtado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究全局单极（global-monopole）几何刚性核引起的标量声波散射，允许立体角亏损或过剩，并将散射分解为拓扑贡献与核心贡献，进而导出辐射力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该几何因度规非渐近平坦而无法定义全局平面波，需用分波相移框架处理散射；同时缺少平移等距性使常规辐射力计算方法失效。

Method: 将散射问题表述为分波相移，相位精确分离为拓扑项 (π/2)(ℓ−s_ℓ) 与非整数阶球贝塞尔函数决定的核心项，并在核心表面直接计算辐射力的分波表达式。

Result: 给出拓扑振幅的闭式表达及绝对收敛余项，其在锥面 θ0=π|1−1/α| 上奇异，对应声测地线的普适偏转角；核心振幅服从广义瑞利律，偶极指数为 √(8+α²)/α；低频下辐射力主项线性依赖于拓扑相位。

Conclusion: 对 α>1/√6 且 α≠1，力在低频区间 ka<ka_c(α) 为负，将核心拉向声源；当 α>α*≈1.372 时归一化力在 ka→0 时发散。

Abstract: We investigate the scattering of scalar acoustic waves by the rigid core of a point defect described by the global-monopole geometry, \(dl^2=dr^2+α^2r^2dΩ^2\), allowing for both a deficit (\(α<1\)) and an excess (\(α>1\)) of solid angle. We show that this geometry can be realized exactly in a fluid with constant radial and tangential mass densities, where \(α^2=ρ_t/ρ_r\). Because the metric is not asymptotically flat, a global plane wave cannot be defined, and we formulate the scattering problem in terms of partial-wave phase shifts. These separate exactly into a topological contribution, \(\fracπ{2}(\ell-s_\ell)\), and a core contribution determined by spherical Bessel functions of noninteger order \(s_\ell\). We derive a closed-form expression for the topological amplitude, supplemented by an absolutely convergent remainder. The amplitude is singular on the cone \(θ_0=π|1-1/α|\), corresponding to the universal deflection angle of acoustic geodesics. The core amplitude obeys a generalized Rayleigh law, with a dipole exponent \(\sqrt{8+α^2}/α\). In the absence of translational isometries, the radiation force must be evaluated directly on the core surface. We derive a partial-wave expression for this force and show that its leading low-frequency contribution is linear in the topological phase. For every \(α>1/\sqrt{6}\), except \(α=1\), the force is negative within a low-frequency interval \(ka<ka_c(α)\), pulling the core toward the source. For \(α>α_*\simeq1.372\), the normalized force diverges as \(ka\to0\).

</details>


### [172] [Geodesic-informed neural networks for testing spacetimes with S2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24370)
*Prajwal Hassan Puttasiddappa,Dennis Fremstad,Abdolali Banihashemi,David F. Mota*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出可微轨道模型 GINN，用优化替代采样处理 S2 恒星绕人马座 A* 的轨道，在多种度规下复现精确求解器的估计与不确定度，且各时空与史瓦西无显著偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中心 S2 等恒星的轨道可检验时空性质，但传统分析需对每次似然评估积分运动方程并进行后验采样，计算代价高。作者希望以可微优化方式同时获得参数、不确定度与贝叶斯证据。

Method: 构建静态球对称时空的可微轨道模型 GINN：守恒量、轨道形状与红移由度规精确计算，径向相位的时间演化由受测地线方程约束的神经网络表示；物理参数与网络权重通过 Adam 与 Gauss-Newton 步最小化单一损失，极小值处的曲率给出不确定度与证据，并与二阶展开及精确测地线求解器对照。

Result: GINN 复现精确求解器的估计与不确定度；史瓦西情形得 M=(4.32±0.22)×10^6 M_⊙、D=8.33±0.21 kpc。各时空均未显著偏离史瓦西，Yukawa 修正限制为 |δ|<0.011（95%），范围约 180 AU；贝叶斯因子从不确定到中等支持史瓦西。

Conclusion: 高斯近似在 hair 线性进入度规时准确、仅以平方进入时偏保守；当后验接近高斯时，单次优化即可给出估计、不确定度与证据，且同一阶导数可量化对高斯的偏离。

Abstract: Stellar orbits around Sagittarius A*, in particular that of S2, test the spacetime of the Galactic Center. Such analyses usually integrate the equations of motion for every likelihood evaluation and sample the posterior. We develop a differentiable orbit model for static, spherically symmetric spacetimes that obtains the parameters, their uncertainties and the Bayesian evidence by optimization rather than sampling. In the Geodesic-Informed Neural Network (GINN), the conserved quantities, the shape of the orbit and the redshift are computed exactly from the metric, and a neural network constrained by the geodesic equation represents the time evolution of the radial phase. The physical parameters and the network weights minimize a single loss with Adam and Gauss-Newton steps, and the curvature at the minimum gives the uncertainties and the evidence. We test this Gaussian approximation against a second-order expansion and an exact geodesic solver, and apply GINN to the S2 data in the Schwarzschild, Reissner-Nordström, hairy Horndeski, Bardeen, Janis-Newman-Winicour and Yukawa spacetimes. GINN reproduces the estimates and uncertainties of the exact solver. For Schwarzschild we find $M=(4.32\pm0.22)\times10^6\,M_\odot$ and $D=8.33\pm0.21$ kpc. No spacetime deviates significantly from Schwarzschild; a Yukawa correction, for example, is limited to $|δ|<0.011$ (95%) at a range of about 180 AU. No additional parameter improves the fit enough to pay its Occam penalty, so the Bayes factors range from inconclusive to moderate in favor of Schwarzschild. The Gaussian approximation is accurate when the hair enters the metric linearly and conservative when it enters only through its square. When the posterior is close to Gaussian, GINN gives the estimates, their uncertainties and the evidence from a single optimization, and the same derivatives quantify departures from it.

</details>


### [173] [Black hole binary mergers in dynamical Chern-Simons gravity with regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24420)
*Llibert Aresté Saló,Maxence Corman,Pau Figueras,Áron D. Kovács,Shunhui Yao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文首次采用数值相对论（numerical relativity）研究Chern-Simons引力的非线性动力学，在有效场论（effective field theory）适用范围内模拟黑洞正碰与双黑洞并合，并提取标量与引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在突破以往对含高阶运动方程引力理论缺乏非线性动力学研究的局限，利用引力波检验包括Chern-Simons引力在内的一般有效场论引力理论。

Method: 采用正则化方案（regularisation prescription），在数值相对论常用规范下获得适定的初值表述（well-posed initial-value formulation），并对黑洞正碰（head-on collisions）与双黑洞并合（binary black hole mergers）进行数值模拟，提取标量波与引力波。

Result: 在有效场论适用范围内成功完成Chern-Simons引力的非线性数值模拟，并提取出相应的标量波与引力波信号。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用引力波检验一般有效场论引力理论（包括运动方程为高于二阶的理论）提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We study the non-linear dynamics of Chern-Simons gravity with numerical relativity for the first time. We use the regularisation prescription to obtain a well-posed initial-value formulation in the gauges commonly used in numerical relativity, and we simulate head-on collisions and binary black hole mergers in the regime of validity of effective field theory, extracting the corresponding scalar and gravitational waves. This opens up the possibility to test general effective field theories of gravity, including those with higher-than-second-order equations of motion, with gravitational waves.

</details>


### [174] [Neural-Network Identification of LIGO Noise Transients Using Binary Auxiliary-Channel Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24485)
*D. Chintala,J. Temple,S. Shapiro,L. Wilson,K. Wayt,M. Van Keuren,P. Neubauer,K. Rose,D. Moffa,L. Wade,M. Wade*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究针对 LVK 引力波探测器中的瞬态噪声伪影（glitch），以现代训练基础设施重新审视神经网络方法，提出 GIANTS 与 TITAN 两种架构，在 LIGO 第三次观测运行的十个独立测试期上多数优于现有 OVL 算法；并证明辅助通道活动性的简单二值指示即可捕捉 Omicron 触发特征的几乎全部预测信息，输入维度可降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬态噪声伪影（glitch）可模仿天体物理信号，降低引力波探测的可信度；现有 iDQ 流程依赖 Ordered Veto List（OVL）算法，需评估更稳健、更简洁的替代方案。

Method: 采用现代训练基础设施重访神经网络途径，提出两种架构：GIANTS 为在全部辅助通道特征上训练的单一网络，TITAN 为由子系统专用网络与可学习组合器构成的专家混合（mixture-of-experts）模型；并在 LIGO 第三次观测运行的十个独立测试期上进行评估，同时对比基于二值辅助通道活动指示的简化输入。

Result: 两种模型在多数测试集上优于 OVL，其中 TITAN 的时间一致性最佳；仅使用二值输入的网络性能与使用完整 8,440 维特征集的模型基本相当，输入维度降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 现代神经网络是 OVL 的稳健替代方案，且更简单的数据表示足以支撑准确的 glitch 识别，可为未来 iDQ 部署提供参考。

Abstract: Transient noise artifacts ("glitches") in the Laser Interferometer Gravitational-Wave Observatory (LIGO), Virgo and KAGRA (LVK) detectors can mimic astrophysical signals and reduce confidence in gravitational-wave detections. The LVK's iDQ pipeline identifies these glitches by analyzing correlations between auxiliary sensors and the strain channel using the Ordered Veto List (OVL) algorithm. We revisit neural-network approaches to this problem using modern training infrastructure and introduce two architectures: GIANTS, a single network trained on all auxiliary-channel features, and TITAN, a mixture-of-experts model composed of subsystem-specific networks and a learned combiner. Evaluated on ten independent test periods spanning LIGO's third observing run, both models outperform OVL on most test sets, with TITAN providing the most consistent performance over time. We further show that nearly all predictive information contained in Omicron trigger features is captured by a simple binary indicator of auxiliary-channel activity. Networks trained on these binary inputs achieve essentially the same performance as models using the full 8,440-dimensional feature set while reducing the input dimensionality by an order of magnitude. These results demonstrate that modern neural networks provide a robust alternative to OVL and suggest that substantially simpler data representations are sufficient for accurate glitch identification in future iDQ deployments.

</details>


### [175] [Boundary preparation and anomalous relaxation near a black hole exceptional point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24488)
*Yu Shi,Sheng Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究考察平面Schwarzschild AdS黑洞稳定例外点附近Robin标量场的弛豫行为，发现边界驱动可在不改变线性阶衰减率的前提下调控恢复时间；当源的零阶矩（zeroth moment）消失而一阶矩非零时，线性时间因子被消除，恢复时间优势随阈值θ→0按γ*^{-1}log log(1/θ)增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解例外点（exceptional point）附近黑洞标量场弛豫中边界驱动与恢复时间的关系，特别是能量保留更多与更快恢复之间可能出现的反直觉情形。

Method: 采用Robin标量场在平面Schwarzschild AdS黑洞背景上的分析，结合源加权矩（weighted moments of the source）的解析讨论，并在固定背景上进行完整标量场演化数值模拟以验证结论。

Result: 例外点处源的两个加权矩决定主导响应的时间依赖；零阶矩消失时线性时间因子被消除，仅剩普通指数衰减，且保留更多能量的扰动可在适当时刻更早达到共同恢复阈值。其恢复时间优势在θ→0时以γ*^{-1}log log(1/θ)增长，源矩还决定渐近时间差的常数项；数值演化在脉冲时长、边界任务、源成本与注入功匹配的条件下证实了该反转。

Conclusion: 注入功通过引力加热（gravitational heating）改变末态谱：弱驱动下温度升高与源振幅呈二次关系，而最终极点劈裂（pole splitting）与振幅绝对值呈线性关系，该劈裂限制了例外点恢复律可观测的时间窗口。

Abstract: We study the relaxation of a Robin scalar field near a stable exceptional point of a planar Schwarzschild AdS black hole, where finite boundary driving controls the recovery time without changing the leading decay rate at linear order. At the exceptional point, two weighted moments of the source determine the time dependence of the leading response. When the zeroth moment vanishes while the first remains nonzero, the linear time factor is eliminated, leaving an ordinary exponential decay. With suitable boundary preparation, a perturbation that retains more energy at release can nevertheless reach a common recovery threshold sooner. At the exceptional point, its recovery time advantage over a fixed reference that retains the linear factor grows as $γ_*^{-1}\log\log(1/θ)$ as the common amplitude threshold $θ$ approaches zero, where $γ_*$ is the common decay rate. The source moments also determine the constant term in this asymptotic time difference. Full scalar field evolution on a fixed background near the exceptional point illustrates the reversal for pulses matched in duration, boundary task, source cost, and injected work, within the time window where the small spectral splitting remains unresolved. The injected work also modifies the final spectrum through gravitational heating. When weak driving begins with a black hole at the exceptional point, the temperature rise is quadratic in the source amplitude, whereas the final pole splitting is linear in its magnitude. This splitting limits the time window over which the exceptional point recovery law can be observed.

</details>


### [176] [Orbit-averaged spinning and eccentric binary black holes: Hamiltonian and integrability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24492)
*Tom Colin,Sashwat Tanay,Manuel Alva-Morales,Prateek Anand*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文从运动方程独立推导出轨道平均（orbit-averaged）自旋进动哈密顿量，构造作用-角变量（action-angle variables），提出可积性（integrability）破缺判据，并推广投影有效自旋（projected effective spin）至双中子星偏心轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶后牛顿（second post-Newtonian）轨道平均进动方程二十余年来是双黑洞自旋动力学标准模型，但其哈密顿量的独立推导、轨道平均为何给出正确方程、可积性破缺判据及常量与完整系统关系等问题尚未系统澄清。

Method: 仅由运动方程（equations of motion）重新推导该哈密顿量，说明轨道平均的正确性，将运动常量与完整系统对应；借助近期提出的虚拟变量法（fictitious variables）构造作用-角变量与频率，并提出可积性条件破缺的判据。

Result: 该判据可重现简单经典系统的分界线（separatrices）及上下不稳定性（up-down instability），并给出偏心轨道的显式阈值；证明当系综内双星参数相同且辐射反作用（radiation reaction）不依赖自旋时，哈密顿结构保持自旋方向各向同性分布，数值上证实参数与自旋方向相关时各向同性会丧失。

Conclusion: 利用轨道平均哈密顿量将投影有效自旋推广至双中子星（binary neutron stars），尽管其在该情形下并非守恒量，并给出适用于偏心轨道的闭合形式。

Abstract: For over two decades, the orbit-averaged precession equations of spinning binary black holes at second post-Newtonian order have been a standard model for their spin dynamics. One of their constants of motion, the projected effective spin, underlies the stability analysis of aligned-spin configurations and the construction of analytical solutions. The system is known to be Hamiltonian and integrable, its Hamiltonian being the orbit average of the full post-Newtonian Hamiltonian. Independently, we derive this Hamiltonian from the equations of motion alone, show why the orbit average gives the correct equations, and relate its constants of motion to those of the full system. We then construct the action-angle variables and frequencies with the recently introduced method of fictitious variables, and propose a criterion for the breakdown of the integrability conditions. This criterion recovers the separatrices of simple classical systems and the up-down instability, whose thresholds we give explicitly for eccentric orbits. We also show that, when all binaries of an ensemble share the same parameters and radiation reaction does not depend on the spins, the Hamiltonian structure preserves an isotropic distribution of spin directions. We confirm numerically that isotropy can be lost when the parameters are correlated with the spin directions. Finally, we use the orbit-averaged Hamiltonian to generalize the projected effective spin to binary neutron stars, for which it is not conserved, and obtain a closed form valid for eccentric orbits.

</details>


### [177] [Electro-Penrose Process in Charged BTZ Black Holes: From Probe Limit to Thermodynamic Backreaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24501)
*Hanjun Zou,Ruo-Cen Wu,Meng Xu,Zoujian Cao,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在(2+1)维带电BTZ时空中研究电-Penrose过程（electro-Penrose process, EPP），从探针极限的粒子动力学拓展到准静态热力学反作用，给出单次事件最大能量、序列提取协议及其类第三定律行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Penrose能量提取机制推广至带电黑洞背景，考察在探针近似之外、考虑准静态热力学反作用（quasistatic thermodynamic backreaction）时，EPP能否持续提取能量及其热力学限制。

Method: 先在探针背景（probe background）下分析中性粒子与带电粒子的圆轨道条件，推导固定衰变数据（碎片质量与电荷分配）下的最大单次事件能量，并在反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）时空中检验有限接收端的俘获与收集；随后在逐次精确的稳态背景上，以自适应入射比荷（adaptive incident specific charge）进行重复径向提取，并借助连续质量-电荷路径识别入射转折点与负能量停止机制，辅以数值验证。

Result: 中性粒子无外部圆轨道，带电圆轨道运动需满足最小排斥耦合；研究识别出转折点机制与负能量停止机制之间的切换，且规定序列在耗尽能量储库之前即终止于非零剩余电荷。

Conclusion: 该终止行为构成依赖协议的类第三定律（third-law analogy）结果；被消耗的储库分为提取能量与不可约质量（irreducible mass）的不可逆增长两部分，其比例取决于所采用的协议。

Abstract: We investigate the electro-Penrose process (EPP) in $(2+1)$-dimensional charged Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) spacetime, from probe-limit particle dynamics to quasistatic thermodynamic backreaction. In the probe background, neutral particles have no exterior circular orbits, and charged circular motion requires a minimum repulsive coupling. We derive the maximum single-event energy at fixed decay data, including fragment masses and charge allocation, and check capture and collection at a finite receiver in anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime. Repetitive radial extraction on successive exact stationary backgrounds uses an adaptive incident specific charge. The continuum mass-charge path identifies the switch between incident turning-point and negative-energy stopping, supported by numerical checks. The prescribed sequences terminate at nonzero residual charge before exhausting the energy reservoir, providing a protocol-dependent third-law analogy. The consumed reservoir is divided between extracted energy and irreversible growth of irreducible mass, with proportions depending on the protocol.

</details>


### [178] [A twist in the tail: GWLENS, a fast and accurate tool for wave-optics lensing of gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24621)
*Sreekanth Harikumar,Ansal Kanu,Anirudh Nemmani,Paweł Ciecieląg,Michał Bejger*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出一种高效计算引力波波动光学透镜放大率的新算法：将菲涅耳-基尔霍夫衍射积分（Fresnel–Kirchhoff diffraction integral）的积分域在透镜像区域之外分割，头段用高斯-勒让德求积（Gauss–Legendre quadrature）实轴积分，尾部旋转至复平面用高斯-拉盖尔求积（Gauss–Laguerre quadrature）处理，避免极点与支割线问题，精度达 10^-13。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波动光学（wave-optics）区间的引力波透镜效应需数值计算高度振荡的菲涅耳-基尔霍夫衍射积分，而闭式解（closed-form solutions）仅对少数透镜模型存在，现有围绕轮廓形变（contour deformation）的方法还需处理极点（poles）与支割线（branch cuts）。

Method: 在包含透镜像的区域之外的某点将积分域分割为有界头段与半无限尾段；头段沿实轴以 Gauss–Legendre 求积计算，尾段旋转入复平面利用被积函数指数衰减以 Gauss–Laguerre 求积计算，从而不对像区域作形变。

Result: 结果与精确点质量（point-mass）解一致到 10^-13 量级，并与其他方法吻合良好；对非平凡透镜模型给出示例，展示用于真实透镜参数估计（parameter estimation）的潜力，且在需对大量源位置计算放大因子（amplification factor）时尤其高效。

Conclusion: 除瞬态宽带致密双星并合（compact binary coalescence, CBC）引力波信号外，该方法亦适用于长时标准单色连续引力波，此时源与透镜的相对运动使放大因子随观测时间变化；实现已公开于 Python 包 GWLENS。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave lensing in the wave-optics regime requires evaluating the Fresnel--Kirchhoff diffraction integral, whose integrand is highly oscillatory and must be evaluated numerically, as closed-form solutions exist only for a few lens models. We present a novel, efficient algorithm that splits the integration domain at a point beyond the region containing the lensed images. The bounded head region is integrated along the real axis with Gauss--Legendre quadrature, while the semi-infinite tail is rotated into the complex plane, where the integrand decays exponentially, and is evaluated with Gauss--Laguerre quadrature. Since the image region is never deformed, the method avoids the treatment of poles and branch cuts required by existing contour-deformation approaches. Our results agree with the exact point-mass solution to $10^{-13}$ and show good agreement with other methods. We present several examples for non-trivial lens models, showing potential for parameter estimation of realistic lenses. The method is particularly efficient when the amplification factor is needed for many source positions. In addition to transient broadband compact binary coalescence GW signals, we apply it to long-duration (continuous), quasi-monochromatic GW signals, for which the relative motion of source and lens makes the amplification factor time-dependent over the observation. The implementation is publicly available in the Python package GWLENS.

</details>


### [179] [LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA stochastic constraints on first-order phase transitions: A tale of priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24625)
*S. AbdusSalam,J. Kersten,S. A. Ojaghi,L. Velasco-Sevilla,A. Zafari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文用贝叶斯统计（Bayesian statistics）重新审视利用引力波搜寻限制宇宙学一阶相变（cosmological first-order phase transitions）的方法论，发现多数参数约束强烈依赖先验（prior）选择，当前数据尚不足以给出稳健约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于引力波搜寻限制一阶相变的研究可能未充分检验先验依赖性，因此需要系统评估参数约束是否稳健、是否与先验无关（prior independence）。

Method: 采用贝叶斯统计分析（Bayesian statistical analysis），基于 LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 在 O1--O4a 观测运行期间搜寻随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background）的结果，针对几何参数、相变热力学参数（thermodynamical parameters）以及阿贝尔希格斯模型（Abelian Higgs model）粒子物理参数三类拟合参数，遍历多种先验选择。

Result: 主要发现是多数描述相变引力波的参数约束强烈依赖先验，数据尚不能给出稳健约束；即使某参数得到有意义约束，其上限（upper limit）也会随先验范围显著变化。

Conclusion: 论文给出评估参数估计稳健性的实用建议，并为未来分析中严格检验先验独立性提供指导准则。

Abstract: We re-examine the methodology for constraining cosmological first-order phase transitions from gravitational-wave searches using Bayesian statistical analysis. Specifically, we use results from LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA searches for a stochastic gravitational wave background during observing runs O1--O4a. We explore multiple prior choices across three categories of fit parameters: geometrical parameters, thermodynamical parameters of phase transitions, and the parameters of an Abelian Higgs particle physics model. Our key finding is that the constraints on most parameters describing gravitational waves from a phase transition depend strongly on the prior. Consequently, the data do not yet allow to derive robust constraints on these parameters. Besides, even if a parameter is meaningfully constrained, the upper limit can vary significantly with the prior range. We provide practical recommendations for assessing the robustness of parameter estimates and guidelines for rigorously testing prior independence in future analyses.

</details>


### [180] [Charge at regular centers in curvature-coupled electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24634)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究在Maxwell不变量（Maxwell invariant）被标量曲率响应函数h(R)乘上后，球对称静态解中电荷产生的奇异性（singularity）如何被改变，并给出磁性与电位移（electric displacement）电荷存活的精确阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常穿过收缩对称球面的电磁通量会产生r^{-4}的Maxwell应力（Maxwell stress）与中心奇异，本文考察曲率耦合响应h(R)能否消除这一阻碍。

Method: 对实、静态、球对称解，取径向与时间引力方程之差得到复合响应变量的线性方程，再结合迹方程（trace equation），在Kretschmann标量有界的条件下界定响应加权的Maxwell源，且不假设Ricci标量的极限或导数界。

Result: 磁性与电位移电荷不能共存；若h有界且远离零则两类电荷均不可能存在。磁荷需h=o(r^4)，电位移需r^4|h|→∞；精确解表明阈值是尖锐的，并实现光滑几何中心下r^6（磁）与r^{-6}（电）的速率。

Conclusion: 曲率响应h(R)可缓解中心奇异性，但需满足特定渐近阈值；零响应扇区（zero-response sector）中电场可在位移电荷消失时仍存活，文章对此类解作了分类。

Abstract: An electric or magnetic flux through shrinking symmetry spheres ordinarily produces an $r^{-4}$ Maxwell stress and a singular center. We examine how this obstruction changes when the Maxwell invariant is multiplied by an arbitrary scalar-curvature response $h(R)$. For real, static, spherically symmetric solutions, the difference between the radial and temporal gravitational equations gives a linear equation for a composite response variable. Combined with the trace equation, it bounds the response-weighted Maxwell source whenever the Kretschmann scalar is bounded, without assuming a limit or derivative bounds for the Ricci scalar. Magnetic and electric displacement charges cannot coexist, and neither charge is possible if $h$ remains bounded and bounded away from zero. On either surviving charged branch, the Ricci scalar converges and the weighted source tends to zero. Magnetic charge requires $h=o(r^4)$, whereas electric displacement requires $r^4|h|\to\infty$. Exact solutions show that these thresholds are sharp and realize the $r^6$ magnetic and $r^{-6}$ electric rates for a smooth geometric center. We also classify the zero-response sector, where an electric field may survive even though its displacement charge vanishes.

</details>


### [181] [Complete quasinormal modes of Type-D black holes. III. Reissner-Nordstrom spectra from zero to extremal charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24647)
*Changkai Chen,Xiaohua Zhang,Zhoujian Cao,Jiliang Jing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作通过解析延拓法追踪带电（Reissner-Nordström）黑洞引力准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）随电荷变化的谱流，系统厘清了其与施瓦西（Schwarzschild）谱的对应关系及代数特殊频率（algebraically special frequencies）的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有计算未能说明带电黑洞引力准正规模如何回归施瓦西谱，也未澄清近代数特殊频率的模式与精确特殊解之间的关系；本文旨在填补这两处空白。

Method: 将Kerr分析推广至Reissner-Nordström黑洞：对入射视界解（ingoing horizon solution）作解析延拓，并通过定位无穷远处入射振幅的零点求QNM；对ℓ=2与3各追踪前42个overtone族，从施瓦西极限延伸至极值电荷，并首次在跨负虚轴到达的非传统Riemann叶上计算极值情形QNM。

Result: 电荷轨迹可区分负虚轴穿越与近轴折返，并分离非传统施瓦西模式与邻近分支；零电荷时极向代数特殊模式本身是QNM，极值电荷时精确轴向导特殊模式为全透射模式（total-transmission modes）；84族中75族趋于不同极值频率且阻尼有限，9族趋于零频率且激发消失；中等电荷下ℓ=3的overtone对(22,23)与(23,24)呈最明显的共振增强（resonant enhancement）。

Conclusion: 结果建立了带电黑洞引力QNM谱与施瓦西谱及精确特殊解之间的明确联系，并给出极值电荷与非传统Riemann叶上的新谱结构，为后续解析与数值研究提供基准。

Abstract: Earlier calculations left open how gravitational quasinormal modes (QNMs) of charged black holes recover the Schwarzschild spectrum and how the modes near algebraically special frequencies relate to the exact special solutions. We extend our Kerr analysis to Reissner-Nordström black holes by analytically continuing the ingoing horizon solution and locating the zeros of the incoming amplitude at infinity. For each of $\ell=2$ and $3$, we trace the first 42 overtone families from the Schwarzschild limit to extremality. The charge trajectories distinguish crossings of the negative imaginary axis from turns near it and separate the unconventional Schwarzschild modes from neighboring branches. At zero charge the polar algebraically special mode is a QNM. At extremal charge the exact axial special modes are total-transmission modes. Of the 84 families, 75 approach distinct extremal frequencies with finite damping, and nine approach zero frequency with vanishing excitation. At exact extremality we also compute, for the first time, gravitational QNMs on the unconventional Riemann sheet reached across the negative imaginary axis. At intermediate charge, the clearest resonant enhancement of both excitation factors occurs for the $\ell=3$ overtone pairs $(22,23)$ and $(23,24)$.

</details>


### [182] [Surrogate Modeling and Reduced-Order Methods in Gravitational-Wave Physics and Numerical Relativity: From Spectral Representations to Adaptive Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24727)
*Manuel Tiglio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为视角文章（perspective），综述基于投影的降阶建模（reduced-order modeling, ROM）与代理模型（surrogate）技术及其在引力波天文学中的应用，涵盖数学基础、数据分析中的作用、近似瓶颈的克服途径，以及与科学机器学习结合的前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学（gravitational-wave astronomy）与高精度数值相对论（numerical relativity）的快速发展带来严重计算瓶颈：直接数值积分爱因斯坦场方程（Einstein field equations）与高维贝叶斯参数估计（Bayesian parameter estimation）需数百万次波形评估，传统求解器难以支撑。

Method: 综述基于投影的降阶建模数学支柱，包括本征正交分解（Proper Orthogonal Decomposition）、贪心基选择（greedy basis selection）与经验插值法（Empirical Interpolation Method），并梳理其在引力波数据分析中通过代理波形族与降阶求积（Reduced-Order Quadratures）的操作性影响。

Result: 指出现有方法在非定常、瞬态与强非线性区域受限于Kolmogorov n-宽度（Kolmogorov n-width）缓慢衰减所致的近似壁垒，而自适应 hp-greedy 域划分（adaptive hp-greedy domain partitioning）与多尺度谱压缩（multi-scale spectral compression）可克服上述限制。

Conclusion: 将经过认证的降阶结构与现代科学机器学习（scientific machine learning）及敏感性感知量化（sensitivity-aware quantization）相结合，可为 LISA、爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）与宇宙探路者（Cosmic Explorer）等下一代探测器提供严谨蓝图。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of gravitational-wave astronomy and high-precision numerical relativity has created an acute computational bottleneck: direct numerical integration of the Einstein field equations and high-dimensional Bayesian parameter estimation require millions of waveform evaluations that cannot be sustained by traditional solvers alone. Reduced-order modeling (ROM) and surrogate techniques have emerged as foundational tools to bridge this gap, compressing high-fidelity solution manifolds into compact, rapidly evaluable representations without sacrificing physical accuracy. This perspective reviews the mathematical pillars of projection-based reduced-order modeling---including Proper Orthogonal Decomposition, greedy basis selection, and the Empirical Interpolation Method---and traces their operational impact in gravitational-wave data analysis through surrogate waveform families and Reduced-Order Quadratures. We then examine the fundamental approximation barriers posed by slow Kolmogorov n-width decay in non-stationary, transient, and strongly nonlinear regimes, showing how adaptive hp-greedy domain partitioning and multi-scale spectral compression overcome these limits. Finally, we discuss how combining certified reduced-order structures with modern scientific machine learning and sensitivity-aware quantization provides a rigorous blueprint for next-generation detectors such as LISA, Einstein Telescope, and Cosmic Explorer.

</details>


### [183] [Efficient search for microlensed gravitational-wave signals with matched filtering and a two-image statistic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24895)
*Soumen Roy,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文针对引力波（gravitational waves）信号被致密天体微引力透镜（microlensing）后波形失真导致的搜索灵敏度损失问题，提出一种无需扩展模板库（template bank）的检测统计方法：利用微透镜信号位于模板与其时间平移副本张成的二维子空间这一结构，解析最大化相对放大率（relative magnification）与Morse相位，仅搜索时间延迟（time delay）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准匹配滤波（matched-filter）搜索依赖未考虑透镜效应的无透镜模板，微透镜信号会因模板失配（template mismatch）损失信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR），并进一步受信号一致性检验（signal-consistency test）惩罚。论文旨在量化并缓解这两类损失，为微透镜信号的搜索与识别奠定基础。

Method: 基于PyCBC搜索流程，在模拟数据中注入微透镜信号以量化两类损失；在几何光学（geometric optics）表述下分析其参数空间分布，并构造一个统计量（statistic）：利用透镜信号位于模板及其时间平移副本所张成二维子空间的性质，解析最大化相对放大率与Morse相位，只对时间延迟进行搜索，且不需扩展模板库。

Result: 模板失配主要由双像的相对放大率决定，一致性检验惩罚主要由双像时间延迟决定，两者占据透镜参数空间中不同区域。所提统计量将重加权SNR（re-weighted SNR）的中位损失从10%降至1.6%，第90百分位数从41%降至9%；在每天一次虚警率（false-alarm rate）下，总体相对探测体积提升约6%，对受透镜影响最大的10%注入提升约1.9倍。

Conclusion: 该检测统计量不仅提升了对微透镜信号的探测能力，还可用于判定触发事件的透镜性质；因此，该工作为在引力波数据中有效搜索与识别微透镜信号奠定了基础。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from compact binary coalescences can be microlensed by compact objects along the line of sight, producing frequency-dependent waveform distortions. As standard matched-filter searches rely on unlensed templates without accounting for lensing effects, microlensed signals can lose signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) through template mismatch and suffer further losses from signal-consistency tests. We quantify both effects for the $\texttt{PyCBC}$ search using microlensed injections in simulated data. The two losses occupy different regions of lens parameter space in the geometric optics representation: template mismatch is governed by the relative magnification of the two images, while the consistency-test penalty is governed by their time delay. We show that a microlensed signal lies in the two-dimensional subspace spanned by a template and its time-shifted copy, allowing the relative magnification and Morse phase to be maximized over analytically, leaving only the time delay to search. The resulting statistic requires no extension of the template bank and reduces the median loss in re-weighted SNR from $10\%$ to $1.6\%$, and the $90^\mathrm{th}$ percentile from $41\%$ to $9\%$. At a false-alarm rate of one per day, the relative detection volume improves by about $6\%$ across all injections, and by a factor of $1.9$ for the $10\%$ of injections most affected by lensing. In addition to increased detection capacities, we also show how the detection statistic can be used to establish the lensed nature of the trigger. Therefore, the work proposed here lays the foundation for an effective search and identification of microlensed signals in gravitational-wave data.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [XANES-Guided Molecular Structure Generation via Conditional Diffusion and Forward Spectral Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23725)
*Yu Fujikata,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“逆-正（inverse-forward）”框架，结合XANES条件扩散生成模型与正向谱预测筛选，从C、N、O K边XANES谱重建完整分子结构，最佳10选1精确匹配率达56.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XANES谱主要编码局部化学环境，且多吸收位点贡献重叠，使从谱到完整分子结构的重建具有挑战性。作者希望仅凭总原子数与XANES谱直接生成全分子结构，而无需分子式或各元素原子数信息。

Method: 构建逆-正框架：逆模型为以C、N、O K边XANES为条件的扩散式分子生成器，输出原子种类、成键连接与三维坐标；正向谱预测模型用于评估候选结构与输入谱的一致性并进行排序（spectral-consistency ranking）。

Result: 融合三个吸收边显著提升结构恢复效果，在2,888个测试分子上10候选精确匹配率为56.6%；生成候选在21类官能团与结构基元上的宏平均F1为0.951。按谱一致性排序使精确匹配率由未排序top-1的24.3%提升至43.9%，并可估计原子数，在7个候选值中65.6%分子数正确。实验谱上咪唑（imidazole）实现精确结构匹配，另两个分子恢复特征官能团，同时暴露模拟-实验谱差异带来的局限。

Conclusion: 研究确立了利用互补的元素特异XANES信息、以逆-正方式重建整体分子结构的方法路径。

Abstract: Reconstructing complete molecular structures from X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES) spectra remains challenging because the spectra primarily encode local chemical environments, with overlapping contributions from multiple absorbing sites. Here, we develop an inverse-forward framework combining XANES-conditioned diffusion-based molecular structure generation with forward spectral prediction for candidate selection. The inverse model generates atomic species, bond connectivity, and three-dimensional coordinates from C, N, and O K-edge XANES, given only the total atom count without molecular formulas or element-specific atom counts. Combining all three absorption edges substantially improves structural recovery, achieving a best-of-10 exact-match rate of 56.6% across 2,888 test molecules. The generated candidates also reproduce functional groups and structural motifs, achieving a macro-averaged F1 score of 0.951 across 21 categories. Ranking candidates by agreement between input and forward-predicted spectra increases the exact-match rate from 24.3% for unranked top-1 selection to 43.9%. This spectral-consistency criterion also enables atom count estimation, identifying the correct count for 65.6% of molecules among seven tested values. Application to experimental spectra yields an exact structural match for imidazole and recovers characteristic functional groups in two additional molecules, while revealing limitations due to simulation-experiment spectral discrepancies. These findings establish an inverse-forward approach for reconstructing whole-molecule structures from complementary element-specific XANES information.

</details>


### [185] [Efficient Spectra and Dynamics with Tangent-Space Chebyshev Tree Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23975)
*Zhangyu Yang,Xingyao Wu,Shuo Sun,Yu Wang,Weitang Li,Jan von Delft,Christian B. Mendl,Zhigang Shuai*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: TaChe将树张量网络态与切空间中的Chebyshev递推及最大熵谱分析结合，用于量子多体谱与动力学计算，在GPU上实现显著加速并成功应用于复杂分子体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络态压缩（tensor network state compression）带来计算复杂度、内存占用与数值不稳定性问题，制约量子多体谱与动力学模拟的效率与规模。

Method: 在基态切空间（tangent space）中实施张量网络态计算，结合树张量网络（tree tensor network）、Chebyshev递推（Chebyshev recursion）与最大熵谱分析（maximum entropy spectral analysis），并在GPU平台上部署。

Result: 相比已有Chebyshev张量网络方法加速最高达46.8倍，相比单点含时变分原理传播（one-site time-dependent variational principle propagation）加速13.7倍，并获得准确谱。

Conclusion: 在乙腈、热聚集体发光、6×6晶格有限温度电荷输运及多达100个分子聚集体的内转换等应用中，TaChe展现出模拟复杂分子体系的高效性与通用性。

Abstract: We present a tangent-space Chebyshev method (TaChe), which combines tree tensor network states with Chebyshev recursion and maximum entropy spectral analysis in a tree tensor network tangent space, for quantum many-body spectra and dynamics. Implemented tensor network state calculations in the tangent space of the ground state, the core advantage of TaChe is that it can substantially reduce the computational complexity, memory usage, and numerical instability associated with tensor network state compression. Implemented on the GPU platform, it produces accurate spectra with speedups of up to $46.8$ over the previous Chebyshev tensor network method and $13.7$ over one-site time-dependent variational principle propagation in the reported benchmarks. Applications to acetonitrile, thermal aggregate emission, finite-temperature charge transport on a \(6\times6\) lattice, and internal conversion in aggregates of up to 100 molecules demonstrate the efficiency and versatility of TaChe in simulating complex molecular systems.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [The $S$ and Double-Exponential Transformations with a Moment Expansion for Three-Centre Coulomb and Hybrid Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23965)
*Hassan Safouhi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究三中心双电子库仑积分与混合积分中半无限振荡径向积分的数值计算，提出将S变换（S transformation）与傅里叶型双指数求积（Fourier-type double-exponential quadrature, DE）相结合的S+DE方法，并辅以弱振荡区的矩展开以提升效率与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数型函数（exponential-type functions）下的三中心双电子库仑与混合积分含有振荡球贝塞尔函数（spherical Bessel functions）的半无限径向积分，直接数值求积在振荡强烈时效率低、精度难保证，因此需要更高效的数值方案。

Method: 采用S变换把导数从球贝塞尔因子转移到径向振幅上，将积分化为适合傅里叶型DE求积的 sine 表示；同时识别与几何构型相关的振荡—阻尼比（oscillation-to-damping ratio），在弱振荡区建立收敛的矩展开（moment expansion），形成互补表示。并以半无限径向积分及基于Slater型函数的完整H2O与C2H4分子积分进行检验。

Result: 数值比较表明，与直接DE相比，S+DE组合在达到高精度时可显著减少所需求积节点数；矩展开的互补表示明显提高了在直接振荡求积失效区间内的数值可靠性。结果与完整S级数（S-series）评估及文献报道值进行了对照。

Conclusion: S变换与傅里叶型DE求积的结合，辅以弱振荡矩展开，为含振荡球贝塞尔函数的半无限径向积分及相应分子积分提供了准确、高效且可靠的数值计算途径。

Abstract: Three-centre two-electron Coulomb and hybrid integrals over exponential-type functions involve semi-infinite radial integrals containing oscillatory spherical Bessel functions. We investigate the combination of the $S$ transformation with Fourier-type double-exponential (DE) quadrature for their accurate and efficient numerical evaluation. The $S$ transformation transfers derivatives from the spherical-Bessel factor to the radial amplitude, converting the integral into a sine representation particularly well suited to Fourier-type DE quadrature. Numerical comparisons demonstrate that the combined $S+\mathrm{DE}$ approach can substantially reduce the number of quadrature nodes required to achieve high accuracy compared with direct DE. We further identify a geometry-dependent oscillation-to-damping ratio and establish a convergent moment expansion in the weakly oscillatory regime. Exploiting this complementary representation markedly enhances the numerical reliability of the algorithm in regimes where direct oscillatory quadrature becomes inefficient. The method is examined through semi-infinite radial integrals and complete H$_2$O and C$_2$H$_4$ molecular integrals over Slater-type functions, with comparisons against complete $S$-series evaluations and values reported in the literature.

</details>


### [187] [Computing with Orientation-Dependent Vector Fields in Crystallographic Texture Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23328)
*Ralf Hielscher,Erik Wünsche*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文提出一个用于表示取向依赖矢量场（orientation-dependent vector fields）的计算框架，将微分几何表述转译为晶体学记号，并以旋转矢量分量（rotation-vector components）作为数值友好的表示，配合广义球谐函数推导梯度、散度与旋度（gradient, divergence, curl）的频域公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体学应用中矢量场常以欧拉角导数（Euler-angle derivatives）分量表示，虽便于对应广义球谐函数（generalized spherical harmonics）经典表述，但数值上不利；因此需将描述单个取向小变化的旋转矢量推广到取向依赖矢量场。

Method: 基于既有微分几何框架（arXiv:2608.21235），转译为晶体学记号；用三个标量分量函数表示矢量场并展开为广义球谐函数，推导梯度、散度、旋度的频域公式，使其可直接在谐波系数上求值。

Result: 给出梯度、散度与旋度的频域计算公式；并指出带限标量函数的梯度仍保持同一带宽的带限性（band-limited）。

Conclusion: 旋转矢量分量表示可替代欧拉角导数表示，为晶体学织构分析（crystallographic texture analysis）中的取向依赖矢量场提供数值上更有利的谐波域计算途径。

Abstract: We present a computational framework for representing orientation-dependent vector fields. Building on (arXiv:2608.21235), we translate the differential-geometric formulation into crystallographic notation and focus on its computational use in crystallographic texture analysis.
  In crystallographic applications, small orientation changes are commonly described by rotation vectors, spin tensors, or infinitesimal misorientations. By contrast, vector fields in harmonic computations are usually represented by components with respect to Euler-angle derivatives, following the classical formulation of generalized spherical harmonics. This representation is convenient but numerically unfavorable. We instead use rotation-vector components, extending a familiar description of individual orientation changes to orientation-dependent vector fields.
  In this representation, vector fields are described by three scalar component functions, which can be expanded into generalized spherical harmonics. We derive frequency-domain formulas for gradient, divergence, and curl, enabling their evaluation directly on harmonic coefficients. In particular, the gradient of a band-limited scalar function remains band-limited with the same bandwidth.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [A Lie-Group Approach to Alfvénic Turbulence: The SO(3) Rotational p-model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24009)
*Daniele Telloni,Sabrina Guastavino,Gary P. Zank,Luca Sorriso-Valvo,Michele Piana*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 提出旋转 p-模型（rotational p-model），通过在球面流形 S² 上以李群形式构造随机微分方程，取代欧氏空间中的加性噪声合成方法，从而保持磁场模长等Alfvénic涨落的几何不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机模型（stochastic models）在平直欧氏空间中注入加性噪声来合成多尺度涨落，根本违背理想磁流体力学（ideal magnetohydrodynamics）的拓扑约束，破坏等离子体的动力学不可压缩性并引入虚假的磁压缩性。

Method: 将多重分形奇异测度（multifractal singular measure）直接映射到李代数 so(3)，并在球面流形 S² 上以 Stratonovich 意义对相关随机微分方程（stochastic differential equations）进行积分。

Result: 模型生成高度间歇、依赖于尺度的旋转动力学（rotational dynamics），保持磁场模长（magnetic field modulus），并完美再现空间等离子体中观测到的分数谱包络（fractional spectral envelopes）与非高斯方向间歇性（non-Gaussian directional intermittency）。

Conclusion: 通过分离纯方向性重取向，该框架对磁间歇性爆发（magnetic intermittent bursts）的等待时间统计（waiting time statistics）和角偏转的多重分形标度指数（multifractal scaling exponents）提供精确的解析控制，为非线性湍流现象研究提供新范式。

Abstract: The inner heliosphere is permeated by fully developed, highly intermittent nonlinear turbulence. Conventional stochastic models synthesize these multiscale fluctuations by injecting additive noise within a flat Euclidean space. However, such approaches fundamentally violate the topological constraints of ideal magnetohydrodynamics, inadvertently breaking the kinetic incompressibility of the plasma and introducing spurious magnetic compressibility. To preserve the exact geometrical invariants of Alfvénic fluctuations, a novel mathematical framework is presented: the rotational p-model. By mapping a multifractal singular measure directly into the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{so}(3)$ and integrating the associated stochastic differential equations in the Stratonovich sense on the spherical manifold $S^{2}$, the model generates highly intermittent, scale-dependent rotational dynamics. This Lie-group formulation conserves the magnetic field modulus while perfectly reproducing the fractional spectral envelopes and the non-Gaussian directional intermittency observed in space plasmas. Furthermore, by isolating purely directional reorientations, the framework provides exact analytical control over the waiting time statistics of magnetic intermittent bursts and the multifractal scaling exponents of angular deflections, offering a powerful new paradigm for the study of nonlinear turbulent phenomena.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Training for sustainable scientific software in the AI era: from code production to scientific stewardship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23330)
*Juliette Alimena*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 生成式与智能体式人工智能（generative and agentic AI）正改变研究者与科学软件的交互方式，可能削弱“产出成功计算成果”与“保留对关键决策和证据的科学掌控”之间的联系。作者主张，高能物理（high-energy physics）领域的训练重点应由代码生产转向科学治理（scientific stewardship），并应将 AI 内容纳入既有软件与分析训练之中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 对生产力的影响因任务和用户而异，但可能使研究者在不真正理解关键选择与证据的情况下获得可用计算产物，从而威胁科学能力。因此需要重新界定训练目标，以应对 AI 介入后科学软件工作流的可信性与可维护性问题。

Method: 采用观点论证（perspective/argument）方式，提出“具备 AI 意识的软件训练”（AI-aware software training）框架：将专门的 AI 系统教学与既有软件和分析训练相整合，在 AI 辅助工作流中训练测试、溯源（provenance）、可复现性、资源判断与科学验证等能力。

Result: 摘要未报告实证数据或实验评估；其论点性结论为：能力评价应以支撑结果的证据为依据，而非仅以代码生成能力为准，并将该问题框定为可持续科学软件（sustainable scientific software）问题。

Conclusion: AI 辅助工作流应保持可信与可维护、负责任地使用计算资源，并避免使核心能力不必要地依赖于对特定工具或基础设施的特权访问（privileged access）。

Abstract: Generative and agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems are changing how researchers interact with scientific software. Their effects on productivity vary across tasks and users, but they can weaken the connection between producing a successful computational artifact and retaining scientific command over the consequential choices and evidence on which it depends. For high-energy physics, this shifts part of the training challenge from code production toward scientific stewardship: researchers must be able to validate, explain, reproduce, maintain, and justify software and workflows partly generated or modified by AI. We argue that AI-aware software training should therefore combine dedicated instruction on AI systems with the integration of AI into established software and analysis training. Testing, provenance, reproducibility, resource judgment, and scientific validation should be exercised in AI-assisted workflows, with competence judged by the evidence supporting a result rather than by code generation alone. We frame this as a problem of sustainable scientific software: AI-assisted workflows should remain trustworthy and maintainable, use computational resources responsibly, and avoid making core competence unnecessarily dependent on privileged access to particular tools or infrastructure.

</details>

### [190] [Effective strain energy of soft laminates: Beyond neo-Hookean elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23538)
*Harold Berjamin,Stephan Rudykh*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于均匀化理论（homogenisation theory）估计软橡胶类交替层状周期介质的有效力学响应，并给出Mooney-Rivlin与Yeoh层合材料的显式或近似解析表达，进一步数值考察纯剪切下的宏观失稳阈值与剪切波速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合材料层合结构（composite laminates）可设计为兼具低重量与高刚度等工程性能，而由软橡胶类固体交替层构成的周期介质（periodic media）其宏观有效响应与稳定性尚需理论刻画。

Method: 假设各层不可压缩（incompressible）且完全粘结（perfectly bonded），通过计算各层储能的空间平均（spatial average）进行均匀化；针对弹性势能一般依赖两个标量不变量（scalar invariants）的层材料，除neo-Hookean固体外，对特殊类Mooney-Rivlin材料给出显式解析解，对Mooney-Rivlin层合板与Yeoh层合板给出近似表达式。

Result: 层状介质在宏观尺度可由横观各向同性（transversely isotropic）弹性理论描述；基于Gent型与Mooney-Rivlin型层组合的近似有效能量，数值估计纯剪切下的宏观失稳阈值，发现若层间能量函数满足特定比例关系，单层力学响应的影响可忽略；预应变压层介质中剪切波速（shear wave velocity）的影响与均质介质相当，非比例层合板（non-proportional laminates）中效应更显著。

Conclusion: 层合介质的宏观响应与稳定性主要由层间能量函数的比例关系决定，单层具体本构形式在比例条件下影响甚微，但在非比例层合情形下需考虑单层力学响应的显著作用。

Abstract: Composite laminates can be designed to reach desired engineering properties such as low weight and high stiffness. This study concerns periodic media made of alternating layers of soft rubber-like solids. Assuming incompressible and perfectly bonded elastic layers, the effective mechanical response of the laminate is estimated by means of homogenisation theory. This involves computing the spatial average of the energy stored in the layers, each of which is in equilibrium and homogeneously deformed. The study is conducted for layers whose elastic energy depends on two scalar invariants, in general. Beyond neo-Hookean solids, explicit analytical expressions are obtained for a special class of Mooney-Rivlin materials, whereas approximate expressions are obtained for Mooney-Rivlin laminates and for Yeoh laminates. These stratified media can be represented by a transversely isotropic elasticity theory at the macroscopic scale. Based on these results, an approximate effective energy for laminates whose layers combine Gent-type and Mooney-Rivlin behaviour is postulated. It is then used to estimate the macroscopic instability threshold under pure shear numerically. Results show that the specific mechanical response of individual layers has negligible influence on the macroscopic stability limit if a certain proportionality relationship between their energy functions holds. Furthermore, in a pre-stressed layered medium under pure shear, the influence of the individual mechanical response of the layers on the shear wave velocity is found to be comparable to that observed in homogeneous media, within the same range of investigated situations. Finally, more significant effects may be observed in non-proportional laminates.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Cwikel and Kato-Seiler-Simon estimates for commutators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24583)
*Rupert L. Frank*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 论文证明：当梯度 $\nabla a\in L^p$、$\nabla b\in L^p_{\rm weak}$ 且 $\max\{1,2d/(d+2)\}<p<\infty$ 时，交换子 $[a(X),b(-\mathrm{i}\nabla)]$ 属于弱 Schatten 类 $\mathcal{S}^p_{\rm weak}$；该结果是 Cwikel 估计的类比，且指数范围更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Cwikel 型估计对交换子的 Schatten 类隶属给出了指数下界限制，本文旨在放宽该指数阈值，并将相关估计（Kato-Seiler-Simon、Birman-Solomyak-Simon）推广到更一般的弱型设定。

Method: 采用弱 Schatten 类（weak Schatten class）$\mathcal{S}^p_{\rm weak}$ 与弱 $L^p$ 空间框架，在 $\nabla a\in L^p$ 与 $\nabla b\in L^p_{\rm weak}$ 的条件下证明交换子的弱 Schatten 隶属；并通过构造反例检验指数下界的必要性及 Lipschitz 端点估计的失效。

Result: 在 $\max\{1,2d/(d+2)\}<p<\infty$ 范围内得到交换子属于 $\mathcal{S}^p_{\rm weak}$ 的结论，其指数范围大于 Cwikel 估计；同时给出 Kato-Seiler-Simon 与 Birman-Solomyak-Simon 估计的类比形式。反例表明下阈值不可去除，且 Lipschitz 端点处的估计不成立。

Conclusion: 结果将 Cwikel 型交换子估计统一推广至弱 Schatten 类并扩展了可用指数区间，同时通过反例界定了该推广的最优性边界。

Abstract: We prove that the commutator $[a(X),b(-\mathrm{i}\nabla)]$ belongs to the weak Schatten class $\mathcal{S}^p_{\rm weak}$ provided $\nabla a\in L^p$ and $\nabla b\in L^p_{\rm weak}$ for $\max\{1,2d/(d+2)\}<p<\infty$. This is an analogue of Cwikel's estimate, with a larger range of exponents. We also prove analogues of the Kato-Seiler-Simon and Birman-Solomyak-Simon estimates. Counterexamples show the necessity of the lower thresholds and the failure of the Lipschitz endpoint estimate.

</details>
