### [192] [Trapped fermions frustrate multiterminal Josephson networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23390)
*Antonio Manesco,Karsten Flensberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究多端约瑟夫森阵列（multiterminal Josephson arrays）中由奇宇称三结（odd-parity Josephson trijunctions）中俘获费米子（trapped fermion）引起的宇称诱导阻挫（parity-induced frustration），发现其在经典极限下映射为Kagome XY反铁磁体（Kagome XY antiferromagnet），产生代数衰减的6e相位相干（6e phase coherence），并体现为电流-相位关系（current-phase relation）中的显著三次谐波（third harmonic）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子的相干控制（coherent control）已成为量子器件工程的标准手段，本文旨在探索宇称诱导的阻挫（parity-induced frustration）在多端约瑟夫森阵列中所能带来的新相干效应，将费米子诱导阻挫确立为复合超导相干与相位介导自旋纠缠的实现途径。

Method: 聚焦奇宇称约瑟夫森三结（odd-parity Josephson trijunctions）：每个三结中俘获费米子诱导相位绕转（phase winding），连接多个三结以约束其共享相位，形成长程相位相干（long-distance phase coherence）。在经典区间将阵列映射为Kagome XY反铁磁体（Kagome XY antiferromagnet），并分析量子相位涨落（quantum phase fluctuations）与自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的作用。

Result: 阻挫构型主导低能物理（low-energy physics）；经典区间中该映射的Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）转变给出代数衰减的6e相位关联；6e相干表现为阵列电流-相位关系中显著的三次谐波；量子相位涨落虽抑制6e相干，但通过自旋-轨道耦合在俘获费米子间产生自旋关联（spin correlations）与纠缠（entanglement）。

Conclusion: 费米子诱导阻挫（fermion-induced frustration）可作为在超导体-半导体人工量子材料（superconductor-semiconductor artificial quantum materials）中实现复合超导相干（composite superconducting coherence）与相位介导自旋纠缠（phase-mediated spin entanglement）的有效路径。

Abstract: Coherent control of fermions has become a standard approach for engineering quantum devices. Here, we explore parity-induced frustration in multiterminal Josephson arrays. We focus on odd-parity Josephson trijunctions, where the presence of a trapped fermion induces a phase winding in each trijunction. Connecting these trijunctions constrains their shared phases, resulting in a long-distance phase coherence where the frustrated configurations dominate the low-energy physics. In the classical regime, these arrays map onto the Kagome XY antiferromagnet, whose established Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) transition yields algebraically decaying $6e$ phase correlations. We show that the $6e$ phase coherence is manifested as a pronounced third harmonic in the current-phase relation of these arrays. While quantum phase fluctuations suppress the $6e$ coherence, we show that through spin-orbit coupling they also lead to spin correlations and entanglement between the trapped fermions. Our results establish fermion-induced frustration as a route to composite superconducting coherence and phase-mediated spin entanglement in superconductor-semiconductor artificial quantum materials.

</details>


### [193] [Real-Space Theory of Nonequilibrium Orbital Magnetization in Inhomogeneous Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23404)
*João M. Alendouro Pinho,Henrique P. Veiga,Alessandro Veneri,Daniel Passos,Ivo Souza,Bruno Amorim,João M. Viana Parente Lopes,Aires Ferreira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文提出一种针对缺乏平移不变性块体材料的实空间线性响应理论，用于描述直流电场诱导的轨道磁化（orbital magnetization），并给出规范不变的表述形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨道磁电效应（orbital magneto-electric effects）理论主要建立在平移不变晶体图像上，难以直接处理无序体系，因此需要发展不依赖平移不变性的实空间框架。

Method: 从对缓变电磁场的电流响应出发，推导无相互作用电子线性于波矢的电导张量（linear-in-wavevector conductivity tensor），以单粒子格林函数（single-particle Green's functions）及速度、位置算符表达；并采用大规模切比雪夫展开（Chebyshev expansions）进行数值实现。

Result: 该形式在平移不变极限下退化为已有的布洛赫轨道磁电效应图像；在存在空间不相关无序的 Haldane 模型（Haldane model）上展示了其适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了适用于完美晶体与无序体系的实空间、规范不变长波非平衡轨道磁化理论，并经由大规模数值方法得到验证。

Abstract: We put forward a real-space linear-response theory of orbital magnetization induced by a dc electric field in bulk materials lacking translation invariance. Starting from the current response to a slowly varying electromagnetic field, we show that the linear-in-wavevector conductivity tensor of non-interacting electrons in terms of single-particle Green's functions and the velocity and position operators. This provides a gauge-invariant real-space formulation of the long-wavelength non-equilibrium orbital magnetization of both perfect crystals and disordered systems. In the translationally invariant limit, the formalism recovers the established Bloch picture of orbital magneto-electric effects. We implement the formalism using large-scale Chebyshev expansions and illustrate its capabilities on a Haldane model in the presence of spatially uncorrelated disorder.

</details>


### [194] [Towards Defect Engineering of Orbital Magnetoelectric Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23405)
*João M. Alendouro Pinho,Henrique P. Veiga,Alessandro Veneri,Daniel Passos,Ivo Souza,Bruno Amorim,João M. Viana Parente Lopes,Aires Ferreira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文采用实空间框架研究金属轨道反铁磁体中的电荷-轨道转换，揭示点缺陷可显著增强时间奇磁电效应并诱发原本禁戒的时间偶响应，同时提出局域标记刻画非平衡轨道磁化的空间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡态下的轨道磁化（orbital magnetization）已有充分研究，但由光、电场等外场刺激产生的轨道磁性理解仍处于起步阶段；且传统布洛赫能带（Bloch-band）图像难以描述超越能带框架的轨道磁电响应。

Method: 采用新颖的实空间框架（real-space framework），在超越常规布洛赫能带图像的前提下研究金属轨道反铁磁体（orbital antiferromagnet）中的电荷-轨道转换（charge-to-orbital conversion），并引入可提供空间分辨信息的局域标记（local marker）。

Result: 常见点缺陷（point defects）导致时间奇磁电效应（time-odd magnetoelectric effects）显著增强，并依其局域对称性激活原本禁戒的时间偶响应（time-even responses）；实空间映射显示点缺陷形成纳米尺度轨道磁化热点（hotspots），深刻改变体磁电响应，甚至引起净轨道矩（net orbital moment）符号反转。

Conclusion: 研究结果提示可借助点缺陷与杂质，以原子级精度（atomic-scale precision）工程化线性响应轨道矩，为轨道磁性的缺陷调控提供可能。

Abstract: Orbital magnetization is a fundamental measure of time-reversal symmetry breaking in solids that is accessible by experiments. However, unlike the well-studied equilibrium case, the understanding of orbital magnetism resulting from external stimuli such as light and electric fields is still in its infancy. Here we employ a novel real-space framework to investigate charge-to-orbital conversion in a metallic orbital antiferromagnet beyond the conventional Bloch-band picture. We find that common point defects give rise to strongly enhanced time-odd magnetoelectric effects and, depending on their local symmetry, further activate otherwise-forbidden time-even responses. Moreover, we introduce a local marker that provides spatially resolved information on the orbital magnetization carried by non-equilibrium states. Real-space maps reveal that point defects create nanoscale hotspots of orbital magnetization leading to a profound modification of the bulk magnetoelectric response, including a sign reversal of the net orbital moment. Our findings may open up the possibility of engineering linear-response orbital moments with atomic-scale precision by point defects and impurities.

</details>


### [195] [Efficient interlayer interactions in non-planar molecule/transition-metal dichalcogenide two-dimensional heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23435)
*Johann Alvarez,Kamal Harrison,John Koptur-Palenchar,Miguel Gakiya-Teruya,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Michael Shatruk,Xiao-Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究将可机械剥离的非平面自旋交叉（spin-crossover, SCO）分子晶体 FTBQS 与单层过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）及六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）间隔层组装为洁净范德华异质结构，并通过光谱手段揭示其层间相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子与二维原子材料集成可拓展混合电子器件，但既有进展多集中于平面分子；非平面分子因配体呈三维取向，难以嵌入二维异质结构，相关层间相互作用尚不清楚。

Method: 利用 [Fe(tBu2qsal)2]（FTBQS）分子晶体的可剥离特性实现确定性洁净组装，构建 FTBQS/TMD/hBN 异质结构，并结合光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）光谱与栅压调控光谱表征层间耦合。

Result: 观测到强烈 PL 猝灭，即使存在 hBN 间隔层仍显著，表明存在长衰减距离的 Förster 共振能量转移（Förster resonance energy transfer）；栅压依赖光谱进一步显示，因 TMD/FTBQS 能带对齐导致的 TMD 层掺杂移动指示电荷转移。

Conclusion: 结果确立非平面离散分子与二维晶体之间存在高效层间相互作用，为混合电学、光学与自旋电子（spintronic）器件提供可行路径。

Abstract: Integration of molecules and two-dimensional (2D) atomic materials offer new opportunities for hybrid electronic devices. Most progress has focused on interfacing planar molecule. In contrast, incorporating non-planar molecules into 2D heterostructures remains challenging due to the three-dimensional orientations of ligands. Here we investigate interlayer interactions between discrete non-planar spin-crossover (SCO) molecules, [Fe(tBu2qsal)2] (FTBQS), monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs), and hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) spacers through optical spectroscopies. FTBQS features bulk molecular crystal layers and can be mechanically exfoliated, enabling deterministic assembly of clean hybrid heterostructures. Robust photoluminescence (PL) quenching, even with an hBN spacer, indicates efficient Förster resonance energy transfer with a long decay distance. Gate-dependent optical spectroscopy further identifies charge transfer from the doping shifts in the TMD layers due to the TMD/FTBQS electronic band alignment. Our results establish efficient interlayer interactions between non-plana, discrete molecules and 2D crystals for hybrid electrical, optical, and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [196] [Two-Dimensional Ferromagnetic Weyl Half-Semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23688)
*Yu-Yang Zhao,Chen-Hao Lv,Qing-Bo Liu,Wen-Neng Zhao,Xin-Yi Chen,Lun Xiong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作基于第一性原理计算与蒙特卡洛模拟，预测单层 BaMn₄O₈ 为动力学、热学与力学稳定的铁磁外尔半半金属，其居里温度估计约 1446 K，且外尔点与费米弧在自旋轨道耦合下保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维铁磁拓扑材料是实现自旋极化拓扑态的理想平台，但外尔费米子（Weyl fermions）与稳健高温铁磁性（ferromagnetism）的共存仍未被实现，构成本文的研究动机。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）结合蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations），并在 20×20×1 格点上进行各向异性蒙特卡洛模拟以估计有限尺寸相变温度，同时分析自旋分辨电子结构及自旋轨道耦合（SOC）的影响。

Result: 单层 BaMn₄O₈ 的有限尺寸相变温度估计约为 1446 K；其自旋向上通道在费米能级附近具有线性能带交叉，自旋向下通道保持绝缘，沿 (100) 边界可观察到连接投影外尔点（WPs）的费米弧（Fermi arcs），且外尔点与费米弧在 SOC 下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 单层 BaMn₄O₈ 被确认为全自旋极化拓扑输运（fully spin-polarized topological transport）的候选材料，为二维铁磁外尔半半金属的实验探索提供理论依据。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) ferromagnetic topological materials provide appealing platforms for realizing spin-polarized topological states, yet the coexistence of Weyl fermions and robust high-temperature ferromagnetism remains elusive. Here, based on first-principles calculations and Monte Carlo simulations, we predict monolayer BaMn$_4$O$_8$ to be a dynamically, thermally, and mechanically stable ferromagnetic Weyl half-semimetal. \textcolor{blue}{An anisotropic Monte Carlo simulation on a $20\times20\times1$ lattice yields a finite-size transition estimate of approximately 1446~K.} The spin-resolved electronic structure reveals a fully spin-polarized state, in which the spin-up channel hosts linear Weyl band crossings near the Fermi level, whereas the spin-down channel remains insulating. Corresponding Fermi-arc edge states connecting the projected Weyl points (WPs) are clearly observed along the (100) boundary, confirming the nontrivial topology. Importantly, both the Weyl points and their associated Fermi arcs remain robust in the presence of spin--orbit coupling (SOC). \textcolor{blue}{These results identify monolayer BaMn$_4$O$_8$ as a candidate for fully spin-polarized topological transport.

</details>


### [197] [Breaking the Myth of Four-Tier Stacked Transistors for Logic: an Investigation from Process to Device and Block-Level PPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23806)
*Jianxiang Jin,Haoran Lu,Yihan Luo,Yanbang Chu,Yibo Zhang,Jingru Jiang,Yuji Cheng,Wanyue Peng,Xun Jiang,Feiyu Teng,Kairang Guo,Ming Li,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang,Heng Wu,Ru Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作首次评估四层堆叠晶体管（4-tier stacked transistors）能否将密度增益转化为实际的芯片级缩放收益，结果表明单纯垂直堆叠不足以保证足够的PPA（power, performance, area）收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四层堆叠晶体管在单元高度上具有名义上的显著缩减，但其密度增益能否在真实芯片层面转化为可实现的缩放收益尚未被验证，因此需要系统评估其实际价值。

Method: 提出实用的双面四层晶体管堆叠 CFFET（Flipped CFET-to-CFET）工艺流程，并在2A节点设计规则下构建覆盖标准单元设计、局部互连、环形振荡器（RO）分析与物理实现的DTCO（design-technology co-optimization）框架，同时与两层堆叠的FFET进行基准对比，并引入类俄罗斯方块（Tetris-like）的单元拼接方法与优化的NanoStack CFFET方案。

Result: CFFET中表现最佳的对称电源（Symmetric Power）配置因复杂的MOL（middle-of-line）寄生效应，RO频率仍落后FFET 11.2%；块级上Ibex RISC-V核面积仅缩减12.8%（远低于40%的名义单元高度缩减），且在等功耗下慢10.9%；Tetris-like单元拼接在1-bit ALU设计中回收7.4%面积。

Conclusion: 直接的垂直四层堆叠不足以确保充足的PPA收益，但由此获得的设计洞见可为未来先进架构提供指导。

Abstract: For the first time, this work evaluated whether 4-tier stacked transistors can translate the straightforward density gains into realistic chip-level scaling benefits. A practical dual-sided 4-tier transistor-stacking CFFET (Flipped CFET-to-CFET) process was proposed, on which a DTCO framework spanning standard-cell design, local interconnects, RO analysis, and physical implementation was built under 2A node design rules. The CFFET was also benchmarked with its 2-tier transistor-stacking counterparts FFET. Among CFFET configurations, the best-performing Symmetric Power still lags FFET by 11.2% in RO frequency due to the complex MOL parasitics. At the block level, CFFET reduces the Ibex RISC-V core area by only 12.8% (far below the 40% nominal cell-height reduction), while running 10.9% slower at iso-power. We further introduced a novel Tetris-like cell stitching to minimize the area overhead, which allows non-rectangular cell design and recovers 7.4% area in a 1-bit ALU design. An optimized NanoStack CFFET was finally proposed, inspired by previous work. This work indicates that straightforward vertical 4-tier stacking proves insufficient to guarantee enough PPA benefits, though the resulting design insights may guide future advanced architectures.

</details>


### [198] [Non-Abelian Hall Effects of Flavor-Waves in $\mathbb{CP}^{2}$ Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24134)
*Carlos Saji,Eduardo Saavedra,Alvaro S. Nunez,Roberto E. Troncoso*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在三角晶格自旋-1磁体的SU(3)相干态框架下研究孤立与晶态CP^2斯格明子的集体激发，揭示多极激发与非阿贝尔输运及拓扑能带现象的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解投影型磁织构（projective magnetic textures）如何将多极（multipolar）集体激发与非阿贝尔输运和拓扑能带现象联系起来，而此前对CP^2斯格明子的激发谱及拓扑输运缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 采用SU(3)相干态框架，以两个耦合玻色子味（bosonic flavors）描述磁偶极与四极矩涨落，并将单自旋翻转与双自旋翻转激发在非均匀CP^2织构下混合；进而建立玻色Bogoliubov–de Gennes问题并考察矩阵值规范联络（matrix-valued gauge connection），对孤立斯格明子计算谱与散射动力学，对数值弛豫的斯格明子晶体计算集体模式能带。

Result: 孤立斯格明子的谱中包含局域模式（localized modes），其散射动力学表现出横向偏转（transverse deflection）伴随同位旋进动（isospin precession）；斯格明子晶体的集体模式能带具有非零陈数（Chern numbers），并据此计算了相应的热霍尔响应（thermal Hall response）。

Conclusion: 结果表明投影型磁织构可将多极激发与非阿贝尔输运和拓扑能带现象耦合起来，为在自旋-1磁体中探索拓扑多极激发提供框架。

Abstract: We investigate the collective excitations of isolated and crystalline $\mathbb{CP}^{2}$ skyrmions in a two-dimensional triangular-lattice spin-1 magnet. Within an $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ coherent-state framework, fluctuations of the magnetic dipole and quadrupole moments are described by two coupled bosonic flavors. In a uniform spin-polarized state, these flavors can be identified with single-spin-flip and double-spin-flip excitations, whereas a nonuniform $\mathbb{CP}^{2}$ texture mixes their character. We formulate the corresponding bosonic Bogoliubov--de Gennes problem and examine the matrix-valued gauge connection governing flavor-wave propagation. For isolated skyrmions, the calculated spectrum contains localized modes, and the scattering dynamics exhibit transverse deflection accompanied by isospin precession. For a numerically relaxed skyrmion crystal, we obtain collective-mode bands with nonzero Chern numbers and calculate the associated thermal Hall response. These findings illustrate how projective magnetic textures couple multipolar excitations to non-Abelian transport and topological band phenomena.

</details>


### [199] [Spin dynamics in disordered systems with generic spin-momentum couplings: A matrix-rational formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24346)
*Masataka Kakoi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种微观框架，用于处理具有各向异性、非线性自旋-动量耦合的赝自旋-1/2体系的无序平均密度矩阵动力学，核心是对Diffuson频率依赖的矩阵值有理近似。该方法将极点方程转化为二次特征值问题并构造实时演化，在多种模型中与直接数值模拟定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖低频或弱耦合展开，难以统一描述超快自旋动力学与长时间弛豫，尤其在各向异性、非线性自旋-动量耦合体系中缺乏适用框架。

Method: 从Diffuson出发，引入不依赖低频或弱耦合展开的矩阵值有理近似（matrix-valued rational approximation）描述其频率依赖；将极点方程重构为二次特征值问题（quadratic eigenvalue problem），据此构造无序平均密度矩阵的实时演化。

Result: 应用于Rashba与线性、立方Dresselhaus耦合的二维电子气、含Rashba耦合的d波交错磁体（altermagnet）及三维双Weyl模型；与直接数值模拟相比，在跨越较宽耦合强度范围（超出扩散区）的淬火后自旋动力学（post-quench spin dynamics）与自旋弛豫时间上均定量吻合。

Conclusion: 该矩阵有理形式化（matrix-rational formulation）为超快自旋动力学与长时间弛豫提供了统一描述。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic framework for the disorder-averaged density-matrix dynamics of pseudospin-$1/2$ systems with anisotropic and nonlinear spin-momentum couplings. Starting from the Diffuson, we introduce a matrix-valued rational approximation for its frequency dependence that does not rely on a low-frequency or weak-coupling expansion. The resulting pole equation is recast as a quadratic eigenvalue problem, from which the real-time evolution of the disorder-averaged density matrix is constructed. We apply the formulation to a two-dimensional electron gas with Rashba and linear and cubic Dresselhaus couplings, a $d$-wave altermagnet with Rashba coupling, and a three-dimensional double-Weyl model. Comparisons with direct numerical simulations demonstrate quantitative agreement for both the post-quench spin dynamics and spin relaxation times across a broad range of coupling strengths, extending beyond the diffusive regime. The matrix-rational formulation provides a unified description of ultrafast spin dynamics and long-time relaxation.

</details>


### [200] [Spontaneous emission of atomic systems near metallic and semiconducting atomic chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24358)
*Abhishek Ghosh,David Dams,Antton Babaze,Garnett Bryant,Carsten Rockstuhl,Andrés Ayuela,Karolina Słowik,Marta Pelc*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过调节Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链的二聚化程度，可将有限原子链从金属性调至半导体性，从而调控其附近量子发射体的自发辐射增强的强度、带宽与空间结构；库仑相互作用进一步修饰光学共振，相关机制在反式聚乙炔与石墨烯纳米带等实际准一维体系中同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解原子尺度散射体的电子结构及照明分布如何决定近场自发辐射（spontaneous emission）增强特性，为原子尺度光-物质相互作用的可调工程化提供依据。

Method: 采用Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型描述有限原子链，通过二聚化（dimerization）实现金属—半导体转变，并分析电子结构、照明分布、光谱匹配与发射体位置对辐射增强的影响，同时考虑库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions）。

Result: 金属性链的近似谐振能谱导致光学跃迁重叠并产生强共振；半导体性链因能隙打开改变电子态密度（density of electronic states），使响应分布于更宽光谱范围且峰值强度降低。库仑相互作用引起蓝移（blueshift）或改变共振的空间特性，对集体激发与单粒子型激发作用不同；相同机制在反式聚乙炔（trans-polyacetylene）链与石墨烯纳米带（graphene nanoribbons）中得以保持。

Conclusion: 研究结果指出了原子尺度光-物质相互作用工程化的一般方向，并可为可调纳米尺度光子学（photonics）与量子光学（quantum-optical）器件提供设计原则。

Abstract: We investigate the spontaneous emission of a quantum emitter placed near finite atomic chains described by the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model. By tuning the chain from a metallic to a semiconducting character through dimerization, we analyze how the electronic structure of an atomic-scale scatterer, together with the illumination profile, determines the strength, bandwidth, and spatial structure of the spontaneous emission enhancement. The resonant enhancement depends on both spectral matching and the emitter position relative to the induced charge and field patterns. In metallic chains, the nearly harmonic energy spectrum leads to overlapping optical transitions and strong resonances. For semiconducting chains, dimerization opens a gap and leads to a modified density of electronic states, redistributing the response over a broader spectral range and reducing its peak intensity. Coulomb interactions affect the optical resonances through blueshift or modification of their spatial character, and act differently on collective and single-particle-like excitations. Finally, we show that the same mechanisms demonstrated in atomic chains persist in realistic quasi-one-dimensional systems, including trans-polyacetylene chains and graphene nanoribbons. Our findings indicate a general direction for engineering light-matter interactions at the atomic scale, and may offer design principles for tunable nanoscale photonic and quantum-optical devices.

</details>


### [201] [Unified Theory for Momentum-Space Nonsymmorphic Symmetry of Spin Space Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24629)
*Peiyuan Wang,Zheng Zhang,Y. X. Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文建立了自旋空间群（spin space groups, SSGs）在动量空间实现非简单（nonsymmorphic）对称性的一般理论，给出分数倒格矢平移的通用公式与可实现性判据，并据此构建覆盖全部 67,475 个 SSGs 的双向可查询数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影晶体对称性（projective crystal symmetry）可在动量空间产生非简单对称结构，但自旋空间群在动量空间中实现这类结构的普适理论此前缺失，制约了自旋-1/2 体系能带理论（band theory）的系统化发展。

Method: 论证动量空间非简单作用仅当幺正平移子群与绕共同轴的旋转相结合时才可能（共面或非共面 SSG，自旋-1/2 体系）；指出其因子系统（factor system）完全源于双覆盖（double cover）Spin(2)→SO(2)，导出分数倒格矢平移及布里渊区（Brillouin zone）点群作用的通用公式；并给出可实现性的充要条件证明。

Result: 证明动量空间非简单群可由 SSG 实现当且仅当其布里渊区作用包含二点轨道（two-point orbit）；由此 1,357 个非简单磁空间群（magnetic space groups）中有 842 个可由 SSG 实现；并构建覆盖全部 67,475 个 SSGs 的数据库，支持从 SSG 到动量空间磁群、以及反向的双向查询。

Conclusion: 该工作为自旋-1/2 体系中 SSGs 的系统能带理论奠定了动量空间对称性基础，并提供了可实际检索的完整数据库工具。

Abstract: Projective crystal symmetry provides a route to nonsymmorphic symmetry structures in momentum space, but a general theory for their realization in spin space groups has been lacking. We show that a nonsymmorphic momentum-space action is possible only when the unitary translation subgroup is combined with rotations about a common axis for a coplanar or noncoplanar spin space group of spin-$1/2$ systems. Its factor system originates entirely from the double cover $\mathrm{Spin}(2)\to SO(2)$, yielding a general formula for the fractional reciprocal-lattice translations and hence for the point-group action on the Brillouin zone. We further prove that a momentum-space nonsymmorphic group is realizable by a spin space group if and only if its Brillouin-zone action contains a two-point orbit. Consequently, 842 of the 1,357 nonsymmorphic magnetic space groups are realizable by spin space groups. Applying this theory, we construct a database covering all 67,475 SSGs that can be queried bidirectionally: from an SSG to its momentum-space magnetic group, or from a given momentum-space magnetic group to all SSGs that realize it. Together, these results establish the momentum-space symmetry foundation for a systematic band theory of SSGs in spin-$1/2$ systems.

</details>


### [202] [Zero-Temperature Energetics of a First-Order Surface Phase Transition from Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24919)
*Mohammad Tajik,Christian Brand,Stefan Wippermann,Wolf Gero Schmidt,Michael Horn-von Hoegen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用反射高能电子衍射（RHEED）强度的德拜-沃勒衰减（Debye-Waller attenuation），实验提取Si(111)-In原子线体系两相的德拜温度（Debye temperature），并据此定量分解一级相变中的零点能、振动能与熵贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一级相变（first-order phase transition）由零温能量、热振动能量与熵之间的平衡决定，但这些热力学参量通常难以通过实验直接获得，因此需要建立可实验测定的途径。

Method: 以原型Si(111)-In原子线（atomic-wire）体系为对象，测量RHEED强度随温度变化的德拜-沃勒衰减，分别确定低温(8×2)基态与高温(4×1)相的德拜温度（Debye temperature），并结合临界温度Tc处的热力学平衡分析内部能与熵的竞争。

Result: 高温(4×1)相的振动谱更软，由此给出稳定该相的熵增益；在Tc = 125 K处评估内能与熵的平衡，得到零温能量差ΔE0 = 138 meV/(8×2)，其中包含两相振动零点能（zero-point energy）之差。

Conclusion: 基于衍射的方法可直接获取一级表面相变（first-order surface phase transition）中此前难以测量的隐含热力学参数，为能量-熵平衡的实验判定提供了可行路径。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions are governed by the balance between zero-temperature energy, thermal vibrational energy, and entropy, yet these contributions are rarely accessible experimentally. Here, we establish a diffraction-based route to determine them for the prototypical Si(111)-In atomic-wire system. From the temperature-dependent Debye-Waller attenuation of reflection high-energy electron diffraction (RHEED) intensities we extract distinct Debye temperatures for the low-temperature $(8{\times}2)$ ground state and the high-temperature $(4{\times}1)$ phase. The softer vibrational spectrum of the high-temperature phase yields the entropy gain that stabilizes this phase above the critical temperature $T_\mathrm{c}$. Evaluating the thermodynamic balance between internal energy and entropy at $T_\mathrm{c} = 125\,\mathrm{K}$ yields a zero-temperature energy difference of $ΔE_0 = 138\,\mathrm{meV}/(8{\times}2)$, including the difference in vibrational zero-point energies of the two phases, thus providing direct access to hidden thermodynamic parameters of a first-order surface phase transition.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [The Price of Discovery: Cost-Optimized Detectors for the GUT-Scale QCD Axion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23374)
*Joshua N. Benabou,Joshua W. Foster,Benjamin R. Safdi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了在0.5 neV及以上质量区间探测DFSZ强度QCD轴子暗物质的实验室方案，以历史数据校准的成本标度律为统一度量，发现集总元件环形探测器（toroid）是最有前景且最具成本效益的拓扑结构。最优设计为一台约14米直径、6 T NbTi磁体的屏蔽环形装置，磁体与低温系统约1.2亿瑞士法郎；若读出指标达标，5米环形装置约2500万瑞士法郎即可胜任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大统一理论与弦理论预言质量在10^-11至10^-8 eV的QCD轴子（QCD axion），异弦（heterotic string）理论则指向0.5-0.8 neV，因此需要明确在该质量区间以最低成本发现或排除DFSZ强度轴子暗物质的实验方案。

Method: 在统一成本度量下优化环形（toroid）、螺线管（solenoid）与磁芯集总元件（lumped-element）三类拓扑，成本由经历史数据校准的标度律估算；同时评估超导射频腔（superconducting radio-frequency cavity）外差探测与核磁共振（CASPEr）方案，并发布开源有限元多物理软件包axionMultiPhysics。

Result: 环形拓扑最优：14米、6 T方案约1.2亿瑞士法郎，读出达标时5米、2500万瑞士法郎方案可行；其最优磁场远低于DMRadio-GUT提出的16 T，后者成本约为最优方案的两倍。优化腔形的超导射频腔外差探测成本与集总元件方案相当至两倍，但所需腔体积达约50-2000 m^3；CASPEr核磁共振方案则需自旋极化与相干性数量级的提升。

Conclusion: 基于已验证读出技术的集总元件环形探测器可在0.5 neV及以上以最低成本探测DFSZ强度QCD轴子暗物质；环形拓扑在成本效益上优于螺线管与磁芯方案，也优于DMRadio-GUT的高场设计与腔式外差方案。

Abstract: Grand unification and string theory point to a quantum chromodynamics (QCD) axion with mass between $10^{-11}$ and $10^{-8}$ eV, while heterotic string theory suggests a mass of $0.5-0.8$ neV. We study which laboratory experiment could discover or exclude DFSZ-strength QCD axion dark matter at and above 0.5 neV at the lowest cost, which we estimate from scaling laws calibrated to historical data. We find that a lumped-element detector, building on the ABRACADABRA concept, can do so with demonstrated readout technology; an example is a shielded toroid about 14 m across with a 6 T NbTi magnet, whose magnet and cryostat we estimate at around 120 million Swiss francs (MCHF). If projected readout targets are met, a 5 m toroid at 25 MCHF suffices. We find these designs by optimizing the toroid, solenoid, and core lumped-element topologies under a common cost metric. The toroid emerges as the most promising and cost-effective topology. The optimal field of the toroid detector is far below the 16 T of the proposed DMRadio-GUT design, which would cost nearly twice as much as our optimum. Heterodyne searches with superconducting radio-frequency cavities, with cavity shapes that we optimize for price, cost between about the same as and twice as much as the lumped-element detectors, but require cavities of volumes $\sim50-2000$ m$^3$. We find that nuclear-magnetic-resonance searches (CASPEr) need orders-of-magnitude gains in spin polarization and coherence. We release axionMultiPhysics, an open-source finite-element and multiphysics package for, e.g., magnetoquasistatic and full-wave electromagnetics, superconductors, readout circuits, and mechanics, from which most of our numerical results were obtained.

</details>


### [204] [Perspective on Proton Transversity Distribution Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23577)
*Ke-Xin Zeng,Peng Cheng,Yang Yu,Daniele Binosi,Craig D. Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用质子张量荷（proton tensor charges）信息与微扰量子色动力学（perturbative QCD）约束，构造质子内价夸克横向度分布函数（transversity DFs）的拟设，并借助 QCD 有效荷（QCD effective charge）给出 ζ₂ = 2 GeV 处 x-依赖的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用于推断质子横向度分布函数的数据极为稀少，亟需在有限信息下给出可靠的理论预测，以指导相关实验与唯象拟合。

Method: 以质子张量荷与微扰 QCD 约束为基础构造强子标度价夸克横向度分布函数的拟设（Ansaetze），并采用 QCD 有效荷所定义的全阶精确（all-orders exact）演化方案进行演化。

Result: 给出 ζ₂ = 2 GeV 标度下这些分布函数 x-依赖的预测；尽管方法以代数计算为主，其结果与计算密集型方法所得结果比较后仍具可行性。

Conclusion: 鉴于当前推断质子横向度分布函数的数据稀缺，本文结果可用于为相关实验与唯象拟合提供参考与约束。

Abstract: Using available information on proton tensor charges and constraints from perturbative quantum chromodynamics (QCD), Ansaetze are introduced for hadron-scale in-proton valence-quark transversity distributions functions (DFs). The concept of a QCD effective charge, which defines an evolution scheme for collinear parton distribution and fragmentation functions that is all-orders exact, is then used to deliver predictions for the $x$-dependence of these DFs at the scale $ζ_{2} = 2\,$GeV. Although the approach is largely algebraic, its predictions remain viable after comparisons with results obtained using computationally intensive methods. Thus, given the scarcity of data that can today be used for inference of proton transversity DFs, it may be worth using the results herein to inform and constrain related experiments and phenomenological fits.

</details>


### [205] [Geoneutrinos (and reactor neutrinos) do not visibly decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23978)
*Mauricio Bustamante*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用地球中铀、钍放射性衰变产生的“地球中微子（geoneutrinos）”这一低能、长基线源，首次在Borexino、KamLAND、SNO+和JUNO实验中检验中微子的可见衰变（visible decay），并在给定地球模型时给出对 $\nu_1 \to \nu_3$ 的首个地面实验限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子在传播至探测器的途中若发生衰变，会在低能端表现得更显著，因为时间膨胀（time dilation）对寿命的延长作用最弱。地球中微子能量仅数 MeV、穿越距离可达地球直径，为检验低能长基线中微子衰变提供了独特且此前未被使用的手段。

Method: 计算可见衰变情形（子中微子本身也被探测）下Borexino、KamLAND、SNO+与JUNO的地球中微子信号，并与各实验已发表数据比对；分别在“地球放射性产热功率自由”与“取自地球模型”两种假设下，对 $\nu_1 \to \nu_3$（倒质量序，inverted mass ordering）做统计分析并给出90%置信水平限制。

Result: 未发现能在不同地球模型下均成立的衰变证据。四实验联合给出：地球产热功率自由时 $\tau_1/m_1 > 3.7\times10^{-9}\ \mathrm{s\,eV^{-1}}$；采用地球模型时为 $\tau_1/m_1 > 4.4\times10^{-9}$ 至 $1.6\times10^{-8}\ \mathrm{s\,eV^{-1}}$。

Conclusion: 假定地球模型时，限制主要由地球中微子给出；若产热功率自由，同批实验探测到的反应堆反中微子（reactor antineutrinos）贡献与地球中微子相当。该工作首次给出对 $\nu_1$ 可见衰变的地面（terrestrial）限制。

Abstract: Neutrinos that decay on their way to a detector arrive depleted, more so at low energies, where time dilation lengthens their lifetime the least. Geoneutrinos---electron antineutrinos from the radioactive decay of uranium and thorium inside the Earth---have energies of a few MeV and travel up to the diameter of the Earth. We use them to test neutrino decay for the first time. We compute the geoneutrino signal at Borexino, KamLAND, SNO+, and JUNO for visible decay, in which the daughter neutrinos are themselves detected, and compare it against their published data. We find no evidence of decay that holds across Earth models. Combining the four experiments, we limit the decay $ν_1 \to ν_3$ in the inverted mass ordering at 90% C.L.: $τ_1/m_1 > 3.7 \times 10^{-9}$ s eV$^{-1}$ when the radiogenic power of the Earth is left free and $τ_1/m_1 > 4.4 \times 10^{-9}$ to $1.6 \times 10^{-8}$ s eV$^{-1}$ when it is taken from Earth models. With an Earth model assumed, the geoneutrinos set the limit; with the power free, the antineutrinos from nuclear reactors that the same experiments detect count as much as the geoneutrinos. These are the first terrestrial limits on the visible decay of $ν_1$.

</details>


### [206] [Total Angular Momentum Assignments in the Hadron Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24435)
*The HADLENS Collaboration,S. Rostami,Omid Zamani Jamshidi,B. Dehghanzad,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个数据驱动的 XGBoost 分类模型，利用 PDG 中已知介子与重子的谱学信息，在给定宇称假设的条件下预测自旋未知强子共振态的总角动量 J，并将预测结果与多种理论方法进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上尚未确定自旋的强子共振态数量可观，而 J 的确定是强子谱学（hadron spectroscopy）的关键环节；因此需要一种能够借助已知强子数据推断未知自旋的数据驱动方法。

Method: 采用 XGBoost 分类器（XGBoost classifiers）并结合嵌套优化（nested optimization），以 PDG 中已知介子（mesons）和重子（baryons）的夸克组分（quark content）、同位旋（isospin）、宇称（parity）、质量及其他谱学特征为输入，挖掘不同强子扇区（hadronic sectors）中的谱学规律；同时整理自旋未知或测定不佳的强子清单并进行自旋-宇称（spin-parity）指派研究。

Result: 由于宇称作为输入假设，模型给出的是在给定宇称条件下的 J 预测，而非独立确定完整的 J^P 组合；对若干已有理论研究的状态，预测结果与夸克模型（quark models）、QCD 求和规则（QCD sum rules）、分子模型（molecular models）及其他唯象方法吻合较好，而对另一些状态的预测与现有理论指派存在分歧。

Conclusion: 预测分歧为后续理论研究提供了动机，并可由未来实验测量加以检验；模型的价值在于以数据驱动方式提示可能被重新审视的自旋-宇称指派。

Abstract: Predicting the total angular momentum $J$ for hadronic resonances whose spins have not yet been experimentally determined constitutes an important step in the spectroscopy of observed hadrons. In the present study, we develop a data-driven model in order to predict the value of $ J $ for hadrons with unknown spins using a dataset of known mesons and baryons from the PDG, including information on their quark content, isospin, parity, mass and other spectroscopic features. In particular, we employ XGBoost classifiers along with nested optimization in order to discover spectroscopic patterns across different hadronic sectors. We also prepare a systematic list of hadrons with unknown or poorly determined values of $ J $, and study spin-parity assignments for those hadrons. Since the parity hypothesis is taken as an input to the model, our model predicts the value of $ J $ conditional on a given parity hypothesis rather than independently determining the full $J^P$ assignment. Our predictions for several theoretically studied states show good agreement with results from quark models, QCD sum rules, molecular models, and other phenomenological approaches, while predictions for some other states differ from existing theoretical assignments. These differences motivate further theoretical investigations and may be tested by future experimental measurements.

</details>


### [207] [Probing the QCD Conformal Window with Four-Quark Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18828)
*Junchen Rong,Emmanuel Stamou,Tom Steudtner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 Veneziano QCD 中计算四圈阶全部四夸克算符（含/不含手征对称性）及三圈阶六维胶子算符的反常维度，经共形展开与重求和后未发现复合算符有效描述与 QCD 不动点合并的确切证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类 QCD 理论的红外命运（infrared fate）及其共形窗口（conformal window，随味数与色数变化）仍属未解问题，且关联高能与凝聚态系统；共形相到对称破缺相的相变常伴随经典无关算符在红外变为相关变形这一特征。

Method: 在 Veneziano QCD 中计算所有四夸克算符（four-quark operators，含或不含手征对称性 chiral symmetry）的四圈（four-loop）反常维度（anomalous dimensions），以及三圈阶的六维胶子算符（dimension-six gluonic operator）；通过系统的共形展开（conformal expansion）导出标度维度（scaling dimensions），并采用多种重求和（resummation）技术改进结果并估计不确定度。

Result: 给出了四夸克算符与胶子算符的高阶反常维度与标度维度，并附带重求和改进及不确定度估计；未发现复合算符（composite operator）有效描述与 QCD 不动点（QCD fixed point）发生合并（merger）的具体证据。

Conclusion: 在所考察的算符范围内，不支持共形窗口边界处存在由经典无关算符驱动、表现为与 QCD 不动点合并的相变情景；相关红外行为仍需进一步探究。

Abstract: The infrared fate of QCD-like theories and their conformal window for different numbers or flavours and colour remains an open question with applications ranging from high-energy to condensed-matter systems. A hallmark of phase transitions from a conformal to symmetry breaking phase can be the presence of classically irrelevant operators that provide relevant deformation in the infrared. We investigate this scenario by computing anomalous dimensions of all four-quark operators, with or without chiral symmetry, at four-loop order as well as the dimension-six gluonic operator at three loops in Veneziano QCD. Scaling dimensions are derived in a systematic conformal expansion. These results are improved with various resummation techniques and we estimate their uncertainties. We find no concrete evidence for a composite operator effectively describing a merger with the QCD fixed point.

</details>


### [208] [An energy-dependent rescaling of the neutrino propagation Hamiltonian: Standard Model Extension identification, matter sector, and long-baseline degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23343)
*Neda Razzaghi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究将中微子传播哈密顿量乘以实标量因子 f(E)=1-η0(E/E0)^2 的后果，指出其真空部分可被重参数化吸收、物质部分为CPT-odd的dimension-five效应，主要可观测效应是Δm31的重标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最小长度（minimal-length）假设考察能量依赖的哈密顿量标度对中微子振荡的影响，并厘清其在标准模型扩展（Standard Model Extension, SME）框架下的算符维度与CPT性质。

Method: 将传播哈密顿量写为 H_η(E)=f(E)H_0(E)，分析其真空项与物质势项在SME中的维度、CPT性质，并对一般幂次 p 讨论算符维度；在常密度下把效应等价为 L→f(E)L，评估对混合角、Dirac相位、CP不对称度与Δm31的影响。

Result: 真空项为dimension-four、CPT-even、质量本征态对角项，可由重参数化移除；物质项为CPT-odd、dimension-five。仅 p=2 给出边缘（marginal）真空算符；p=0 被已测质量平方差吸收，非整数 p 无局域算符。η0=0.02、E0=2.5 GeV 时 |(cL)33|≈4×10^-24，与当前大气中微子界限相当；Planck压低情形给出 3.2×10^-58。1297 km 处，仅重拟合 Δm31 可吸收23%的概率平方差，四参数重拟合吸收41.5%。

Conclusion: 该变形在常密度下不改变混合角与Dirac相位，CP不对称度只是被重参数化而非产生；主导可观测效应为Δm31的重标度。文中未做统计分析，所称“简并”为概率空间中的距离而非置信区间。

Abstract: We study the consequences of multiplying the neutrino propagation Hamiltonian by a real, energy-dependent scalar, $H_η(E)=f(E)H_0(E)$ with $f(E)=1-η_0(E/E_0)^2$, a minimal-length ansatz. Its vacuum part is a dimension-four, CPT-even Lorentz-violating term of the Standard Model Extension with coefficient $(\cL)_{ii}=-η_0(m_i^2-m_1^2)/(2E_0^2)$, diagonal in the mass basis and fixed by the mass matrix, hence removable by a reparametrization. The matter-potential rescaling has dimension-five behaviour and is CPT-odd. For $f=1-η_0(E/E_0)^p$ the vacuum term behaves as a dimension $p+2$ operator and the matter term as $p+3$, so only $p=2$ gives a marginal vacuum operator; $p=0$ is absorbed into the measured mass splittings, and non-integer $p$ admits no local operator. For $η_0=0.02$ at $E_0=2.5$~GeV we find $|(\cL)_{33}|\simeq4\times10^{-24}$, comparable to present atmospheric bounds, whereas a Planck-suppressed deformation with $β_0=\mathcal{O}(1)$ gives $3.2\times10^{-58}$. At constant density it is equivalent to $L\to f(E)L$: mixing angles and the Dirac phase are unchanged, the CP asymmetry is reparametrized rather than generated, and the leading observable is a rescaling of $\dm_{31}$. At $1297$~km a re-fit of $\dm_{31}$ alone absorbs $23$~per cent of the squared probability difference, and a four-parameter re-fit absorbs $41.5$~per cent. No statistical analysis is performed; degeneracy means a distance in probability space, not a confidence interval.

</details>


### [209] [A New GUT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24671)
*Prateek Agrawal,Michael Nee,Mario Reig*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一类四维大统一理论（Grand Unified Theories），其标准模型规范扇区在统一群中的嵌入不经过 SU(5) 子群；最小实例基于 su(12) 规范代数，可无额外手征物质地给出三代费米子，并发展出 GUT bootstrap（大统一自举）以系统约束此类嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大统一理论通常经由 SU(5) 子群嵌入标准模型规范群，本文旨在探索不因子化通过 SU(5) 的新型嵌入方式，并系统刻画满足"三代、无额外手征物质"条件的统一群嵌入。

Method: 构造四维大统一模型，最小例子取规范代数 su(12)，其无反常谱恰好产生标准模型三代；同时发展 GUT bootstrap（大统一自举），用于约束满足三代且无额外手征物质的 SU(n) 嵌入，并针对至多两指标表示（up to two-index representations）中的统一费米子进行分类证明。

Result: 该嵌入预言轴子-光子反常比（axion-photon anomaly ratio）E/N=2/3，以及统一标度处弱混合角（weak mixing angle）sin²θ_W=1/2；规范介导的质子衰变（gauge-mediated proton decay）经由类翻转 SU(5)（flipped-SU(5)-like）算符进行，最小电荷光滑磁单极子（smooth monopole）携带两个狄拉克电荷单位（Dirac charge units）；整体规范群可为 SU(12) 或 SU(12)/ℤ₂，前者允许半整数电荷的色单态。分类结果表明，所有新嵌入均来自 SU(8m+4) 家族，包括 m≥2 的味道统一模型（flavor unified models）。

Conclusion: 不经过 SU(5) 因子的新型统一嵌入是可行的，且受"三代、无额外手征物质"条件严格约束，其解统一归属于 SU(8m+4) 系列；最小 su(12) 模型给出可检验的预言（E/N=2/3、sin²θ_W=1/2、特定质子衰变与磁单极子性质）。

Abstract: We present a new class of 4-dimensional Grand Unified Theories where the gauge sectors of the standard model have a novel embedding in the unified group that does not factor through an $SU(5)$ subgroup. The smallest example has gauge algebra $\frak{su}(12)$ and an anomaly-free spectrum that produces the three standard model generations with no additional chiral matter. The embedding predicts the axion-photon anomaly ratio $E/N=2/3$ and the weak mixing angle $\sin^2θ_W=1/2$ at the unification scale. Gauge-mediated proton decay proceeds through flipped-$SU(5)$-like operators and the minimally charged smooth monopole carries two Dirac charge units. The global gauge group can be $SU(12)$ or $SU(12)/\mathbb{Z}_2$, with the former allowing color singlets of half-integer electric charge. We develop a GUT bootstrap to constrain $SU(n)$ embeddings with three generations and no additional chiral matter. For unified fermions in up to two-index representations, we prove that all new embeddings arise from the $SU(8m+4)$ family, including flavor unified models for $m \geq 2$.

</details>
